Apotheosis – Ascension to Godhood
Vol.7

Chapter 601
"Not bad!" Luo Zheng praised.
The corners of Xia Shuang's mouth rose slightly as she smiled, "I'm still far from your level!"
After all, Xia Shuang was a level-10 warlord, and Luo Zheng had only been a level-10 warlord before. Even after refining the Light of Creation, Luo Zheng was only a level-1 warlord now.
However, Xia Shuang knew that she was far from being Luo Zheng's opponent.
Aside from Xia Shuang, the other human warriors on the tenth floor also gathered around. Even Xing Hui, who was mad with jealousy towards Luo Zheng, had a respectful expression on his face.
Now, not only had Luo Zheng shocked the entire Tower of Sin, but he had also alarmed the entire Purple Heart Holy Land.
When Luo Zheng first entered the Tower of Sin, the Purple Heart Holy Land believed that as long as Luo Zheng matured, he would be able to obtain a seat for the Human Race.
The Human Race was so weak that they would be very satisfied if they could obtain another seat in the Tower of Sin. After all, the four seats they currently had weren't easy to obtain.
As Luo Zheng climbed up, the strength and potential he displayed became greater and greater. The higher-ups of the Tower of Sin and the Purple Heart Holy Land paid more and more attention to Luo Zheng. Especially after Lord Jing met with Luo Zheng, he concluded that not only would Luo Zheng obtain a seat, but he would also obtain a seat at the front. At the very least, he would be able to obtain one of the top thirty seats out of the 128!
Lord Jing's evaluation was already very high! This was because ever since the Human Race entered the Tower of Sin, no one had been able to obtain a seat in the top fifty!
It was also from that moment onwards that the four seat holders at the top of the Tower of Sin paid close attention to Luo Zheng's movements. No one expected that Luo Zheng would have such a huge conflict with the Demon Race after coming out of the Innate Stele. He had even implicated the Demon Night Clan.
What was even more unexpected was that Luo Zheng had turned the entire situation around by himself. Even the two Battle-Saints that the Demon Race had kept in the Tower of Sin had fallen at Luo Zheng's hands …
Luo Zheng's performance had exceeded Lord Jing's evaluation time and time again. Of course, it had also exceeded the expectations of all the races on the Sea God Continent.
All the Holy Lands on the Sea God Continent had their eyes fixed on the Tower of Sin. Now, Luo Zheng's name had spread across the entire Sea God Continent in just a few days.
The Sea God Continent was the same as the Central Domain. Geniuses who had the potential to enter the Divine Sea Realm were all called God-Level Geniuses. However, since Luo Zheng's name had the word "Sky Rank" on the Talent Stele, Luo Zheng had naturally become a "Sky Rank Genius" in the mouths of millions of creatures!
Only Luo Zheng himself knew why he was called a Demi-Immortal. Most likely, Luo Zheng had been recognized by the Demi-Immortal Clan.
"Lord Jing told me to tell you something. Let's go to the residential area to talk," Xia Shuang said. This was naturally not a good place to talk.
Luo Zheng naturally knew that the Purple Heart Holy Land had a request for him. Luo Zheng's appearance was a great opportunity for the human race. The Holy Lands of the Sea God Continent's more than a dozen races circled around the Tower of Sin. Almost all of them were built around the Tower of Sin.
The strength of the Tower of Sin directly reflected the strength of the Holy Lands.
Just like how the Demon Race and the Demon Night Clan controlled the most seats, the Demon Race's Holy Lands and the Demon Night Clan's Holy Lands were the most powerful.
If Luo Zheng could help the human race obtain more than ten seats, the more seats the human race would have. The chances of entering the Mysterious Underworld Cave would also be higher. These things were practically of the same origin. All the races on the Sea God Continent had to pay attention to it!
Outside a secret room on the tenth floor of the Tower of Sin, several tenth rank human champions were standing guard.
In the secret room, under the faint yellow light, Xia Shuang's face was somewhat gentle. She did her best to make her smile more casual, but she was still slightly nervous.
It wasn't just Xia Shuang, Lord Jing, and the many War Reverends in the Tower of Sin, including the peak War Reverends and the four seat controllers, were all nervous and worried.
The reason was that Luo Zheng did not come from the Purple Heart Holy Land.
During this period of time, the Purple Heart Holy Land had launched a large-scale investigation on Luo Zheng. In the end, they had found a crucial piece of information. Luo Zheng had first appeared in the Heavenly Eagle Prairie!
The Purple Heart Holy Land had a faction stationed at the corner of the Heavenly Eagle Prairie. According to their feedback, Luo Zheng had suddenly appeared in the Heavenly Eagle Prairie without the Demon Race knowing. When Luo Zheng had first appeared, he had even rescued a group of people from the Demon Race's pasture!
The Heavenly Eagle Prairie was not a strategic location. Therefore, the Demon Race, the Human Race, and the Demon Night Clan had only sent a small portion of their forces to guard this place.
However, the three old monsters in the Divine Sea Realm in the Purple Heart Holy Land knew that there was actually an ancient teleportation array hidden in the Heavenly Eagle Prairie. The Human Race and the Demon Race had sent people into that teleportation array, but none of them had returned alive.
This ancient teleportation array must be a super long distance teleportation array.
Therefore, the humans in the Purple Heart Holy Land soon speculated that Luo Zheng did not come from the Sea God Continent. It was very likely that he came from another continent!
Luo Zheng was just a passerby. Although he was also a human, who could guarantee that he would be willing to help the Purple Heart Holy Land?
Moreover, the Sword Spirit that suddenly appeared next to Luo Zheng seemed to have a deep relationship with the Demon Night Clan. It was actually a king of the Demon Night Clan. Although the Demon Night Clan was fundamentally different from the Demon Race, they were not on good terms with the Human Race.
Everything depended on Luo Zheng's willpower and determination. Therefore, Master Jing gave this task to Xia Shuang. He wanted Xia Shuang to find out what Luo Zheng's intentions were.
Lord Jing even hinted that if it was possible, Xia Shuang had to make a sacrifice herself as well …
Xia Shuang was not a child. When she heard the word "sacrifice," she immediately blushed. The Human Race had been oppressed for so many years. It was not easy for them to grab onto a life-saving straw. They were indeed trembling with fear. They were afraid that Luo Zheng, this straw, would fly away.
As a god-level genius nurtured by the Purple Heart Holy Land, Xia Shuang could indeed "sacrifice" herself without hesitation. However, Xia Shuang knew that Luo Zheng was not someone who would do that. Master Jing and the higher-ups in the Purple Heart Holy Land were overthinking.
"What message did Master Jing ask you to send?" Seeing Xia Shuang's hesitation, Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Xia Shuang's eyes glittered. She was originally an extremely intelligent woman. After thinking for a while, she directly said, "Master Jing asked me to ask you. How far can you help us?"
Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows. He instantly understood why Xia Shuang was hesitating. However, this straightforwardness was in line with Luo Zheng's character. He laughed mischievously and said, "If possible, I will clear out all the demons from the Tower of Sin!"
Luo Zheng's words made Xia Shuang suck in a breath of cold air. "Clear out the demons from the Tower of Sin? Can you do that? "
Luo Zheng shrugged. "I can't be sure. I can only try my best!"
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Xia Shuang felt relieved. Although she had not been in contact with Luo Zheng for a long time, based on her understanding of Luo Zheng, he was not the kind of person who would speak without thinking. Unless he did not say anything, as long as he said something, he would do it.
"Right now, the demons should be divided internally. However, I suspect that they will look for you to negotiate," Xia Shuang said with a trace of worry on her face.
She only said half of her words, the other half was left unsaid. If the Devil Race promised Luo Zheng great benefits during the negotiation, would Luo Zheng change sides?
"Negotiate?" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows and said with a faint smile, "That will depend on their sincerity!"
The demons were indeed divided internally.
At the top of the Tower of Sin, there were a total of 128 spaces. Each space was a separate world, similar to the Sumeru Space in a Storage Ring. However, a Storage Ring could not store living things, while the spaces at the top of the Tower did not have this restriction.
Every seat holder had their own space. This was also the reason why there were always 128 seats at the top of the Tower of Sin.
Each space had a corresponding door, and each door had a number. For example, Ling Yan of the Yao Ye Clan was ranked first among the 128 seats, so her space was the first space.
As for Meng Tian, he was ranked second among the 128 seats, so his space was the second space.
During this period of time, the 26 seat holders of the fiend race would gather together every day, arguing endlessly!

Chapter 602
In Space 2, there was a huge mountain range. Lava flowed slowly on the mountain range, and hundreds of lava tributaries gathered together to form a huge lava waterfall!
The scarlet lava waterfall gathered in a lava pool at the bottom of the mountain range. The lava pool was like a demon's lair!
These spaces were actually equivalent to small chiliocosm.
Even a Divine Sea Realm Ranker was unable to create a small chiliocosm. Only the supreme experts in the upper realm could create a small chiliocosm.
These one hundred and twenty-eight spaces were equivalent to one hundred and twenty-eight small chiliocosm, and they originally existed in the Tower of Sin.
After obtaining the authority of this small chiliocosm, one could freely change the terrain of this small chiliocosm. One could even open up a mountain and create a pure land.
However, in this small chiliocosm, there was no Heaven and Earth Origin Qi, so it was impossible to cultivate.
Around the lava pool, there were twenty-six black stone pillars. The twenty-six seat holders of the Demon Clan were standing on top of the black stone pillars.
Meng Tian stood on the first stone pillar, his cold gaze swept across the area, and then said: "Luo Zheng has returned, but our opinions have not reached a consensus."
"We can just kill him directly! He is just a small war general. If we twenty-six Peak war reverends attack together, I'm afraid we can crush him into dust! "A Peak war reverend said sternly.
"Yes, if a war general can make us so afraid, where is the dignity of our Demon Clan? Where is our courage? "Another Peak war reverend echoed.
Meng Chong interrupted, "You guys are stronger than the two War Saints, Tianqiong and Tianhui? Even the two of them were unable to retaliate in front of Luo Zheng. Are you sure you can resist that brat? "
Meng Chong's words immediately caused many Peak war reverends to be speechless.
But immediately after, another Peak war reverend opened his mouth and said: "So what? Luo Zheng can indeed use the power of the Tower of Sin to kick us out of the Tower of Sin! But... Can he borrow this power to kill people? I don't think so! "
That day, Luo Zheng didn't personally kill the two Battle-Saints of the Demon Race. He only kicked them out of the Tower of Sin and borrowed the power of the Cursed Land to kill Tianqiong and Tianhui.
However, everyone present was a peak War Reverend, so they would not be affected by the Cursed Land. So what if they were kicked out of the Tower of Sin by Luo Zheng? They could easily climb up from the first floor of the Tower of Sin …
Furthermore, with so many Peak war elder s swarming forward, they did not believe that Luo Zheng would be able to kick them all out in one go!
That was the point of contention.
How much control does Luo Zheng have over the Tower of Sin?
Could he directly use the power of the Tower of Sin to kill people? Or was it only possible to kick them out?
Only Luo Zheng himself knew the answer to this question …
Because of this, the Peak War Reverends were divided into two factions and had an intense debate. One faction believed that they should use all their strength to kill Luo Zheng. As long as Luo Zheng died, everything would be over. They believed that even if Luo Zheng died, the Demon Race would not act blindly without thinking. As for the Human Race, they were not worthy of the Demon Race's scruples.
The other faction believed that Luo Zheng definitely had a hidden trump card. His control over the Tower of Sin was definitely beyond the imagination of the Demon Race. If they didn't kill Luo Zheng, the consequences would be unimaginable. They wouldn't even have the qualifications to negotiate.
"I have a compromise," said a short Peak War Reverend.
"Kuang Yuan, speak," said Meng Tian indifferently.
"Send the Night Clan's assassins to assassinate Luo Zheng again. This time, send the Night Clan's War Reverends," suggested the Peak War Reverend called Kuang Yuan.
"What kind of compromise is this!" sneered another Demon Race man.
Hearing his colleague's ridicule, Kuang Yuan's expression did not change. He continued, "Probe! Probe the extent of Luo Zheng's control over the Tower of Sin! "
If Luo Zheng could use the power of the Tower of Sin to kill people, then all the Peak War Reverends present would be fish on the chopping board. If even Battle Saints couldn't resist, then they wouldn't be able to resist!
But Luo Zheng could only kick people out of the Tower of Sin, so the Demon Race wouldn't be so worried about Luo Zheng …
Kuang Yuan pondered for a few days before coming up with this idea.
But just as he suggested it, Meng Tian shook his head and said, "I'm afraid it won't work. Luo Zheng isn't an idiot. Even if we assassinate him, it'll be very difficult to probe him. Moreover, the Demon Night Clan and the Human Race have secretly sent people to guard Luo Zheng. Even if the Night Clan's War Reverends are willing to sacrifice themselves for our Demon Race, it'll be very difficult for them to get close to Luo Zheng!"
"Then let's negotiate!" As soon as Meng Tian said this, another Demon Race War Reverend spoke up.
"Our Demon Race has long been unable to completely control the Tower of Sin. It's not impossible for us to give up some seats to the Human Race!"
"I just don't know what that brat wants! I feel that giving the Human Race five seats is enough! "
Many Peak War Reverends voiced their opinions, while Meng Tian stood on the stone pillar with his eyes closed. After everyone finished speaking, Meng Tian opened his eyes and said indifferently, "The Twin Devil Holy Land has already given their opinion. They want us to negotiate with that brat."
When Meng Tian said this, many Peak War Reverends couldn't help but roll their eyes.
Since the Holy Land had already given their opinion, why ask for their opinion? Did they have nothing better to do?
Although the Holy Land's hands couldn't reach into the Tower of Sin, the Holy Land was the foundation of the Demon Race. There would be a day when the Peak War Reverends would leave the Tower of Sin. Many things were still controlled by the Holy Land.
"Then let's negotiate!"
"I hope that brat doesn't push his luck!"
"He dares? Even if he can summon the wind and rain in the Tower of Sin, he can summon the wind and rain in the Purple Heart Holy Land. Outside the Cursed Lands, our Twin Devil Holy Land can easily destroy a small Purple Heart Holy Land! "
This was where the Demon Race's confidence came from. Even if Luo Zheng really became the controller of the Tower of Sin, he couldn't affect the outside of the Cursed Lands. The Purple Heart Holy Land couldn't resist the Demon Race's army!
Luo Zheng wasn't in a hurry to reach the eleventh floor of the Tower of Sin.
Climbing to the top of the tower wasn't Luo Zheng's goal. His goal in entering the Tower of Sin was to cultivate and to obtain the inheritance of the Mysterious Underworld Cave. Staying on the tenth floor would allow him to obtain a large amount of Light of Creation, which was more than enough for Luo Zheng to cultivate. There wasn't much meaning in advancing too early.
However, the first few days were still fine. Although Luo Zheng defeated Yue Shou and Mu Qing Yang, there were still many Level 10 Champions who challenged Luo Zheng.
In this world, there would never be a lack of arrogant warriors. In their opinion, Yue Shou couldn't defeat Luo Zheng, and Mu Qing Yang couldn't defeat Luo Zheng, but that didn't mean they couldn't defeat him.
On the first day after returning to the Tower of Sin, three Champions were defeated by Luo Zheng. On the second day, another three Champions were defeated by Luo Zheng. On the third day, a War Reverend challenged Luo Zheng.
It was a Beastman Race's Level 2 War Reverend.
Normally, very few creatures on the Sea God Continent would enter the Tower of Sin after reaching the War Reverend rank.
This Beastman War Reverend seemed to be an independent warrior who didn't come from the Beastman Race's Holy Land. This kind of independent warrior only focused on cultivating and didn't pay attention to the outside world.
However, once a War Reverend entered the Tower of Sin, he would be able to advance freely from the first floor to the tenth floor. He would only encounter obstacles on the eleventh floor!
This Beastman Race's Level 2 War Reverend didn't seem to know about Luo Zheng's deeds. From the first floor to the tenth floor, he only stayed on each floor for three days. When he reached the tenth floor and saw Luo Zheng win in the arena, no creature dared to challenge Luo Zheng again.
The Beastman War Reverend sneered, "A Level 1 War Champion of the Human Race? Heh! "
After saying that, he casually walked onto the arena.
Seeing a War Reverend challenge Luo Zheng, many Level 10 Champions on the tenth floor became interested. Mu Qing Yang, Xia Shuang, and the others also stood on the spectator stands.
However, looking at the Beastman in front of him, Luo Zheng frowned.
"Human brat, you're not satisfied with me as an opponent?" The Beastman War Reverend showed an unhappy expression. His bark-like face had a sneer.
"Indeed, I'm not satisfied. You climbed up without any Light of Creation …" Luo Zheng stayed on the tenth floor with the purpose of earning Light of Creation. The Beastman War Reverend in front of him didn't have any Light of Creation in his nameplate, so it was useless even if he won.
The Beastman War Reverend's face suddenly erupted with hostility. Luo Zheng's tone made it clear that he could defeat him. He suddenly grinned and said, "A few years ago, I ate human flesh. It was very delicious. If I defeat you, leave your flesh to me!"
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 603
This was the first time Luo Zheng had faced a war reverend after becoming a champion. Moreover, it was a second rank war reverend.
However, Mu Qing Yang and the other members of the Yao Ye Clan, as well as Xia Shuang and the other humans, weren't too worried.
When Luo Zheng was a tenth rank fighter, he had defeated Yue Shou, who was a tenth rank warrior. Everyone knew of Yue Shou's abilities. As the strongest god-ranked genius of the Twin Demon Holy Land, even a beginner war reverend might not be a match for him.
The beast race war reverend seemed impatient. As soon as he finished speaking, he unsheathed his two giant axes and charged at Luo Zheng.
The beastmen were short. The beast race war reverend in front of Luo Zheng didn't even reach Luo Zheng's chest. However, their bodies were sturdy, and their vitality was far stronger than other races. Fatal wounds to other races were nothing to the beastmen. Their recovery ability was also far stronger than other races.
Facing the beast race's charge, Luo Zheng had a calm smile on his face. He took out a longsword from his spatial ring!
Everyone was stunned when they saw the longsword in Luo Zheng's hand.
"Did this kid change his weapon again?"
"Wasn't it a low-grade saint grade longsword before? His current sword … "
"High-grade, a high-grade saint grade longsword. This guy's worth, oh my god …"
Even for the Holy Land, high-grade Saint Grade weapons were rare existences that could only be found by chance.
As a powerhouse of the Yao Ye Clan, Liu Yu naturally wouldn't lie to Xun. This sword and the weapon Liu Yu used were the only high-grade saint artifacts left in the Yao Ye Clan.
In the Sea God Continent, only the Flame Demon Holy Land and the Fear Demon Holy Land each had a high-grade saint artifact. The Sky Feather Holy Land only had two high-grade saint artifacts. The human race's Purple Heart Holy Land had a few mid-grade saint artifacts, but they didn't have a single high-grade saint artifact.
"Roar!"
As the beast race war reverend charged, the vital essence in his body erupted, releasing a black brilliance. At the same time, he let out a bestial roar.
Then, the battle-ax in his hand was thrown out. It circled in the air, and drew an arc in the air. Then, it strangled Luo Zheng from behind!
The Beast Race's War Senior also held another battle suit in his hand and slashed at Luo Zheng.
This way, Luo Zheng would have to face a pincer attack from the Beast Race's War Reverends. With the power of this battle axe, if he wasn't careful, he might be split in two.
However, Luo Zheng was very calm. He didn't attack with his sword. Instead, he suddenly somersaulted in the air. It was as if he had eyes on his back. He accurately dodged the battle axe that was attacking him from behind. At the same time, he kicked out heavily!
The originally spiraling battle axe's speed more than doubled as it continued to fly towards the Beast Clan's war elder.
Unexpectedly, the Beast Race Reverend grinned and caught his battle axe with a flip of his hand. Before Luo Zheng landed on the ground, the Beast Race Reverend released his Vital Spirit a second time and quickly approached Luo Zheng.
"Savage Slash!"
The huge battle axe waved out rays of golden light, twisting Luo Zheng's figure into pieces. While twisting, the Beast Race Reverend roared, "Break into pieces for me, wahahaha! You can't even withstand a single blow! "
The Beast Race was like this. Once they started fighting, they would be very crazy!
In an instant, the Beast Race Reverend slashed out dozens of times with his axe. Only when he stopped panting did he realize that Luo Zheng was standing not far away, safe and sound. He was staring at him with a faint smile on his face.
This was the power of the Law of Space. It could directly travel through space. No matter how powerful the movement technique was, it couldn't compare to this!
"It seems that Luo Zheng's war elder is not weak at all!"
"I'm afraid that even an ordinary mid-stage war elder might not be able to take down Luo Zheng!"
"In that case, only a peak war elder can suppress Luo Zheng in the Sin Tower!"
In fact, many creatures had overestimated Luo Zheng. After all, Luo Zheng was only a first stage war general. The main reason was that this second stage war elder was an independent warrior and not from the Beast Race Sacred Land.
According to the conventions of the Central Region, independent cultivators were generally stronger than cultivators from sects. This was because independent cultivators relied entirely on their own abilities to climb up step by step. On the other hand, although sect cultivators had the legacies of sects, their combat experience was far inferior to independent cultivators.
However, the Sea God Continent was the complete opposite. At the level of the Sacred Land, inheritance was more important than battle experience.
Moreover, most of the cultivators on the Sea God's Continent had climbed up from the Tower of Sin by stepping over the corpses of various races. Even the cultivators from the Holy Land did not lack battle experience. Therefore, the independent cultivators on the Sea God's Continent were far inferior to the various geniuses in the Holy Land.
Therefore, it was easier for Luo Zheng to deal with this second stage war elder than when he was in the Moon Guardian Formation!
However, if he were to face a war elder from the Sacred Land, Luo Zheng estimated that a second stage war elder or even a third stage war elder would be Luo Zheng's limit. Beyond the fourth stage, Luo Zheng would probably have to choose to avoid the battle or even run away. After all, the difference in cultivation was too great.
This was also the reason why Luo Zheng did not choose to advance. At the eleventh floor of the Sin Tower, Luo Zheng could indeed earn some points. However, if he were to encounter some difficult war elders, it would be quite troublesome for Luo Zheng.
There might be more Light of Creation on the eleventh floor, but it would not be much more than the tenth floor. Therefore, accumulating Light of Creation on the tenth floor was a wise move for Luo Zheng.
Hearing those discussions, it was clear that they were not a match for this first stage war general. The beastmen had a fiery temper, so how could they endure it?
The war elder's eyes turned red. Layers of vital essence immediately wrapped around his battle axe. At the same time, two balls of red light appeared in his hands!
"It's Berserk Transformation. This war elder has been completely enraged!"
"Once the beastmen enter Berserk Transformation, they will not rest until they are dead!"
"You just realized that the beastmen are all brainless? If they had some brains, their strength would definitely be on par with the demon race! "
Many of the spectators' comments were correct.
The beastmen were ranked fourth on the Sea God Continent.
However, the beastmen were not inferior to the demon race in terms of talent. They were just much smaller than the demon race, but they had a miraculous healing ability. Their speed was also not inferior to the demon race. In terms of pure physical speed, the beastmen were probably second only to the demon race.
However, the beastmen had a fatal problem. Their brains were not very good. Simply put, their intelligence was very low. They were especially easily angered or used as weapons.
When the beastmen entered Berserk Transformation, their strength would multiply. However, the downside of Berserk Transformation was obvious. Once they entered Berserk Transformation, they would not be able to control themselves unless their target died or they themselves died …
Looking at the two balls of red light, Luo Zheng's expression turned serious. No matter how weak this Beast Race War Reverend was, he was still a War Reverend. The difference of a large realm couldn't be completely ignored!
"Die! Roar! "
The war elder roared like a wild beast once more. Like a rolling boulder, he charged at Luo Zheng once more.
This time, Luo Zheng did not choose to dodge. The longsword in his hand began to bloom with a faint golden light. Boundless sword intent spread from the blade!
"Universe Rotation!"
When the Beast Race War Reverend was three meters away from Luo Zheng, the Beast Race War Reverend started spinning rapidly like a top.
Two demonic red lights constantly circled around his arms. Suddenly, they looked like a red wheel of light. Together with the shining light of the Vital Essence of the battle axe, they looked exceptionally beautiful!
As long as one was twisted into this "top", the only outcome one could imagine was being smashed to pieces.
Although Xia Shuang believed that Luo Zheng could defeat this war elder, she did not expect Luo Zheng to choose to meet the enemy head on!
For Xia Shuang to be able to stand firm on the tenth floor, she had naturally fought with the beasts before. She had defeated three tenth level beasts, but that did not mean that Xia Shuang was stronger than the beasts. The reason was still the same as mentioned before. Although the beasts were extremely strong and had astonishing recovery abilities, they were generally more stupid.
Xia Shuang only needed to constantly dodge and slowly exhaust the beastmen on the stage. Even if the other party entered Berserk Transformation, they would not be able to do anything to the nimble Xia Shuang.
If she were to fight a beastmen head on, not to mention rank 10 beastmen, even rank 8 beastmen, or even rank 7 beastmen, Xia Shuang was not certain she could win.

Chapter 604
Facing the berserk Beast Race War Reverend, Luo Zheng did not choose to retreat.
Luo Zheng was bound to climb up the Tower of Sin and encounter more and more War Reverends in the future. Although Luo Zheng's cultivation was also increasing, he had to measure his true strength.
If he wanted to go further on the path of martial artists, he had to make ample preparations and plans. Luo Zheng's destiny was strong, but luck would not always be on his side.
Facing this berserk Beast Race War Reverend, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. He lightly flicked his sword and jumped onto the other party's battle-ax.
But just as the sword and battle-ax touched, Luo Zheng felt a powerful force pass over.
If it were anyone else here, their sword would have already left their hands!
Even Luo Zheng felt his hand go numb from the shock.
Ding ding ding ding …
The Beast Race War Reverend's two battle-axes alternately struck Luo Zheng's sword, producing crisp sounds.
But as the battle-ax and sword intersected, Luo Zheng's sword quickly left sword marks on the large battle-ax.
This Beast Race War Reverend was originally an independent martial artist, so he naturally did not have any good weapons. These two battle-axes could barely be considered high-grade immortal artifacts, but how could they block Luo Zheng's high-grade sacred artifacts?
By the time the Beast Race War Reverend's attack dissipated, the battle-axes in his hands were already riddled with holes.
But what Luo Zheng did not expect was that the Beast Race War Reverend casually threw away the battle-axes and suddenly roared.
Layers of astral essence covered the Beast Race War Reverend's body in an extremely orderly manner, like a layer of shining black scales.
"Another transformation? This is beast transformation! "
Compared to War Generals, War Reverends had endless methods. Luo Zheng had once seen Ao Xiang use this move in the Central Domain. But the Beast Race War Reverend's bloodline was originally inherited from wild beasts, so most Beast Race people were born with the ability to transform!
After the Beast Race War Reverend's scales were completely covered, he quickly pounced at Luo Zheng.
"You want to block my sword with just a layer of scales?"
How sharp was a high-grade saint artifact? Facing the Beast Race War Reverend's charge, Luo Zheng slashed out with his sword.
According to Luo Zheng's expectations, this layer of scales would be as fragile as a piece of paper before Luo Zheng's high-grade saint artifact. But he never imagined that as soon as Luo Zheng's sword touched the scales, a powerful force would suddenly erupt from the black scales!
It was this force that caused Luo Zheng's sword to bounce away.
"Boom!"
The moment Luo Zheng's long sword bounced off, this Beast Race War Venerable crashed into Luo Zheng.
War Reverend, moreover, it was a Beast Race War Reverend. How fierce would that be? With this collision, Luo Zheng was sent flying thirty feet away. He fell heavily on the ground, and his long sword also fell to the side.
Xia Shuang and the others revealed worried expressions. Originally, they were very confident in Luo Zheng, but they did not expect this Beast Race War Reverend to be so difficult to deal with.
"It seems that Luo Zheng is still in trouble against a War Reverend."
"We overestimated Luo Zheng. The gap between a War Reverend and a War Reverend is not so easy to cross!"
"Luo Zheng might have a backup plan …"
After this collision, Luo Zheng was indeed in a very sorry state. The War Reverend's Vital Essence erupted, and that force was not to be underestimated.
However, Luo Zheng's Qi and blood were in turmoil. Streams of Primordial Chaos Dipper continued to circulate, so Luo Zheng was not injured.
"I was careless." Luo Zheng got up from the ground with a calm expression. The scales on the Beast Race War Reverend's body were just an illusion. He was waiting for Luo Zheng's long sword to strike, then suddenly explode and bounce off Luo Zheng's sword. Then, he would immediately launch another attack.
The Beast Race War Reverend exuded a ferocious aura as he sneered. "Human brat, I want to eat your flesh!"
The Beast Race War Reverend, who had gone berserk and transformed into a beast, had completely transformed into a wild ferocious beast. He once again charged toward Luo Zheng!
Everyone's hearts suddenly tightened. Luo Zheng had already lost his weapon. How could he deal with the powerful Beast Race War Reverend when he was unarmed? At this time, the right choice was to pull away from the opponent first.
However, under everyone's astonished gazes, Luo Zheng still did not retreat. He suddenly clenched his fists and began to accumulate power in his body. The moment the Demon Race War Reverend charged forward, all his dragon scales opened at the same time!
A tidal wave of power converged on Luo Zheng's fist. Every vein on his right fist bulged. If it were not for his high-grade immortal artifact body, his entire hand would have been crushed by his own power under such a powerful surge of power!
This punch was headed straight for the Beast Race War Reverend!
Seeing this scene, everyone shook their heads.
In the previous battle, many people were already clear about Luo Zheng's strength. If he used tricks, Luo Zheng's chances of defeating the Beast Race War Reverend were very high. However, Luo Zheng chose to use his weakness to attack the other party's strength.
"I'm afraid Luo Zheng's hand is going to be crippled." Someone suddenly said.
As soon as he finished speaking, Luo Zheng's punch had already landed heavily on the Beast Race War Reverend's chest. The Beast Race War Reverend's chest was also covered with a layer of black scales, but under this punch, the black scales suddenly exploded and were unable to bounce off Luo Zheng's fist!
Luo Zheng's full strength exploded out. Even a peak war reverend would be seriously injured if he received this punch. How could the Beast Race War Reverend's move offset Luo Zheng's ferocious power?
"Bam!"
With this punch, half of the Beast Race War Reverend's body instantly caved in, and his entire body seemed to have been squeezed through a narrow door!
Although the Beast Race War Reverend still maintained his momentum and rushed a few steps to the side, his footsteps were already staggering and he was unable to stand up. With a bang, he fell to the ground, and a large amount of blood burst out from his body.
The vitality of the Beast Race was exuberant, and the amount of blood in their bodies was far more than that of other living beings. However, this kind of gurgling blood was completely absorbed by the Tower of Sin.
"Swish swish …"
A few weak rays of Light of Good Fortune spiraled out from the Beast Race War Reverend's nameplate and shot towards Luo Zheng's chest.
Although the Beast Race War Reverend came up empty, he would take the initiative to challenge others every time he stepped onto a new floor. Therefore, it wasn't that there weren't any Light of Good Fortune in the nameplate, it was just that there weren't many of them.
Seeing Luo Zheng kill the Beast Race War Reverend with a single punch, Luo Zheng's strength had clearly risen to another level in the eyes of many creatures.
"Not only is that brat proficient in Laws, his strength and physique aren't weak either!"
"A cultivator who cultivates both Law and Body would normally find it very difficult to balance both. In the end, they would end up as a nobody. Luo Zheng doesn't have this problem at all!"
"I'm really looking forward to the day when he becomes a War Reverend! At that time, even if he doesn't use the power of the Tower of Sin, no one in the Tower of Sin will be his match! "
Luo Zheng put away his sword. Just as he walked down the stage, a Demon Race War Reverend walked towards him from afar. It was a Rank 6 War Reverend.
The instant the Demon Race War Reverend appeared, Xia Shuang's gaze flickered, and she looked towards another direction. Mu Qing Yang did the same.
Although Luo Zheng could control the power of the Tower of Sin, and it was more than enough to protect himself, the Yao Night Clan and the Human Race were still worried and secretly sent people to protect Luo Zheng. At this time, no one wanted anything to happen to Luo Zheng.
"Luo Zheng!" The Demon Race War Reverend called out Luo Zheng's name as he approached.
"What's the matter?" Luo Zheng asked indifferently.
"Our Demon Race's Lord Meng Tian wants to talk to you." The Demon Race War Reverend said.
"When?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"Tonight …"
"…"
The Demon Race War Reverend naturally came to help Meng Tian pass on the message. After completing his task, the Demon Race War Reverend turned around and left. At this time, Mu Qing Yang and Xia Shuang walked up together.
Luo Zheng smiled at Xia Shuang, "You guessed right, they're already in a hurry to negotiate."
"I, I'll go inform Lord Ling Yan!" Mu Qing Yang immediately said.
No one could guarantee that Meng Tian wouldn't play any tricks. As the second seat holder of the Tower of Sin, if he suddenly attacked Luo Zheng, it would be a huge threat to Luo Zheng. And the only person who could intimidate Meng Tian was Ling Yan.
Mu Qing Yang informed Ling Yan, and Xia Shuang naturally informed Lord Jing. At night, many peak War Reverends came down from the upper levels. The four seat holders of the Human Race were all present, and Ling Yan of the Yao Night Clan and the other three seat holders also came down.
Since the Demon Race had chosen to negotiate, the content of tonight's negotiation was especially important.

Chapter 605
In a certain hall of the tenth floor's living area, all unrelated people were cleared out.
Although according to the rules, one could freely enter and exit the safe area, these peak war reverends were the ones who made the rules. Moreover, at this point, why would they care about the rules?
As for some of the generals on the tenth floor, although they had some complaints, how could they dare to say anything in front of these peak war reverends?
In the hall, Meng Tian sat boldly on the side. The demon race was incomparably tall, and Meng Tian was one of the bigger ones. Even if he was sitting, he was still taller than ordinary humans.
Behind Meng Tian stood three peak war reverends. Their expressions were cold and their auras were like blades.
The death of two war saints was a huge blow to the demon race. Naturally, they would not feel good about it!
What was even more troublesome was Luo Zheng. He had put the demon race in a dilemma.
Zhao Fenqin and Zhou Zhuohe stood guard beside Luo Zheng. Even if Luo Zheng could control the Tower of Sin, they still had to guard against Meng Tian's sudden attack.
Fortunately, Ling Yan also brought three peak war reverends from the Yao Ye clan. If the demon race really wanted to fight here, they would not be able to gain any advantage.
After everyone sat down, Meng Tian smiled and said, "As expected of a young hero. For thousands of years, no one has been able to completely control the Tower of Sin, but you did it. I didn't believe in fate before, but now that I think about it, this is fate!"
Luo Zheng smiled faintly, "I still don't believe in fate. I only believe that man can conquer the heavens."
"You humans have a good saying. A person's strength is limited. Sometimes, you have to know how to act appropriately and judge the situation. Otherwise, you will be the one to suffer in the end." Meng Tian suddenly smiled.
Meng Tian was indirectly reminding Luo Zheng. Perhaps Luo Zheng could gain the upper hand in the Tower of Sin by joining forces with the Yao Ye clan. It was even possible for them to expel the demon race from the tower. However, one could not ignore the existence of the Flame Demon Holy Land and the Fear Demon Holy Land!
Luo Zheng laughed mischievously and did not refute Meng Tian's words.
Then Meng Tian said, "But I'm curious. How much control do you have over the Tower of Sin? Legend has it that this Tower of Sin is actually a divine weapon of an almighty being from the Upper Realm. Have you refined it? "
There were many speculations about the Tower of Sin. Some said that it was a cultivation tower of a certain race, while others said that it was a divine weapon of a peerless expert.
No matter what kind of speculation it was, Meng Tian's words were just to test the extent of Luo Zheng's control over the Sin Tower!
In Meng Tian's opinion, Luo Zheng would most likely avoid talking about it.
However, Luo Zheng nodded and said, "I didn't refine this Tower of Sin, but …" Luo Zheng waved his hand and the walls around the hall began to disappear layer by layer!
This hall was originally located at the periphery of the living area, and there were many secret cultivation rooms around it. However, at this moment, the walls began to dissolve and disappear.
Some of the warlords who were cultivating in the secret cultivation rooms were still sitting inside, calmly cultivating. Suddenly, the disappearance of the walls around the secret cultivation rooms gave them a fright. Seeing several peak warlords sitting in the hall, the warlords who were cultivating revealed panicked expressions.
Seeing Luo Zheng's methods, the peak warlords of the Demon Clan revealed grave expressions. Meng Tian's gaze also became deep.
Luo Zheng appeared to be very confident. Since you want to test me, then I'll show you.
Although he did not refine the Tower of Sin, Luo Zheng could indeed control everything in the Tower of Sin through the tool spirit!
He did not need to hide anything. He simply told the other party that he could control everything in the Tower of Sin. Wanting to fight against him in the Tower of Sin was undoubtedly wishful thinking!
Ling Yan and Zhao Fenqin's eyes flashed with a bright light. With Luo Zheng's degree of control over the Tower of Sin, the Demon Clan and Luo Zheng simply did not have the capital to bargain.
Meng Tian stretched out his huge palm-leaf fan hand and clapped, "Even if you did not refine the Tower of Sin, to be able to do this is already unimaginable! However … "
"However?" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows and his eyes flashed with an extremely aggressive gaze, "However, you guys have been trying to kill me before. How should we settle this debt?"
Meng Tian chuckled, "You are indeed a straightforward person. In this negotiation, I actually hope that everyone can coexist peacefully!"
Seeing Meng Tian's tone and expression, Ling Yan and Zhao Fenqin sighed in their hearts. When did Meng Tian ever mention anything about coexisting peacefully with others?
Even if Meng Tian lost to Ling Yan on the top floor of the Tower of Sin, Meng Tian had never restrained himself. Not only Meng Tian, the Demon Clan in the Tower of Sin had never restrained themselves!
Aside from the Demon Night Clan, the Demon Clan had never been willing to back down when they had disputes with other clans!
Even if it was the Demon Night Clan, Meng Tian would not give face to them. Six months ago, Meng Tian and Ling Yan had a dispute. That dispute was not big, but Meng Tian had the attitude of a fish dying net breaking a net. At that time, Ling Yan had considered the bigger picture and did not want to make a big fuss over such a small matter. In the end, Ling Yan had backed down.
Meng Tian's personality had always been rude and unreasonable. Asking him to talk about coexisting fairly with other clans was harder than ascending to the heavens. However, today, Meng Tian had finally compromised. He had actually decided to back down!
Zhao Fenqin and Zhou Zhuohe's hearts were beating wildly.
Since the Demon Clan had chosen to coexist peacefully, they would at least use some conditions to appease Luo Zheng. What was the most valuable thing in the Tower of Sin? The opportunity of the Mysterious Nether Cave!
Of course, it was impossible to obtain the opportunity of the Mysterious Nether Cave in the Tower of Sin. However, if they could obtain a seat, they would be able to lead more clansmen into the Mysterious Nether Cave, and the possibility of obtaining the opportunity would be greater!
Just the Mysterious Nether Cave, how many clans had their opportunities changed?
Whether it was the establishment of the Purple Heart Holy Land or the Heavenly Feather Holy Land, they were all martial artists with heaven-defying luck who had obtained great opportunities in the Mysterious Nether Cave. Only then were they able to establish their own Holy Land step by step.
"Three seats, I'm satisfied with just three seats!" Zhao Fenqin thought to herself.
In Zhao Fenqin's heart, if the Demon Clan was willing to give up three seats, they would be burning incense to the gods! As for Zhou Zhuohe, he looked at Zhao Fenqin's somewhat excited expression. His thoughts were almost exactly the same as Zhao Fenqin's. However, while Zhao Fenqin wanted three seats, Zhou Zhuohe's thoughts were even more unbearable. He would be satisfied with two seats …
In contrast, Ling Yan, who was ranked first among the 128 seats, naturally had much higher standards than Zhao Fenqin and Zhou Zhuohe.
Now that Luo Zheng had the absolute advantage, if she didn't follow up on this victory, then she would be a fool. How many seats were the Demon Clan willing to give up would depend on the Demon Clan's bottom line and Luo Zheng's request.
If it was in her place, Ling Yan's lowest request would be ten seats!
"As a show of sincerity for peaceful coexistence, our Demon Clan can give up five seats to your Human Clan." Meng Tian smiled and said.
The peak War Reverends behind Meng Tian all revealed indignant expressions. Five seats, and to give it up to a trash race like the Human Clan. To them, this was indeed very sullen. Moreover, the Demon Clan had lost five seats, which meant that five seat controllers would lose their seats!
Meng Tian's words immediately caused Zhao Fenqin and Zhou Zhuohe's hearts to beat faster!
This condition was indeed not bad. Five seats, in an instant, the Human Clan had 25 more seats! In addition to the previous four seats, the Human Clan had a total of nine seats. Although they weren't ranked third in the Sin Tower, they were still ranked fourth!
At this time, Zhao Fenqin and Zhou Zhuohe's eyes seemed to sparkle like little stars. They really wanted to agree to Meng Tian's request in place of Luo Zheng! Moreover, they couldn't wait to report this "good news" back to the Purple Heart Holy Land!
After all, when the Purple Heart Holy Land first paid attention to Luo Zheng, they only hoped that he would be able to obtain a seat in the future.
They never expected that Luo Zheng, who hadn't even climbed to the top of the tower, would actually help the Human Clan obtain five seats on the tenth floor! This was something that they didn't even dare to think about!
"Hurry up, boy!" Zhao Fenqin eagerly hoped that Luo Zheng would quickly agree.
Zhou Zhuohe's eyes were about to drip water. The Demon Clan had accumulated power for a long time. To take the initiative to give up five seats was already a great favor. Therefore, he also eagerly hoped that Luo Zheng would agree. Otherwise, what if Meng Tian went back on his word?
Zhao Fenqin and Zhou Zhuohe's expressions were seen by Ling Yan from the bottom of her heart. She couldn't help but sigh in her heart. The Human Clan had been weak for many years and didn't have any future prospects. Fortunately, they had Luo Zheng. This matter clearly showed that they could bargain. These two guys were actually so excited after hearing about five seats …

Chapter 606
Everyone's eyes finally gathered on Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng's expression was calm, as if he was pondering.
Looking at Luo Zheng's expression, Meng Tian and the other War Reverends of the Demon Clan all suppressed their impulses and waited for him to speak.
Most of these War Reverends had lived for decades, or even hundreds of years. At this time, they were actually staring at each other, waiting for a young man who was not even twenty years old to speak. This was indeed a very strange thing.
The Demon Clan had a fiery temper to begin with. A Peak War Reverend behind Meng Tian finally couldn't hold it in any longer and said coldly, "Boy, have you considered it? Five seats is already enough to give you Human Clan face! "
This Peak War Reverend was called Mo Qi, and he was one of the ten Peak War Reverends of the Demon Clan. He was ranked fourth among the 128 seats, and his strength and status were second only to Meng Tian.
Luo Zheng glanced at the Peak War Reverend indifferently. He was still deep in thought, and there was even a trace of ridicule at the corner of his mouth.
"Don't be so shameless. When the time comes, let alone five seats, even if it's just one seat, we will still have to consider it carefully!" Seeing Luo Zheng's smile, the fire in Mo Qi's heart was ignited.
The Demon Clan was easily angered. This was almost their nature.
Seeing that the situation was not good, Zhao Fenqin and Zhou Zhuohe's faces immediately became anxious. What was Luo Zheng thinking!
However, at this moment, they couldn't speak. Since it was a negotiation, they couldn't let the other party see that they didn't have any confidence.
In the end, Zhao Fenqin couldn't hold it in and quietly said to Luo Zheng with a True Essence Sound Transmission, "Luo Zheng, why don't you agree with them? Five seats, we are already very satisfied!"
Zhao Fenqin's True Essence Sound Transmission entered Luo Zheng's ears, but Luo Zheng acted as if he didn't hear it.
"Boy …" Mo Qi finally couldn't hold it in and suddenly sat up from his chair.
At this moment, Luo Zheng stared at Meng Tian and said, "Peaceful coexistence?"
Then Luo Zheng sneered and said coldly, "When I entered the first layer, you didn't intend to coexist peacefully with me. When I entered the second layer, you didn't intend to coexist peacefully with me. The third layer, the fourth layer, until I entered the tenth layer and came out of the Talent Stele, you still didn't intend to coexist peacefully with me. When you brought out two Battle-Saints, I used the Cursed Land to curse them to death. Now, you're talking to me about peaceful coexistence? "
Upon hearing Luo Zheng's words, Zhao Fenqin and Zhou Zhuohe immediately panicked. It seemed that they had guessed wrong. Originally, Zhao Fenqin had thought that Luo Zheng wanted to bargain for more benefits for the Human Race.
What he didn't expect was that Luo Zheng didn't seem to have this intention at all. Then what did he want to do?
Ling Yan's face exposed a trace of a strange expression, and her gaze began to intensely flicker. It seems that this brat's appetite is even bigger than I thought! That's right, he is the subordinate of our King. How broad is his vision? He didn't even set his sights on a small Tower of Sin. Even the vastness of the Sea God Continent didn't allow him to display his strength.
This human youth's future stage would definitely be the entire universe!
If she looked at it from this angle, Ling Yan was also indifferent.
From beginning to end, Meng Tian had a smile on his face. He had already tried his best to make himself look as friendly as possible. After Luo Zheng finished speaking, Meng Tian's face gradually became gloomy, "Since you don't intend to coexist peacefully, then what do you want?"
The corner of Luo Zheng's mouth rose slightly, "The demons will give up all their seats and scram out of the Tower of Sin!"
Just as these words were spoken, Zhao Fenqin and the others almost stopped breathing. Ling Yan slightly lowered her body, guarding against these Battle-Saints of the Demon Race. Not only Ling Yan, but the other Battle-Saints of the Demon Night Clan also revealed vigilant expressions. At the same time, their hearts were wildly beating.
This Luo Zheng was also a guy who wouldn't stop until he shocked them to death. He had come with sincerity to negotiate, but you actually wanted all the demons to scram out of the Tower of Sin? How was this possible?
Meng Tian was still sitting steadily on the wide chair. Although his face was gloomy, there was no trace of anger on it.
On the contrary, the few Peak Battle-Saints of the Demon Race behind Meng Tian could no longer hold it in, especially the number two figure of the Demon Race, Mo Qi. His face was already incomparably angry, and his majestic aura erupted uncontrollably. It was possible that he could attack at any time.
Other than Mo Qi, the chests of the other Peak Battle-Saints were also undulating. They stared at Luo Zheng, as if they wanted to swallow Luo Zheng in one gulp.
Seeing that the Peak Battle-Saints behind Meng Tian were about to attack, Meng Tian waved his hand and stopped their thoughts. Then, he said in a muffled voice, "You want us to give up the Tower of Sin? That's impossible. Your request is too much. Change your condition."
"Too much?" Luo Zheng sneered, "You want to kill me, isn't that too much? If I didn't have the strength, I would have already died over a hundred times. I don't plan to kill all of you, I just want you to leave the Tower of Sin. This request is not too much! "
Meng Tian's gaze became more and more complicated. Luo Zheng dared to say such words, either the Demon Race did not have enough bargaining chips, or Luo Zheng was an idiot. The latter was obviously impossible, and it seemed that Luo Zheng felt that he had the Demon Race under his control? This brat, what courage!
"Our Twin Devil Holy Land has never been afraid of other races for so many years. Who knows how many Peak Battle-Saints have walked out of the Tower of Sin, and we have never lost the Tower of Sin for a moment. Forgive me for not being able to fulfill this request of yours. I'll say it again, change your condition." At this time, even Meng Tian admired his own patience!
"Wasting your breath on this brat. I've already said it. Kill him and end it all!"
Mo Qi, who was behind Meng Tian, could no longer hold it in. His tall and sturdy figure appeared in a flash, and his cultivation of Peak Battle-Saint erupted. It was like a tsunami that was over a hundred meters tall, sweeping towards Luo Zheng.
When Mo Qi's figure moved, Ling Yan also moved. A fine net appeared from her body, blocking in front of Luo Zheng.
"It's over!"
This thought flashed through the minds of the others at the same time, and they prepared to make their move at the same time.
Zhao Fenqin and Zhou Zhuohe's mouths were bitter, they didn't know what to say. Just now, Zhao Fenqin was almost anxious to death, but Luo Zheng didn't listen to them at all! He had to force the Demon Race to their deaths. Even a rabbit would bite when cornered. How could the Demon Race agree to Luo Zheng's request?
Only Meng Tian and Luo Zheng were still sitting there, looking at each other, as if they didn't notice that Mo Qi and Ling Yan had already made their move.
When Mo Qi rushed out halfway, before he could even get close to the net that Ling Yan had woven, an invisible force suddenly stopped Mo Qi!
Mo Qi roared crazily, "Human Race brat, when I tear you into pieces, you will regret it!"
He struggled crazily, as if he wanted to break free from the bindings of the invisible force. However, no matter how hard he struggled, it was futile. It was as if he was a little bug that had fallen into a spider's web. The more he struggled, the deeper the invisible force bound him.
Even the War Sage was unable to break free from the bindings of this invisible force. Mo Qi was only a Peak War Elder, how could he break free?
Luo Zheng didn't even look at Mo Qi, and only said indifferently: "Kill him."
"Puchi puchi!"
That invisible force immediately tore Mo Qi's body apart, and brown blood splattered all over the hall. Mo Qi's death was too horrible to look at.
This scene caused all the Peak War Elders present to suck in a breath of air!
Not only the Peak War Elders of the Demon Race, even the Peak War Elders of the Demon Night Clan and the Human Race revealed looks of fear.
Luo Zheng's control over the Tower of Sin far exceeded everyone's expectations!
Mo Qi's strength was ranked fourth among the 128 seat controllers, second only to Ling Yan of the Demon Night Clan, Meng Tian of the Demon Race and another Peak War Elder.
Since Luo Zheng could borrow the power of the Tower of Sin to easily kill Mo Qi, then Luo Zheng had the ability to kill anyone in the Tower of Sin!
Even if it was Ling Yan, Meng Tian was not a match for Luo Zheng …
Powerhouses would not place themselves under a wall of danger. Although Ling Yan believed that Luo Zheng would not make a move against her, her face still revealed a taboo expression.

Chapter 607
From beginning to end, Luo Zheng's gaze was fixed on Meng Tian, and Meng Tian's gaze never left Luo Zheng's body.
Brown blood stained the hall and was quickly absorbed. No trace of blood could be seen.
If Mo Qi's body wasn't moved, then his body would also be absorbed by the Sin Tower within a day.
Because of this characteristic of the Sin Tower, it was able to continuously absorb foreign objects and replenish the Sin Tower's energy, allowing the Sin Tower to continue operating.
This characteristic was very advantageous to the Demon Race. They could continuously kill their opponents in the arena. Not only could they absorb the opponent's Light of Creation, but they could also replenish the Sin Tower's energy. The Demon Race could also accept the Sin Tower's training, forming a perfect closed loop!
The Demon Race had risen from here!
But now, the Sin Tower's power was controlled by Luo Zheng.
Meng Tian now understood that Luo Zheng had the right to decide the life and death of anyone in the Sin Tower!
The Demon Race had no right to negotiate!
After a long silence, Luo Zheng's voice broke the silence, "Now, do you accept my conditions?"
Meng Tian revealed a bitter smile. In the past, Meng Tian felt that he was the controller of the Sin Tower. Even if Ling Yan defeated him, he led the Demon Race's ten War Reverends. The Demon Race didn't fear the Demon Night Clan at all!
He felt that he had done a good job. Every time he returned to the Sacred Land, he would be rewarded by the Demon Race's Divine Sea Realm experts!
But now, facing Luo Zheng, whose bone age was less than 20 years old and whose strength was only at the first rank War General realm, he suddenly felt powerless. It was as if all the strength in his muscles had been sucked out in an instant.
This was too unreasonable. This brat had only entered the Sin Tower for a few months and had only climbed to the tenth floor. How could he!
But Luo Zheng had the power of the Sin Tower. This power was so strong that he couldn't resist!
If the Demon Race was expelled from the Sin Tower, he knew what he would face! Although the Divine Sea Realm experts of the Twin Demon Sacred Land valued him, once Meng Tian and all the Demon Race were expelled from the Sin Tower, Meng Tian would directly face the wrath of his own clansmen!
He would very likely become a sinner, spurned by the Flame Demon Sacred Land and the Fear Demon Sacred Land!
But how could he resist now?
Although Meng Tian's strength was slightly stronger than Mo Qi's, the two of them were both Peak war elder s. Even among the 128 seats, there was only a difference of two places, so the difference in strength was not very big.
How could he resist Luo Zheng?
In the end, he would still end up like Mo Qi, torn to pieces.
Meng Tian was silent for a while before he said, "I'm sorry, but I can't accept your conditions."
"Then I'll kill all the Devil Clansmen," Luo Zheng said indifferently. His calm eyes were like the eyes of a devil in the eyes of the Devil Clansmen. Although his tone was indifferent, the murderous intent that was reflected from it was terrifying.
If they were disobedient, he would kill them all …
The temperament of this human brat …
The few Peak war elder s of the Demon Clan had different thoughts, but the only thing they had in common was their deep fear of Luo Zheng. They believed that if Luo Zheng said it, he would definitely do it.
The Peak war elders of the Yao Night Clan all sighed inwardly.
When Luo Zheng was surrounded by the Peak war elders of the Demon Clan on the tenth floor, they still looked down on the Human Clan. If it wasn't for the appearance of their "King", they definitely wouldn't have stepped into these muddy waters!
Because offending the Devil Clan for the Human Clan was something only an idiot would do.
Now that they thought about it, they felt extremely fortunate. In other words, their "King" had brought good luck to the Yao Night Clan!
After all, Luo Zheng did not stand against the Yao Night Clan.
If it wasn't for the appearance of their "King", if the Yao Night Clan started to fear Luo Zheng and started to target Luo Zheng, the consequences of angering this human brat could be imagined. The Yao Night Clan's fate wouldn't be any better!
As for the four seat controllers of the Human Clan, they … were still in a daze.
The Human Clan in the Sin Tower had endured for hundreds and thousands of years. Who would have thought that because of Luo Zheng's appearance, everything would be completely reversed!
Luo Zheng had the power to decide the life and death of the Sin Tower! Now was the time for the Human Clan to hold their heads up high … As for how to hold their heads up high, they had not thought about it yet. At this moment, they only felt extremely fortunate that Luo Zheng was from the Human Clan. He was a Human!
"If you don't accept it, I'll really kill all of you," Luo Zheng said indifferently, "I'm not threatening you."
Meng Tian bitterly smiled and said, "I know, but our Twin Devil Sacred Ground still has the strength to fight. If you chase us out of the Sin Tower, I think the Purple Heart Sacred Ground will be flattened by the Twin Devil Sacred Ground. With your Purple Heart Sacred Ground's strength, either the Flame Devil Sacred Ground or the Fear Devil Sacred Ground will be able to flatten you. Have you thought about it?"
Meng Tian's words immediately made the four Peak war elders of the Human Clan nervous.
Luo Zheng was not from the Purple Heart Sacred Ground. They had already guessed that he had never stepped into the Purple Heart Sacred Ground in his life. He had no sense of belonging to the Purple Heart Sacred Ground. Even if the Purple Heart Sacred Ground was flattened by the Demon Clan, he would not feel anything.
Luo Zheng did not care about the Purple Heart Sacred Ground, but Zhao Fenqin, Zhou Zhuohe, and the others cared. That was the Human Clan's only land of inheritance in the Sea God Continent!
Although Meng Tian said those words with a bitter smile, the threat in his words was clear. Luo Zheng, you can chase our Demon Clan out. The Sin Tower is yours, and our Twin Devil Sacred Ground can't do anything to you, Luo Zheng. However, we can flatten the Purple Heart Sacred Ground. You should think about it carefully!
Zhao Fenqin wanted to dissuade Luo Zheng, but he swallowed his words.
What right did they have to control Luo Zheng?
Luo Zheng was indeed a Human, but the problem was, other than being of the same race, did Luo Zheng have any other relationship with them?
What did the Purple Heart Sacred Ground help Luo Zheng? Nothing at all! Instead, it was Luo Zheng who helped the Human Clan!
"Think about it?" Luo Zheng curled his lips and raised a finger, "No need to think about it. I'll give you a month's time. After a month, I'll kill any Demon Clan member I find in the Sin Tower!"
As soon as Luo Zheng said this, Zhao Fenqin, Zhou Zhuohe, and the other two seat controllers' expressions changed drastically!
The Purple Heart Sacred Ground was in trouble …
The Human Clan's Divine Sea Realm Almighties were no match for the Demon Clan. Not to mention the Twin Devil Sacred Ground, even the Fear Demon Sacred Ground and the Flame Demon Sacred Ground could flatten the Purple Heart Sacred Ground.
"Luo Zheng, this …" Zhao Fenqin could not help but open his mouth. There was even a hint of pleading in his tone.
Luo Zheng did not even look at this Human Clan's Peak War Reverend. His gaze was cold, as if he did not care about the Purple Heart Sacred Ground at all.
Meng Tian's face turned completely gloomy, "You don't care about the survival of the Purple Heart Sacred Ground? The survival of the Human Clan? "This was Meng Tian's last life-saving straw. Now, Meng Tian could only use this to threaten Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng smiled faintly, "I'm sure you've already done your research. I'm not from the Sea God Continent, so what does the survival of the Purple Heart Sacred Ground have to do with me? Besides … "
"Besides what?" Meng Tian asked.
"Do you really think the Twin Devil Sacred Ground can really do anything to the Purple Heart Sacred Ground?" Luo Zheng continued to ask with a smile.
"Why not?" Meng Tian's eyes flashed. He suddenly realized that he seemed to have overlooked something.
Specks of red light flickered and Xun's figure appeared beside Luo Zheng again. When Xun appeared, the Peak War Reverends of the Demon Night Clan immediately knelt down.
Ling Yan even knelt in front of Xun and said, "Greetings, King!"
Luo Zheng smiled at Xun and said to Meng Tian, "You forgot about the Sky Feather Sacred Ground."
There were 14 major races on the Sea God Continent. These 40 major races all had their own sacred grounds.
However, there were strong and weak sacred grounds. Take the Human Clan's Purple Heart Sacred Ground for example. Because of the existence of a few Divine Sea Realm almighties, it was a sacred ground that had just passed the fifth grade and was promoted to the sixth grade. Two thousand years ago, the Human Clan didn't have their own sacred ground. At that time, there was only the Purple Heart Sect!
Among the 14 sacred grounds, it was considered the weakest.
The Flame Devil Sacred Ground and the Fear Devil Sacred Ground were the top of the sixth grade sacred grounds! When these two sacred grounds were combined, they could be promoted to the seventh grade sacred ground!
However, there was only one seventh grade sacred ground on the Sea God Continent, and that was the Sky Feather Sacred Ground! The reason why the Sky Feather Sacred Ground could be promoted to the seventh grade was because of the existence of Liu Yu!
Liu Yu also promised that she would do her best to assist Luo Zheng.
