Chapter 608
As the King of the Yao Night Clan, Xun always exuded a lofty aura in front of anyone!
Even Battle Sages and Divine Sea Realm experts would feel inferior when they felt Xun's aura, let alone these peak Battle-Reverends.
But when Luo Zheng was alone, Xun was much more casual.
For example, when Luo Zheng entered the secret cultivation room, Xun would come out of Luo Zheng's body and walk around Luo Zheng. She would even talk to Luo Zheng …
This contrast made Luo Zheng feel very strange. Occasionally, she would spit out a few vulgarities, which made Luo Zheng speechless.
Hearing Luo Zheng's words and seeing Luo Zheng summon his sword spirit, Meng Tian's face became gloomy.
Meng Tian wasn't an idiot. How could he forget the Sky Feather Sacred Land?
However, would the Sky Feather Sacred Land really help the Purple Heart Sacred Land for this so-called "King"? Meng Tian had doubts about this.
It seemed that the peak Battle-Reverends of the Yao Night Clan respected their "King" a lot. However, the Sky Feather Sacred Land might not be willing to help when it came to the war between the Sacred Lands!
If Meng Tian went with Luo Zheng to the Sky Feather Sacred Land, he would know that Luo Zheng wasn't lying.
Xun was the spiritual totem of the Sky Feather Sacred Land. Even if she ordered the Sky Feather Sacred Land to attack the Twin Devil Sacred Land, Liu Yu wouldn't say no, let alone help the Purple Heart Sacred Land!
Xun didn't say anything, but Ling Yan understood Luo Zheng's thoughts. Ling Yan stood up and said, "If our King gives the order, the Sky Feather Sacred Land will go through fire and water!"
As the representative of the Yao Night Clan in the Sin Tower, Ling Yan's words were equivalent to Liu Yu's.
Xun blinked and said, "Whatever Luo Zheng says, you'll do."
Xun wanted to complete her revenge. Almost all of her hopes were pinned on Luo Zheng. Of course, she would do her best to help Luo Zheng.
Meng Tian muttered to himself. How trustworthy were the Yao Night Clan's words? Although the Heavenly Feather Holy Land was powerful, they would have to pay a heavy price if they went to war with the Twin Demon Holy Land.
This kind of thinking was a kind of torment for Meng Tian, because his judgment would affect the fate of the entire demon race.
Luo Zheng still had a faint smile on his face as he waited patiently. After a while, he said, "That's why I gave you a one-month buffer period!"
"What do you mean?" Meng Tian asked.
Luo Zheng shook his head. The intelligence of the Demon Race wasn't that great. He estimated that they were only slightly smarter than the Beastmen and Giants. "In this one month, you can try to 'flatten' the Purple Heart Holy Land! Let's test the Heavenly Feather Holy Ground's reaction … "
Meng Tian frowned. Only now did he understand why Luo Zheng had given them a month's time.
In this one month buffer period, the Demon Race did not need to leave the Sin Tower. At this time, the game in the Sin Tower was handed over to the Holy Lands outside the Cursed Lands!
If the Twin Demon Holy Land won! They could indeed use the Purple Heart Holy Land to threaten Luo Zheng. If the Twin Demon Holy Land lost, or if they were afraid of the Heavenly Feather Holy Land and did not dare to make a move against the Purple Heart Holy Land, then Meng Tian and the others would have to leave the Sin Tower!
At this time, Meng Tian's eyes suddenly lit up!
Leaving the Sin Tower would not cause any loss to Meng Tian. He was already a peak War Reverend, so he would have to leave the Sin Tower sooner or later.
However, after being kicked out of the Sin Tower, he would have to bear a great responsibility. When he returned to the Holy Land, he would have to face severe punishment!
However, Luo Zheng's plan reduced his responsibility by a lot.
After all, the one who would decide whether or not the Demon Race could stay in the Sin Tower was not Meng Tian, but the Twin Demon Holy Land. It was the Divine Sea Realm experts of the Twin Demon Holy Land …
Therefore, Meng Tian felt as if a heavy burden had been lifted off his shoulders!
"So that's how it is …" Meng Tian stood up and ordered the demon beside him, "Clean up Mo Qi's corpse. Today's negotiation ends here!"
Then, Meng Tian prepared to leave the hall. When he was halfway there, Meng Tian turned around and said, "Thank you."
This word of "thank you" made many people in the hall reveal strange expressions. Only a few people with quick minds, such as Xun, Ling Yan, and the others, understood. As for the others, their faces were full of blank expressions.
Why did Luo Zheng kill Meng Tian's people and force him to leave the Sin Tower with all the Demon Race? Why did Meng Tian have to thank Luo Zheng?
Luo Zheng smiled and shrugged, "There's no need to thank me. We're in different positions."
After the Demon Race left, Zhao Fenqin said in a low voice, "Luo Zheng, will the Demon Night Clan help our Purple Heart Holy Land?"
"Let's wait and see. Even if the Demon Night Clan doesn't help, I won't kick the Demon Race out of the Sin Tower," Luo Zheng said with a smile.
Luo Zheng's words caused Zhao Fenqin's mind to be in a mess. No matter what, this news had to be passed on to the Purple Heart Holy Land as soon as possible so that they could prepare in advance!
On the Sea God Continent, there was an intense battle going on around the Sin Tower!
Although the Sea God Continent was extremely chaotic, it had been peaceful for over a dozen years. Besides the Twin Demon Holy Land constantly expanding their territory, the other races were relatively peaceful.
Facing the oppression of the Twin Demon Holy Land, the other races were angry, but they didn't dare to speak up. Whether it was in the Sin Tower or on the Sea God Continent, they could only endure!
Recently, the Twin Demon Holy Land had made a new move. The Flame Demon Holy Land and Fear Demon Holy Land had suddenly declared war on the Purple Heart Holy Land!
This rumor caused the trillions of people on the Sea God Continent to be at a loss.
The Twin Demon Holy Land and the human race's Purple Heart Holy Land were separated by the Giant Race's Holy Land. Generally speaking, when a Holy Land declared war between two Holy Lands, it meant that they were prepared to completely annihilate the other!
But they didn't know why the Demon Race would suddenly attack the Purple Heart Holy Land.
Destroying the Purple Heart Holy Land wouldn't benefit the Demon Race much, not to mention that the Twin Demon Holy Land had the Demon Night Clan's Sky Feather Holy Land behind them!
All kinds of information and analysis were circulating in the various Holy Lands. The other races naturally wouldn't join in the fun. Now that the Twin Demon Holy Land had made a move, the Purple Heart Holy Land was destined to be destroyed. The other races were happy to watch the show from the side.
But the following information was worth pondering over!
The Purple Heart Holy Land had actually declared war on the Twin Demon Holy Land …
The Purple Heart Holy Land and the Twin Demon Holy Land weren't on the same level. What confidence did the human race have to declare war on the Twin Demon Holy Land?
The Twin Demon Holy Land was like an adult armed to the teeth. He could say to a weak child that he wanted to kill him.
And the human race was that child.
Not only did the child not run away, but he also didn't beg for mercy. Instead, he declared war on the adult. This went against common sense.
A war between Holy Lands was not child's play. It was a war where blood flowed like rivers. Every step taken by both sides had been through countless deductions and competitions. If the human race dared to turn against the Twin Demon Holy Land, then they definitely had something to rely on …
It was just that they didn't understand. What did the human race rely on?
However, as the news spread bit by bit, they soon understood that the Human Race was relying on Luo Zheng!
The 14 Holy Lands had countless spies planted in the Sin Tower. They were well aware of the Sin Tower's movements. There were more than a trillion living beings on the Sea God Continent. There were always some who were incomparably intelligent.
After the Purple Heart Holy Land declared war on the Twin Demon Holy Land, some intelligent beings predicted that the Fiend Night Clan would also declare war on the Twin Demon Holy Land.
Sure enough, ten days later, the Fiend Night Clan declared war on the Twin Demon Holy Land …
During this month, the Sin Tower was immersed in slaughter and plunder as usual. This was a normal state that had been maintained for countless years.
However, the three races on the Sea God Continent were in the midst of a repeated game of probing.
The Purple Heart Holy Land entered a state of alert. As for the Flame Demon Holy Land and the Fear Demon Holy Land, they were led by many Divine Sea Realm Supreme Elders. They sent a million martial artists to close in on the Purple Heart Holy Land.
But at the same time, Liu Yu of the Sky Feather Holy Land personally led many elites of the Sky Feather Holy Land to the side of the Flame Demon Holy Land.
The Sky Feather Holy Land's intentions were clear. If the Twin Demon Holy Land moved against the Purple Heart Holy Land, then the Sky Feather Holy Land would also raze the Twin Demon Holy Land to the ground!
Although the Sky Feather Holy Land would suffer great losses if they attacked the Twin Demon Holy Land, the Sky Feather Holy Land couldn't disobey Xun's orders. Clearly, now was not the time to calculate the price.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 609
The predecessor of the Purple Heart Holy Land was the Purple Heart Sect.
The two words "Purple Heart" came from the name of a spring in Mount Zhuori. It was called the Purple Heart Spring.
This incomparably clear spring water flowed out of Mount Zhuori. It was fused with a special mineral, so it was purple. Legend had it that drinking the Purple Heart Spring could make one immune to all diseases and strengthen one's body.
Back then, the Purple Heart Sect had also developed from a small second-grade Sect. Later, under the pressure of other races, the Purple Heart Sect continuously merged with other human Sects and finally grew into a colossus. It also became the most important habitat of the human race.
The Purple Heart Spring of the past had now become the Sacred Spring of the Purple Heart Holy Land. Although this spring water didn't have much effect on cultivators, the humans of the Purple Heart Holy Land still took a sip of the Purple Heart Spring as an honor!
Next to the Purple Heart Spring, there were three human men.
The one in the lead wore a purple crown and had a jade-like face. His cultivation was unfathomable. He was the current leader of the Purple Heart Holy Land, the Divine Sea Realm powerhouse Ouyang Haotian!
The other two were old men. Although the two of them looked old, their eyes were like lightning, bright and full of spirit. They seemed to be full of energy.
These two old men were also Divine Sea Realm powerhouses. Divine Sea Realm powerhouses had a long lifespan. Some Divine Sea Realm martial artists who had lived for a long time could even live for eight or nine thousand years, or even ten thousand years, becoming an eternal giant!
"Right now, the Holy Land is in a state of panic. If the Demon Race doesn't send their troops, I'm afraid our Holy Land will fall into chaos first …" Ouyang Haotian bent down and drank a handful of water.
"They don't dare to make a move," one of the old men said with certainty.
"If we wanted to make a move, we would have done so long ago. If we were to be frightened by this group of Devil Clan, our Purple Heart Holy Land would be in a mess. That would be a real joke!" Another old man revealed an angry expression.
Ouyang Haotian shook his head, "There's no other way. Our human race has been weak for many years. It would be a lie to say that they don't panic when facing the Demon Race. Now that the Demon Race is standing at our doorstep and the people in the Tower of Sin are in the minority, do you think they will believe the Demon Race or Luo Zheng who isn't from the Purple Heart Holy Land?"
During this period of time, the Purple Heart Holy Land kept sending people into the Sin Tower.
At the same time, Zhao Fenqin and Zhou Zhuohe were shuttling back and forth between the Sin Tower and the Purple Heart Holy Land.
Zhao Fenqin was very confident. He swore that the Devil Race would not dare to attack the Purple Heart Holy Land. Moreover, the Heavenly Feather Holy Land would definitely take action.
At first, Ouyang Haotian was still a little hesitant. After all, compared to trusting Luo Zheng, who was only a rank 1 warlord, whom he had never met before, he trusted his own strength more!
However, no matter how powerful Ouyang Haotian was, he couldn't fight against the many Divine Sea Realm cultivators in the Twin Devil Sacred Ground!
Just as the Purple Heart Holy Land was preparing for war, the Sky Feather Holy Land had personally sent someone over to discuss countermeasures with him.
Thus, when the Twin Devil Holy Lands moved out, the Heavenly Feather Holy Lands also moved out …
At this time, Ouyang Haotian understood that whether he was willing or not, the fate of the Purple Heart Holy Land was already tied together with that mysterious brat from the Sin Tower.
From Ouyang Haotian's point of view, the Purple Heart Holy Land now had a considerable advantage. The Devil Race was just putting on an act. Up until now, besides destroying a small rank 2 sect on the periphery of the Purple Heart Holy Land, the Devil Race hadn't made any other moves. The Devil Race didn't dare to take a step closer to the Purple Heart Holy Land because Liu Yu was leading people into the Twin Devil Holy Land's territory.
Ouyang Haotian wasn't worried, but that didn't mean that the humans of the Purple Heart Holy Land weren't worried.
These people were still unwilling to tie their fates to a mere rank 1 warlord!
Not to mention the low-level martial artists of the Purple Heart Holy Land, even some of the Battle-Saint level powerhouses couldn't trust Luo Zheng!
Even if Situ Haotian relayed what happened in the Sin Tower, many people still chose not to believe him. Many Battle-Saints and Battle-Saints even began to leave the Purple Heart Holy Land with their families …
Situ Haotian also had a headache about this, but there wasn't much he could do. He couldn't enter the Cursed Lands, and it was even more impossible for him to go out and compete with the Divine Sea Realm experts of the Devil Race. Right now, the only thing he could do was wait.
One month, as long as he could endure this month, everything would be clear!
If what Zhao Fenqin said was true, and Luo Zheng expelled all the Devil Race, then the human race wouldn't just have five seats. The human race could take all the Devil Race's seats! Even if Luo Zheng wanted to give some to the Yao Ye Clan, the human race would be able to keep at least a dozen seats.
This way, the human race would soar into the sky and become the second strongest force in the Sin Tower!
The Sin Tower was a mini version of the Sea God Continent. The strength of the various races in the Sin Tower was a replica of the forces on the Sea God Continent!
With so many seats occupied by the human race, more and more powerhouses would be born. In a few years, the Purple Heart Holy Land would also soar into the sky, and the human race would truly rise on the Sea God Continent.
Situ Haotian was still very young. As long as he didn't fall, he would be able to see this scene in his lifetime!
Under his responsibility, the human race would prosper, and the Purple Heart Holy Land would prosper. Situ Haotian was naturally happy.
"No matter what, we must endure!"
"It would be best if that kid Luo Zheng came to our Purple Heart Holy Land …"
The two old men continued to give advice.
Situ Haotian bitterly smiled, "That kid's face is too big. I asked Zhao Fenqin to invite him twice, but he didn't come! If we can endure this month, I hope he can come to the Purple Heart Holy Land … "
In fact, it wasn't just the human race that was worried.
The Devil Race's situation wasn't much better.
The Sin Tower was a catalyst for geniuses and powerhouses. It was also a place that all martial artists would fight for!
If the Devil Race was truly expelled from the Sin Tower, they would be fine for a short period of time. But if this continued for a long time, their decline was almost inevitable.
The Twin Demon Holy Land didn't have much interest in the Purple Heart Holy Land, but only the Purple Heart Holy Land could threaten Luo Zheng. They hoped that Luo Zheng would change his mind, so the Twin Demon Holy Land declared war on the Purple Heart Holy Land because they had no other choice.
The problem was that just as the Twin Demon Holy Land declared war, the Heavenly Feather Holy Land declared war on the Devil Race. Liu Yu personally led a group of people into the territory of the Flame Demon Holy Land.
All the Devil Race people found themselves in a dilemma.
Could they really flatten the Purple Heart Holy Land? Although they could destroy the human race, they still wouldn't be able to change the situation in the Sin Tower. What was even more troublesome was that the Heavenly Feather Holy Land would take this opportunity to flatten the Twin Demon Holy Land. As a result, the Twin Demon Holy Land would collapse, and the Devil Race would lose the Sin Tower forever, their destruction imminent.
Let go of the Purple Heart Holy Land? Then one month later, the Devil Race would also lose the Sin Tower. After losing such an important training ground, what awaited the Devil Race was a slow death. It was conceivable that the Devil Race would decline bit by bit, eventually drowning in the long river of history.
In the Sea God Continent, some powerful races had been eliminated. No one could resist the law of natural selection!
What to do next? Many Divine Sea Realm experts were at a loss …
They only wanted to use the Purple Heart Holy Land to threaten Luo Zheng. But now, not only was it difficult to threaten Luo Zheng, but their own backyard was also about to catch fire.
At this time, the most comfortable person was Liu Yu.
She was wearing a purple robe and sitting at the top of a dark mountain. Black smoke constantly floated out from this mountain.
The Flame Demon Holy Land was like this. There were thousands of active volcanoes of various sizes everywhere. That was why the Holy Land had the word "Flame" in its name.
The mission that Xun gave to Liu Yu was very simple. She only needed to sit here with her people. If the Devil Race didn't attack the Purple Heart Holy Land, then Liu Yu wouldn't attack the Twin Devil Holy Land.
Two hundred years ago, Liu Yu entered the Sin Tower. At that time, she had accumulated a lot of hatred with the Devil Race. She was also heavily targeted by the Devil Race.
In Liu Yu's heart, she had always wanted to find an opportunity to eliminate the Devil Race. But such an opportunity had never appeared. Although the Sky Feather Holy Land was a seventh-rank Holy Land, if she wanted to take down the Twin Devil Holy Land head-on, she was afraid that she would lose most of her forces. At that time, it was hard to guarantee that the other races wouldn't become restless.
But now, Liu Yu found that Luo Zheng's appearance had created a perfect opportunity for her. Without losing a single soldier, she could weaken the Devil Race and even slowly erase them!
It could be said that even if Xun didn't give this order, Liu Yu would still choose to take action at this time. She and the Devil Race weren't friends, but the enemy of an enemy was a friend!

Chapter 610
The situation on the Sea God Continent was delicate. Even the people in the Sin Tower were panicking.
Because the Cursed Land was a small version of the Sea God Continent, the information flow was much faster here. Many races would send people to the Cursed Land every day to obtain information.
It was as if a huge typhoon had swept through the Sea God Continent. The Cursed Land was at the center of the typhoon, while the Sin Tower was at the eye of the typhoon.
Luo Zheng, who was at the eye of the typhoon, was very calm. He did not pay much attention to the outside world.
However, Luo Zheng still had a small worry. Luo Zheng could not stay on the tenth floor any longer.
After a period of battle, the Light of Creation he had accumulated had already allowed Luo Zheng to advance to a Second Order Champion.
The Sin Tower was indeed a mystical place. The speed of advancement through the Light of Creation was countless times faster than cultivating in the outside world!
This was also the reason why the various races attached importance to the Sin Tower. As long as one's talent was strong enough, they could rapidly advance in the Sin Tower and train themselves.
Based on Luo Zheng's training speed, it would not be long before Luo Zheng could surpass the Champion rank and advance to a War Reverend.
However, ever since Luo Zheng defeated the Second Order War Reverend from the Beast Race, the Champions on the tenth floor finally came to a realization.
The tenth floor of the Sin Tower was a place where Deity rank geniuses from various races gathered. Although Luo Zheng had defeated Yue Shou and the others, there were still many Deity rank geniuses who were unconvinced. No Deity rank genius was willing to fall behind others. Moreover, other than the original Deity rank geniuses on the tenth floor, there were also geniuses who had advanced from the ninth floor.
However, ever since Luo Zheng defeated the Second Order War Reverend, those geniuses finally understood that the gap between them and Luo Zheng was not small.
With Luo Zheng's current cultivation, his opponent should be a War Reverend, not a Champion!
These Deity rank geniuses were willing to take a gamble. If they could defeat Luo Zheng, they would obtain great glory! However, even though they knew that they could not defeat Luo Zheng, they still went to the arena. They would either be throwing away their lives or giving away their Light of Creation for free …
Then, Luo Zheng began to encounter empty spots.
After being cleared for three consecutive days, Luo Zheng obtained the authority to ascend to the eleventh floor of the Tower of Sin. He originally wanted to stay on the tenth floor for a while. He hoped to accumulate more Light of Good Fortune on the tenth floor before entering the next floor.
However, on the fourth day, the fifth day, and the sixth day … it was still empty.
Staying here any longer would be a waste of time.
In desperation, Luo Zheng could only choose to leave the tenth floor and enter the eleventh floor.
At this time, half of the buffer period Luo Zheng gave the demons had already passed.
This matter had slowly spread throughout the Tower of Sin, and every race was discussing it in private.
The other races, of course, held the attitude of watching a joke as they discussed this matter. The Demon Race had always been arrogant and overbearing in the Tower of Sin. In the past, the other races didn't dare to say anything, but now that the Demon Race had been expelled from the Tower of Sin by Luo Zheng, it was definitely a good thing for them. Many of the weaker races couldn't wait to clap their hands and send off the Demon Race.
As for the Demon Race, their faces were filled with dismay.
Many Demon Race members rarely went to the arena, and even rarely appeared in the public areas of the Tower of Sin. They were all very careful, and even their voices were lowered.
This month was a very crucial month for the Demon Race.
If the Demon Night Clan didn't give way, then the Purple Heart Holy Land wouldn't give way. If the Purple Heart Holy Land didn't give way, then Luo Zheng wouldn't give way.
If Luo Zheng didn't give way, then all the Demon Race would have to get out of the Tower of Sin, including all the Peak War Reverends of the Demon Race!
Just as Luo Zheng stepped onto the eleventh floor of the Tower of Sin, two War Reverends of the Demon Race walked towards him. These were two War Reverends of the third rank.
"Luo Zheng, right? You finally climbed to the eleventh floor! Are you interested in going to the arena now? "
"Second rank War General, climbing to the eleventh floor, you really don't know how to write the word 'death', hehe!"
The two War Reverends of the Demon Race provoked Luo Zheng.
Although the Demon Race already understood that they couldn't fight against Luo Zheng in the Tower of Sin, even Mo Qi, who was ranked fourth among the Peak War Reverends, was instantly killed by Luo Zheng, how could the other races be a match for Luo Zheng?
However, to the Demon Race, there was still one last way, and that was to kill Luo Zheng in the arena.
In fact, with the personality of the Demon Race, even if they couldn't resist Luo Zheng, there would still be many Demon Race members who would be willing to fight Luo Zheng to the death. On the tenth floor of the Tower of Sin, there was not only one Moon Guardian, but also many Tenth Rank War Generals of the Demon Race.
After Luo Zheng and Meng Tian's negotiation, many Tenth Rank War Generals volunteered to kill Luo Zheng in the arena, but they were stopped in the end.
Luo Zheng was puzzled at first. According to the personality of the Demon Race, how could they resist? Since they couldn't deal with him in the arena, this impulsive race would naturally choose to deal with him in the arena.
As a result, he encountered an empty arena for several days and had no choice but to advance.
However, after seeing these two War Reverends, Luo Zheng understood that these guys knew that it would be difficult for War Generals to kill him, so they simply endured in the tenth floor and forced him to enter the eleventh floor.
Starting from the eleventh floor of the Tower of Sin, there were very few War Generals. The majority of Divine Rank geniuses would stay on the tenth floor. Even if they had the qualifications to enter the eleventh floor, they would make sufficient preparations on the tenth floor and break through to War Reverend before entering the eleventh floor.
Even Moon Guardian and Mu Qing Yang, whose strength was absolutely qualified to challenge First and Second Rank War Reverends, still chose to stay on the tenth floor and continuously absorb the Light of Good Fortune. They were bound to break through to War Reverend on the tenth floor.
Luo Zheng was forced to go up to the eleventh floor. How could the Demon Race Reverends on the eleventh floor endure?
Because of this brat Luo Zheng, all the Demon Race in the Tower of Sin were very passive, all the Peak War Reverends were very passive, and even the Flame Demon Holy Land and the Fear Demon Holy Land were very passive …
No matter who it was, as long as they could kill Luo Zheng, they would be the savior of the Demon Race!
Therefore, just as Luo Zheng entered the eleventh floor, before he could even stabilize his footing, two Demon Race Reverends appeared.
Facing the provocation of these two Demon Race Reverends, Luo Zheng narrowed his eyes and smiled faintly, "I accept your challenge, but you must be prepared to lose your lives!"
"We Demon Race don't have anyone who is greedy for life and afraid of death! Besides, you're just a Second Rank War General. "One of the Demon Race Reverends sneered.
The other Demon Race Reverend also laughed wildly, "Yes, as long as you don't use your bullshit ability! Hahaha … "
Luo Zheng raised his hand slightly, "Are you talking about this ability?"
An invisible force immediately wrapped around the War Reverend of the Devil Clan. The War Reverend's expression changed greatly. Although the death of the Peak War Reverend Mo Qi was not spread, the inner circle of the Devil Clan was very clear that as long as Luo Zheng moved his finger slightly, he would be torn in two!
"You, the rules of the Tower of Sin forbid you from fighting in the safe zone!" The Demon Race Reverend was anxious, his face pale.
"Safe zone?" Luo Zheng sneered, "The rules of the safe zone were set by all the seat holders, but I think it won't be long before this rule is abolished!"
"This is the rule set by all the races," the Demon Race Reverend quickly said.
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed, his finger moved slightly, and the Demon Race Reverend fell to the ground with a plop. Then, Luo Zheng walked towards the battle arena, as if there was no one around, he said, "The new rules will be set by me."
When the other races saw this scene, there was a burst of discussion.
"This Luo Zheng is too domineering, he really doesn't seem like a human!"
"What he said is not wrong. If we put aside his identity as a human, he is now equivalent to the controller of the Tower of Sin. Who can resist him in the Tower of Sin? Ling Yan? Meng Tian? Didn't he kill her in the blink of an eye? It's not inappropriate for him to set the rules! "
"The rules will be set by whoever has the biggest fist. This is the real rule! The Tower of Sin has been operating according to this rule all these years. When the Demon Race took over the entire Tower of Sin, who dared to question the Demon Race? "
Just as the crowd was discussing, Luo Zheng walked onto the battle arena and pointed at the two Demon Race Reverends, "There are still fifteen days. In these fifteen days, I will give you a chance to challenge me!"
"I'm going to kill him!" The Demon Race Reverend crawled up from the ground, his face filled with fury. After he finished speaking, he rushed towards the battle arena.
For the Demon Race, there were still two opportunities. Either they could take down the Purple Heart Holy Land in these fifteen days, or they could kill Luo Zheng in the Tower of Sin.

Chapter 611
The War Venerable of the Devil Clan leaped up and fiercely stomped on the stage. The huge force even caused the stage to slightly cave in. However, the ground of the stage quickly recovered.
A little red light surged around Luo Zheng, and Xun's figure appeared beside Luo Zheng. She stretched out her hand towards Luo Zheng and said, "Give it to me."
"Give what?" Ever since Xun regained her consciousness, she often jumped out on her own.
"The spear!" Xun said faintly.
"What for?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"Let me exercise a bit in these few battles," Xun's lips curled up slightly.
Perhaps it was because she had been stuck in Luo Zheng's body for too long that Xun made this request.
Luo Zheng wanted to see Xun's spear skills in the Heavenly Feather Holy Land. So, Luo Zheng lightly tapped his spatial ring and a long spear flew towards Xun.
Xun held the long spear in her hand, and her aura suddenly changed. From Xun's fiery eyes, endless killing intent bloomed!
"Why, why did it change?"
"That's our Demon Night Clan's King. It's an honor to be able to see King fight!"
"That's Luo Zheng's Sword Spirit body. It can be regarded as Luo Zheng's avatar. It's very common to use avatars in battle, but this avatar has its own consciousness, this …"
On the stage, the Demon Race War Reverend's eyes flashed. At this time, he couldn't care less. In his eyes, there was only Luo Zheng! Even if he had to sacrifice his own life, he had to kill Luo Zheng. As long as Luo Zheng was killed, even if he died, he would still be the hero of the entire Demon Race!
The Twin Demon Holy Land pursued heroism, and dying on the battlefield was a kind of glory for them.
Therefore, he didn't care about the Sword Spirit or whether Luo Zheng wanted to fight. In any case, as long as he stood on the stage, his first goal was to kill Luo Zheng.
The Demon War Senior then circulated his Vital Spirit to his legs. After that, his Vital Spirit broke out. Like a rampaging elephant, this Demon War Senior directly bypassed Xun and rushed towards Luo Zheng!
"Your opponent is me." Xun's expression was indifferent. The long spear in her hand silently pierced through the space and pointed at War Venerable of the Demon Race.
"Scram!" While the Demon Race's War Senior was rushing forward, he blocked Xun's spear with his saber. He did not stop at all and continued to rush towards Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng, on the other hand, was standing in a corner of the arena. His arms were hanging down and his expression was indifferent. Facing this War Reverend of the Demon Race, he was not in a fighting state at all.
"How dare you insult our king! This demon must be executed!"
"My king will naturally take his life!"
When the Demon Race's War Venerable scolded Xun, many people from the Yao Ye Clan began to make a fuss. How could they allow their king to be insulted? If it weren't for the rules of the arena, the Yao Ye Clan's War Reverends would have already rushed forward!
After Xun's spear was blocked by the Demon, the tip of the spear drew a long arc in the air. Then, with a gentle shake of Xun's spear, the shaft of the spear bent like a venomous snake raising its head!
Then, she lightly shook her arm and the spear was thrown out. The tip of the spear tore through the air and made a loud 'pa' sound as it pierced towards the Demon Race's War Senior's back.
Luo Zheng stared at the Demon Race's War Senior with a calm expression. He even had the time to remind the Demon Race's War Senior that there was danger behind him.
How sensitive was a War Senior's cultivation? How could the Demon Race's War Senior not feel the danger behind him?
But Luo Zheng was right in front of him! And from the looks of it, he had no intention of resisting!
So what if the spear pierced through him? As long as he chopped Luo Zheng in half, he would be a great hero of the Demon Race!
Therefore, the Demon Race's War Senior didn't have any scruples about the spear behind him. He originally had the intention to fight with his life on the line. He wasn't the only one who had this thought, all the Demon Race's War Senior had it!
The moment he rushed in front of Luo Zheng, the Demon Race's War Senior's War Senior's astral essence erupted. Surging power poured into his saber, as if this saber contained all the power he had in his life.
"Die for me!"
The Demon Race's War Senior roared.
Unfortunately, just as he raised his saber, the tip of Xun's spear had already pierced through his back.
The Demon Race's War Senior originally wanted to rely on his tall and sturdy body to resist Xun's spear and kill Luo Zheng in one strike even if he had to risk his life.
But the Demon Race's War Senior had underestimated the power of Xun's spear.
In the Heavenly Feather Holy Lands, Xun had only casually thrust out her spear. But, from the strange aura contained within that spear, not only had Luo Zheng been startled, but even Liu Yu had felt his heart skip a beat.
A sword spirit's strength increased along with its master's cultivation. When Xun had only used a sword dance, her strength had been on par with Luo Zheng's.
But now, Xun used a spear, and the martial skill she cultivated was a spear technique. It was the peak inheritance of the Demon Night Clan!
Although Xun hadn't said what sort of martial skill her spear technique was, Luo Zheng estimated that this spear technique was at least a transcendent divine might!
"Boom!"
Xun's spear pierced through the Demon Race's War Senior's back. Then, a turbulent infernal energy vortex erupted from within.
This infernal energy vortex didn't revolve towards the center, but spread out in all directions!
As it spread out, the Demon Race's War Senior's body was twisted to pieces. As for the Demon Race's War Senior's saber that was aimed at Luo Zheng, it was no longer able to cut down.
Clang! The Demon Race's War Senior's saber fell to the ground, emitting a crisp sound.
From start to finish, Luo Zheng hadn't moved a single step. Facing the War Senior's frontal attack, she was still so calm. Luo Zheng truly believed in Xun's strength.
However, Xun revealed a dull expression. She curled her lips and said, "He won't fight me."
Luo Zheng chuckled. "Someone will fight you."
Luo Zheng had already thoroughly understood the temperament of the demons. As long as he was in the arena, the demons would constantly jump at him!
When he was on the tenth floor, Luo Zheng had carefully considered his options before he chose to enter the eleventh floor.
The battle outside the Cursed Lands had already reached a stalemate. As long as the Sky Feather Holy Lands didn't withdraw their troops, the demons wouldn't dare to attack the Purple Heart Holy Lands. The Purple Heart Holy Lands could afford to delay, and the Sky Feather Holy Lands could also afford to delay. However, to the Twin Demon Holy Lands, there wasn't much time left. Half a month's time was just the blink of an eye for cultivators.
Other than looking for an opportunity to break the stalemate outside the Cursed Lands, killing Luo Zheng in front of them was obviously more direct. Based on this, the Demon Race's War Seniors would continuously walk onto the stage!
Just as Luo Zheng's voice fell, the second Demon Race's War Senior also jumped onto the stage …
Luo Zheng spread his hands towards Xun and said with a faint smile, "Why don't you do it?"
Xun nodded and suddenly turned around. Her spear shot out, the tip of the spear pointed at her second opponent.
The first opponent was completely charging towards Luo Zheng with his life on the line. He didn't dodge or defend against Xun's spear because he had underestimated Xun's strength.
The second Demon Race's War Senior was much more cautious. In his opinion, if he could kill Luo Zheng with his life on the line, he would naturally choose to do so. But in the current situation, let alone going all out, even if he managed to get close to Luo Zheng, he would still be pierced to pieces by that woman's spear! There was simply no chance for him to go all out.
The most important thing was to kill this woman in front of him …
Unfortunately, this Demon Race's War Senior still didn't correctly assess Xun's strength. Xun's spear technique contained a special kind of 'power'. This kind of 'power' was something that even Luo Zheng couldn't comprehend. Every thrust of her spear contained an invisible fluctuation. This fluctuation contained a power that Luo Zheng couldn't understand. In fact, even the number one genius of the Sky Feather Holy Land, Liu Yu, couldn't understand it.
Luo Zheng sometimes felt that the invisible fluctuation was somewhat similar to the power of the tool spirit in the Sin Tower. The power possessed by the tool spirit in the Sin Tower should come from the original owner of the Sin Tower. Was there some kind of connection between them?
Perhaps Luo Zheng would have to find an opportunity to ask.
Anyone with discerning eyes could see that the second Demon Race's War Senior was no match for Xun. The sharpness of Xun's spear technique was far beyond the imagination of these Demon Race's War Seniors on the 11th floor.
In just 10 spears, the weapon of that Demon Race's War Senior was directly shattered into rice-sized fragments. The last spear directly pierced through his throat.
After the Demon Race's War Senior fell to the ground, a blazing white streamer shot towards Luo Zheng's chest.
The Light of Good Fortune accumulated by the two Demon Race's War Seniors was already comparable to the Light of Good Fortune accumulated by 10 Demon Race's War Generals. Now, it seemed that Luo Zheng had no choice but to climb up to the 11th floor.
It took Xun less than the time it took for two incense sticks to burn to kill the two Demon Race's War Seniors.
There were still a few Demon Race's War Seniors who were eager to give it a try, but now they were a little hesitant. They couldn't even get past Xun, so how could they challenge Luo Zheng?
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 612
On the top floor of the Tower of Sin, in the second small world.
Many of the Peak War Elder s of the Demon Clan had dejected looks on their faces.
Although many of the Demon Clan's War Elder s on the eleventh floor were still challenging Luo Zheng, they knew in their hearts that this was a futile struggle.
Back then, when Luo Zheng was only a War General, he could easily defeat Yue Shou. Now that Luo Zheng was a War General of the second step, he was not afraid of facing an ordinary War Elder.
The most important thing was that Luo Zheng was not an idiot.
So what if he could win against Luo Zheng in the arena? He could still use the power of the Tower of Sin to kill people …
Rules did not exist in the eyes of the strong. They were only used to restrain the weak.
Expecting Luo Zheng to abide by the rules? That would be a fool's dream.
These Peak War Elder s also saw through it.
Many of the Demon Clan's War Elder s had already understood the situation. Right now, the Demon Clan in the Tower of Sin had already lost, and many of them had already started to clean up the mess, preparing to return home.
"We still have one last chance." At this time, Meng Tian suddenly said, "Who is willing to stay?"
"You mean to fight with Luo Zheng to the death?" A Peak War Elder asked.
Meng Tian coldly glared at the Peak War Elder. Sometimes, his colleague's brain was not very good.
Another Peak War Elder said, "Indeed, there is one last chance!"
Meng Tian laughed, "And the chances of winning are very high."
More and more war elder s also reacted, and hope flashed in their eyes.
Yes, in less than half a month, Luo Zheng would expel all the Demon Clan s from the Tower of Sin, but he would not be able to expel all of them!
Back then, they had hidden two War Sage Rankers in the secret passage below the Tower of Sin, and they could even avoid the Xuan Ming Death Ray in the secret passage.
When the Xuan Ming Death Ray in the Xuan Ming Cave weakened, there was a high chance that Luo Zheng would enter the Xuan Ming Cave to explore. Even if he did not go this year, Luo Zheng would still go next year.
The lifespan of a Peak War Elder was long, a mere few years was enough to wait!
Furthermore, he could cultivate in the secret passage!
Many of the Peak War Elder present, such as Meng Tian and the others, had been suppressing their cultivation to prevent themselves from breaking through to the War Sage Realm and being affected by the Cursed Grounds.
If they chose to hide in the secret tunnel at this time and seek a breakthrough to become a Battle-Saint, and then wait for Luo Zheng to enter the Xuanming Cave...
Luo Zheng was indeed a genius, and the strength he unleashed was indeed strong, but that was only for ordinary war elder, and was not worth mentioning in the eyes of these Peak war elder. Moreover, it would not be long before the Peak war elder who was hiding in the secret passage would be able to advance to the War Sage Realm.
Inside the Dark Nether Cave, Luo Zheng would not be able to borrow the power of the Sin Tower. It would not be too difficult to kill Luo Zheng!
"If we plan this matter now, the Purple Heart Sacred Land will definitely not be able to take it down. Half a month later, everyone can only give up the Tower of Sin." Meng Tian continued, "I've already suppressed my cultivation for several years. It won't be difficult for me to break through the Battle-Saint realm. Who else is willing to follow me?"
"Me," a peak war elder said in a muffled voice.
"And me!"
"I can also break through in a short period of time!"
Most of the Peak war elders in the Tower of Sin would suppress their cultivation. After all, the cultivation speed in the Tower of Sin was much faster than the outside world. Once they left the Tower of Sin, they would not have the chance to enter again.
The benefits of suppressing one's cultivation were obvious. The stronger the suppression, the faster one's cultivation would rise after breaking through to the War Sage Realm. It was as if the more a person was suppressed, the stronger the rebound.
Meng Tian glanced at them a few times before saying, "Yuan Bai, Tian Hu, the two of you stay behind."
Yuan Bai and Tian Hu were ranked 18th and 22nd respectively among the seat controllers.
It was impossible for Meng Tian to stay behind. He was the leader of the Demon Clan in the Tower of Sin.
Meng Tian's eyes flickered as he stared at Yuan Bai and Tian Hu and said, "Whether or not our Demon Clan can return to the Tower of Sin will depend on the two of you!"
Yuan Bai and Tian Hu, the two peak war elder's faces suddenly revealed a dense killing intent, "Don't worry, as long as that brat dares to enter the Dark Nether Cave, I will definitely tear him to pieces!"
Half a month passed in the blink of an eye.
The entire Sea God Continent was immersed in a strange atmosphere.
Although the Twin Devil Sacred Ground declared war on the Purple Heart Sacred Ground, they did not fight. As for the Sky Feather Sacred Ground, it was the same …
When the one month buffer that Luo Zheng gave to Meng Tian was up, the Twin Devil Sacred Ground retreated. At the same time, Liu Yu also returned to the Sky Feather Sacred Ground.
As for the Tower of Sin, the Demon Clan left the Tower of Sin one after another.
The Demon Clan relied on the Tower of Sin to prosper. When their momentum was at its peak, they almost controlled the entire Tower of Sin with the strength of a single clan. Now that they had no choice but to leave, this scene made the living beings of the other clans sigh.
"That human brat really created a miracle. He chased the entire Demon Clan out of the Tower of Sin with his strength alone!"
"Why do I suddenly feel that the Demon Clan is somewhat pitiful?"
"Pitiful my ass. If you were suppressed by the Demon Clan back then, you would understand that the Demon Clan brought this upon themselves!"
On this day, many living beings left the Tower of Sin. They stood at the entrance of the Tower of Sin and watched as the Demon Clan left.
The Demon Clan warriors, Generals, and even many Peak War Reverends left the Tower of Sin one by one. Although their faces were filled with unwillingness, they had no other choice at this time.
Luo Zheng had already said that from tomorrow onwards, as long as they saw any Demon Clan member in the Tower of Sin, they would kill them.
Under such circumstances, the Demon Clan staying in the Tower of Sin was just courting death.
Many Demon Clan members formed a thin line and headed north of the Cursed Lands. There were two Teleportation Arrays that led to the Flame Demon Sacred Ground and the Fear Demon Sacred Ground.
Most of the Demon Clan members were filled with disappointment, hatred, and unwillingness.
Why couldn't they understand that such a powerful clan like them had fallen to a human brat for no reason?
Only a few Peak War Reverends knew that they would return sooner or later.
This depended on when Luo Zheng would enter the Mysterious Underworld Cave. As long as they killed Luo Zheng, the Demon Clan would be able to fight for the top seat of the Tower of Sin and regain control of the Tower of Sin!
Standing on the platform at the end of the Tower of Sin, Luo Zheng's gaze was calm.
During this period of time, Luo Zheng had absorbed a hundred times more Light of Good Fortune than before. In half a month, at least three to four War Reverends from the Demon Clan would challenge Luo Zheng every day. With the high intensity of training and the large amount of Light of Good Fortune, Luo Zheng's cultivation had rapidly increased. The Tower of Sin was indeed a cultivation Holy Land. Now, Luo Zheng's cultivation was already at the sixth level of War General!
According to what Luo Zheng knew, the Mysterious Underworld Cave still had three to four months before the Mysterious Underworld Deathray would slowly weaken.
Therefore, Luo Zheng still had plenty of time.
According to what Luo Zheng knew, the Mysterious Underworld Deathray would weaken for a period of time every year. However, this time wasn't fixed. Sometimes, it would weaken a month earlier, and sometimes it would weaken a month later.
However, once it was activated, the Deathray wouldn't cause any harm for a month.
I will definitely obtain the inheritance in the Mysterious Underworld Cave! I believe that it won't be long before I can return to the Central Domain …
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng narrowed his eyes. However, as he looked around, he suddenly saw Meng Tian in the distance suddenly turn his head and reveal a strange smile at Luo Zheng.
Seeing Meng Tian's smile, Luo Zheng's heart slightly jumped and he frowned.
"It seems that the Demon Clan won't give up so easily," Luo Zheng indifferently said in his mind.
Then, Luo Zheng's legs started to sink. His whole body had already merged with the Tower of Sin.
Because the Tower of Sin was stuck in the Cursed Land, Luo Zheng was actually standing at the bottom of the Tower of Sin. After his body merged with the Tower of Sin, he kept shuttling back and forth in the Tower of Sin.
First floor, second floor … tenth floor, fourteenth floor, fifteenth floor.
Soon, Luo Zheng arrived at the top of the Tower of Sin!
Now, it was meaningless to talk about the empty stage and the qualifications to advance to the next level for Luo Zheng.
Even the Demon Clan had been driven away by Luo Zheng alone. He had already drifted away from the rules of the Tower of Sin.
Standing in the central hall of the fifteenth floor, Luo Zheng suddenly said indifferently, "All seat holders, come out. It's time to set new rules."

Chapter 613
Now that the demons had been driven out of the Tower of Sin, it was time to reshuffle the cards.
Previously, the demons had taken 26 seats, so how would they be distributed?
Every race's seat controller had their own plans.
At the same time, they knew that Luo Zheng could distribute the 26 seats however he wanted!
Judging from the current situation, the only race that Luo Zheng had to be wary of was the Yao Ye Clan. After all, the Purple Heart Holy Land was under the protection of the Heavenly Feather Holy Land. If the Yao Ye Clan didn't appear, the Twin Devil Holy Land would still have the power to flatten the Purple Heart Holy Land.
Although the Tower of Sin was important, if a Holy Land wanted to truly rise, it would take decades, centuries, or even millennia!
Thus, the other race's seat controllers estimated that some of the 26 seats would be given to the Yao Ye Clan, while the rest would be given to the humans.
As for the other races … As long as Luo Zheng didn't touch their seats, they would be thankful.
Many peak war reverends slowly gathered in the round hall. They all looked at Luo Zheng with complicated gazes, each of them thinking different things.
Who would have thought that the person who made the new rules would be a human general?
Back then, in order to obtain a seat, each race had used all their strength, even sacrificing their lives! However, a human general had driven the demons out of the Tower of Sin and taken 26 seats in one go.
The difference was too great, and many peak war reverends couldn't help but admit defeat.
Moreover, with Luo Zheng's current strength, he could even drive out all the other races, including the Yao Ye Clan.
Of course, Luo Zheng obviously wouldn't do that.
Driving out all the other races wouldn't benefit Luo Zheng much. Moreover, although the Tower of Sin was important, the development of the Holy Land was even more important. As the saying goes, if the skin doesn't exist, the hair won't exist. Everyone had their eyes on the Tower of Sin because they wanted to develop their own Holy Land.
If Luo Zheng did the same to all the other races, the Purple Heart Holy Land would be destroyed by the Twin Devil Holy Land.
As for the rules of the Tower of Sin, Luo Zheng had thought deeply about them.
In fact, after so many years of development, the set of rules was indeed quite good. As long as one entered the Tower of Sin, they would have to constantly walk on the edge of life and death. Only the true elites would be able to climb up step by step.
Powerful ancient martial art practitioners could obtain more resources, more Light of Creation, and more points. Weak ancient martial art practitioners were directly eliminated and even became nutrients for the Tower of Sin.
The Tower of Sin was just a miniature version of the world of martial artists.
"How should the rules be changed?" A peak war reverend of the Giant Clan asked.
Luo Zheng smiled faintly. "The rules of the Sin Tower from the first floor to the fourteenth floor remain unchanged."
"And then?" another peak war reverend of the Beast race asked.
The Tower of the Tower of Sin's Of Sin, the peak war reverends, the peak war reverends's to the Tower of Sin,, the peak war reverends, the peak war reverends
Most importantly, how would the seats be distributed on the fifteenth floor? What did Of the Sin to do? To The Of the seats seats seats to themselves? Or to keep the rules? Was for them be changed Of the time
"All the seats on the fifteenth floor will be reserved. I was the one who chased away the Demon Clan, so the twenty-six seats will be freely allocated by me," Luo Zheng said indifferently.
These words made many peak war reverends sigh in relief. It seemed that Luo Zheng was not as domineering as they had imagined.
Although these peak war reverends had a lot of information about Luo Zheng, they were afraid that Luo Zheng would be even more domineering than the Demon Clan. At least from Luo Zheng's current tone, he would not deprive so many peak war reverends of their seats. However, Luo Zheng did not finish his words, so they did not know what other requests he had.
Luo Zheng paused for a moment, then said, "One more thing, the seats of the Human Clan and the Demon Night Clan will be permanent and will not be rotated with other races!"
"Permanent seats?"
"This way, won't the Human Clan and the Demon Night Clan always occupy the seats?"
"This is too …"
The one hundred and twenty-eight seats at the top of the tower were not fixed. Every once in a while, the seat controllers would compete with each other to determine the ranking of the seats. The weaker seat controllers would lose their seats, and the newly promoted peak war reverends would replace them.
The Demon Clan and the Demon Night Clan were the strongest, so they controlled the most seats.
In fact, the rules were set to favor the stronger races from the very beginning. After all, only the Demon Clan and the Demon Night Clan could produce so many geniuses to fight for more seats.
This created a situation where the strong were stronger and the weak were weaker.
Therefore, after so many years of development, the Human Clan only had four seat controllers. Moreover, these four seats were not very stable. Perhaps the next time the Human Clan fought, they would have to give up their seats.
When the Human Clan was at its worst, they usually only had one seat, or even none. Therefore, when Luo Zheng saw that Luo Zheng had four seats, he was still relatively strong.
If the permanent seats were set, the Human Clan and the Demon Night Clan would not have to compete, and the number of seats would not decrease every year!
Luo Zheng had also thought about this carefully.
Luo Zheng knew very well that even if he temporarily changed the situation of the Human Clan and the Purple Heart Holy Land, this was not a long-term plan. Luo Zheng did not have that much time to hide in the Sin Tower. After obtaining the inheritance, Luo Zheng would choose to return to the Central Domain.
There, Luo Zheng's most important person would be waiting for him.
This time might be a few months, or a year, but it would not be more than three years.
Once he left, the Purple Heart Holy Land would not be able to rise in such a short time. Perhaps the Demon Night Clan would be able to take care of the Human Clan because of Xun, but it would be wishful thinking for the Human Clan to rise to the same level as the Demon Night Clan.
This was why Luo Zheng wanted to set up permanent seats. This way, at least the Human Clan's seats would not decrease.
More importantly, not only would the Human Clan have permanent seats, but so would the Demon Night Clan. This meant that the Human Clan and the Demon Night Clan would be tied together. If the other clans were not satisfied with the permanent seats, they would first have to ask the Demon Night Clan. At that time, the Demon Night Clan would not have to care about anyone's face and would be the first to rush out and secure the permanent seats.
This was Luo Zheng's plan.
"That's right. The Human Clan and the Demon Night Clan don't have to compete for the seats. They will be kept forever. There are pros and cons to this. The downside is that you will not be able to compete for the permanent seats of the Human Clan and the Demon Night Clan. The upside is that the Human Clan and the Demon Night Clan will not expand their seats," Luo Zheng said calmly.
Now, Luo Zheng still had 26 seats from the Demon Clan to distribute. If he gave all of them to the Human Clan, then the Human Clan would have 30 seats, which was more than enough.
"Is there anyone who objects?" Luo Zheng asked.
Many peak War Reverends did not say anything. They knew that it was useless to say anything. Moreover, Luo Zheng's words made sense. The Demon Night Clan and the Human Clan together had close to 50 seats. The remaining 70 or so seats would be distributed by the remaining clans.
The situation was similar when they were with the Demon Clan.
Moreover, the other clans were worried that the Demon Clan and the Demon Night Clan would continue to expand their influence. If they continued to develop according to the previous rules, the Demon Night Clan and the Demon Clan would have more and more seats while the other clans would have fewer and fewer seats. In the end, all the seats would fall into the hands of the Demon Night Clan and the Demon Clan.
Now that Luo Zheng's rules had stopped the Demon Night Clan from expanding, it meant that the Demon Night Clan and the Human Clan's seats would be forever locked at this number … This was not a bad thing.
"No objections …"
"I don't have any objections either."
Luo Zheng did not ask for their permission at all.
Luo Zheng only gave 8 of the 26 seats to the Demon Night Clan. This way, the Demon Night Clan would have 24 seats, while the Human Clan would have 22 seats. They would become the second most powerful clan in the Tower of Sin.
As for how Ling Yan and Zhao Fenqin planned to distribute the seats, Luo Zheng did not need to interfere.
Ling Yan was fine. Although having 8 more seats was a good thing for the Demon Night Clan, it was not enough to make Ling Yan so excited.

Chapter 614
The vast majority of the Peak War Reverends were on the fourteenth floor of the Sin Tower.
The most outstanding Peak War Reverend on the fourteenth floor had to obtain the qualifications to be the seat controller through competition before he could enter the fifteenth floor and obtain an independent space, which was a small world.
The problem was that there were only fourteen Peak War Reverends in the Human Clan. This included Zhao Fenqin, Zhou Zhuohe and the other three seat controllers.
Zhao Fenqin was stunned by this sudden happiness. He was even very worried now, what should he do without so many Peak War Reverends?
After thinking about it, he still used his true essence to send a sound transmission to Luo Zheng.
The solution that Luo Zheng gave was very simple. If the Peak War Reverend was not filthy, then he would use the Ninth Stage War Reverend. If the Ninth Stage War Reverend was not enough, then he would use the Eighth Stage …
However, in the end, the seats were still filled. Of course, Zhou Fenqin would not let the Ninth Stage War Reverend be the seat controller. In the end, he found a few Peak War Reverends from the Purple Heart Sacred Land to take up this position. After all, in such a large Purple Heart Sacred Land, there were still quite a few Peak War Reverends.
After dealing with this matter, under Zhao Fenqin's invitation, Luo Zheng still made a trip to the Purple Heart Sacred Land.
As a member of the Human Clan, Luo Zheng had never entered the Purple Heart Sacred Land.
However, his reputation in the Purple Heart Sacred Land had already reached a point where it made people's hair stand on end.
When Luo Zheng had just started climbing the Sin Tower, the Purple Heart Sacred Land had already begun to publicize Luo Zheng.
After all, at that time, Luo Zheng, as a warrior, had actually challenged the generals of other races. This was enough to make the humans of the Purple Heart Sacred Land proud and elated.
At that time, the Purple Heart Sacred Land had solemnly told the tens of millions of Human Clan s in the Sacred Land that Luo Zheng's appearance could very well bring them a new seat.
A new seat meant an additional right to speak, as well as an additional five spots and hope …
However, just as everyone was paying attention, Luo Zheng's performance could only be described as soaring into the sky. This was not a matter of one more seat. This meant that Luo Zheng had relied on his own strength to control the entire Sin Tower!
Luo Zheng's performance had greatly exceeded everyone's expectations.
Although there were some voices of distrust in the Purple Heart Holy Land due to the encirclement of the Twin Devil Holy Land, after the Sky Feather Holy Land made a move, their mood quickly stabilized.
But now, Luo Zheng had successfully driven away all the demons from the Tower of Sin. This nightmare that had once loomed over the heads of the Human Race had actually dissipated …
One had to know that in the Sin Tower, countless humans had been slaughtered by the Devil Clan!
One day later, Luo Zheng, Xia Shuang, and the rest, as well as several seat controllers, passed through the cursed lands and arrived at the Purple Heart Holy Ground through a transmission array.
As soon as Luo Zheng appeared, a huge sound wave rushed towards him, giving him a fright!
"Luo Zheng! Luo Zheng! Luo Zheng! "
The Purple Heart Holy Land's teleportation array to the Cursed Lands was built at the peak of a tall mountain. Standing at the peak, Luo Zheng scanned his surroundings. At the bottom of the mountain, there was a dense mass of people! These people looked at him from afar, constantly cheering, as if a great hero had returned.
Xia Shuang had a sweet smile on her face as she stood beside Luo Zheng. Her soft and voluptuous body deliberately moved towards him.
It was said that beauties loved heroes. From the first time Luo Zheng met Lord Jing, when he released his soul force to drive away the two War Reverends, Xia Shuang's heart was already thumping, and there was a trace of hope in her heart.
Unfortunately, Xia Shuang could not guess Luo Zheng's attitude. Coupled with his mysterious identity, she gradually understood that Luo Zheng would not stay on the Sea God Continent for long.
Where did this young man come from?
Xia Shuang guessed thousands of times in her heart. Unfortunately, the Sea God Continent was a relatively isolated place. Even if Xia Shuang searched through all the historical records, she could not find any trace of him.
If he suddenly left that day, would she be reluctant to part with him? Hearing those loud cheers, Xia Shuang's face flashed with confusion.
At this moment, three streamers of light appeared in the sky. The person in the lead stepped on a flying sword and suddenly flew towards this side.
That person looked to be in his early forties. He was handsome, like a celestial figure.
From the momentum transmitted from his body, Luo Zheng immediately understood that these three people were all mighty figures of the Divine Sea Realm. The leader was naturally the leader of the Purple Heart Holy Land, Situ Haotian. Along the way, Xia Shuang had introduced to Luo Zheng in detail that the old men beside Situ Haotian were the previous leaders of the Purple Heart Holy Land, Sikong Ming and Li Jiuhai.
Situ Haotian stood on the peak of the mountain and bowed to Luo Zheng, "I have long heard of Young Hero Luo Zheng's name. Today, I finally meet you. You are indeed a heroic young man, handsome and extraordinary!"
"You flatter me!" Luo Zheng greeted with a smile.
"You are a guest from afar. Young Hero Luo Zheng, please come to the main hall to discuss in detail …"
After some greetings, everyone moved towards the main hall of the Purple Heart Holy Land.
This was the second Holy Land that Luo Zheng had been to.
To be called a Holy Land, it was naturally much larger than the fifth-rank and fourth-rank Sects. Moreover, on the Sea God Continent, because there were so many races, there seemed to be no ordinary humans in this Holy Land. Almost everyone was a warrior.
This was easy to understand.
The environment of the Sea God Continent was different from that of the Central Domain. The Central Domain was almost entirely occupied by humans. Even if there was a war between Sects, the ordinary people still lived their own lives and didn't have much interaction with the warriors.
The Sea God Continent, on the other hand, was different. There were many different races here, and the rules of natural selection were on full display here. The human race was weak to begin with, so there was very little space for the weak ordinary humans to survive here!
Flying was forbidden in the central area of the Holy Land. However, under the leadership of Situ Haotian, Luo Zheng and the others naturally didn't have this restriction.
The main hall was built on a gentle hillside. The entire hillside was paved with a layer of white jade. It looked like a celestial palace in the sky.
On both sides of the main hall, the geniuses of the Human Race's younger generation were gathered.
Although these people's cultivations weren't high, they were all rare talents. In order to cultivate more powerhouses, the Purple Heart Holy Land would screen out these geniuses every year and carefully cultivate them. Once they became battle generals, they would be sent to the cursed land and enter the Tower of Sin to cultivate.
However, as the number of seats controlled by the Human Race increased, these people didn't need to wait until they were battle generals to enter the Tower of Sin. They could be sent in as warriors. With the Human Race's influence in the Tower of Sin, they wouldn't have to worry about these people being casually killed. After all, the environment in the Tower of Sin had been greatly improved!
"Look, that's Luo Zheng. I saw him on the letter tablet!"
"I heard that although he's only a battle general, those Revered Warriors aren't his match!"
"Senior-apprentice Sister Xia Shuang is actually following behind him! Do you think Senior-apprentice Sister Xia Shuang will marry Luo Zheng? "
Hearing the words of these human geniuses, Xia Shuang's face turned red. These words naturally reached the ears of Situ Haotian and the others, but they only faintly smiled. In the eyes of a martial artist, marriage was also a matter of fate.
Among these young geniuses, there was a 14 year old child. As he watched Luo Zheng fly past, his red face shined with a strange luster. This young boy didn't speak, but at this moment, a strong obsession was constantly gathering in his heart of martial arts!
"In the future, I will definitely become someone like Luo Zheng!" This child shouted in his heart.
In fact, several years later, this child did indeed achieve this. He became the second human in the Tower of Sin to be recognized by the Demi-Immortal Clan. This was because he had the same fate as Luo Zheng. But this was a story for another time!
After entering the main hall, all sorts of etiquette were displayed. Luo Zheng was indeed treated as an honored guest.
To be treated like this by a Divine Sea Realm expert was already an incomparable honor. However, this wasn't the first time Luo Zheng had experienced this. Last time, he had encountered a situation even more ridiculous than this in the Heavenly Feather Holy Land …
Then, Situ Haotian and Luo Zheng began to discuss the Tower of Sin.
As for Luo Zheng's arrangement, Situ Haotian couldn't find any flaws. With a permanent seat, the human race didn't have to worry about losing control of the Tower of Sin. Not to mention, Luo Zheng had also dragged the Demon Night Clan into this.
"However, with Young Hero Luo Zheng's arrangement, does it mean that he already has the intention to leave?" Situ Haotian guessed.
This wasn't difficult to guess. As long as Luo Zheng was in the Tower of Sin, no one in the Tower of Sin could oppose him. However, Luo Zheng's arrangement was clearly to prevent Luo Zheng from leaving the Tower of Sin and the human race from declining again. Now that they were tied together with the Demon Night Clan, from a certain perspective, they could rest easy.
