Many people felt a trace of shame in their hearts, but even more people had resolute expressions on their faces.
Luo Zheng was right. Since he was going to die anyway, why not take a gamble?
Therefore, in the face of over a thousand strange birds, everyone began to use their own ultimate techniques!
Bang!
"Phew …"
"Hua …"
All kinds of attacks were launched towards the sky!
Flame, frost, lightning, fist radiance, sword radiance …
It was these attacks that actually managed to stop the black mass of strange birds!
Some of the strange birds were sucked into the attacks of many Peak War Reverends. Although their defense was strong enough, they could not withstand the attacks of so many Peak War Reverends. They were instantly riddled with holes and fell from the sky.
It was a pity that there were too many strange birds. They were only able to block them for a moment before many of the strange birds dispersed the attacks below and surged forward once again.
"It's really over this time …"
"Clench your teeth, fight to the death!"
"That's right, even if we die, we won't let these strange birds have a good time!"
Just as everyone was preparing to fight with their lives on the line, another "Gu Gu" sound came from their ears.
Soon after, everyone saw a thick tongue pop out from a deep black pool not far away! That tongue was actually over a hundred feet wide, and when it extended out like this, it pulled out a distance of three to four miles, and suddenly swept towards them!
The thick tongue came like lightning. In the next moment, everyone saw a black shadow appear, and in the next moment, everyone realized that the huge tongue had already appeared above their heads!
Compared to this thick tongue, those strange birds were like little mosquitoes!
On the thick tongue, mucus continuously dripped down. Even though they were standing below the tongue, maintaining a distance of several zhang from the thick tongue, everyone could still feel the warm and rotten smell emitted by the tongue.
"Everyone, be careful of the mucus on your tongues. It's highly poisonous!" Zhao Fenqin reminded.
But just as he finished speaking, a Peak War Reverend was wrapped up by the mucus dripping from his tongue!
The Peak War Reverend didn't even have time to scream before his entire body melted bit by bit! It was as if he had disappeared into thin air in front of everyone!
One had to know that the Peak War Reverend still had a weapon in his hand, and it was even a low grade sacred artifact. But now, the Peak War Reverend and the sacred artifact in his hand had all melted into nothingness.
Everyone's expression changed, including Luo Zheng.
Although Luo Zheng's immortal artifact body was known to be impervious to poison, looking at the power of the mucus, even a sacred artifact could be easily melted. His own body would definitely not be able to withstand it!
Fortunately, the pressure from the tongue was not fast. Everyone was on guard and dodged, so it was not difficult to avoid it.
The huge tongue continued to roll around above everyone's heads. As long as the strange birds were rolled up by the huge tongue, they would be stuck to it. Not long later, most of the strange birds were rolled up by the tongue!
The remaining dozens of strange birds screamed and fled in all directions!
As for the vast majority of the strange birds, they were firmly stuck to the tongue and were quickly melted by the mucus on the tongue!
After rolling up these strange birds, the huge tongue rolled back. Luo Zheng looked in the direction of the tongue. The red light in the Profound Nether Cave was very weak, and the line of sight was not very good. Even a Peak War Reverend could not see anything in the distance.
However, the green light in Luo Zheng's eyes flashed, and the Clear Eyes Spirit Eyes could clearly see the scene hidden in the darkness!
He saw a deep black pool in the distance, and in the deep pool, there was a huge toad hiding in the pool!
"This is the Giant Python Toad!"
The Giant Python Toad's body was almost as big as a mountain, but most of its body was hidden under the water line of the deep pool, only revealing a pair of eyes the size of a house, emitting a dark red light!
After rolling back the tongue, the Giant Python Toad's mouth refused for a moment, as if it was still unsatisfied, and cried out again.
"Gugu!"
The violent sound wave immediately set off a huge wave in the deep pool, and at the same time, spread towards Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng did not move, and his gaze was still fixed on the toad. He saw that the Giant Python Toad actually stretched out two thick forelimbs, preparing to leave the deep pool.
"It's coming over …" Luo Zheng's heart tightened.
At this time, many Peak War Reverends were still immersed in the joy of narrowly escaping death. They never thought that in their moment of despair, they would be saved by such a thick tongue. Although one of the Peak War Reverends was corroded by the mucus of the tongue, at least the majority of them survived!
However, Luo Zheng did not move at all, as if he had been immobilized, staring straight ahead.
"Alright, everyone, quickly leave this place. Although we have not entered the territory of the Giant Python Toad, we are very close. If it …" Zhao Fenqin still said very cautiously.
At this moment, Luo Zheng shouted, "Everyone, quickly leave! Spread out, spread out! "
After Luo Zheng finished speaking, the power of the dragon scales in his body suddenly erupted. With the force of his feet, his entire body shot towards the sky!
Hearing Luo Zheng's reminder and seeing the speed at which Luo Zheng was escaping, a strong sense of danger rose in everyone's hearts. The many Peak War Reverends who were originally gathered together also started to scatter!
Just as the crowd dispersed, they saw the gigantic tongue lashing out at the spot where they were just a moment ago!
"Bam!"
That huge tongue directly created a large crater with a diameter of seven to eight meters on the ground! The mucus secreted from the tongue continued to corrode the ground, making sizzling sounds.
Everyone's faces flashed with palpitations.

Chapter 622
Seeing the tongue coming towards him, Luo Zheng's expression changed drastically!
Could it be that the giant toad knows that I've seen it?
There was no time for Luo Zheng to think too much at this moment!
The others dodged the tongue, but Zhao Fenqin and Zhou Zhuohe's expressions instantly darkened!
Luo Zheng was now the most important person in the Tower of Sin. The others could die, but Luo Zheng could not be in any danger.
Although Zhao Fenqin did not want to anger the giant toad, they had no other choice.
"Luo Zheng, retreat!"
Zhao Fenqin and Zhou Zhuohe attacked at almost the same time!
The two of them cooperated well. With their combined strength, they could fight against a beginner level Battle-Saint!
Zhao Fenqin and Zhou Zhuohe each sent out two sword beams. The two sword beams intertwined and shot towards the giant tongue!
Although the giant tongue was fast, its size was huge. With such a huge target, the two's attacks were bound to hit.
However, even though the two intertwined sword beams were comparable to the attack of a Battle Saint, they did not cause any damage to the tongue at all. It was as if the sword beams had merely tickled the tongue and could not stop it in the slightest!
"It's over!"
Seeing this, Zhao Fenqin and the others' hearts sank. No matter how lucky Luo Zheng was, it was useless. No one could save him in this situation!
This was the Mysterious Underworld Cave, located under the Cursed Land. It was impossible for a Divine Sea Realm warrior to appear here, right? Moreover, even if a Divine Sea Realm warrior came, so what? Zhao Fenqin estimated that even if Situ Haotian came, he would still be no match for the toad.
Facing the rapidly approaching giant tongue, Luo Zheng's mind was spinning rapidly. The mucus on the tongue could even dissolve a Saint Tool. He would not be able to resist it! Even if his special body could resist it for a moment, he would still die if he entered the giant toad's stomach.
Xun, who was floating beside Luo Zheng, looked serious. She thrust out her spear, and an invisible wave burst toward the tongue!
At the same time, Yun Luo attacked as well. She stretched out her hand and pointed lightly, causing a ray of light to shoot towards the tongue as well!
"Pu pu!"
The attacks of the two women were effective, especially Xun. After thrusting out with her spear, the spear danced in her hand, and the invisible force kept destroying the insides of the tongue, pulling out countless small cuts on the side of the tongue!
These cuts were about the size of a person!
However, although the wound wasn't small, it was still a small wound for such a huge tongue. It couldn't stop the tongue from rushing towards Luo Zheng!
"Retreat!"
Of course, Luo Zheng couldn't just sit there and wait for his death. The moment he saw the big tongue rushing towards him, he quickly retreated.
However, he knew very well that the toad had already crawled out of the pitch-black pool. If he retreated, it would definitely follow him ….
How to counterattack?
"Stars Sink and Earth Quake!"
Luo Zheng's first move was his strongest punch. Holding back a trump card at a time like this was courting death!
"Peng!"
Luo Zheng's punch created a vortex of stars, causing the stinking tongue to cave in. Unfortunately, it was useless. Just like Xun's attack, although it could cause damage to the tongue, how difficult was it to completely destroy such a huge tongue?
This was the first time Luo Zheng's "Stellar Combat Body" had failed. Firstly, Luo Zheng had only cultivated the "Stellar Combat Body" for a short period of time. Secondly, his cultivation level was too low. This giant toad wasn't something people of their level could fight against. It was because of this that this place was classified as a forbidden area!
It was also because of this that more than half of the people in the Mysterious Underworld Cave died every year. And this was only in the outer area. Those who entered the inner area had a higher mortality rate. As for those who entered the core area, their mortality rate was 100%. This was because those who entered the core area had never returned!
"What do I do?"
Luo Zheng retreated quickly. He even crossed space, hoping to put some distance between himself and the tongue. However, the tongue was clearly faster than him!
"Fire! Black Fire! Qinglong! "
Luo Zheng didn't have time to dawdle. He directly shouted at Qinglong in his mind.
The Black Fire wasn't big, but it could burn everything. He could only give it a try …
Under normal circumstances, Luo Zheng wouldn't use the Black Flame, especially in front of so many people. However, he had no choice but to use the Black Flame now. He didn't want his body to be melted by the tongue and become the toad's food!
"Whoosh!"
A small but agile flame suddenly emerged from between Luo Zheng's eyebrows!
As soon as the flame emerged, it happily danced in the air!
When Yun Luo saw the Black Fire, her eyes flashed. Then, she frowned as if she was lost in thought!
When Xun saw the Black Fire, her expression changed. She subconsciously looked at Yun Luo …
As Luo Zheng's body, Xun had always had her own thoughts. She had seen the Black Fire before, but Yun Luo had never seen Luo Zheng's Black Fire.
Obviously, Xun was very unhappy that Yun Luo had discovered the Black Fire. However, Luo Zheng was in a critical situation, so Xun didn't have time to worry about it.
Everyone watched as the Black Fire emerged from between Luo Zheng's eyebrows. The Black Fire seemed to have a mind of its own. Luo Zheng didn't control it and rushed toward the tongue.
Compared to the huge tongue, the Black Fire was smaller than an ant. It instantly drilled into the tongue. As the Black Fire was too small, no wound could be found when it drilled into the tongue …
However, the moment the Black Fire drilled into the tongue, the tongue that was originally flicking toward Luo Zheng suddenly twisted violently.
It was like a snake that had its head chopped off. The tongue twisted crazily without any purpose. Fortunately, the people around had already distanced themselves from the tongue. Otherwise, if they were accidentally sucked in, they would also die!
"Bang, bang, bang, bang …"
The tongue whipped the ground again and again, creating huge holes in the ground.
Such a huge movement must have alarmed the demons within dozens of miles. However, the strength of these demons was naturally not as strong as the Giant Python Toad, so they all hid deeply. In the outer layer of the Xuanming Cave, this Giant Python Toad was considered a ferocious creature at the top of the food chain!
"What is that Black Fire? How can it make the Giant Python Toad suffer so much? "
"I don't know. Who knows how many secrets Luo Zheng has!"
"This toad is too stupid. Why doesn't it retract its tongue?"
"Do you think the Giant Python Toad is stupider than you? The Black Fire is obviously still in its tongue. Although I don't know what the Black Fire is, seeing the Giant Python Toad in so much pain, if the Black Fire doesn't come out, it will retract its tongue. Won't it burn in its mouth? "
After the Giant Python Toad's tongue lashed on the ground for a while, everyone saw a fierce flame suddenly erupt from the front part of the tongue. From the tip of the tongue to about one zhang long, the entire tongue was completely burned!
Everyone suddenly smelled the smell of roasted meat mixed with the smell of roasted meat!
"Whoosh!"
That wisp of Black Fire drilled out of the tongue!
Just as the black flames burrowed out, the tongue that had burned off the tip of the tongue chose to roll back. It was as if it was fleeing in a panic to avoid the black flames. The tongue that was close to one kilometer long rolled back and instantly disappeared into the darkness not far away.
At this time, the Black Fire seemed to be very happy. It hovered in the air for a while and even made a chirping sound. Then, it directly disappeared into the space between Luo Zheng's eyebrows.
Luo Zheng didn't even stop. He looked at the Giant Python Toad that had already left the pool and said, "Run!"
The Giant Python Toad was like a mountain, creeping in the darkness. This distance was just a jump for such a huge toad. When the Giant Python Toad really pounced on them, no one would be able to escape!
Everyone followed Luo Zheng's gaze and looked over. They didn't see anything, but they didn't ask Luo Zheng. It was very simple. The Giant Python Toad's tongue was already so terrifying. There was no need to see what would happen next. They were no match for it. Luo Zheng had just burned a part of the Giant Python Toad's tongue. If they didn't run now, when would they run?
As for what Luo Zheng saw, they didn't want to see it, and they didn't dare to see it!

Chapter 623
Everyone left the place as fast as they could.
Even in the outer layer of Xuan Ming Cave, any place with great danger was unapproachable. If they were not careful, everyone would die here.
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed with a faint green light. Although he was retreating, he was also very vigilant!
Although the Giant Python Toad's tongue was fast, there was still some distance between them. He still had room to dodge.
However, if the toad pounced over, he would be helpless. Everyone would die.
Luo Zheng saw that although the toad had left the huge pond, it had only moved two steps forward. Its black figure had completely blended into the darkness. Only two house-sized eyes were staring at him!
"Luckily, it didn't plan on chasing us." Luo Zheng retreated backwards. However, his eyes were fixed on the toad in the shadows. After he had put a considerable distance between them, he turned around and caught up with the group.
"That toad, what's its background?" Xun asked Yun Luo.
The few peak war reverends looked at Xun, but did not know why Xun asked this question. This toad might have been here for a long time. It was clearly marked on the map of Xuan Ming Cave. Moreover, this toad had never moved and had always been here.
The demonic creatures in Xuan Ming Cave were very complicated. There were many types and they were usually very strong.
Furthermore, any powerful demonic creature that charged out of the Cursed Lands could bring about a calamity to the Neptune Continent. Take this Giant Python Toad as an example. There was probably no one in the entire Neptune Continent who could stop it!
Fortunately, the demonic creatures in Xuan Ming Cave were very strong. However, until now, none of them had walked out of Xuan Ming Cave. The reason was unknown. However, most of them guessed that these creatures were afraid of the power of the Cursed Land and did not dare to walk out of Xuan Ming Cave.
However, this guess was quickly overturned. Xuan Ming Cave was underground. These demonic creatures could dig a tunnel and come out from another part of the Sea God Continent. However, until now, no powerful demonic creature had done so. Therefore, Xuan Ming Cave was not as simple as imagined.
"That's a Giant Python Toad. It's one of Yu Chanzi's spiritual beasts." Yun Luo directly answered.
"Quasi-Realm Master, Yu Chanzi?" Xun asked.
"Yes, he's dead." Yun Luo's expression remained unchanged.
Although the conversation between the two was short, everyone understood it. However, it didn't matter if they understood it or not, even if the big toad could be subdued as a spirit object, and some of these Peak war elder s were proficient in capturing spirit beasts and planting enslavement imprints.
But to capture the spirit beast of a quasi-sector lord? That was no different from courting death.
Following Zhao Fenqin's continuous retreat, everyone finally returned to the normal route.
Many people's faces were still deathly pale …
Zhou Zhuohe counted the number of people. Just now, after being chased by those strange birds, there were only less than fifty people left out of the hundreds of people in the Human Clan.
In such a situation, not being completely wiped out was already the best outcome.
"Let's go back the way we came?" A peak war elder suggested.
The peak war elder who said this was not afraid of death. This peak war elder had entered the Profound Nether Cave before, but he had never encountered such a great danger. He felt that there might be some changes in the Profound Nether Cave, which was why he made this suggestion.
As soon as the Peak War Elder said this, many War Elder s and some War Generals also agreed.
"Yes, the activities of the demons in the Profound Nether Cave this year are too frequent. I feel that something is wrong."
"To be safe, it's better to go first. Since the Profound Nether Cave opens every year, we can wait for a chance in the future."
"I don't want to stay in this damned place anymore. Everyone says that the Profound Nether Cave is filled with death. I think it's a certainty!"
With everyone agreeing, Zhao Fenqin and Zhou Zhuohe also revealed expressions of interest. Their trip to the Mysterious Underworld Cave this time was truly unlucky. They had encountered such a great danger the moment they entered. For safety's sake, it would be better to return to the Sin Tower first.
However …
Zhao Fenqin looked at Luo Zheng. He still had to consult Luo Zheng on this question.
When Zhao Fenqin looked over, Luo Zheng smiled faintly, "If you are willing to go back, of course I won't have any objections, but I will stay."
Xun's gaze was calm, and there seemed to be a faint flame flowing in her red eyes. She did not speak, but Yun Luo said, "I will also continue to follow Luo Zheng to explore the Profound Nether Cave."
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Zhao Fenqin was in a difficult position.
To put it bluntly, including Zhao Fenqin himself, all the peak war elders here added together were not as important as Luo Zheng!
To the Purple Heart Sacred Land, it was equivalent to relying on Luo Zheng to control the Sin Tower. If they lost all their peak war elders, the Purple Heart Sacred Land could still nurture more peak war elders. However, if they lost Luo Zheng, the Human Race's position in the Sin Tower would return to its original state.
For example, the alliance between the Demon Night Clan's Sky Feather Sacred Land and the Purple Heart Sacred Land was because of Luo Zheng's Sword Spirit Body woman, Xun. To put it bluntly, it was because of Luo Zheng. If it wasn't for Luo Zheng, why would a dignified Rank Seven Sacred Land ally with a Rank Six Sacred Land like the Purple Heart Sacred Land?
If Zhao Fenqin could think of the pros and cons, many peak war elders might not be able to think of it.
"The Profound Nether Cave is too dangerous this time. I suspect that a chaotic tide has erupted, so …" Zhao Fenqin did not give up. He wanted to persuade Luo Zheng to return.
"Chaotic tide?" Luo Zheng asked doubtfully.
"Yes, but this is only a guess. However, the Profound Nether Cave seems to have an outbreak every ten years or so. When the chaotic tide erupts, the demon creatures in the Profound Nether Cave will be very active. The danger level will also increase greatly. Normally, under these circumstances, we will cancel this year's exploration." Zhao Fenqin stared at Luo Zheng and said.
He said this to remind Luo Zheng of the dangers in the Profound Nether Cave. He couldn't just watch so many humans die with him, right?
Luo Zheng and Zhao Fenqin had different considerations.
After looking at the Giant Python Toad, how could Luo Zheng not know the dangers of the Profound Nether Cave?
However, Luo Zheng did not have much time left. He had been on this continent for close to a year. With each passing day, Luo Zheng's heart would be in suspense!
If Luo Zheng missed this opportunity, he would have to wait another year!
Although if Luo Zheng was given a year, it was very likely that he would become a war elder, or even a peak war elder, and with his strength, it would be relatively safer for him to enter the Profound Nether Cave. However, in a year's time, there were too many variables that could happen in the central region, Luo Zheng could not wait!
"I'm sorry. Although I know that this place is very dangerous, I really cannot wait another year." Luo Zheng shook his head and looked at Zhao Fenqin with a persistent gaze.
Zhao Fenqin took a deep breath. He should not have left Luo Zheng in the Profound Nether Cave. If he really did that, Luo Zheng would be fine. However, if Luo Zheng could not return to the Sin Tower, the Purple Heart Holy Land would be the first to come after him!
However, many people had already thought of retreating. He could not bring those people into the fire pit!
In the end, he said, "Zhou Zhuohe, you and I will stay here. The rest of you will return to the Sin Tower!"
Zhou Zhuohe did not hesitate. He understood Zhao Fenqin's situation very well and had his own thoughts.
Hearing Zhao Fenqin's words, many peak War Reverends heaved a sigh of relief. Many of them did not want to stay in this damned place for even a moment longer.
It was not difficult to go back the way they came. Just now, they were chased by the Tuna Birds all the way, but they only ran for a few dozen miles. To experts of their level, this journey did not take long …
After the peak War Reverends left with the Generals, Zhao Fenqin turned his head and said, "Since that is the case, we will also risk our lives to accompany you! However, Young Hero Luo Zheng, the opportunity you want to obtain in the Profound Nether Cave must not be an ordinary opportunity, right? "
There were opportunities everywhere in the Profound Nether Cave. Even in the outer region, many people had obtained quite a few opportunities. Although the opportunities in the outer region had decreased due to many years of searching, there were still people who found many good things every year!
"I'm not sure either," Luo Zheng replied.
"Not sure?" Zhao Fenqin's face revealed a strange expression. "Then do you have a goal?"
"Probably. If possible, I want to enter the core region of the Profound Nether Cave," Luo Zheng said.
When these words came out, Zhao Fenqin and Zhou Zhuohe's faces were full of sobs. Did this kid eat the wrong medicine?

Chapter 624
The outer region of the Mysterious Underworld Cave was already extremely dangerous. However, Luo Zheng was well aware that so many people had died not long after entering the Mysterious Underworld Cave.
From the beginning, Zhao Fenqin and Zhou Zhuohe had no intention of entering the inner region.
The inner area had to go through a lot of preparation, and it often required the cooperation of several races before they could enter.
Even so, who knew how many peak war venerables had died in the inner region.
However, there were more opportunities in the inner region. Those who could return from there had obtained considerable benefits. Therefore, even though the inner region was extremely dangerous, there were still creatures willing to take the risk.
However, Luo Zheng did not say that he wanted to enter the inner region. He wanted to enter the core region …
The core region was simply a land of death. No one who entered could return. So far, no one knew what was in the core region. It was an unpredictable existence!
On the map, the outer region was marked with many dangerous places. However, there were fewer dangerous places in the inner region. Because people had not explored and understood much, it was more dangerous.
As for the core region … it was completely blank on the map. There was nothing there. There was no need to mark that place because everyone knew that entering it meant certain death.
"Luo Zheng, y-you're joking, right?" Zhao Fenqin stared blankly at Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng chuckled. "Just take it as a joke."
Back then, Immortal Tian Miao had given Luo Zheng this token to let him obtain the inheritance. However, he did not explain it very clearly. Tian Miao had never entered the Mysterious Underworld Cave. Although he knew that the inheritance was inside, he did not know where it was. He only told him that when he approached the inheritance, the jade butterfly would react.
Luo Zheng was not a reckless person. He understood that no matter how heaven-defying his luck was, he would still die when it was time to die.
At this stage, the jade butterfly had not reacted. Luo Zheng naturally chose to explore the outer region first. If he could not find the inheritance in the outer region, he would naturally choose to enter the inner region. If he could not find it in the inner region …
Since the core region was a land of death, Luo Zheng would not rashly barge in. If he still could not obtain the inheritance, Luo Zheng would choose to leave.
He had indeed promised Tian Miao to obtain the inheritance in the Mysterious Underworld Cave. Since it was something important to the entire human race, Luo Zheng would also weigh his own strength. If he could not obtain it with his strength, he would not die in vain.
Hearing Luo Zheng's answer, Zhao Fenqin sighed in relief. No one would die in vain. Fortunately, Luo Zheng still had some rationality.... then he said, "Since that's the case, let's follow the path!
....................................................
As he went deeper and deeper into the outer layer, the surrounding environment was constantly changing. At the same time, the dim red light was constantly increasing in intensity.
The weakened Mysterious Underworld Death Ray was still powerful. If it was an ordinary human without cultivation, they would probably die from being pierced through by this red light.
As the red light strengthened, Zhao Fenqin, Zhou Zhuohe, and Yun Luo all had a faint layer of Protective True Essence on their bodies. It was used to resist the attack of the red light. Because Luo Zheng was a Sword Spirit, he did not use Vital Essence. The piercing feeling did not cause much discomfort to Luo Zheng.
As for Xun, she was a Sword Spirit, so she naturally could not feel pain.
After walking for about an hour, the sound of fighting suddenly came from the side. Then six figures shot out from the bushes.
Two of the six silhouettes held longbows in their hands. They turned around and shot an arrow into the forest. Just as the two of them released the arrow, a greenish-black ghostly claw suddenly stretched out from the forest and grabbed at the two people in the air. The two archers had no time to dodge and were instantly crushed into pieces!
"You guys go quickly. I'll cover you!"
When the ghost claw continued to grab upward, a woman, who was originally the strongest among the six people, shouted. Then, an infinite brilliance bloomed from her body. In the brilliance, there were shadows of flowers. Wherever the brilliance shrouded, a scene of blooming flowers appeared!
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
Countless colorful petals brewed within the brilliance. As soon as those petals appeared, they stuck to the ghostly claw. In the blink of an eye, the ghostly claw that was brimming with murderous aura was covered in petals!
"Explode!"
The woman spat out a single word.
"Boom!"
The violent explosion immediately slowed down the ghost claw. Then, the remaining four people hurriedly fled and rushed toward Luo Zheng.
The four people hurriedly rushed over and saw Luo Zheng. Then, they saw Xun beside Luo Zheng and immediately said, "My king, this place is extremely dangerous. Quickly retreat with us!"
Luo Zheng and the others already saw who it was. It was Ling Yan of the Demon Night clan. Other than Ling Yan, the other three were also peak war reverends of the Demon Night clan!
"Let's go!"
Everyone did not say much and turned their heads to follow. Just now, the strength of that Ghost Claw was not the slightest bit weaker than that Giant Python Toad. This was also fortunate that it was Ling Yan.
After all, Ling Yan was the number one person in the Sin Tower! She was the strongest peak war reverend. Meng Tian was not her match.
"There are only four people left in your Demon Night clan?" Zhao Fenqin said with a face full of surprise.
The Demon Night clan had more seats than the human clan. This time, they had a few dozen more people than the human clan. Now, there were only four people left. Could it be that the Demon Night clan had also encountered a great calamity?
Ling Yan shook her head and said, "I discovered that a chaotic tide might have erupted in the Profound Nether Cave, so I sent most of my clan members back."
Zhao Fenqin nodded, "I also suspected it, so I let them return to the tower first."
Just as everyone fled to a relatively safe area, Ling Yan and the other three Demon Night clan members wanted to kneel again. However, Xun said indifferently, "This place is dangerous. No need for formalities."
Zhao Fenqin then asked, "If that's the case, why didn't you guys return?"
"Since a chaotic tide has erupted, the danger has increased, but the opportunity has also come." Ling Yan said.
Ling Yan was right. The chaotic tide was indeed very dangerous, but at the same time, it was accompanied by great opportunities. For example, there were some places that could not be entered in the past because they were blocked by some extremely ferocious things. However, the chaotic tide had moved those ferocious things, and the dangerous places had been opened.
However, in comparison to the opportunity, the danger was also many times greater. This was also because Ling Yan was skilled and daring. The place they had barged into was originally a place of great danger. Ling Yan brought a few subordinates and barged in, but obtained several Onyx Pearls!
These Dark Nether Pearls were extremely precious. The value of each Dark Nether Pearl was comparable to the value of a medium grade saint artifact. However, Ling Yan had also paid an unknown price. The two peak war reverends who had entered with her had died inside.
"My king, these two Dark Nether Pearls were obtained by this subordinate. Please accept them." Ling Yan respectfully walked in front of Xun and presented the two Dark Nether Pearls to her.
Xun's soul was only a hundred-thousandth of what it used to be. However, at her level, her soul was abnormally powerful. Even if it was only a hundred-thousandth of her soul, it was still far more powerful than the cultivators of the lower realms.
However, it was precisely because of this that it was very difficult to nourish and strengthen her soul. The pills and secret techniques that nourished the soul in the lower realms were completely ineffective. Even when Xun had entered the Heavenly Feather Sacred Land and absorbed the life force of the entire sacred tree, the effects were minimal.
However, these Dark Nether Pearls were items from the upper realms. They were specifically used to repair damaged souls. Xun naturally knew that they could strengthen her soul. She nodded and had Luo Zheng accept them.

Chapter 625
When Zhao Fenqin and the others saw this scene, they were envious, but that was their king, they couldn't be envious.
When Luo Zheng threw the two Abyssal Beads into the Sumeru Ring, Ling Yan suddenly knelt and said to Xun, "My king, this time the chaotic tide broke out in the Profound Nether Cave, it is already an extremely dangerous place. It is not suitable for you to personally explore, please return to the Sin Tower!"
Ling Yan originally wanted to say that if they could obtain any opportunity, they would naturally offer it, but at this moment, it wasn't good to say so. Since Zhao Fenqin knew about the chaotic tide in advance, he must have advised Luo Zheng. Now that Luo Zheng was still here, it must have been them who persisted.
The second time she was advised to return to the Sin Tower, although it was for the good of Xun and Luo Zheng, Xun's face still sank.
Right now, only Luo Zheng could help Xun return to her throne, and Luo Zheng would definitely not give up. She certainly didn't want to hear Ling Yan say such words.
"I won't go back," Xun coldly stared at Ling Yan, then added: "Don't make me say it a second time."
Ling Yan's heart trembled, she also understood that her advice was useless, then she stood up and said: "In that case, I implore my king to let us stay and explore the Profound Nether Cave with you."
"This is a good suggestion," Zhao Fenqin's face revealed joy. Ling Yan's strength was the strongest peak war elder in the Sin Tower! Back then, when Ling Yan was on the tenth floor, she had already climbed up to the fourth row of the Talent Monument. Now that she was at the peak war elder, she was probably not much weaker than an ordinary War Sage. If she were to join them, Zhao Fenqin would have a lot more confidence.
A few days ago, Ling Yan was worried about Luo Zheng following the human war elder, so she discussed with Luo Zheng to follow her Demon Night Clan into the Profound Nether Cave. Xun was now Luo Zheng's sword spirit, if Luo Zheng died, the sword spirit body that Xun relied on would also be destroyed. Ling Yan didn't care about Luo Zheng, but she cared about Xun's comfort.
However, this suggestion was rejected by Luo Zheng.
The current situation was in line with Ling Yan's wishes.
Then, Ling Yan and Zhao Fenqin took out a map, planning to choose a relatively easy route.
At this moment, her eyes flashed, her figure turned, and her slender finger pointed in a direction!
"Whoosh!"
"Bang!"
In the forest not far away, four people's's face had a smile.

After that, everyone planned a route. Now, this temporary team finally set off again.
However, at the same time, at the top of the Tower of Sin, in a secret passage, two tall and sturdy figures were squeezing inside. They were the two Peak war elder s of the Demon Clan, Meng Chong and Tian Hu.
Before the Demon Race retreated from the Tower of Sin, Yuan Bai and Tian Hu were ordered to hide in the Tower of Sin. However, Meng Chong insisted that Yuan Bai be replaced!
Not being able to kill Luo Zheng had always been a knot in Meng Chong's heart. Because of this, his heart had become extremely depressed. Because of this problem, even his heart of martial arts had become unstable.

As for the seats taken by the humans and even the Demon Night Clan, the Demon Clan would have to spit them out one by one!

Chapter 626
The Xuan Ming Cave was illuminated by the Xuan Ming Death Ray all year round, so the environment was very harsh. To be able to grow here, the creatures that reproduced here were definitely much stronger than the outside world.
Under the dim red light, a stalk of grass on the ground was bent by a foot. When the foot moved away, the stalk of grass tenaciously rose up again. It even waved its blade-like leaves in an attempt to counterattack! The leaf was thin, but it could easily cut through an ordinary profound artifact!
If ordinary beasts and poor people passed through this kind of forest, they would never be able to walk out. Because before they could even walk halfway, they would find that there were countless sharp blades cutting their legs. Soon, they would be torn into pieces.
However, this kind of grass was no threat to the many peak war elders. It could not even penetrate their protective true essence!
Ling Yan nimbly swayed her steps as she led the way in front.
Luo Zheng and the others followed closely behind.
As long as the few peak war elders of the Night Clan were like ghosts, they blocked all of their auras and shuttled back and forth in the surroundings.
"In front of us is the Bloody Mouth Pass. Everyone, remove all of your true essence and astral essence. Don't make any sound." Ling Yan said with a true essence sound transmission.
"Bloody Mouth Pass?" Luo Zheng looked ahead. Two short mountain peaks formed an angle in front of them, and a path flowed out. It was indeed a pass, but what was going on with all of their true essence and astral essence?
"On both sides of the Bloody Mouth Pass are the nests of two powerful monsters. One is the Azure Wolf, and the other is the strange bird we saw before, the Swallowing Bird. After passing through this pass, we must block all of our auras." Zhao Fenqin took out a cloak from his spatial ring and covered his body.
The others did the same, using cloaks to cover themselves.
Not everyone could be like Luo Zheng, relying on their bodies to block the red light. Although the Xuan Ming Death Ray was weakened, this kind of light was like a fine needle, piercing the skin would be very uncomfortable. After removing the protective true essence or the protective astral essence, they naturally needed to use a cloak to block it.
After they were ready, everyone held their breaths and carefully walked forward.
The four Peak war elder s of the Night Clan were like agile leopards as they swept past the forest in front of them without making any sound.
Ling Yan and the others could not do this, and the speed of their advance was much less.
As the group got closer and closer to the Bloodmouth Pass, everyone became more and more vigilant. In such a quiet environment, everyone could faintly hear the strange cries of the tuna birds not far away. The scattered cawing sounds indicated that there was at least a large group of tuna birds resting nearby.
On the other side of the Bloody Pass, they could faintly hear the howls of wolves …
Although Luo Zheng had never seen what the Azure Wolf looked like, Luo Zheng reckoned that it was not weaker than the Tuna Bird. No matter which tribe was alerted, they would not be able to bear the consequences!
Everyone held their breaths and carefully moved forward in the darkness. The thumping of their hearts could almost be heard.
Fortunately, the Bloodmouth Pass was not big. The two short mountain peaks formed a natural barrier. As long as they entered, they would be safe.
This careful stealth lasted for the time it took for an incense stick to burn, and they were about to enter the pass. They were less than three hundred feet away from the pass.
"This time, we passed safely." Zhao Fenqin heaved a sigh of relief.
Everyone's expressions immediately relaxed.
At this moment, a light suddenly bloomed from the corner of everyone's eyes.
A faint light flickered not far away.
Luo Zheng's gaze followed the light and saw a stalk of grass.
The grass was the size of a palm, with three leaves extending in three different directions. At the top of the straight and tall roots, there was only a furry stamen blooming with a faint orange light!
"Moon Shadow Begonia!"
When everyone saw the stalk of grass, their expressions changed greatly, especially the four peak war reverends of the Night Clan. Their faces revealed greedy expressions, as if they could not control themselves and walked towards that direction.
The Moon Shadow Begonia was a kind of strange flower and grass produced in the Mysterious Nether Cave. It was said that the Moon Shadow Begonia could expand the dantian, which was very useful for warriors trying to break through to the Divine Sea Realm. So far, only three Moon Shadow Begonia had been born in the Mysterious Nether Cave. The Battle-Saints who had taken these three Moon Shadow Begonia had already stepped into the Divine Sea Realm!
And now, the strongest warrior of the Deep Sea Continent, Liu Yu, had taken one of the three Moon Shadow Begonia!
The fact that Liu Yu could become the most powerful warrior in the Spirit Sea Realm might not be due to the effect of the Moon Shadow Begonia. However, it was a fact that the Moon Shadow Begonia could greatly increase a warrior's ability. After the Dantian expanded, the total amount of Genuine Essence would increase as well. It would be easier to perform certain skills.
Ling Yan looked at the Moon Shadow Begonia and waved her hand at the four peak war reverends of the Night Clan, making a gesture of prohibition!
The Moon Shadow Begonia grew in the territory of the Azure Wolf. Although the Night Clan's concealment skill was very powerful, it was still very risky to go up and pick it!
But at this time, Ling Yan could not speak at all, nor could she use true essence to transmit her voice. This place was very close to the Tuna Bird and the Azure Wolf. The slightest fluctuation of true essence would alarm them.
Ling Yan stared coldly at the peak war reverends of the Night Clan. Unfortunately, they did not listen!
Zhao Fenqin and Zhou Zhuohe were sweating profusely. Not to mention this damned Moon Shadow Begonia, if they startled those monsters, the ten of them would undoubtedly die.
"Xun," Luo Zheng glanced at Xun next to him.
Xun was Luo Zheng's sword spirit body. Like Luo Zheng, she didn't need to circulate her true essence to resist the weakened Mysterious Underworld Death Ray. Moreover, her greatest advantage was that she could fly!
Her Sword Spirit Body originally belonged to Luo Zheng, so when she flew, she would not use true essence at all. Naturally, there would not be any fluctuations of true essence.
Xun immediately understood from Luo Zheng's eyes. Her figure was like a soul without legs, floating towards the Moon Shadow Begonia.
Although Xun's actions might alert the group of demons, Luo Zheng also believed that the Night Clan might be alerted. Since the Night Clan was determined to obtain the Moonshadow Begonia, then why not let him pluck it?!
The four peak war reverends of the Night Clan were still slowly sneaking over. Xun directly flew over, so her speed was much faster. Almost in the blink of an eye, she was close to the Moon Shadow Begonia.
When the four people of the Night Clan saw that Xun wanted to pick the Moon Shadow Begonia, their faces turned cold. How could they not know the danger? It was just that the effect of this strange flower was worth risking their lives for. However, after carefully searching for half a day, before they could even get close to the Moon Shadow Begonia, someone else had already taken it. How could they not be angry?
How could Xun care about their expressions? She gently flicked her spear, and the tip of the spear accurately pointed at the Moon Shadow Begonia. Then, with a gentle shake, the Moon Shadow Begonia's stem was cut off by the tip of the spear. Only then did the Moon Shadow Begonia obediently lie on the tip of the spear.
Xun looked indifferently at the Night Clan and sneered. She turned her head and flew towards Luo Zheng.
The four people of the Night Clan could not take it anymore. One of them lowered his voice and said, "Give it back to us!"
"You bunch of pigs, what right do you have to take it?" Xun also lowered her voice and retorted.
"Otherwise, we'll lure those azure wolves over!" A peak war reverend of the Night Clan gritted his teeth and said.
"If you dare, then go and lure them over." As the king of the Night Clan, when has Xun ever been afraid of other people's threats? She did not seem to care about the words of the peak war reverend.
However, Ling Yan and Zhao Fenqin's expressions were very ugly. They should not have let the Night Clan follow them. They were so obedient just now, but now they were in trouble.
A Night Clan member stared at the tip of Xun's spear. The Moon Shadow Begonia was still emitting a faint glow. That person's figure suddenly shot out, but he did not dare to face the dangers on both sides of the pass and rushed towards Xun.
"Catch." Xun shook the spear and threw the Moon Shadow Begonia she picked to Luo Zheng. Then, the spear turned around in Xun's hand. She did not even look back as the tip of the spear stabbed towards the blurry figure behind her!
The figure of the peak war reverend of the Night Clan was also extremely mysterious. Even if Luo Zheng faced him, he would not have the confidence to break through it in a short period of time. However, Xun's spear was extremely accurate. Just like that, with a casual spear, accompanied by an invisible wave, it hit the chest of the peak war reverend of the Night Clan!
After the peak war reverend of the Night Clan was hit, his face was filled with disbelief. He did not believe that he could not even dodge this spear!
As the peak war reverend fell, he also let out a miserable cry …
Hearing this voice, everyone's hearts trembled!

Chapter 627
Everyone was careful in order to safely pass through the Bloodmouth Pass.
It was not the first time Zhao Fenqin and Ling Yan had come here. The two types of demons that lived on both sides of the Bloodmouth Pass did not interfere with each other, and under normal circumstances, they would not cross the border. Although it looked dangerous, as long as one was careful, it was still relatively safe, at least much safer than those fierce places.
In the past, they had passed through safely many times, because no one would be stupid enough to alarm those demons.
However, they did not expect that the Night Clan would disturb them this time.
"This bunch of stupid pigs, if we can return to the Sin Tower, we will definitely expel the entire Night Clan!" Ling Yan gritted her teeth and said, "Charge!"
After losing the protection of the Demon Clan, the situation of the Night Clan in the Sin Tower was already precarious, and Luo Zheng had even chased away the Demon Clan, so expelling the Night Clan was just a piece of cake. A Twin Devil Sacred Lands was enough to suppress the Heavenly Feather Sacred Lands, but if the other races joined hands, the situation would be more passive.

These Peak war elders of the Night Clan did not have any good intentions. They followed Luo Zheng and naturally sent a message to Meng Chong. Whether or not the Demon Clan could make a comeback, it would have a huge impact on their Night Clan. As long as the Demon Clan could return to the Sin Tower, their Night Clan would still be able to get a share of the pie!
Other than this goal, if they could obtain benefits from the Profound Nether Cave, they would naturally not give up. Seeing the Moon Shadow Begonia, they understood that this was an extremely good opportunity, and they did not want to believe that these guys would dare to make a move here!
He did not expect that the woman with the spear did not even consider the seriousness of the matter and directly killed him. If it was a normal situation, he might not be so weak. After all, he was a Peak war elder, and his Night Clan's movement technique was unfathomable, how could he be so easily pierced by a spear?
It was just that he never thought that Xun would make a move, and that she would directly kill him …
As he screamed, a wolf howl came from not far away.
"Awoo …"
In the forest, there were constantly young shadows swaying. Large groups of demons were rushing over, and the forest was constantly swaying.
At the same time, from the other side of the Bloody Mouth Pass, a series of strange squawks rang out. Clearly, the action just now had alarmed the tuna birds on the other side.
Soon, a huge cloud was formed in the sky. While fleeing, Luo Zheng glanced back and was secretly shocked. There were at least four or five thousand strange birds piled up together …
"Go!"
"Swish swish swish swish …"
More than ten streams of light shot towards the cave at high speed.
The remaining three Peak war elder s of the Night Clan also followed behind Ling Yan. Ling Yan's expression darkened, and she shouted: "Scram!"
A strange light flashed from Ling Yan's hand, and bright flowers bloomed one after another. Those petals floated towards the Peak war elder of the Night Clan.
If these petals were to stick to his body, it would be fatal.
However, no matter how powerful Ling Yan was, it was impossible for her to kill three Peak war elder at the same time. After all, Ling Yan was only a Peak war elder, not to mention that she had casually sent out those petals. Her goal was to leave the Peak war elder of the Night Clan behind!
At this time, running was different. He had to be the fastest, as long as he was faster than others!
If they had known earlier that this group of people would not care about their lives, they would not have gone to retrieve the Moon Shadow Begonia. Although their figures and speed were top-notch, facing the petals that filled the sky, they could only continuously dodge, greatly slowing down their movement speed.
In a few dozen breaths of time, everyone had already rushed through the Bloody Mouth Pass, but the few Peak war elder s of the Night Clan were slowed down by Ling Yan's attacks, and at this time, the tuna birds and azure wolves were getting closer and closer to the few Peak war elder s of the Night Clan!
Ling Yan's expression was cold, she hoped that these few Peak war elder s of the Night Clan could use their lives to resist for a while. Even if these fellows were lucky enough to not die, Ling Yan would personally take their lives.
These few Peak war elder s of the Night Clan were not nameless people. They clearly knew that there was a tide of chaos in the Profound Nether Cave, yet they still dared to stay. The Night Clan did the same thing as the Demon Night Clan and the Human Clan. After discovering that the tide of chaos had erupted, they had sent their clansmen back to the Tower of Sin.
The remaining four people were the seat holders of the Night Clan, and they were the four people at the front.
After being caught off guard and assassinating one of them, the remaining three could only fight to the death.
However, the Night Clan did have their own methods. The strange birds in the sky were like a torrential rain, enveloping the three of them, and the Azure Wolf not far away also surged over like a tide. The true essence on the three of them flickered, and suddenly turned into black mist, slowly drifting about.
"Concealment Technique …"
After the Night Clan turned into a bit of smoke, the Azure Wolf and the Tuna Bird really couldn't do anything. However, how sharp was the sense of smell of these demons? They were very clear that the three of them were hiding in the clouds of smoke, so the group of demons followed the smoke and moved bit by bit …
The three Peak war elder s who had turned into smoke moved very slowly, and could only float a little. However, this was also a helpless action, if not, they would have already died.
"Hmph, as far as I know, these concealment techniques consume a lot of true essence. They won't be able to hold on for long and will still die. They only have themselves to blame!" Ling Yan said hatefully.
"The ones in trouble are us …" Zhao Fenqin shook his head and said in annoyance.
A considerable portion of the Azure Wolf and Tuna Bird saw that they were unable to do anything to the three people of the Night Clan for a while, and now pounced towards them once again.
Everyone did not dare to slow down in the slightest. At this time, they ran as fast as they could.
"Damn it, why aren't these wolves and birds fighting each other!" Zhou Zhuohe said gloomily.
Generally speaking, there were conflicts between demons, but it was unknown how these two types of demons could coexist peacefully.
After rushing through the bloody pass, two forks appeared in front of them. Normally, the vast majority of people would choose the left path, but when they arrived at the forks, they saw two fiery red snakes coiled in the corner of the left path.
The two fiery red snakes were entangled together, winding and coiling, constantly twisting. The two snakes were one male and one female, and were currently reproducing.
Luo Zheng saw the two snakes that looked like they were made of flames, but he did not feel anything. After entering the Dark Nether Cave, the thing that made Luo Zheng feel danger was the big toad. These two snakes did not look big, and could not even be called pythons. They could only be considered ordinary snakes.
However, when Ling Yan and Zhao Fenqin saw the two snakes, their expressions immediately changed!
"Crap!"
"What should we do?"
The two of them cried out at the same time.
"What should we do?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
"Purgatory Fire Snake …"
"Why did one of the most powerful demons in the inner layer come here!"
"The seventeen Peak war elder s of the Giant Clan were killed in an instant by these two little snakes. Only one of them used a secret technique to escape with his soul!"
Although the Dark Nether Cave would be very chaotic when the chaotic tide erupted, generally speaking, the demons in the inner layer would rarely run to the outer layer.
Although the Mysterious Underworld Cave looked chaotic, there were still some unseen rules. For example, the distance between the inner layer and the outer layer was not far, but the monsters in the inner layer would not take a single step into the outer layer!
No one knew why these demons had to follow this rule, but it was precisely because of the existence of this rule that many peak war elders dared to enter the Dark Nether Cave, and even brought some generals into the Dark Nether Cave to train.
The demons in the inner layer were completely different from the demons in the outer layer, and their strength was also countless times stronger. Moreover, these two little fiery snakes were the strongest demons in the inner layer, so how could they dare to rush in?
Hearing their words, Luo Zheng's expression also became serious. He did not expect that these two little snakes, which did not look strong, actually had such a background …
"Isn't there another way?" Luo Zheng looked at the fork in the road, but there was another road on the right.
"No!"
Ling Yan and Zhao Fenqin said in unison.
"Why?" Luo Zheng asked again.

Chapter 628
The eruption of the chaotic tide was an opportunity for Ling Yan. However, the route that Ling Yan planned was still around the outer area of the Mysterious Underworld Cave.
She wanted to explore a few places that might have opportunities. Those places were originally inaccessible, but because of the chaotic tide, a path was opened.
Zhao Fenqin also had the same idea.
Even when there was no chaotic tide, one had to be fully prepared to enter the inner area. At the same time, one had to be prepared to die because the survival rate was too low!
Now that the chaotic tide had arrived, the danger level had increased exponentially. No one dared to enter the inner area!
"Then enter the inner area." Luo Zheng winked and said to Yan Jing.
"Entering now is courting death!" Zhao Fenqin replied.
Luo Zheng looked behind him at the black mass of demons and then looked at the two Purgatory Fire Snakes that were entangled not far away. He shrugged his shoulders, "Staying here is also courting death."
Everyone's expression was very complicated. Only the two freaks, Yun Luo and Yun Luo, remained calm no matter what happened.
What Luo Zheng said wasn't wrong. The Tuna Birds and Azure Wolves that blotted out the sky and covered the earth behind them were absolutely capable of tearing them to pieces. And those two Purgatory Fire Snakes … Although they didn't seem to be that powerful, Ling Yan and Zhao Fenqin were even more clear that it was absolutely impossible for them to pass through these two little snakes unscathed.
In other words, they had no choice.
Ling Yan was conflicted for two breaths of time, then said sternly, "Let's enter the inner area!"
The peak war reverends present had also grown up from killing. At the crucial moment, they were very decisive. Moreover, the choice in front of them was not difficult. They could only take one step at a time. If they delayed any longer, they would not be able to leave even if they wanted to!
As soon as Ling Yan's voice fell, everyone chose the right path and rushed over!
The Tuna Birds and Azure Wolves behind them were getting closer and closer to them.
"Speed up!"
Now, everyone did not care about anything and rushed towards the inner area.
"They're catching up!"
Someone exclaimed. The thousands of Tuna Birds in the sky had already begun to dive down!
"Ahhh!"
Caught off guard, one of the Demon Night Clan's War Sovereigns was swallowed up by the Tuna Birds. His screams instantly stopped.
Seeing this scene, everyone's hair stood on end, and their sprinting speed increased a bit.
"This won't do. I don't think we can make it into the inner area," Zhou Zhuohe grumbled.
"It's right in front of us. Let's charge in in one go!" Ling Yan said without any distractions. Her eyes were burning as she stared straight ahead.
In front of everyone, there was a clear dividing line. The scenery outside the dividing line could still be distinguished, but inside the line, it was gray, as if there was only gray in that world.
After the Tuna Birds and Azure Wolves tore apart a peak war elder of the Demon Night Clan, they once again swept towards the crowd. The closest group of Tuna Birds was only two to three meters away from them, and they once again swooped down towards them!
"Soon!"
"We'll be safe for now if we rush in!"
"Good luck!"
Everyone encouraged each other.
As the black mass of Tuna Birds swooped down, they had already covered everyone. Some of the Tuna Birds had already opened their mouths and were swallowing Luo Zheng and the others.
As long as they were caught by these strange birds, they would definitely die without a burial place!
"Die for me!"
As Luo Zheng sprinted forward, his body spun, and the black fire between his eyebrows was released once again!
Luo Zheng had not used the black fire for a long time. After all, he had not encountered any overly dangerous situations before. Unless it was absolutely necessary, he did not want to use the black fire in front of everyone.
The wisp of black fire circled around Luo Zheng, and then shot up into the sky.
"Swoosh swoosh …"
The wisp of black fire flickered lightly, and instantly pierced through dozens of Tuna Birds. The flames exploded one after another, accompanied by the Tuna Birds' miserable cries.
In the blink of an eye, at least fifty Tuna Birds had been killed!
Zhao Fenqin and Zhou Zhuohe had already seen the power of Luo Zheng's black fire. Even the tongue of the Giant Python Toad could not withstand the burning of Luo Zheng's black fire, let alone these Tuna Birds.
On the contrary, the other peak war reverends and Ling Yan of the Demon Night Clan revealed a horrified expression. What was Luo Zheng's black fire? Wasn't it a little too sharp?
Although the Tuna Birds were afraid of fire, if it was an ordinary flame, it might not be able to burn these strange birds for an hour. However, Luo Zheng's black fire seemed to be unhindered. As long as the Tuna Birds touched the black fire, they would be instantly burned to ashes!
It was because of Luo Zheng's action that he had successfully created a space for everyone.
However, this space was not big, and the intelligence of these monsters was not too high. Even if their companions were killed like this, they still rushed forward and filled up the space in an instant.
However, at this time, everyone had already rushed into the clear line!
"Ga ga ga ga …"
The Tuna Birds were like a tide as they surged over. However, when they approached the clear line, it was as if they had hit a dam and were pushed back. These Tuna Birds did not dare to enter the inner area!
Just like the Tuna Birds, the Azure Wolves rushed to the side of the line and stopped. The eyes of the wolves flashed with a dangerous light as they stared at the crowd. Some of the Azure Wolves were still whimpering. It was a pity that Luo Zheng and the others had escaped!
Luo Zheng stood in the grey area and looked back at the monsters as he panted. Not only Luo Zheng, even Ling Yan, Zhao Fenqin and the other peak war reverends' chests were also heaving up and down.
Everyone rested for a while, but the Azure Wolves and Tuna Birds were still blocking the way, showing no signs of leaving.
"Let's wait here. I don't believe that these monsters will stay here forever!" Zhou Zhuohe sat down.
However, he had a good plan. These monsters would eventually lose their patience. After these monsters left, they would leave the inner area.
Zhao Fenqin and Ling Yan also agreed with this method. Staying here was safer than entering the inner area!
However, just as Luo Zheng stepped into the inner area, he suddenly felt the Sumeru Ring on his chest start to heat up!
Luo Zheng sent a strand of consciousness into the Sumeru Ring. Just as he poured his consciousness into it, he was shocked. In the Sumeru Space of the ring, a small butterfly was constantly circling, and that butterfly seemed to be on fire, constantly emitting intense flames!
"What's going on?"
Of course, Luo Zheng recognized it. This was the jade butterfly that Immortal Tian Miao had passed down to Luo Zheng. According to Immortal Tian Miao, with this token, he could obtain the incomparably important inheritance in the Profound Nether Cave.
He had been in the Profound Nether Cave for so long and this token had been peacefully lying there without the slightest reaction.
But now, as soon as he entered the inner area, this jade butterfly's entire body began to emit flames. It seemed that … Immortal Tian Miao's token should be in the inner area!
Zhao Fenqin and Ling Yan planned to stay here and wait for the monsters to leave. Then, they would return. It seemed that they didn't plan on exploring the inner area.
In truth, Luo Zheng agreed with their judgment. After all, the most important thing for a martial artist was their life. If they lost their life, they would lose everything.
But now, the token in Luo Zheng's ring had reacted. He had no choice but to reconsider. It seemed that this time he would have to enter the inner area to explore!
In truth, Luo Zheng could have waited until next year to enter the Tower of Sin. A year was enough time for Luo Zheng to become a peak war saint. At that time, the 'Stellar Battle Body' would be able to reach large success. By relying on the might of his transcendent divine might, Luo Zheng could even contend with a battle saint powerhouse.
If he entered the Profound Nether Cave again, his chances would be much higher …
However, Luo Zheng understood that he didn't have that much time left.
He looked at Ling Yan and Zhao Fenqin and shook his head. "You two stay here. I will continue to explore the inner area."
Xun raised his spear and followed behind Luo Zheng. Yun Luo also silently followed.
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Zhao Fenqin and Ling Yan's complexions immediately became ugly. The two of them almost simultaneously blocked Luo Zheng's path. Zhao Fenqin resolutely said, "Luo Zheng, no matter what, I will not allow you to continue in!"

Chapter 629
Even if the Chaotic Tide did not erupt, Zhao Fenqin and Ling Yan would do their best to stop him.
What's more, under the Chaotic Tide's eruption, everything in the inner layer had changed. Rashly entering was definitely a dead end.
Seeing Zhao Fenqin blocking in front of him, Luo Zheng's face revealed a helpless expression. Zhao Fenqin was not him, he did not understand what he was thinking. Moreover, Luo Zheng did not intend to tell his secret to others.
"Get out of the way, I have something very important to do," Luo Zheng said indifferently.
Zhou Zhuohe also blocked Luo Zheng's path. "Luo Zheng, you are too important to our Purple Heart Holy Land. We cannot let you continue forward and throw away your life!"
"Is that so?" Luo Zheng's face revealed a faint smile. "But I have something more important to do. Could it be that just because I am important to your Purple Heart Holy Land, I have to give up on myself? "
"This … How can this be called giving up?" Zhou Zhuohe asked, puzzled.
Luo Zheng shook his head and said, "I don't need you to follow me, I can go by myself!"
Xun also looked indifferently at Ling Yan who was kneeling on the ground, lowered his head and asked, "What do you want to do?"
"My king, the inner layer is not something you can enter now. It's better to stay here and wait for the demons to retreat before leaving," Ling Yan said with her head lowered.
In Ling Yan's heart, the king was a supreme existence. It was reasonable to say that Ling Yan did not have the right to worry about Xun's safety. However, Ling Yan also understood that the Demon Night Clan had changed in the upper realm. Xun's statue was already dim. In other words, the Sword Spirit in front of them was Xun's last Divine Clone!
Once Luo Zheng died, this Divine Clone would also disappear, and the final result would be the fall of the king!
This was something that Ling Yan did not want to see.
"It's not up to you to decide my matters." Xun once again regained her aloof expression and coldly said to Ling Yan.
Ling Yan clenched her teeth. She was very clear that she did not have the qualifications to persuade or preach to Xun. Not to mention Ling Yan, even Liu Yu of the Heavenly Feather Holy Land did not have the qualifications!
"Since that's the case, I'd better follow by my king's side!" Ling Yan resolutely said.
At the same time, the few peak war reverends beside Ling Yan also knelt down. Their faces were filled with determination.
They knew very well that entering the inner layer at this time would not bode well for them, but they still chose to follow.
When Zhao Fenqin and Zhou Zhuohe saw the Night Demon Clan's actions, their expressions became even more gloomy. If these Night Demon Clan members followed Luo Zheng, then they would be the only ones left. In the end, Zhao Fenqin held his head and said, "Alright, I think we still need to go to the inner layer!"
Sometimes, he was indeed forced by circumstances!
Moreover, the demonic creatures in the outer layer had no intention of leaving at all. If these demonic creatures refused to leave, then it wouldn't be a good idea for them to continue wasting their time here. In the end, they still headed towards the inner layer!
Not long after Luo Zheng and the others left,
Near the Xuekoukou Pass, three streaks of light shot in the opposite direction!
The three members of the Night Demon Clan were indeed quite unique. The three of them turned into smoke and slowly moved towards the outside of the Xuekoukou Pass.
Maintaining this kind of concealment technique consumed a lot of true essence. However, when they only had 30% of their true essence left, the three of them would suddenly appear under the beaks of the Tuna Birds and the sharp claws of the Azure Wolves. Then, they would quickly dash for a distance and take out true essence stones to replenish their true essence!
After replenishing their true essence, they would turn into smoke again and do the same thing over and over again!
Regrettably, although they managed to keep their lives for the time being, those demonic creatures were still following them like ghosts. Their crisis was still not over yet.
Moving like this was extremely inefficient. They couldn't maintain this state and repeatedly move back to the Sin Tower. Even if they wanted to, there wasn't enough time, and they didn't have enough true essence stones!
They had a lot of top grade true essence stones in their hands, but they weren't infinite. Every time they used one, there would be one less. In such a short period of time, each of them had consumed half of their true essence stones. If this continued, it wouldn't be long before they were swallowed up by these demonic creatures.
"What should we do, big brother?"
"You're asking me, but how would I know?"
"Who's going to lead these damned demonic creatures away?"
In the smoke, the three of them were constantly communicating with each other using true essence sound transmissions.
Just as the three of them were in a state of panic, two rays of light, one black and one red, suddenly appeared on their path. It was two figures that were like small mountains rapidly approaching.
The three of them were stunned. Then, a peak war elder of the Night Clan said, "I, I think we're saved!"
"It's Meng Chong! They're here! "Another person shouted.
"Haven't the Demon Race been expelled from the Sin Tower? They can really enter the Mysterious Underworld Cave! "
The Demon Race didn't share the secret of the Sin Tower with the Night Clan. They couldn't share such an important secret with other races, even if the Night Clan was their vassal.
However, in order to achieve their goal, the Demon Race only left an order for Xue Luo Tian. Once he discovered Luo Zheng's secret in the Mysterious Underworld Cave, he was to immediately report it to Meng Chong!
For this reason, Xue Luo Tian was puzzled for a long time. He originally thought that the Demon Race would give him an order to assassinate Luo Zheng. He didn't expect to receive such an order. Report to Meng Chong? What's the use of reporting to Meng Chong? Could it be that Meng Chong could enter the Mysterious Underworld Cave?
Or was Meng Chong originally in the Mysterious Underworld Cave?
Xue Luo Tian couldn't figure it out. However, he had no choice but to follow the Demon Race's order. Although the Demon Race couldn't enter the Sin Tower, his Night Clan's Eternal Night Holy Land was still a vassal of the Twin Demon Holy Land!
He didn't expect that not long after he quietly used the sound transmission talisman, Meng Chong and Tian Hu really rushed over!
"Meng Chong, Meng Chong's cultivation seems to be … a war saint!"
"Heavens, the Demon Race must have other secrets of the Sin Tower. Originally, there was Tian Qiong and Tian Hui, two war saints. Now, Meng Chong has also advanced to war saint!"
At this moment, the three peak war elders of the Night Clan seemed to see hope. Obviously, the higher Meng Chong's cultivation, the more advantageous it would be for them.
However …
Although the Azure Wolves and Tuna Birds had retreated, there were still quite a number of demons surrounding the three of them. Meng Chong had broken through to become a Battle-Saint, but even a Battle-Saint would not be able to handle so many demons, right?
Although they were worried, they wouldn't let go of their hope now.
"Meng Chong! Lord Meng Chong! "
Meng Chong was originally a seat controller like these peak war elders of the Night Clan, but now that Meng Chong was a war saint, the three of them naturally had to be respectful!
The true essence sound transmission of the peak war elder of the Night Clan rang in Meng Chong's ears. Meng Chong looked forward and saw a dense crowd of demons surrounding the three clouds of smoke. The three clouds of smoke slowly moved and the demons followed closely behind …
"It's Xue Luo Tian," Tian Hu said indifferently, "Should we go save them?"
Meng Chong looked at the dense crowd of demons and frowned.
Meng Chong, who had broken through to the Battle-Saint Realm, had a huge advantage here. Even so, he was still very cautious. In the Dark Nether Cave, even a Divine Sea Realm Almighty would have to be careful. One wrong step and they would die! Moreover, Meng Chong was only a first level war saint!
So many demons were quite troublesome for Meng Chong.
"Where is Luo Zheng?" Meng Chong stopped and asked with true essence sound transmission.
Xue Luo Tian was looking at Meng Chong. When he saw Meng Chong suddenly stop, he naturally understood that Meng Chong didn't intend to save the three of them. Xue Luo Tian immediately said with true essence sound transmission, "They rushed over! But I left a mark on them, we can track them! "
Xue Luo Tian certainly wouldn't tell Meng Chong where Luo Zheng was. If so, how could he count on Meng Chong to save him?
"This old fox, he doesn't want to say!" Tian Hu sneered.
Meng Chong shook his head, "It's human nature. Xue Luo Tian treats us as lifesavers, how could he let us go? These three must be saved. Take out the Imperial Fire Scale that the Flame Demon Holy Land gave us. "
Tian Hu nodded and then took out a small golden scale. The Demon Clan had controlled the Tower of Sin for the longest time, so they knew more about the Mysterious Underworld Cave than the other clans.

Chapter 630
Tuna Birds and Azure Wolves were the most common demonic beasts in the Xuanming Cave. Other than these two, there were also demonic beasts like the Blackthorn Rats, which were considered the lowest level of demonic beasts in the Xuanming Cave.

At that time, the Demon Clan obtained all kinds of opportunities in the Xuanming Cave and was able to stand out among the other clans in the Sea God Continent!
If not for Liu Yu's amazing talent, no one in the Sea God Continent would be able to fight against the Demon Clan.

This Fire Controlling Scale was only a mid-grade holy artifact, so it wasn't particularly powerful when used to defend against enemies. However, the devilish race had discovered that the tuna birds and azure wolves were extremely fearful of fire, so they had asked a tool refinement master to forge this treasure for them.
Tianhu held the Flaming Balance in his hand, and then he poured a wisp of Stellar Energy into it from one end of the balance. As soon as the Stellar Energy was poured in, a ball of light blue flames sprayed out from the other end of the balance, and it instantly enveloped half the sky!

After the arrival of the Chaotic Tide, even as a War Sage, Meng Chong was unwilling to enter the inner area.

The Demon Clan in the Sin Tower had suffered a crushing defeat. He was the only hope for the Demon Clan to turn the tables!
Even if Luo Zheng really entered the core area, as long as Luo Zheng didn't die, he would follow him in without hesitation.
"In that case, take me there," Meng Chong said indifferently.

Tianhu held the Imperial Fire Balance in one hand and led the three Night Clan members and Meng Chong towards the Bloody Mouth Pass!

Luo Zheng did not know that he had become the prey.
Now, he was leading the group to explore the inner layer.
Luo Zheng's exploration did not require a map. Moreover, the maps in Zhao Fenqin and Ling Yan's hands had lost their usefulness.
Originally, the map of the inner area was rather rough, and many races knew too little about the inner area. Although there had been many Peak war elder s who had entered the inner area of the Profound Nether Cave since ancient times, most of them had died miserably inside. Perhaps they had discovered some extremely dangerous places in the inner area, or even some opportunities, but they did not have the life to return alive, nor did they have the chance to mark out these places.
At this time, the only thing Luo Zheng used to determine the direction was the jade butterfly in his ring.
After exploring for a while, Luo Zheng understood. When he went in the right direction, the jade butterfly burned fiercely in the Sumeru Space. If he went in the opposite direction, the burning would calm down.
Luo Zheng used this to determine whether his path was correct or not!
It was strange. After exploring the inner layer for so long, they did not encounter any danger.
However, this still could not dispel everyone's nervousness. Instead, it made them even more vigilant because danger was often hidden in this calm.
"This silence is too strange!" Zhou Zhuohe said with a face full of vigilance.
Ling Yan also nodded. She also saw that something was wrong.
It was not that Ling Yan had not entered the inner layer before. Three years ago, she had entered once. Although there were not many low level demons in the inner layer, there were many extremely powerful demons hidden everywhere. Three years ago, Ling Yan had almost died inside. She had a very deep impression of the inner layer.
And now, with the eruption of the chaotic tide, the demon activity in the inner layer should be even more frequent!
But today, the surroundings were quiet. It was so quiet that it was frightening.
"This gloomy feeling is really unpleasant." Zhao Fenqin clenched his teeth and resisted the urge to roar. Although he knew that making too much noise in the inner layer was seeking death, this kind of silence was very stifling!

"Re-really?" Zhao Fenqin was tongue-tied.

"I'm not sure." Although Ling Yan had entered the inner layer, she had never heard of this kind of thing.

Chapter 631
Hearing that voice, a chill ran down everyone's spine.
All the warriors couldn't help but want to turn around. They didn't want to leave their backs to the enemy. This was the first reaction of almost all the warriors!
But just as everyone was about to turn around, Yun Luo looked straight ahead and said coldly, "Don't turn around! Those who turn around will die! "
Hearing Yun Luo's reminder, Luo Zheng abruptly turned his face back to her.
Ling Yan, Zhao Fenqin, Zhou Zhuohe and the others didn't turn around, but a peak war reverend from the Yao Ye clan turned his head too quickly and saw something behind everyone!
Although no one could turn around, they could see the expression of the peak war reverend from the Yao Ye clan!
The peak war reverend's eyes were wide open, as if he had seen something unbelievable. The expression on his face was extremely shocked. The peak war reverend seemed to want to scream, but when he opened his mouth, he couldn't. He could only make a futile gurgling sound in his throat …
Those who could become peak war reverends were all creatures who had experienced life and death calamities and grew up step by step. What kind of things had these creatures not seen before? They had seen piles of corpses and countless creatures had died at their hands. It could be said that they had walked step by step through mountains of corpses and seas of blood!

His mouth made that terrifying gurgling sound, and soon, a strange smile appeared on his face. The gaze in his eyes had already begun to slacken, and he had lost the spirituality he should have had.

Seeing this scene, Zhao Fenqin was about to collapse. "What the hell is behind us!"

. Xun, she looked forward and frowned, as if she was thinking about something.
She was not afraid of any malicious ghosts. With the level that Xun had reached, she was even qualified to go to the Underworld's Yellow Springs River. Evil beings were not invincible in her eyes. She was only thinking about what was behind them and how to deal with them.
.......

. At the same time, Luo Zheng saw a faint light shining from the corner of his eye.
