Although she knew that she couldn't turn around, Ling Yan wouldn't give up so easily. Her martial arts heart was also very stable. The light was getting brighter and brighter, and the sound was getting louder and louder.
At this moment, long and slender tentacles slowly extended from behind the crowd and pulled them in.

However, although it was simple, Ling Yan didn't dare to underestimate it!
Because the soul that used the Frightening Spirit Thorn wasn't simple. Luo Zheng's soul had a faint jade color. That meant that the soul was about to enter the Blue Soul Realm. In other words … Luo Zheng already had the soul of a War Soul Realm!
It was no wonder that Ling Yan was surprised. Among the Peak War Elder, there were still no Martial Warriors whose soul had stepped into the War Soul Realm. Not to mention the Peak War Elder, even the War Saint Stage Rankers only had a small number of souls that had stepped into the War Soul Realm.
However, Luo Zheng was only a ninth rank War General. His soul had already entered the War Soul Realm. Moreover, it was faintly emitting a faint green light. Obviously, it wouldn't take long for him to enter the Blue Soul Realm.
This was really inconceivable …
However, being surprised was one thing. Luo Zheng had already given her too many surprises. Obviously, now wasn't the time to be surprised.
Luo Zheng's "Frightening Spirit Thorn" saved Zhao Fenqin's life. Zhao Fenqin hugged his head and knelt on the ground as he wailed. However, he didn't have the chance to turn his head and look behind him.

Chapter 632
Following which, Yun Luo stretched out her slender and white fingers. Her ten fingers danced in the air, and little bits of true essence were pulled out by her, forming runes one after another!

"Then what does she want to do?" Luo Zheng asked Qinglong.
Qinglong also noticed the seals in Yun Luo's hand. He said, "If I'm not wrong, she wants to seal the Divine Runes!"
"Seal the Divine Runes?" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. Although Yun Luo was drawing Divine Runes, she was only using True Yuan to draw them out of thin air. She didn't need any materials. Was this useful?
"Haha, the origin of Divine Runes is more complicated than you think. If you ascend to the Upper Realm in the future, you will naturally come into contact with these things," Qinglong replied.
As more and more seals appeared in Yun Luo's hand, the structure of the seals became more and more complicated.
10, 20, 30, 50 …
When the number of seals in Yun Luo's hand reached 100, she waved her hand and the seals fell down like raindrops and sped behind her!
"Freeze!"
Yun Luo shouted.
Just as Yun Luo shouted the word "freeze", the mournful wailing behind them suddenly stopped …
"Did it work?" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed.
After the sound disappeared, Ling Yan also let out a deep breath. It seemed that she had succeeded.
"I only sealed it temporarily. It won't last long. Let's go," Yun Luo said.
Yun Luo's words made everyone nervous again. They continued to run forward. After running for dozens of miles, they stopped. They still didn't look back.
"Ouch …"
At this time, Zhao Fenqin let out a long groan and a sigh. He was finally freed from the pain caused by the Soul-frightening Spike. Although he was in so much pain that he couldn't think, he knew what had just happened. He said miserably, "Fortunately, Luo Zheng, you saved my life!"
"Can we turn back now?" Ling Yan asked. Not only Ling Yan, at this time, everyone was still immersed in fear.
Yun Luo nodded. "I think we've gotten rid of that thing."
Only then did everyone turn their heads …
As warriors, they were not afraid of those powerful monsters. What they were really afraid of were those unknown things, just like the thing floating behind them. Until now, no one knew what it was!
He turned around, but there was nothing behind him.
Luo Zheng turned his head and looked into the distance. A green light appeared above his pupils. His Clear Eyes Spirit Eyes allowed him to see things very far away.
However, due to the distance, Luo Zheng's vision was still very blurry. He could see a pale white dot of light pulsating in the distance. Around that dot of light, there were many runes floating. Those runes were the runes that Yun Luo had just sent out. The dot of light seemed to be constantly struggling, trying to break free from the runes.
"Don't look, it … can feel your gaze." Yun Luo quickly stopped Luo Zheng's dangerous action.
"It can feel that?" Luo Zheng was shocked. He immediately withdrew his Clear Eyes Spirit Eyes and quickly retracted his gaze. The dot of light quickly dissipated into the darkness.
"Yes, it was very dangerous just now. Fortunately, that thing is not an adult yet!" Yun Luo pursed her lips and revealed a rare smile.
"Underage?" Luo Zheng said in surprise. "What is it?"
"I can't say it, and I can't think about it. If I say it, it will know and it will probably chase after us." Yun Luo shook her head and said.
Everyone was completely speechless. What was that thing? So evil? They couldn't even think about it?
At this time, Xun also said faintly, "I know what it is!"
"What is it?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"I already told you, you really can't talk about it!" Xun also shook her head.
Everyone was already driven crazy. It could only be said that there were too many strange things in this world. However, no matter what that thing was, everyone already didn't want to talk about it. After experiencing that kind of extreme fear, no one wanted to experience it a second time.
At this time, Ling Yan just barely looked at the surrounding environment. Suddenly, she exclaimed, and a ray of light appeared in her eyes!
"We actually came here!" Ling Yan said in surprise.
Zhao Fenqin took out a soul healing pill and popped it into his mouth. Although Luo Zheng's Frightening Spirit Spike didn't cause too much damage to him, his soul wasn't as strong as Luo Zheng's. He couldn't be careless with his fragile soul. After swallowing the pill, Zhao Fenqin asked, "Where?"
Ling Yan waved her hand and a map appeared in her hand. It was the map of the Mysterious Underworld Cave. Then, Ling Yan pointed at a mark on the map and said, "Three years ago, I marked this place. There should be the remains of a super strong expert here! However, at that time, this area was guarded by more than ten Poisonous Ghost Martens. We couldn't enter. "
The strength of the experts in the Mysterious Underworld Cave was hard to estimate. The place where any expert died contained unimaginable benefits!
At that time, the Demon Night clan had paid a considerable price to come here. How could they give up?
However, these Poisonous Ghost Martens were too strong. They weren't existences that peak war saints could contend against. The Demon Night clan had spent more than ten lives trying to lure the Poisonous Ghost Martens away, but they had never succeeded. In the end, they had no choice but to give up.
What Ling Yan didn't expect was that the eruption of this chaotic tide had actually left the inner area empty. Then, this expert's remains were naturally wide open. How could Ling Yan not be pleasantly surprised?!
When Ling Yan said this, Zhao Fenqin and Zhou Zhuohe's eyes also revealed pleasantly surprised expressions. They had risked their lives to walk around the inner area. They couldn't possibly leave without any benefits!
What did the experts of the Mysterious Underworld Cave mean? They were very clear that within the remains of an expert, there were countless unimaginable treasures!
"Since it's like this, let's go in and take a look." Luo Zheng said.
After running all the way, the flames emitted by the Jade Butterfly in the spatial ring had clearly become smaller. It was clear that the direction was wrong.
However, Luo Zheng wasn't in a hurry now. Since Ling Yan was sure that there was a great opportunity here, he didn't care if he didn't go in and explore.
Thus, under Ling Yan's lead, everyone continued to move forward. After walking through a forest, Ling Yan pointed ahead and said, "It's here!"
Just as everyone walked out of the forest, they saw a door appear in front of them. It was a lonely door!
This scene looked very strange. There was no house, no roof, no walls. Other than a door standing here, there was nothing else.
Moreover, this door looked quite ordinary. It was just a small door that opened on one side in an ordinary house.
"This is the relic?" Zhao Fenqin asked.
"Yes, there should be words on that door." Even at this time, Ling Yan still maintained a high level of vigilance. From afar, she struck a stream of True Essence towards the door.
When Ling Yan brought the people of the Demon Night clan here, she had just figured out a little when she was besieged by a group of Poisonous Ghost Martens. In the end, she had no choice but to give up and choose to leave!
After Ling Yan's True Essence poured into the door, a row of large words appeared on the door!
"I am Qing Yuanzi. I wasted my life and had my dream destroyed here. In fact …"
Obviously, the words on the door were the epitaph that Qing Yuanzi had written for himself.
Yun Luo looked at the door indifferently. Her eyes flickered slightly, and there was a faint sadness on her face.
Xun's eyebrows furrowed. "Qing Yuanzi is dead? He also died here! "As she spoke, Xun looked at Yun Luo with a very strange expression on her face.
"My king, this Qing Yuanzi is?" Ling Yan asked.
Xun smiled faintly. "A powerful person, a person who shook the world in the past. I didn't expect him to die here. How ironic!"
Hearing Xun's words, Yun Luo gently bit her lips and did not speak.
"Then this door should be the treasure left behind by Qing Yuanzi, right?" Ling Yan asked again.
"Yes, you can say that! This is Qing Yuanzi's natal magic treasure, the Door of Reincarnation! " Xun said faintly. In fact, Xun's knowledge was not less than Yun Luo's. However, the secret of the Xuanming Cave came from the Human Race, so she naturally did not know as well as Yun Luo.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 633
The Heart of Martial Arts could only be honed through various experiences. It was related to a person's environment and experience.
For example, if a genius' life was smooth sailing and he was surrounded by various halos and rarely encountered bottlenecks in his cultivation, the Heart of Martial Arts of such a genius was actually very fragile. It was very easy for him to suffer a heavy blow! Once the Heart of Martial Arts suffered a setback, it was very likely that he would not be able to recover from the setback and would never be able to continue on this path.
However, if this genius could withstand this setback, then his Heart of Martial Arts would grow and become more tenacious and confident!
The honing of the Heart of Martial Arts came from repeated setbacks. It was an unavoidable process for a cultivator to grow.
The opportunity to hone the Heart of Martial Arts was hard to come by. If it was not handled properly, it would bring about great negative effects. Therefore, when they heard that it was possible to cultivate the Heart of Martial Arts, everyone was naturally surprised!
"I'll go first." Zhao Fenqin took the lead and walked towards the door.
Although this door was difficult to open, there were no restrictions on it. Zhao Fenqin gently pushed and the door opened.
A small vortex appeared in the center of the door and slowly rotated in a reverse direction.
"This door … won't be dangerous, right?" Zhao Fenqin was still a little worried. It was better to be safe than sorry. Although Yun Luo and Xun seemed to know the origin of this door, they did not know what dangers were lurking inside.
Xun shook her head. "There won't be any danger. You can go in."
Zhao Fenqin nodded and stepped into the vortex. As soon as he stepped into the vortex, a faint golden light flickered. Everyone could feel the passage of time in this light.
"It's the law of time," Luo Zheng said as he stared at the light.
Moreover, this was not a low-level law of time with one or two levels. It was a high-level law of time. No one present could see the mystery behind it.
After all, on the Sea God Continent, one could only comprehend the first or second level of the law of time. In the Central Domain, no one had comprehended the law of time. Compared to the law of space, the law of time was more mysterious and rare.
The light on the Gate of Reincarnation flickered slowly. Everyone thought that they would have to wait for a long time, maybe even a day or a few days. After all, when it came to cultivation, the speed would not be fast. Some cultivation techniques required years to be cultivated. It would take decades or even centuries before one could fully master them.
Fortunately, there wasn't much time left in the Mysterious Underworld Cave. The weakening of the Mysterious Underworld Death Ray would last for about a month.
However, to everyone's surprise, after a few breaths, Zhao Fenqin walked out of the door.
"So fast?"
"What can he cultivate in such a short time?"
"It might be related to the Law of Time. Such a profound Law of Time should be able to compress time to the limit. Perhaps a lot of time has already passed …"
"Zhao Fenqin, say something!"
Only then did everyone realize that Zhao Fenqin was actually standing in front of the Gate of Reincarnation, staring straight ahead in a daze.
"Have you lost your mind?" Zhou Zhuohe appeared in front of Zhao Fenqin in a flash and waved his hand in front of his face. Unexpectedly, Zhao Fenqin waved his hand and slapped Zhou Zhuohe's hand away. He said indifferently, "Don't touch me. I'm thinking about something …"
"Thinking about something here?" Zhou Zhuohe was speechless. "What the hell is inside the Gate of Reincarnation? It can't turn people into idiots, right?"
"You're the idiot!" Zhao Fenqin rolled his eyes, then stepped aside and pulled Zhou Zhuohe aside. "You should go in too. The Gate of Reincarnation is indeed very beneficial!"
"What the hell is that …" Zhou Zhuohe mumbled. Zhao Fenqin, who had come out of the Gate of Reincarnation, was indeed fine. However, Zhou Zhuohe felt that Zhao Fenqin seemed to have undergone some subtle changes. At the very least, his gaze had become more profound!
However, the two of them were like brothers. Zhou Zhuohe also knew that Zhao Fenqin wouldn't harm him, so he didn't think too much about it and also stepped into the Gate of Reincarnation.
After a few breaths of time, Zhou Zhuohe also walked out of the Gate of Reincarnation. Like Zhao Fenqin, Zhou Zhuohe was also a bit dumbfounded. His face was even a bit sluggish. He curled the corner of his mouth and muttered, "So it's like this, so it's like this …"
Yun Luo and Xun didn't say anything. The two of them seemed to understand what had happened.
On the other hand, Ling Yan and the other two peak war reverends from the Yao Ye clan were puzzled. They were also extremely curious about the Gate of Reincarnation!
Thus, Ling Yan also took a step forward and entered the Gate of Reincarnation.
When Ling Yan walked out, her gaze was as calm as water. Her pitch-black eyes were like black pearls, blooming with a faint light. Then, she silently walked beside Xun.
Next, the remaining two peak war reverends from the Yao Ye clan also entered the Gate of Reincarnation.
The last to enter was Luo Zheng.
Just now, Luo Zheng also asked Xun what this thing was. However, Xun only smiled faintly and didn't answer his question. She only told Luo Zheng that he would understand once he entered the Gate of Reincarnation.
Fortunately, they didn't have to wait for long. Soon, it was Luo Zheng's turn.
He walked in front of the Gate of Reincarnation and looked at it. Then, he stepped inside.
The surging power of the law of time wrapped around Luo Zheng. At this moment, Luo Zheng felt as if time had stopped flowing. All sound disappeared, and he was the only one left in the lonely darkness.
Luo Zheng was very interested in how to cultivate the heart of martial arts. Since the Gate of Reincarnation had the word "reincarnation", then it must have something to do with the reincarnation of life.
As for the reincarnation of life and death, it was even more difficult to guess than fate. Whether or not people reincarnated, whether or not there was an afterlife, it was impossible to determine.
Legend had it that after a person died, they would enter the six paths of reincarnation and fall into the Avici Hell. However, where was the hell and where were the six paths?
Last time, when Luo Zheng cultivated the Stellar Battle Body, he used his consciousness to roam the universe and saw the true face of the great world. It greatly broadened his horizons. However, Luo Zheng also understood that what he saw was only the tip of the iceberg. The mysteries of the universe were endless, and who knew how long it would take to explore them one by one?
Perhaps the process of exploration was the meaning of life!
While thinking, Luo Zheng saw a huge golden spiral appear in front of him. Then, Luo Zheng was sucked into the golden spiral, and the surrounding scenery gradually changed …
"This is!" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed.
He found himself wearing golden armor and standing on the city walls of the Burning Sky Imperial Capital. He was holding a golden blade and looking into the distance!
"I can't control my hands and feet!" Luo Zheng instinctively wanted to move, but he realized he couldn't move at all!
After that, Luo Zheng couldn't help but wave his blade and roar out, "Soldiers, the Burning Sky Imperial Capital has been conquered! Wait until I wear the golden robe, and I'll rule this world with all of you!"
"Charge!"
Below the city walls, countless soldiers wearing golden armor were swarming towards the Burning Sky Imperial Capital like a tidal wave.
Within the Burning Sky Imperial Capital, smoke could be seen everywhere, and cries could be heard everywhere …
While Luo Zheng was wondering what was going on, dozens of green lights suddenly shot into the sky in front of him. The person leading the group was Su Lingyun!
As for Su Lingyun, there were many Divine Core warriors surrounding her!
"Traitor Luo Zheng, the Burning Sky Palace will fight with our backs against the wall! Even if we have to use our last bit of strength, we'll kill you!"
Su Lingyun brought all the Divine Core warriors with her. They turned into beams of light and surrounded Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng let out a wild laugh. He waved the golden blade in his hand and charged forward as well. Both sides began fighting fiercely.
Although Luo Zheng was in this body, his voice and movements were completely out of his control …
He only knew that he had become a traitor in the Burning Sky Imperial Capital, and now, he was about to steal the Burning Sky Imperial Capital. As long as he could defeat all these people in front of him, he would be able to rule the world and become the new Emperor of the Eastern Region!
"Is this something that has already happened? Or was it created in the dreamland? "

Chapter 634
The battle lasted for an entire day and night. The entire Burning Heaven Imperial Capital was littered with dead bodies, and flames were burning everywhere.
Luo Zheng had killed ten Divine Core warriors by himself, and only Su Lingyun had managed to escape …
After that, Luo Zheng led all his soldiers and charged straight toward the Burning Heaven Palace!
In the end, Luo Zheng had successfully ascended to the throne he had always dreamed of. He stood at the top of the Burning Heaven Palace, overlooking the entire world.
Millions of soldiers were wearing golden armor, and they were kneeling in front of Luo Zheng. It was a glorious scene, and this was the epitome of Luo Zheng's life.
As for Su Lingyun, the last descendant of the Family Su, she had been captured.
"So, this is my other life?" Luo Zheng furrowed his brows. Although these scenes were illusory in Luo Zheng's eyes, they were still vivid in his mind, and he could clearly see all the details. It was real, but it was also absurd!
"The last princess of the Family Su, I'll kill her with my own hands!"
Luo Zheng leapt up into the air. With the golden blade in his hand, he slashed toward Su Lingyun. However, right before the blade was about to reach Su Lingyun, a sword beam suddenly appeared!
"Ding!"
The sword beam easily knocked away the golden blade in Luo Zheng's hand.
"Hua Tianming?" Luo Zheng saw Hua Tianming. He was wearing a white shirt, and he had a calm expression on his face. He was standing in front of millions of soldiers, but he didn't show any fear.
"Who are you?" Luo Zheng couldn't help but ask.
Hua Tianming responded with a faint smile. "Me? I'm a swordsman. "
"Why are you here?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"The Fengwang Dynasty is an ally of the Blue Cloud Sect, and the Blue Cloud Sect belongs to the Cloud Hall. I come from the Cloud Hall." Hua Tianming looked at Luo Zheng indifferently. "If you want to usurp the country, you have to ask the Cloud Hall first!"
"Go to hell!" Luo Zheng roared and rushed towards Hua Tianming.
"Ding ding ding …"
The two of them continued to attack, exchanging thousands of blows in an instant.
In the end, Hua Tianming retreated a hundred feet and raised his sword. The entire world changed colors, and the aura of this sword suppressed the aura of the million-strong army.
"With your strength, you're far from qualified to usurp the throne!" Hua Tianming finished speaking and stabbed down with his sword.
Luo Zheng couldn't resist this powerful sword. He was directly pierced by Hua Tianming's sword, and his head was separated from his body …
At the moment when his body was beheaded, Luo Zheng suddenly felt the various emotions contained in this body. They swept over him one after another. Unwillingness, anger, helplessness, all kinds of emotions were swept into Luo Zheng's mind.
"This … is the fate of a reincarnation?"
All the scenes gradually disappeared, and Luo Zheng was once again immersed in darkness. He now somewhat understood the meaning of the Gate of Reincarnation.
No wonder one could cultivate the heart of martial arts through the Gate of Reincarnation. To put it bluntly, the Gate of Reincarnation seemed to have the ability to continuously create the reincarnation of life, and after experiencing it personally, one would absorb the emotions in the reincarnation! To empathize, and then to comprehend, and to comprehend …
The mysterious things that existed in the Upper Realm were indeed amazing. This method of cultivating the heart of martial arts was indeed an eye-opener for Luo Zheng. It was unbelievable!
Immediately after, a golden whirlpool appeared in front of him again, and then a new scene appeared around him.
In this life, Luo Zheng wasn't a martial artist, but a cook who guarded the stove. He was always busy beside the stove with smoke and fire. Until one day, when Luo Zheng was slaughtering a wild dog, he found a divine jade pendant in the wild dog.
It was this jade pendant that changed his life's path, and he once again embarked on the path of a martial artist.
However, Luo Zheng didn't enter the Blue Cloud Sect. Instead, as an independent martial artist, he rapidly rose in the Eastern Region, established his own sect, and finally established a large sect comparable to the Blue Cloud Sect, and became the sect master …
In the third reincarnation, Luo Zheng became a famous scholar in the Eastern Region! In the face of the demon race's attack, he resolutely entered the path of literature, and led White Emperor City to resist the demon race's attack …
The fourth reincarnation …
The fifth reincarnation …
The sixth reincarnation …
Reincarnation after reincarnation flew around Luo Zheng. These reincarnations were vivid in his mind, and formed a brand in Luo Zheng's heart!
The more things a person experienced, the more profound their thoughts, and the more profound their thoughts. In this reincarnation, Luo Zheng's heart of martial arts was also sublimated little by little.
Reincarnation after reincarnation continued to rotate, and all kinds of fates flashed. From the beginning to the prologue, Luo Zheng experienced the uniqueness of each fate.
The beginning of each new reincarnation was the end of an old reincarnation. In this short period of time, Luo Zheng experienced ninety-nine reincarnations!
When all the reincarnations disappeared and Luo Zheng walked out of the Gate of Reincarnation, he was slightly dazed.
The impact of these reincarnations was too great, and it wasn't something that could be digested in a short period of time. However, the effect it had on the heart of martial arts was obvious. After experiencing such a rich life, one had the most painful understanding of life and death, glory, power, and desire!
"How many reincarnations have you experienced?" Ling Yan asked Luo Zheng.
Everyone also looked at Luo Zheng, waiting for his answer.
Luo Zheng was slightly surprised. "What? Didn't everyone experience the same number of reincarnations? How long has it been? "
"Two hours," Xun said with a smile.
"That long?" Luo Zheng was also stunned.
He didn't know that everyone thought Luo Zheng had died inside …
"The number of reincarnations in the Gate of Reincarnation is determined by the strength of a person's heart of martial arts. The stronger the heart of martial arts, the more reincarnations there will be," Yun Luo explained.
The impact of a single reincarnation cycle was already very strong. If one's heart of martial arts was not strong enough, they would gradually lose themselves in one reincarnation cycle after another. They would not even be able to distinguish between reality and reincarnation.
Therefore, when undergoing reincarnation cultivation, as long as the heart showed the slightest sign of being lost, the reincarnation would disappear, and the person would be released from the Gate of Reincarnation.
"I've undergone nine reincarnations," Zhao Fenqin said.
"I've only undergone six," Zhou Zhuohe said.
Ling Yan said, "I've undergone thirteen reincarnations … Luo Zheng, what about you?"
"Me? It seems to be ninety-nine times, "Luo Zheng replied.
"Ninety-nine times!" Zhao Fenqin's eyes almost popped out of their sockets. "So many times, you didn't lose yourself at all?"
"Lost? How could that be? "Luo Zheng said, puzzled.
Everyone, including Ling Yan, was puzzled. It seemed that Luo Zheng's heart of martial arts was too strong …
"Luo Zheng's heart of martial arts is indeed much stronger than the average person. This Gate of Reincarnation is made from the power of the Six Paths of Reincarnation. Although it can't replace the real path of reincarnation, the power of reincarnation it contains is enough to make many people lose themselves. Ninety-nine times is the limit of the Gate of Reincarnation …" Yun Luo seemed to understand the Gate of Reincarnation very well.
"I see …" Luo Zheng finally understood why he had undergone as many as ninety-nine reincarnations. However, from beginning to end, his heart of martial arts did not feel lost at all.
Zhao Fenqin smacked his lips, looked at the Gate of Reincarnation, and said, "It's really a divine treasure. If only we could bring it back to the Purple Heart Sacred Land."
Xun sneered. "Bring it back? Do you think you can protect the Gate of Reincarnation if you bring it back? "
"Why not?" Zhao Fenqin was not convinced.
"A treasure of this level, if it leaves here without the protection of the Cursed Land, the aura it emits is enough to attract the attention of the strongest in the Upper Realm. With the strength of those strong people, destroying the entire Sea God Continent is just a matter of raising their hands …" Xun said lightly.
Hearing Xun's words, Zhao Fenqin was stunned.
Xun was right. Some of the inheritances in the Xuanming Cave were too precious. It was not something that a small force in the Lower Realm could preserve.
Luo Zheng looked at the Gate of Reincarnation. He was still slowly digesting the complicated emotions that came from reincarnation. At this time, he said, "If that's the case, let's continue exploring!"
Although the Gate of Reincarnation was wonderful, it was not the inheritance that Daoist Tian Miao was talking about.
"I'm afraid you won't have the chance to continue!" At this time, a deep voice rang in everyone's ears. It was like thunder, shaking everyone's ears until they were buzzing.
Hearing this familiar voice, Luo Zheng's face sank. He looked over and saw Meng Chong's burly figure appear not far away!

Chapter 635
"Battle-Saint!"
Ling Yan's pupils suddenly shrank. Judging from the aura emitted from Meng Chong's body, he had already stepped into the Battle-Saint realm!
Zhao Fenqin and Zhou Zhuohe's complexion immediately sank …
Didn't the Demon Race already leave the Tower of Sin? Why were these two still able to appear here?
This strange situation immediately reminded them of Tianqiong and Tianhui. They had also abruptly appeared in the Tower of Sin. The Demon Race had operated the Tower of Sin for so many years, so there were still some secrets that were not known by others.
However, although everyone's complexion was not good, they did not have an overly fearful expression. On the contrary, everyone's aura was very calm. Facing a Battle-Saint like Meng Chong, they were unexpectedly so calm.
This time, it was Meng Chong who was somewhat puzzled. A doubtful expression appeared on his face, and he even became somewhat vigilant.
What was wrong with these guys? Death was at hand, yet they did not seem to panic at all?
Let's not talk about the two freaks, Xun and Yun Luo. These two fellows could not be judged by common sense. In fact, Yun Luo and Xun, who came from the upper realm, had a heart of martial arts that these people from the lower realm could not compare to.
For example, Yun Luo had never thought of entering the Gate of Reincarnation. This was because the reincarnation cultivation method in the Gate of Reincarnation was already useless to her. She had cut her soul into millions of pieces, and millions of clones were constantly training. This kind of experience was countless times better than the Gate of Reincarnation!
However, Luo Zheng and Ling Yan were still so calm?
Even the usually timid Zhao Fenqin and Zhou Zhuohe, the two peak Battle-Saints of the Human Race, had the same indifferent expression. What was going on?
Meng Chong was somewhat disappointed, and even somewhat unwilling. He had hidden in the narrow secret passage under the Tower of Sin for several months, waiting for this moment. He originally wanted to see Luo Zheng and the others lose their heads out of fear, and even kneel down and beg for mercy. He did not expect these fellows to not have much of a reaction!
Meng Chong thought for a moment, then sneered: "Do you still think this is the Tower of Sin? Do you still think that Luo Zheng has that omnipotent ability? "
When Zhao Fenqin heard Meng Chong's words, he replied faintly, "Of course we're not as stupid as you think. However, since you're here, we can only deal with you calmly!"
What Meng Chong didn't know was that these people had just experienced the reincarnation of life. They had seen their lives from the perspective of life and death, so their mentality was naturally very stable! This was the effect of cultivating the heart of martial arts!
If one's heart of martial arts was easily agitated, it would often spoil things at crucial moments, and one might not even be able to display one's true strength!
For example, when the group was being chased by that strange thing, Zhao Fenqin was on the verge of collapse. Even his instinct to escape had disappeared. If Luo Zheng hadn't used the Stun Spirit Thorn to knock Zhao Fenqin out, Zhao Fenqin would have been dead by then.
Yun Luo, on the other hand, was able to think of a way to deal with it in that environment. She used the Demon Sealing Technique to seal that thing and lead everyone to escape!
The more stable a person's heart of martial arts was, the more they would be able to remain calm at any time and display 100% of their strength.
Everyone's heart of martial arts had been greatly strengthened by the Gate of Reincarnation. At this moment, they were surprisingly calm!
"Deal with it? Haha. "Meng Chong laughed sinisterly:" With just you few Peak War Reverends? "
Ling Yan stared at Meng Chong and said: "When you were at the Peak War Reverend, you were far from being my match. Even if you broke through now and became a War Saint, so what? Are you sure you're my match? "
Among the one hundred and twenty-eight seat controllers, Ling Yan was ranked first. She was the strongest Peak War Reverend, and Meng Chong could only be ranked in the middle of the Peak War Reverend. Even if Meng Chong stepped into the War Saint realm, he would only be a First Order War Saint.
"Hmph, won't you know if I'm your match after trying?" Meng Chong didn't want to waste any more time here. There was a huge change in the inner layer. Even if he was a Battle-Saint, it was still very dangerous. He had to end this battle as soon as possible!
When he entered the secret passage in the lower level of the Tower of Sin, Meng Chong understood that he had actually become a death warrior! And his only goal was to kill Luo Zheng!
As long as Luo Zheng died, all of the disadvantageous situations in the Demon Clan would vanish into thin air! The Demon Clan would once again return to the Tower of Sin!
If he could kill Luo Zheng and safely return to the Tower of Sin, then he, Meng Chong, would be the great hero of the Twin Devil Holy Land. The Demon Clan attached great importance to the glory of their clan. This kind of glory was a supreme existence to him, just like how Xun was a supreme existence to the Demon Night Clan …
"Go!" Meng Chong gave the order and also launched an attack. His huge body suddenly flashed and directly rushed towards Luo Zheng!
When Meng Chong's figure moved, Tian Hu also moved at the same time. Together with Tian Hu were the three Peak War Reverends of the Night Clan!
"Protect Luo Zheng." If it was in the past, Zhao Fenqin would be at a loss. But at this time, his expression was calm. Even though he knew that he was not Meng Chong's match, he still acted methodically! This was the effect of the Gate of Reincarnation!
"I have indeed just stepped into the War Saint realm. With your strength, Ling Yan, you can even kill an ordinary Second Order War Saint! But don't forget, I, Meng Chong, was also a God Rank genius! " Vital essence suddenly erupted from Meng Chong's body, and a turbulent aura burst out. At the same time, a slender saber appeared in his hand.
"Demonic God Slash!"
Meng Chong's first attack was his strongest attack. He was determined to kill Luo Zheng in one strike!
He could clearly distinguish the priority. The people in front of him were not important. The most important thing was Luo Zheng. Once he killed Luo Zheng, everything would be over! As long as the Demon Clan controlled the secret of the Tower of Sin, with his strength as a War Saint, no one in the Tower of Sin would be able to resist the Demon Clan!
Zhao Fenqin and Zhou Zhuohe, one on the left and one on the right, raised their swords at the same time. Sword intent began to intertwine, forming little spirals.
"Twin Jade Sword Aura!"
"First Sword Break!"
"Second Sword Kill!"
"Third Sword Destroy!"
"Fourth Sword Calamity …"
Zhou Zhuohe and Zhao Fenqin worked together in an orderly manner. Profound and exquisite sword intent surged towards Meng Chong's saber.
But when the sword intent collided with Meng Chong's saber, all of the sword intent was instantly shattered!
Zhao Fenqin and Zhou Zhuohe's faces sank. When the two of them worked together, they were said to be able to deal with an ordinary War Saint. But Meng Chong was clearly not an ordinary War Saint.
Zhao Fenqin coldly shouted, "Change your move!"
The two of them continued to block Meng Chong's saber …
Luo Zheng naturally did not remain idle. Facing Meng Chong's saber, he quickly retreated. His eyes flashed, and starlight began to flicker on the surface of his body!
When Meng Chong's Demonic God Slash appeared, it was as if a fiendish Demonic God was rushing towards Luo Zheng. Even though Zhao Fenqin and Zhou Zhuohe tried their best to block it, they still could not stop it …
But when the saber arrived in front of Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng met it with a punch!
"Sinking Stars Earthquake!"
A whirlpool formed by starlight immediately sucked the Demonic God into it.
"Break for me!"
Luo Zheng was very clear about the power of the 《Astral Combat Body》. The strength of this punch was enough to kill a beginner War Saint. It should be able to resist Meng Chong's saber!
But at this moment, a strange scene occurred. Just as the ferocious Demonic God was about to be sucked into the whirlpool created by the 《Sinking Stars Earthquake》, it suddenly split into three and transformed into three even more powerful saber energies. They surrounded Luo Zheng from three different directions!
"Not good!"
Luo Zheng's expression sank. It was obvious that he had underestimated the strength of a War Saint. Or rather, he had underestimated Meng Chong's strength!
Before the Demon Race left, they left Meng Chong and Tian Hu here. They definitely had a foolproof plan! After Meng Chong broke through to the War Saint realm, it was not as simple as it seemed!
"Hmph, break for me!"
At this moment, Xun stabbed her spear from Luo Zheng's side. The tip of her spear suddenly pierced one of the saber energies. Then, with a light twist, the saber energies were immediately shattered by an invisible wave.
At the same time, Yun Luo also struck out with her finger and shattered another saber energy!
As for the last saber energy, it was forcibly blocked by Luo Zheng's high-grade Sacred Long Sword!

Chapter 636
After Meng Chong finished speaking, he took out a black fruit from his chest. His big hand, which was like a palm-leaf fan, gently pinched it, and the fruit was instantly crushed.
A black aura came out of the fruit and surrounded Meng Chong.
"Five months ago, I said, Luo Zheng, I will kill you! Today, you must die here! " After Meng Chong finished speaking, the black aura formed a pair of huge black wings behind Meng Chong!
Seeing the black wings, Xun and Yun Luo frowned at the same time.
"The demon race actually obtained that guy's inheritance …" Xun said with a cold expression.
Luo Zheng felt that the black wings were extraordinary, but with his experience, he naturally couldn't recognize what it was.
"This is the Dark Sky Demon God's fruit," Yun Luo added.
"Dark Sky Demon God?" Luo Zheng was stunned.
"It's just that its name is Dark Sky Demon God. It's also a Realm Lord, but it has the strength of a Realm Lord, but it doesn't control a Realm. It's probably … equivalent to an independent warrior in your Central Domain," Xun said.
"What's the use of this fruit?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"As you can see, it can greatly increase this guy's strength in a short period of time!" Xun shook her head and said in a low voice, "Get ready to run!"
Xun and Yun Luo's vision and insight were far from what Luo Zheng, Ling Yan and the other lower realm warriors could compare to. Xun didn't suggest running because she judged that both sides still had the strength to fight. Even if Meng Chong was a War Reverend, they might not be able to defeat him!
Ling Yan and the two peak War Reverends of the Yao Night clan were enough to fight against Tian Hu and the three peak War Reverends of the Night clan. As for Luo Zheng's side, as long as the five of them delayed Meng Chong and waited for Ling Yan to deal with her opponent, Meng Chong shouldn't be able to defeat them.
However, Xun wasn't a fortune teller. She couldn't have calculated that Meng Chong actually had the Dark Sky Demon God's fruit. In fact, the Dark Sky Demon God's fruit could not only stimulate the body's potential, but it could also use some kind of secret technique. As for whether Meng Chong had this secret technique, it would depend on whether the demon race had obtained the complete Dark Sky Demon God's inheritance …
She raised her spear with both hands and thrust it towards Meng Chong. A formless yet fatal wave of energy shot towards Meng Chong.
"Go!" Of course, Xun didn't think that this spear would be able to kill Meng Chong. She had only bought some time for Luo Zheng to retreat.
Of course, Luo Zheng would not show off his courage here. As soon as Xun's voice fell, his figure shot backward like a flying shuttle.
On Ling Yan's side, she led the two pinnacle war reverends of the Yao Night Clan and forced the three pinnacle war reverends of the Sky Tiger Clan and the Night Clan to retreat! The members of the Night Demon Clan were much more outstanding than the other clans. Although Tian Hu was cultivating in the secret passage, he had not broken through to the Battle Saint realm, so his strength had not improved by much. As for the other three clansmen, although they were also peak War Reverends, the Night Demon Clan was not good at fighting head on, so how could they be Ling Yan's opponent?
Ling Yan was constantly suppressing Tianhu, but after hearing Xun's order, she immediately gave up the plan to attack. The King's order, to Ling Yan, was the supreme imperial edict!
"Let's go!"
The three figures immediately turned into streams of light and flew behind Luo Zheng.
"Can you guys escape?" A cold smile appeared on Meng Chong's face. After dodging Xun's spear, the pair of black wings on his back suddenly flapped. His flying speed was astonishing. His figure was like a whirlwind as he chased after Luo Zheng.
The humiliation he had suffered in the Sin Tower and the grievances he had suffered in the past few months were constantly released in Meng Chong's heart. At this moment, Meng Chong even hoped that this game of cat and mouse would last a little longer. He really enjoyed this game of cat and mouse!
"Let's hurry up and follow!" Tian Hu led the three members of the Night Clan and followed.
The three members of the Night Clan secretly rolled their eyes …
Actually, what Xue Luotian wanted to say was that since they had found Luo Zheng with Meng Chong and the others, their mission should have been completed. Killing Luo Zheng was their job, right? Xue Luotian didn't want to stay in this damned place for even a moment longer. Just now, he had been chased by those monsters all the way here and had almost lost his life!
However, Xue Luotian could only think about this in his heart. If he said it out loud, he was afraid that he would be killed on the spot by Meng Chong. Moreover, in this situation, even if the three members of the Night Clan were to return, they didn't have the guts to do so!
"You can't escape, Luo Zheng. Just obediently surrender and I'll let you die a little more comfortably!" Meng Chong grinned from behind.
His speed was extremely fast, and the distance between him and Luo Zheng was constantly shrinking. It seemed that Meng Chong was about to attack and kill Luo Zheng.
The eyes of Xun, who was originally following behind Luo Zheng, flashed as he suddenly came to a halt. His long spear danced wildly as a flickering white light suddenly pierced out. That invisible ripple swiftly enveloped Meng Chong, who was chasing after him.
"Your spear technique is really powerful. It's really surprising that you can exert such extreme power with such strength. Unfortunately … it's still useless!" Meng Chong, who had swallowed the black fruit, brandished his saber.
With just one strike, Meng Chong broke Xun's spear light. Not only that, the saber didn't weaken at all and directly cut towards Xun's body!
"Haha, die for me!"
The saber instantly cut into Xun's chest and then twisted her tall body into pieces!
Xun was indeed killed by Meng Chong. However, she was originally a Sword Spirit, a body formed by a Sword Spirit. Even if she was killed, she wouldn't be fatally injured.
Xun, who had turned into a little Sword Spirit, wrapped the spear and spiraled towards Luo Zheng!
But soon, the Sword Spirit reformed and once again rushed towards Meng Chong with the spear. It was still the same spear and the same terrifying wave that made people's hearts palpitate.
Xun's attack was very fierce. Although Meng Chong's strength had greatly increased, he had no choice but to stop and seriously deal with it. Otherwise, even if he was stabbed by the spear, he would still die.
Meng Chong swung his saber again and twisted Xun's body into pieces. But in the blink of an eye, Xun returned unscathed and once again stabbed with the spear …
Soon, Meng Chong realized that this couldn't go on.
His flying speed was indeed much faster than Luo Zheng's, but the Demon Night Clan woman in front of him was an immortal existence. Under her constant obstruction, the distance between him and Luo Zheng didn't decrease at all!
What made Meng Chong even angrier was that Luo Zheng's group was running very fast in front, but Tianhu and Xue Luotian behind them couldn't keep up. The distance between them was getting bigger and bigger, and he couldn't help at all!
However, Meng Chong had chased them all the way here. How could he give up?
He didn't believe that Xun could continue fighting like this!
Xun's Sword Spirit body was attached to Luo Zheng. In theory, she was indeed an immortal existence. As long as Luo Zheng didn't die, she wouldn't disappear or perish.
However, the constant shattering, reassembling, shattering, and reassembling was a big burden for Xun. The red light emitted by the Sword Spirit was constantly fading. If the Sword Spirit's energy was exhausted to a certain extent, it could only slowly recover in Luo Zheng's body.
The current situation was very unfavorable for Luo Zheng …
Ling Yan, Zhao Fenqin, Zhou Zhuohe, and the others' minds were spinning rapidly. They hadn't resisted all the way, but that didn't mean that they had given up. Being chased by Meng Chong like this wasn't a problem for them either!
Under this chase, the two sides unknowingly moved forward rapidly. 10 li, 100 li, 200 li …
At this time, everyone slowly realized that something was wrong with the scenery in front of them.
After entering the inner region from the outer region, the surrounding scenery would slowly lose its color. But now, the surrounding scenery seemed to have disappeared. Apart from the black ground, the trees, the jungle, and the mountains had all disappeared. Everything was so monotonous!
"Fog, there's fog around us," Luo Zheng suddenly said.
"Fog?" Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Ling Yan's expression suddenly changed. Even though she had experienced 13 reincarnations, she couldn't suppress the fluctuations in her state of mind at this moment.
Zhao Fenqin and Zhou Zhuohe raised their heads and looked, but said, "We don't see fog …"
However, Yun Luo said with certainty, "There is fog, but it's very faint!"
"Oh no, I'm afraid … I'm afraid we've entered the core area!" Ling Yan's expression sank, and her figure suddenly stopped.
While they were talking, the fog around them became thicker. Even Zhao Fenqin and the others could see a faint layer of fog slowly floating up. This fog was actually spreading at a visible speed.

Chapter 637
Ling Yan glanced at Meng Chong indifferently. Beams of True Essence spread out and hundreds of flowers bloomed, forming a semicircular domain that protected everyone.
Only after entering the Divine Sea Realm could one have their own domain. However, Ling Yan's cultivation method was a bit special. She could simulate the unique "Myriad Flower Domain."
"You think you can stop me with just this?" Meng Chong snorted coldly. The wings on his back flapped. He took a step forward and punched out. It was as if he was crushing dry weeds and smashing rotten wood. Instantly, the flower-like domain was broken into pieces …
Looking at Ling Yan's expression that gradually turned cold, a faint smile appeared on Meng Chong's face. In the past, Ling Yan was an undefeatable existence in Meng Chong's eyes. Even Meng Tian was no match for Ling Yan. But now, she couldn't even block one of his punches!
However, Ling Yan's expression didn't change at all. She gently waved her hands and more flowers bloomed rapidly, blocking the front. At the same time, she said indifferently, "Meng Chong, didn't you notice the change in the surroundings?"
"Change?" He narrowed his eyes and scanned the area. Looking at the faint mist, he seemed to have suddenly thought of something very important. His face suddenly changed. "This, this is …"
At this moment, Tianhu, Xue Luotian, and the other three hurried over. At the same time, Tianhu shouted, "Not good! Meng Chong … We seem to have fallen into … "
"Fog …" Meng Chong helped Tianhu finish his sentence.
Fog was a very common thing. In the Sea God Continent, fog would often appear during the transition between winter and spring.
However, it was different in the Mysterious Underworld Cave. There was only one place in the Mysterious Underworld Cave that had fog, and that was the core area! Encountering the fog was equivalent to entering the core area.
The reason why there was fog in the Mysterious Underworld Cave was still an unsolved mystery for many warriors on the Sea God Continent. However, every warrior in the Tower of Sin had to thank this fog.
As everyone knew, the Mysterious Underworld Cave was covered in the Mysterious Underworld Death Ray all year round. Other than the demonic creatures inside the Mysterious Underworld Cave, the creatures outside couldn't resist this death ray at all. Even the most powerful warriors would turn into a pool of pus under the Mysterious Underworld Death Ray.
According to his speculations, the Profound Nether Deathlight of the Profound Nether Cave should be emitted from the core region. However, every year, there would be a period of time where a dense fog would rise up from the core region. It was because of the existence of this dense fog that the Profound Nether Deathlight was greatly weakened, allowing the martial artists of the Sin Tower to have the opportunity to enter and explore!
If it wasn't for this layer of fog, no one on the Sea God Continent would be able to step foot into the Xuanming Cave.
However, although many of the martial artists were grateful for this fog, they also feared it from the depths of their hearts. The fog covered the core region, so what unknown existences existed in the core region? To this day, no one knows! In the past, all the races had joined forces to explore the fog, but none of them had survived.
Therefore, regardless of whether it was Meng Chong or Ling Yan, in their hearts, the bewildering fog was equal to the core region, and the core region was equal to the forbidden area!
"Impossible! Impossible! " With a whoosh, Meng Chong directly pulled out a sheepskin from his chest. The map of the demon race was drawn on this sheepskin. "We shouldn't have stepped into the core region. This can't be the core region!"
Zhao Fenqin and Ling Yan also took out a map and carefully compared it for a few breaths. Then, Zhao Fenqin shook his head. "This is indeed not the core region, but …"
"But, the core region has expanded." Ling Yan's gaze was very complicated.
There was a very obvious dividing line between the outer region and the inner region. Furthermore, the demonic creatures in the outer region would not enter the inner region. However, there was no obvious dividing line between the core region and the inner region. Wherever the dense fog spread, that was where the core region was!
Hearing everyone's words, Yun Luo's expression was still indifferent. However, a faint smile hung on Xun's face. Her pair of beautiful eyes stared unwaveringly at Yun Luo. She didn't know why, but from beginning to end, Xun was very vigilant against Yun Luo. Even the dense fog that made people's faces change did not cause Xun to have any thoughts.
As for Luo Zheng, his brows were completely furrowed. Because he had just been chased by Meng Chong, Luo Zheng's spatial ring was getting hotter and hotter. The Jade Butterfly in the spatial ring had already turned into a ball of fire!
This meant that the direction the Jade Butterfly was pointing to was precisely the dense fog! It seemed that the inheritance that Immortal Tian Miao had mentioned was definitely in the core region.
Although Luo Zheng was still very calm, he also knew that the current situation was not very optimistic. The famous core region definitely contained some strange things. The most frightening thing was that they knew nothing about this dense fog. How could Luo Zheng break it?
Ling Yan stared at Meng Chong and lightly said, "Now, do you still want to fight?"
Meng Chong's expression was very interesting. He had stayed below the Tower of Sin to cultivate. He was also very clear about his mission. From a certain perspective, he was a death warrior! He had to ensure that Luo Zheng was unable to leave the Profound Nether Cave. He had to make sure that Luo Zheng died in the Profound Nether Cave. His mission was complete!
But after he truly understood that he had entered the core region, Meng Chong's mentality underwent a drastic change!
Meng Chong did not want to die.
In Meng Chong's mind, after he killed Luo Zheng, he would continue to hide in the Tower of Sin. In the future, he would be the sole king of the Tower of Sin!
Within the Tower of Sin, one would also be affected by the power of the curse. However, according to past experience, the power of the curse would not erupt on the spot. Instead, it would take a month for it to erupt.
In the past, there had been a peak war saint that had become a battle saint within the Tower of Sin. However, after that peak war saint had become a battle saint, the power of the curse did not immediately descend. A month later, that battle saint was killed by the curse …
Every time Meng Chong returned to the Tower of Sin, he would only stay for ten days to half a month before immediately returning to the Profound Nether Cave. This way, he would not be affected by the power of the curse!
As Meng Chong's strength increased step by step, he would become a high-level battle saint and a Divine Sea Realm expert. In the future, he would become the true ruler of the Tower of Sin. Even the Twin Devil Holy Land would have to give him face when doing things!
After all, with his strength, who would be able to contend against him in the Tower of Sin?
However, he did not expect that because he had chased after Luo Zheng, he would fall into the fog. How could Meng Chong not be at a loss?
"No! Kill Luo Zheng first, then we will discuss how to leave the fog! "Meng Chong waved his battle blade and pointed it at Luo Zheng.
Ling Yan's expression sank and a sneer appeared on her face, "Now that everyone has fallen into the fog, we will talk about serious matters after we get out of the fog. What's more, do you think we will let you succeed so easily?"
Meng Chong, who had swallowed the fruit of the Dark Sky Demon God, was indeed powerful. However, it was not as if Ling Yan did not have a trump card. If both parties were to use their full strength and fight to the death, the final result would be hard to say!
"That's right, Lord Meng Chong … We, we should think about how to leave the fog!" At this time, Xue Luotian, who was at the side, stammered. In his heart, he thought that today was the worst day of his life. If he had known earlier, he would not have informed Meng Chong and the others. He, Xue Luotian, would rather be torn to pieces by those Tuna Birds and Azure Wolves than die in this damned place.
Meng Chong took a deep breath and then said coldly, "The fog is only a legend. It is very scary, but it doesn't mean that we can't get out!"
Everyone knew very little about the fog. Their understanding of the fog came from guesses and legends. It was said that once one fell into the fog, there was no possibility of getting out. However, this was not necessarily true!
It was just a thin layer of fog. Could it really prevent many warriors from getting out?
"Let's make peace for the time being and put aside our dispute. We will talk about everything after we get out of the fog," Ling Yan suggested.
"That's a good suggestion!" Xue Luotian quickly laughed.
Meng Chong glared fiercely at Xue Luotian, making him shut up immediately. Then Meng Chong nodded. "Okay! We will talk about it after we get out of the fog! We will go back the way we came! "
The road they took just now was not complicated. This area was barren everywhere. It was so flat that there was not even a small mound. It was very desolate.
The two groups of people, one in front and one behind, put aside their dispute for the time being and carefully walked back the way they came …
Just now, it was still a thin layer of fog. Now, it was getting thicker and thicker. The white fog was like milk in the air, floating slowly and almost blocking everyone's sight.
