Chapter 638
Due to the fog, both sides temporarily put aside their dispute, but everyone still carefully maintained a certain distance.
After all, both sides were enemies and not friends.
Under Meng Chong's pursuit, they had run for about twenty to thirty miles, but it should not have been long since they entered the fog. They were probably only four to five miles away.
With the speed of this group of people, they should be able to return in a few dozen breaths of time!
Everyone's hearts were hanging high because no one knew if they could walk out of the fog.
The time it took for an incense stick to burn passed, and everyone's complexion became worse and worse. According to their flying speed, they could cover a distance of several dozen miles in the time it took for an incense stick to burn, but there was still an endless fog in front of them, and they simply couldn't find the edge of this fog.
"Oh no, oh no, it's just like the rumors say. Once you enter the fog, there's no turning back …" Xue Luo Tian said with a long face.
"Shut up," Meng Chong scolded. Although he remained calm on the surface, there was a trace of panic in his heart. The Demon Race was the race that understood the Xuanming Cave the most. The last time they worked together to explore the core area, the Demon Race sent the most people and suffered the most losses!
Moreover, the Demon Race was the most well-prepared for the exploration of the core area. Everyone made life cards and set up voice transmission talismans at the same time.
However, after entering the fog, all the voice transmission talismans were disconnected and could not communicate with the outside world, but the life cards were fine.
If the life cards did not shatter, the people did not die. After entering the fog, they were definitely fine at the beginning, but three days later, all the life cards shattered at the same time! More than a dozen Peak War Venerables of the Demon Race died in an instant!
What was it that made those Peak War Venerables die in an instant?
This was probably a mystery that would never be solved.
Now, Meng Chong had the chance to uncover this mystery, and the price was probably his life!
Therefore, Meng Chong was also worried at this time and forced himself to be calm!
At this time, Luo Zheng, who was not far behind, suddenly said, "Stop!"
Everyone looked back at Luo Zheng, then Zhao Fenqin asked, "What's going on?"
"Look," Luo Zheng pointed to the ground, where there was a small mark.
Everyone looked at the mark and asked, "What is that?"
"Before we set off, I secretly left this mark … Now," Luo Zheng looked at the mark with a complicated expression. He frowned and said, "We'll go back to where we started."
Xue Luotian smiled bitterly. "It seems that we're going around in circles."
Luo Zheng nodded. Everyone's heart sank.
"Why is this happening? We were flying in a straight line! "Zhao Fenqin said.
Luo Zheng smiled faintly. "Are you sure we're flying in a straight line?"
"I'm sure!" Zhao Fenqin replied firmly.
Luo Zheng shook his head and said with a sigh, "Let's not talk about the strangeness of this fog. It's not that easy to keep moving forward in a straight line."
"Luo Zheng, are you laughing at our intelligence?" Xue Luotian sneered.
A group of peak war reverends didn't even know how to walk in a straight line? Even a child could do it. Luo Zheng's words were a bit unimaginable.
Luo Zheng glanced at Xue Luotian indifferently. Then he reached out and tore a piece of cloth from his sleeve. He said to Xue Luotian, "Come here."
"What are you doing?" Xue Luotian stared at Luo Zheng warily.
"Don't worry, I'm just letting you do an experiment. I won't do anything," Luo Zheng said.
Xue Luotian looked at Meng Chong and the others. He naturally didn't want to go. If Luo Zheng suddenly killed him, how could he resist with Ling Yan and the others around?
But seeing Luo Zheng's posture, Meng Chong nodded and ordered, "Xue Luotian, you go."
Xue Luotian didn't dare to disobey Meng Chong's order. He could only bite the bullet and walk to Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng raised the cloth and said to Xue Luotian, "Now you don't need to fly. Block all your True Essence and perception, and then walk forward!"
"What are you doing …" Xue Luotian was speechless.
"Do it," Luo Zheng said sternly.
Xue Luotian looked back and saw Meng Chong's eyes. He could only do as he was told. Then Luo Zheng covered Xue Luotian's eyes with the cloth and said, "Go forward."
Under Luo Zheng's order, Xue Luotian stepped forward.
"Go, go straight!"
Under Luo Zheng's urging, Xue Luotian's pace became faster and faster.
100 feet, 200 feet, 300 feet, 400 feet …
Xue Luotian had already walked far ahead, and his figure slowly disappeared into the fog.
"Where am I? Can I go now? " Xue Luotian wasn't the kind of person who was bold, not to mention that he was in such a damned place. Who knew if a monster would jump out and swallow him in the next moment?
Meng Chong was about to speak, but Luo Zheng stopped him. At the same time, he continued to say to Xue Luotian through True Essence Sound Transmission, "Go, keep going! We'll be there soon! "
Xue Luotian could only speed up and move forward quickly …
Everyone watched as Xue Luotian's faint figure made a big circle in the fog. Finally, he returned to the starting point and came to Luo Zheng's side. Then Luo Zheng reached out and pulled the cloth off Xue Luotian's face.
Xue Luotian looked at everyone strangely and said, "You've been following me all this time?"
Everyone shook their heads in unison. They hadn't moved a single step just now.
"But, but … I was walking in a straight line just now!" Xue Luotian said strangely.
"You thought it was a straight line, but it wasn't!" Luo Zheng said lightly, "We can usually walk in a straight line because we have something obvious that we can use as a reference! For example, we can slowly approach a mountain so that we can maintain a straight line! "
"But in this fog, all the objects of reference have disappeared, and this thick fog has become a cloth covering our eyes. So … we have taken a detour," Luo Zheng continued.
"So that's how it is. Why haven't I heard of it?" Zhao Fenqin and the others said strangely.
Luo Zheng shook his head lightly. The Sea God Continent had always been in chaos. There was only killing and survival here. It wasn't as stable as the Central Domain. Naturally, there was no cultural inheritance. It wasn't strange that they didn't know this principle.
"What do we do now?" Ling Yan asked.
At this time, everyone looked at Luo Zheng. Obviously, they saw hope in Luo Zheng. In a situation where they didn't understand the reason, Luo Zheng's performance was indeed magical.
"Let's continue!" Luo Zheng took out his long sword and cut a mark on the ground. Then, he chose a direction and walked!
Everyone looked at each other, and their expressions became more and more complicated. Everyone had pinned all their hopes on Luo Zheng.
In fact, Luo Zheng didn't finish his words. It wasn't that he didn't have a reference!
The sumeru ring on his chest was a reference!
The further away Luo Zheng was from the inheritance that Immortal Heavenly Mist mentioned, the lower the temperature of the ring on his chest. And the closer he was to the inheritance, the higher the temperature of the ring on his chest!
Then, as long as the temperature of the ring dropped, the flames of the jade butterfly in the sumeru space would decrease. This meant that everyone was constantly moving away from the core area …
Using this point, it shouldn't be difficult to leave the core area.
After all, the jade butterfly was like a guiding light …
But this was the truly strange part of this fog. Just now, when everyone was returning, the temperature of the sumeru ring was clearly getting lower and lower. But suddenly, it became hotter and hotter. At the same time, it was constantly alternating between hot and cold.
This meant that everyone wasn't walking in a straight line, and they weren't going in a circle. Instead, they were going left and right, sometimes far and sometimes close.
It was as if they were lost in a special space!
Luo Zheng didn't reveal this secret. First, there was no need. Second, he was afraid that it would cause even more panic!
This time, everyone was moving forward even more cautiously. Not only Luo Zheng, but the others were also constantly making marks.
In the time it took for an incense stick to burn …
Two incense sticks of time …
Three incense sticks of time …
Two hours …
Four hours …
As time passed little by little, everyone's hearts gradually sank to the bottom of the valley.
Everyone had indeed used their own methods to leave marks on the ground. But these marks were in disorder. For example, when they had just found the first mark, there should have been a second mark next to it. But what jumped out was a sixth mark!

Chapter 639
"I, I can't take it anymore!"
Xue Luotian suddenly screamed while everyone was still moving forward silently.
In this dense fog, everyone was extremely nervous and the atmosphere was very oppressive. Xue Luotian and the others were on the verge of collapse.
Zhao Fenqin, Zhou Zhuohe, and Ling Yan were still fine. After all, they had passed through the Gate of Reincarnation, and their hearts of martial arts had been greatly stabilized. However, Xue Luotian and the others had not entered the Gate of Reincarnation, so their hearts of martial arts were far less stable than theirs. One could well imagine their current state of mind!
"If we stay here, we will only die here," Meng Chong said indifferently. He was not in the mood to care about Xue Luotian.
"We can't leave! We will definitely die here! "Xue Luotian said with a crazed expression.
"Is that so?" Meng Chong clenched his fist at Xue Luotian, "Why don't we just let you die now!"
Seeing that Meng Chong was really going to attack, Xue Luotian immediately shut up.
This was indeed not the time to say such discouraging words. It could easily destroy other people's tense nerves!
At this moment, a peak war reverend from the Yao Ye clan suddenly pointed forward and shouted, "What is that!"
Everyone looked in the direction that the peak war reverend was pointing and saw a red dot in the distance!
The demonic red dot hung on the horizon, like the sun rising in the morning fog.
One must know that the fog could almost block all sight. Everyone couldn't see anything more than a hundred feet away, but that red dot could penetrate the layers of fog and let everyone see it clearly!
Luo Zheng raised his head and looked over, his eyes flashing with a green light. Even his Clear Eyes Spirit Eyes couldn't see through the strange fog, but when he activated his Clear Eyes Spirit Eyes, the red dot became slightly clearer.
"This light is very similar to the Xuanming Death Ray. What do you think?" Luo Zheng said.
Ling Yan nodded and agreed, "Indeed. That red dot is wrapped in fog. It is very likely that the Xuanming Death Ray in the entire Xuanming Cave is emitted by this red dot!"
"In other words, after walking for half a day, not only have we not left the fog, but we are getting closer and closer to the core? Or rather, we've been walking for half a day, but we're actually heading towards the core! "Zhao Fenqin's expression was extremely ugly.
Everyone couldn't help but gulp. Who the hell knew what was in the core? But even if they used their toes to think, they could understand that no matter what it was, it would be too easy to kill them!
"Leave it!" Meng Chong's eyes lit up. "Now that we have a reference point, we can just walk in the opposite direction! Anyway, let's not go near that red dot! "
Meng Chong's suggestion immediately received everyone's approval!
According to what Luo Zheng had said earlier, they didn't have a reference point, so they were just walking in circles. But now that they had a reference point, things were much easier. As long as they got further and further away from the red dot, they would eventually be able to leave the fog!
Therefore, the appearance of the red dot gave everyone a glimmer of hope!
Everyone looked excited. Only Luo Zheng showed a bitter smile. I'm afraid … it won't be that easy.
Luo Zheng originally had his own reference point, but even so, he felt that the direction everyone was moving in was completely chaotic. How could this fog be broken so easily?
But at this time, Luo Zheng didn't say anything. Since everyone had chosen to try, he had no reason to stop them. Therefore, everyone followed Meng Chong and continued forward.
Ever since they discovered this reference point, everyone's speed rapidly increased. They ran in the opposite direction of the red dot, as if they could leave the fog as long as they kept walking …
During this process, Luo Zheng found that the sumeru ring on his chest was getting hotter and hotter. Needless to say, they were getting closer and closer to the core!
Sure enough, they soon heard Xue Luotian let out a strange cry. Then, he pointed at the red dot in the sky and stammered, "That, that red dot is getting bigger and bigger …"
Everyone looked up and their expressions suddenly changed!
The red dot hanging in the sky was just a dim little dot, but now it had become the size of a fist. This meant that everyone was getting closer and closer to the red dot.
"It's too strange … We're clearly walking in the opposite direction. Why is the distance getting bigger and bigger!" A peak war reverend from the Night Clan said with a trembling voice.
Ling Yan bit her lips. She couldn't explain what was happening in front of her.
"There's only one explanation. There's an invisible force in the fog that's guiding us to the core area. Or rather, it's dragging us there!" Meng Chong analyzed.
At this time, Yun Luo, who had been silent for a long time, suddenly spoke, "We can't walk out of the fog."
Meng Chong and the others didn't know, but Ling Yan and Zhao Fenqin knew that Yun Luo seemed to know a lot about the Mysterious Underworld Cave. The two said almost at the same time, "What's going on? Is there a way to break it? "
Yun Luo said indifferently, "There is."
"Why didn't you say so earlier!" Zhao Fenqin opened his eyes wide. Everyone was on tenterhooks for so long. You already knew, but you were silent. At this time, Zhao Fenqin didn't know what to say. "
Yun Luo said expressionlessly, "But you can't do it."
"What do you mean?" Ling Yan said with a frown.
"This fog is actually an array. It contains the power of the Law of Space," Yun Luo replied. "If you want to walk out of this fog, you must be proficient in the Law of Space above the third level. None of you can do it."
"The Law of Space above the third level!" Zhou Zhuohe smiled bitterly.
Two of them were proficient in the Law of Space. One was Luo Zheng, and the other was Zhou Zhuohe. Luo Zheng was slowly digesting the second level of the Law of Space. When Luo Zheng was in the True Dragon Realm, he relied on the Talent Stele to comprehend the first level of the Law of Space. And from the Talent Stele, he comprehended the foundation of the second level of the Law of Space.
After that, he relied on points to rent the remaining stone steles in the Sin Tower to cultivate. However, he only comprehended half of the second level of the Law of Space. There was still a long way to go before he could reach perfection of the second level. His speed of cultivating the Law of Space was already very fast.
As for Zhou Zhuohe, he was even worse than Luo Zheng. Zhou Zhuohe had not cultivated the first level of the Law of Space to perfection, and he had only comprehended a trace of the second level of the Law of Space!
And according to Yun Luo, one needed to be proficient in the Law of Space above the third level to leave this fog …
They could not possibly stay here and comprehend the Law of Space by themselves, right? How long would they have to cultivate before they could reach the third level?
By then, the fog would have dispersed, and everyone would die from the Xuanming Death Ray!
"Is there no other way?" Zhao Fenqin asked again.
"There is," Yun Luo replied again.
Everyone was very dissatisfied with Yun Luo's method of squeezing toothpaste. They all stared at her angrily. Xun sneered. She knew Yun Luo's personality well. She seemed to always be like this!
"What is it? Can you say it all at once?" Meng Chong said angrily.
Yun Luo did not care about everyone's feelings at all. She said calmly, "Find the array disc that controls this array, and you will naturally be able to leave."
"Where is the array disc?" someone asked again.
Yun Luo shook her head. "I have never been to the Xuanming Cave, and this is my first time here. How would I know? I guess it should be somewhere in the core area. "
"Since you have never been here, how do you know about the Xuanming Cave?" Meng Chong stared at Yun Luo with a complicated expression.
Yun Luo blinked and said, "I won't tell you!"
This sentence again …
Hearing this, Meng Chong almost went berserk. He almost fought with Yun Luo!
In the end, he suppressed the anger in his heart and sighed deeply. "Let's take it one step at a time! If it doesn't work, we will go directly to the core area. If we are lucky enough, we might be able to obtain a greater opportunity! "
Everyone was silent. At this point, they really did not have any other choice.
However, at this time, everyone's mentality was relatively calm. In any case, they were ready to go all out. This was also the so-called 'take things as they come.' If they were all buried here in the end, then it would be because their luck was not enough!

Chapter 640
The rumors weren't wrong. The Xuanming Diablo Ray was indeed emitted from the core area. To be exact, it was emitted from the red dot!
As the fog rose and covered the Xuanming Diablo Ray, the Xuanming Diablo Ray in the entire Xuanming Cave was weakened. Therefore, many creatures in the Tower of Sin had the opportunity to enter the Xuanming Cave.
But now, as everyone slowly approached the red dot, the Xuanming Diablo Ray became stronger and stronger. The threat to everyone was also increasing!
Because of the fog, they could barely resist it. But as they got closer to the red dot, the Xuanming Diablo Ray's power would increase. How could they resist it then?
When this question appeared, everyone's footsteps began to hesitate again …
Previously, they thought that there could be no construction without destruction. At most, they would advance towards the core area. Maybe they would have a chance to escape!
But now, they understood that they were advancing on a path of no return. Not to mention the powerful existence in the core area, just the Xuanming Diablo Ray alone was enough to kill them all!
"I'm not going! If I go any further, I'll definitely die. The Xuanming Diablo Ray alone can kill me! "Xue Luotian sat down on the ground.
Everyone stopped. Although Xue Luotian was a bit timid, he was right. No one here could resist the Xuanming Diablo Ray …
At this time, Yun Luo glanced at Xue Luotian indifferently. Perhaps she was also a bit tired. She gently rolled up her long skirt and sat beside Luo Zheng, murmuring, "It's the same whether we go or not."
"What do you mean?" Meng Chong asked.
Yun Luo hugged her knees and said indifferently, "You'll know in a while."
Meng Chong was also used to Yun Luo's inexplicable temper and didn't continue to ask. Everyone had been rushing for half a day and temporarily rested here.
Ling Yan took out a bottle of water from her Sumeru Ring and drank it. Everyone also took the opportunity to rest.
But without resting for a moment, the corner of Zhao Fenqin's mouth trembled. He continued to point to the sky and said, "That, that red dot seems to be getting bigger …"
Everyone looked up. Sure enough! Even though they didn't move, the red dot was still getting bigger!
"What, what's going on?" Xue Luotian covered his head, his face full of despair.
Thinking of what Yun Luo had just said, Meng Chong stared at her and said coldly, "You already knew?"
Yun Luo nodded seriously. "That's right. Even if we don't move, we'll still be dragged by the Space Laws …"
Only now did everyone understand that this fog was a huge quicksand trap! As long as one took a step into this trap, they would not be able to leave. No matter how much they struggled, it would be useless. And the more they struggled, the faster they would fall!
This discovery once again dragged everyone to the bottom of the valley.
Looking at Yun Luo's calm face, Meng Chong suddenly raised his eyebrows. "Why aren't you afraid?"
Xun said coldly, "Of course she's not afraid!"
"Why? Does she have a way to protect herself? "Meng Chong asked again.
Xun couldn't be bothered to answer Meng Chong. What she wanted to say was that Yun Luo was just a doppelganger, and one of the million doppelgangers. Even if one of them died, it would be an insignificant loss to Yun Luo!
After the repairs were done, Ling Yan then said: "Let's continue on our way!"
Everyone continued to move forward in silence. However, not long after, something that hadn't been there before finally appeared in front of them. Some white things were scattered in front of them!
When everyone got closer to take a look, they saw that they were skeletons! The skeletons were scattered all over the place …
"These are … human skeletons!" Zhao Fenqin exclaimed.
Zhou Zhuohe also lowered his head and reached out to grab a skeleton. However, just as his fingers touched the skeleton, the skeleton turned into a pile of powder and scattered on the ground!
"These skeletons have been here for who knows how many years. When I touched them, they turned into powder," Zhou Zhuohe said, shaking his head.
As everyone continued to move forward, more and more skeletons appeared on the ground. The road ahead was no longer flat. Instead, it was a huge sunken pit.
At that moment, Yun Luo's eyes emitted a strange light. Then, she placed her slender index finger on her lips. That was something to keep quiet.
Although Yun Luo was often crazy, she still understood the Xuanming Cave better than anyone else. When Yun Luo asked everyone to keep quiet, no one said a word. Their faces were full of vigilance as they carefully looked at Yun Luo.
Her face was expressionless as she walked toward the center of the huge pit filled with white bones!
Along the way, the skeletons that her wooden clogs touched all turned into a pile of powder and scattered on the ground …
This huge pit was a huge pit filled with skeletons! Countless skeletons were buried here. There were especially many skeletons at the bottom of this huge pit. The skeletons were even piled up into a small mountain that was several feet high.
When Yun Luo stepped on her wooden clogs and carefully walked to the front of the small mountain of skeletons, everyone became even more nervous. Who knew what kind of demon was hidden in these skeletons! After all, this was the core area! Furthermore, everyone had been walking for so long. This was probably the core of the core area. It wouldn't be strange if there were even more powerful demons!
At that moment, Yun Luo took a deep breath and suddenly used the power of the Law of Wind. Then, she blew a breath toward the small mountain of skeletons in front of her.
It was just a breath, but because it contained a strong power of the Law of Wind, this breath turned into a gust of wind!
These skeletons had been here for who knew how many years. When this gust of wind blew, the small mountain of skeletons instantly turned into dust and was blown away by Yun Luo!
The entire pit of skeletons was instantly covered by fine white dust. Yun Luo's figure was covered by it. No one could see her …
After a while, the dust settled and Yun Luo's figure was slowly revealed. Everyone saw a corpse in front of Yun Luo. It was a dried corpse!
This dried corpse was sitting cross-legged and maintained a state of cultivation. Unlike the skeletons, this dried corpse was still in perfect condition after so many years. They could even recognize this dried corpse's appearance!
Soon, a shocking scene occurred. Yun Luo stretched out her hand and hugged the dried corpse. Then, Yun Luo said softly, "I found you …"
"What is she doing?" Luo Zheng stared at Yun Luo and asked.
Xun looked coldly at Yun Luo and said, "If I'm not wrong, that's the Realm Lord of your human race's Righteous Sun Realm. He's also Yun Luo's brother."
"So Yun Luo's brother also died here." Luo Zheng nodded.
Hearing the conversation between Xun and Luo Zheng, Meng Chong, Ling Yan, and the others' eyes flashed slightly. Figures of the Realm Lord Realm … To them, they were too far away. It was also very difficult for those great figures of the Upper Realm to cast their gazes on the Sea God Continent. However, since this mummy was a Realm Lord expert, then how astonishing would the treasures he left behind on his body be?
Thinking of this, Meng Chong and the others' eyes burned with passion!
Although they knew it would be difficult for them to leave this place, it was up to the heavens to plan. If they left and missed this opportunity, wouldn't they regret it for the rest of their lives?
Thinking of this, Meng Chong took a step forward and rushed towards the bottom of the pit.
At this time, Yun Luo was taking a bracelet from the dried corpse. At the end of the bracelet was a golden skylark.
"Sumeru bracelet?" Refiners could make items from the sumeru space into any shape. It could be a ring or a bracelet. Meng Chong's expression changed. Just as he was about to grab it, Yun Luo indifferently turned her head and said, "If you want to leave this fog, I advise you not to."
At this time, everyone walked down the pit. Since Meng Chong wanted to make a move, Ling Yan and the others were naturally on guard. After all, Yun Luo belonged to the human side.
Hearing Yun Luo's words, everyone's eyes twitched. Meng Chong asked in a deep voice, "You mean, you have a way out of this fog?"
Yun Luo didn't answer Meng Chong's question. Instead, she carefully put away the bracelet with an expressionless face. Then, she took out a yellow talisman from her Sumeru Ring and gently stuck it on the corpse's chest. With a gentle pat, the corpse suddenly opened its eyes!
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 641
The withered corpse opened its eyes, giving everyone a fright.
Especially Meng Chong, who was closest to Yun Luo. It was as if he was bitten by a poisonous snake. He retreated seven or eight steps and stared at the corpse with vigilance.
He was a sector lord after all. When he was alive, his strength was enough to control a large realm! Even if he died here and only a corpse was left, the moment he opened his eyes, it still made people tremble with fear!
Yun Luo glanced at Meng Chong and said, "There wasn't before, but now there is."
Everyone carefully stared at the corpse. They were afraid that the corpse could come back to life. The strength of a sector lord wasn't something they could imagine. Even if they only had a ten thousandth of their strength, they could still kill everyone!
However, other than being vigilant, many people were like Meng Chong. Their eyes revealed a trace of greed!
No matter what this mummy's identity was, the secret it contained was a great opportunity for everyone. Not to mention the value of this mummy, just the Skylark Bracelet that Yun Luo had just taken away was unimaginable.
Although they were tempted, everyone still had a trace of rationality. Yun Luo's understanding of the Mysterious Underworld Cave was greater than everyone else. Also, she said that she had a way to leave this place.
Although opportunities were important, their lives were more important. No matter what, they had to leave this damned fog first. Otherwise, no matter how good the treasure was, they wouldn't have the life to enjoy it!
Luo Zheng's thoughts were different from others. He was just curious and asked, "Since Yun Luo already knew that her brother died here, why did she wait until now to enter the Mysterious Underworld Cave?"
According to what Luo Zheng knew, Yun Luo had been in the Sin Tower for a long time. If her goal was only to take her brother's items, she didn't need to wait until now to enter the Mysterious Underworld Cave. After all, there was a chance to enter the Mysterious Underworld Cave every year.
Xun shook her head and said, "I'm not sure." Then she tilted her head and thought for a bit before saying, "Maybe it's because of you."
"Because of me?" Luo Zheng frowned.
"I am only guessing. She has been following you closely since the beginning of this trip to the Mysterious Underworld Cave. Her goal should not be to obtain her brother's remains. She should have other goals." Xun blinked and said, "That fellow called Tian Miao gave you a jade butterfly. He may have his own goals. If you find that inheritance, you will need to act according to circumstances. He … may not be a good person."
What Xun said was just a guess. From Xun's point of view, the human race was an extremely large race in the upper world. They controlled many more worlds than the Yao Ye clan. Moreover, the human race was not united. From Xun's point of view, what happened in the Xuanming Cave was likely an internal conflict among the human race. It did not involve other races.
Xun only had one goal, and that was for Luo Zheng to grow quickly so that she could return to the Yao Ye clan one day. As such, she did not want Luo Zheng to become a sacrifice for the internal conflict of the human race.
Luo Zheng nodded. Although Tian Miao was only a soul that was struggling at death's door, he had his own goals. When the time came, Luo Zheng would have to make a choice. Xun's reminder was not wrong.
Not long after, Yun Luo put the corpse into the Sumeru Space. The Sumeru Space was different from the Spirit Space. Living things could not be stored in the Sumeru Space. If it was forcibly taken in, it would be rejected by the Sumeru Space. However, if weak living things were taken in, they would be directly obliterated.
After collecting the corpse, Yun Luo took out the bracelet again. She bit her slender white finger and squeezed out a drop of blood from her fingertip, dripping it on the small golden skylark at the end of the bracelet.
The drop of blood quickly seeped into the skylark.
Everyone's eyes were fixed on the bracelet. Of course, they knew that Yun Luo was refining the bracelet, especially Meng Chong, Tian Hu, Xue Luotian, and the others. They all wanted to know what kind of amazing treasure was in the bracelet if there was really a Sumeru Space in it! Any one of the treasures of such a powerful expert would benefit them immensely!
After Yun Luo refined the bracelet, she gently touched the skylark with her finger and took out something from the bracelet.
Everyone's eyes were burning. There was indeed something in the bracelet!
Yun Luo was holding something that looked like a crab in her hand. Below the crab was a long metal chain, which looked like a small pendant.
Yun Luo slowly climbed up from the bottom of the pit and walked towards Luo Zheng.
"What's the matter?" Luo Zheng stared at Yun Luo and asked.
"Don't move." Yun Luo walked in front of Luo Zheng, then pulled the metal chain and put it on Luo Zheng's head.
After hanging the crab on Luo Zheng's chest, she didn't explain much and just said, "I'll give it to you." Then she sniffed at Luo Zheng, turned her head, and continued to walk forward!
Everyone was completely confused. Yun Luo's actions were too strange, and they couldn't figure it out at all …
They didn't ask much. Since Yun Luo gave them a glimmer of hope, it was better to follow her obediently at this time.
Yun Luo walked in front, Luo Zheng and the others followed behind, and Meng Chong followed closely at the back.
The road before was very desolate. As they got closer and closer to the red dot in the sky, more and more strange things appeared around them.
Not long after they walked past the big pit, a huge sword was stuck in the ground not far from them!
The huge sword of the Demon Blood Emperor that Luo Zheng took away from the Trial Takers' Road was already big enough, but compared to this huge sword in front of them, it paled in comparison.
The huge sword in front of them was hundreds of feet high, taller than some medium-sized mountains. Looking up, they couldn't see the hilt of the sword under the cover of the fog …
Such a huge sword, even the Giant Tribe couldn't wield it. They didn't know what kind of person could drive this huge sword.
Ling Yan, Zhao Fenqin, Meng Chong, and the others were all interested in this sword …
Ling Yan and Zhao Fenqin originally wanted to use the sumeru ring to take away this sword, but they quickly discovered that this was a futile action. Even if their sumeru space could accommodate this huge sword, this huge sword still didn't move in the slightest.
As for Meng Chong, he was even more straightforward. He directly flew up and stood on the hilt of the huge sword. He hugged the hilt with both hands, trying to pull the huge sword out of the ground.
"Ha!"
Meng Chong shouted. The Demon Race was originally much stronger than the other races. Almost all of them were born with divine strength. Moreover, Meng Chong had just swallowed the Dark Sky Demon God's fruit. His cultivation had soared, and the astral essence in his body suddenly erupted. With such a violent strength, even a small mountain would be lifted up by him!
But no matter how hard he tried, the huge sword still didn't move.
Finally, Meng Chong could only resentfully jump down from the huge sword.
"This huge sword can't be taken away!" Meng Chong glared and walked to the side with his hands behind his back. To enter a treasure mountain and return empty-handed, this was indeed a depressing matter!
Right at this moment, Luo Zheng walked towards the giant sword on his own.
"Humph, you think you can pull out this sword?" Meng Chong sneered.
Zhao Fenqin also reminded, "Luo Zheng, the weight of this sword is immeasurable. Even if your strength is different from an ordinary person's, you still won't be able to pull it out."
Luo Zheng smiled faintly. "Who said I'm going to pull out this sword?"
As soon as he said this, Yun Luo immediately understood what Luo Zheng wanted to do. Xun agreed. "This sword is already ownerless. Since we can't take it away, it's not a pity to smelt it."
"Smelt?" Xue Luotian narrowed his eyes and smiled. "It's not that easy to smelt this sword!"
"This thing from the Upper Realm has stood here for countless years. The power contained in this huge sword is far beyond their imagination. Even if all the refiners in the world were invited here, I'm afraid they wouldn't be able to smelt it …" Zhou Zhuohe could only shake his head and say.
Luo Zheng didn't speak. He walked to the side of the huge sword and sat down cross-legged. The black fire in his mind had been displayed in front of everyone. Luo Zheng didn't need to avoid it. His eyes flashed, and the black fire in his mind had already spiraled out and rushed towards the huge sword!

Chapter 642
It wasn't just Meng Chong. Even Zhao Fenqin and Ling Yan were shocked when they saw this scene.
Previously, when Luo Zheng's black fire erupted, it could be said to be indestructible. Even the giant toad's tongue was burned by Luo Zheng's strange flames.
However, no matter how powerful the giant toad was, it was still made of flesh and blood. It was not particularly unacceptable that Luo Zheng's black fire could burn the giant toad's tongue. However, this giant sword in front of them had definitely surpassed the existence of a divine artifact!
It might even be a legendary divine artifact!
Although they could not be sure, their guesses were not far from the truth. In the end, Luo Zheng's black fire appeared and burned it just like that? And the speed of smelting was so fast?
Ling Yan and the rest thought it was fast, but Luo Zheng thought it was slow.
Previously, when the black fire burned any weapon, it only needed a light flick to burn away all the impurities in the weapon. However, this giant sword was different. After the black fire burned it back and forth two or three times, it gradually melted. At the same time, the Heaven's Divination Essence flowed toward Luo Zheng like spring water!
"So much Heaven's Divination Essence!"
"How much would it be if it was used to refine weapons …" Zhao Fenqin sighed.
However, there were no weapon refiners present. Although the Heaven's Divination Essence was rare, it was not very useful to ordinary people.
As Luo Zheng continued to smelt, the black fire slowly burned a small hole in the bottom of the giant sword. Then, the small hole became a large hole. Finally, the bottom half of the giant sword was directly burned off by Luo Zheng!
The moment the giant sword broke, the top half fell with a loud crash and heavily smashed into the ground, causing the entire ground to cave in …
Luo Zheng did not look sideways. He continued to focus on smelting the giant sword. The Heaven's Divination Essence produced by this giant sword was equivalent to millions of immortal weapons! The Heaven's Divination Essence flowed toward Luo Zheng like a flood, and Luo Zheng collected it one by one.
As Luo Zheng's strength increased, the power of the dragon scales became more and more important. When used at a critical moment, it could often produce miraculous effects.
Now that Luo Zheng had opened the Baihui Stage and refined his body, his physique had improved further. With his current physique, he could naturally withstand more power.
While smelting the huge sword, Luo Zheng suddenly asked Qinglong in his mind, "What will happen if all the dragon scales are lit?"
Back then, Luo Zheng had only lit up a few dragon scales. He had never thought that he would be able to light up all the dragon scales. After all, based on Luo Zheng's experience, lighting up all the dragon scales would probably require tens of millions or even hundreds of millions of Heaven Amplification Essence. Where could he get so much Heaven Amplification Essence?
Now, Luo Zheng understood that the Heaven's Derivation Essence contained in a Sacred Artifact was countless times more than an Immortal Artifact. In this way, Luo Zheng really had the possibility of lighting up all the dragon scales in the future.
Qinglong smiled faintly and heard him say, "If I light up all the dragon scales, I will be able to reconstruct the Heaven's Amplification Body."
"Heaven's Amplification Body?"
Qinglong then sighed faintly. "When you lit up the dragon scales, I also did not think that you would be able to grow to this day. The essence of the nine of us brothers has been suppressed under this furnace. Our physical bodies have been completely destroyed. The Heaven's Amplification Essence is derived from the understanding of the Heavenly Dao that a weapon refiner has of the Heavenly Dao. If we can reconstruct our bodies, I'm afraid that we will really be able to see the sun …"
Qinglong's words contained a trace of very complicated emotions. Reconstructing a True Dragon's body was something that Qinglong had not even dared to think about. However, later on, Qinglong had guessed that Luo Zheng's fate was to fight for the Great Era. The possibility of that happening was much higher.
As Luo Zheng continued to grow, this possibility became more and more real. Now, it seemed that even if Luo Zheng could help the nine brothers reconstruct their bodies, it was not impossible!
How could Qinglong not be filled with emotions?
"Luo Zheng, thank you!" Qinglong said faintly.
Luo Zheng smiled slightly. "You don't have to thank me, do you? I also need to borrow the power of the dragon scales. Moreover, I don't know when I will be able to light up all your dragon scales. "
"Hehe, it may not be a matter of time." Qinglong understood that Luo Zheng now had the potential to soar. As long as he did not die, it would not be difficult for him to obtain the Heaven's Amplification Essence in the future. When that time came, it would not be difficult for him to light up all the dragon scales of the other eight True Dragons, let alone Qinglong's dragon scales!
At this moment, a voice floated into Luo Zheng's mind. "Luo Zheng, you helped Old Nine light up so many dragon scales. You have to help me too!"
The one who spoke was Old Seven, the red dragon.
Luo Zheng chuckled and said, "There's no hurry. I will help you after I help Qinglong light up all his dragon scales!"
The red dragon was naturally joking. If Luo Zheng could light up Qinglong's dragon scales first and help Qinglong reconstruct his Heaven's Amplification Body, Qinglong would be able to recover all his strength! Although Old Nine was the weakest of the nine dragons, he was still a genuine Superior Divine Beast. When the time came, he would be a great help to Luo Zheng …
Now that there was a delay, it was obvious that he had to help Qinglong first.
Under the spread of the black fire, the giant sword was refined little by little and then stored in Luo Zheng's Sumeru Ring.
In everyone's mind, this giant sword was already comparable to a Divine Weapon. It was indeed a bit wasteful for Luo Zheng to refine it into Heaven's Amplification Essence. However, there was no other way. No one could take this giant sword away. They could only watch Luo Zheng destroy this sword.
About an hour later, Luo Zhengcai finished smelting this huge sword. Only then did he get up in satisfaction, and said to Ling Yan and the others: "Everyone, we can set out."
Yun Luo glanced at Luo Zheng indifferently and said nothing.
Then everyone continued to move forward.
Along the way, they could see skeletons and pits everywhere. Everywhere was full of potholes. Even looking at the devastated ground, they could understand how fierce the battle was. God knew how many powerful warriors had died in this battle!
Even for those skeletons that turned into ashes upon contact, their original owners were probably much more powerful than the Spirit Sea Realm warriors.
As for Meng Chong, Tianhu, Xue Luotian, and the others, their eyes were wide open as they looked around for opportunities. As long as they saw something unusual, they would rush forward to explore it. Since they had come to the core area and Yun Luo had promised to take them out, wouldn't it be wonderful if they could find some opportunities?
However, what disappointed Meng Chong and the others was that after searching for a long time, they didn't find anything decent. They did find a few magic weapons, but they were basically incomplete. Broken weapons and magic weapons could easily dissipate the Heaven's Amplification Essence, causing the weapon to lose its spirituality and become a piece of scrap iron.
As a result, they had become garbage collectors. They didn't find anything good, but collected a lot of garbage!
While they were searching and advancing, everyone finally felt a change!
The Xuanming Death Ray was getting stronger and stronger!
The red dot in the sky was now the size of a car wheel. Although there were layers of fog blocking it, the Xuanming Death Ray still increased the pressure on everyone's Protective Ethereal Force.
Although everyone's Protective Ethereal Force could withstand it, it was being consumed faster and faster. In just a short while, Zhao Fenqin had taken out two top-grade Ethereal Stones, crushed them, and absorbed the Ethereal Force inside to replenish his Protective Ethereal Force.
As for Meng Chong and Tianhu, it was even more troublesome. They walked the path of body refinement and relied on Vital Essence to protect their bodies. However, Vital Essence couldn't be replenished by Ethereal Stones. It could only be produced by their bodies bit by bit!
At first, it was okay. The Xuanming Death Ray didn't put much pressure on them and consumed very little Protective Ethereal Force. At that time, Meng Chong and Tianhu could barely withstand it with their strong bodies. They didn't need Vital Essence to protect themselves.
As they got closer and closer to the red dot, the Xuanming Death Ray became stronger and stronger. The two of them had to use Vital Essence to protect themselves.
However, they weren't too anxious about using Vital Essence to protect themselves. After all, the consumption of Vital Essence couldn't keep up with the constant production of Vital Essence in their bodies.
But now, it was very troublesome. The consumption of Vital Essence had greatly increased, and they couldn't replenish their Vital Essence.
Once their Vital Essence was exhausted, their bodies would have to face the increasingly intense Xuanming Death Ray.

Chapter 643
Meng Chong and Tianhu were not fools. They realized that the situation was getting worse and worse.
Meng Chong's expression suddenly turned cold as he shouted, "Stop!"

Although they could use vital essence stones to replenish their vital essence, as their protective vital essence was being consumed faster and faster, their vital essence stones were also being consumed faster and faster. Yun Luo glanced at Luo Zheng indifferently and said, "Luo Zheng, pour vital essence into that thing on your chest."

A small flower bud quickly grew in the middle of the two leaves, and the flower bud bloomed in an instant!

Only then did everyone understand why Yun Luo had dragged Luo Zheng away. These creatures did not have hands or feet, but they were able to travel underground!

Chapter 644

Chapter 645

........ is...... is....... is......

Moreover, it was impossible to build a teleportation array in the Tower of Sin. In other words, Meng Chong couldn't leave the Tower of Sin and the Mysterious Underworld Cave for the rest of his life.

This was because this peak war reverend called Xiu Ye had controlled the Sin Tower for over a hundred years!
Generally speaking, the peak war reverends in the Sin Tower would do their best to suppress their cultivation and wait until they had accumulated enough Light of Good Fortune before leaving the Sin Tower to break through. This way, the effect of breaking through bottlenecks was very good, so the peak war reverends who walked out of the Sin Tower had almost no bottlenecks in breaking through to the War Sage stage!
However, suppressing one's cultivation was not an easy task. If one's cultivation was suppressed for a long time, it was also possible for one to naturally break through. Therefore, many peak war reverends felt that they were about to break through and would take the initiative to leave the Sin Tower and leave the Cursed Lands.
However, there was an anomaly in the demon race, and that was Xiu Ye!
At that time, Xiu Ye had suppressed his cultivation to the peak war reverend for over a hundred years. During these hundred years, he had always been the most powerful seat controller. It could be said that the demon race was able to control the Sin Tower because of Xiu Ye!
As for how Xiu Ye had firmly suppressed his cultivation to the peak war reverend for over a hundred years, it was a huge mystery to all living beings.

Chapter 646
Because of Xiu Ye's existence, the other races had no chance at all in the Sin Tower. Even though the Demon Night Clan was innately strong, the Demon Race was able to suppress them until Liu Yu appeared.
However, the Demon Race's true decline in the Sin Tower was because of Xiu Ye. Not long after, Xiu Ye suddenly disappeared.
No one knew whether Xiu Ye was dead or alive. He just mysteriously disappeared in the Sin Tower. No one knew where he went.
From then on, when Xiu Ye had just disappeared, the Demon Race was still able to control the seats in the Tower of Sin. However, with the sudden rise of the Demon Night clan, the position of the first seat controller had always been held by the Demon Night clan. Until now, Ling Yan still held the position of first seat controller, while Meng Tian could only hold the position of second.
"Xiu Ye's disappearance is a great loss to our Demon Race." Meng Chong's expression was somewhat solemn. If Xiu Ye hadn't disappeared, the Demon Night Clan wouldn't have been able to suppress the Demon Race.
Yun Luo indifferently said, "Don't you feel that Xiu Ye's disappearance is strange?"
"Strange? Of course it's strange! " Meng Chong frowned. At this moment, he suddenly thought of something and stared blankly at Yun Luo. He stuttered, "Could, could it be … you?"
Yun Luo nodded and turned her head. She indifferently looked at the unimaginably huge beast in the distance and said, "I killed Xiu Ye. He has already turned into nourishment for the Sin Tower and his corpse has been completely absorbed by the Sin Tower."
"This …" Tian Hu was also speechless.
The reason Meng Chong guessed this was because he thought of the time when Xiu Ye disappeared. It was very close to the time when Yun Luo appeared.
When Yun Luo entered the first floor of the Sin Tower, her talent was already extremely terrifying. Naturally, she would be suppressed by the Demon Race! However, just like Liu Yu, Yun Luo's strength far exceeded the Demon Race's expectations. No matter how they attacked Yun Luo, she would always be able to defeat them!
As a result, Yun Luo easily climbed to the tenth floor of the Sin Tower.
Just as the Devil Race was preparing to suppress Yun Luo even more fiercely, Yun Luo did not choose to enter the Talent Stele. Moreover, she did not seem to have the intention to continue climbing. She stayed on the tenth floor. This action was not only out of the Human Race's expectations, but also out of the other races' expectations.
As long as Yun Luo didn't climb to the top, she wouldn't be a big threat to the demons. After enough observation, although the demons didn't understand Yun Luo's purpose, since she wasn't a big threat, they just let her be …
No one would have thought that this Tenth Order War General would quietly kill the Peak War Reverend of the Demon Clan, the strongest person who had controlled the Tower of Sin for over a hundred years.
"You, why did you want to kill Xiu Ye?" Although this matter had little to do with Meng Chong, it was impossible for Meng Chong not to be curious since they were of the same race.
Only then did Yun Luo shift the topic to the right path. "Because Xiu Ye was a puppet of Daoist Tian Miao. He was waiting for Luo Zheng in the Tower of Sin."
At this point, the mystery in front of Luo Zheng became clear. Tian Miao, who was far away in the Central Domain, didn't know that Xiu Ye was already dead. In Tian Miao's opinion, as long as Luo Zheng entered the Tower of Sin, Xiu Ye would definitely discover him immediately and then bring him into the Mysterious Underworld Cave to fulfill Tian Miao's last wish.
Therefore, Tian Miao didn't explain too much and only told him that he would understand after entering the Tower of Sin.
If Xiu Ye was still alive, Luo Zheng's climbing of the Tower of Sin would have been much smoother. At least, he wouldn't have been suppressed and attacked by the Demon Clan because protecting Luo Zheng to the top of the tower was Xiu Ye's mission.
However, Yun Luo had entered the Tower of Sin a step ahead of him and silently killed Xiu Ye. In this way, Yun Luo could only become his guide …
Yun Luo blinked her eyes and shifted her gaze to Luo Zheng's face. She then said, "If Xiu Ye had brought you here, he would have also made you throw the Jade Butterfly down because this was the only way to open the passage."
"I understand," Luo Zheng nodded.
He didn't know whether Yun Luo was telling the truth or not, nor could he distinguish between the forces represented by Yun Luo and Xiu Ye. Who was right and who was wrong? But no matter who was right and who was wrong, Luo Zheng would walk down his own path. Every step he took would leave a footprint. Only in this way could he walk further and his path be longer!
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng suddenly opened his hand and the burning Jade Butterfly in his hand freely fell down.
"Bang …"
The Jade Butterfly flickered with sparks as it hit the edge of the abyss and sank into the darkness. Its falling speed was extremely fast. At first, everyone could only see a small red dot, but it quickly disappeared into the darkness.
"What will happen if I throw it down?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Wait," Yun Luo spat out a word.
"How long do we have to wait?" Luo Zheng asked again.
Yun Luo shook her head. "This abyss has no bottom, so I don't know where that thing grows …"
"The abyss has no bottom?" Luo Zheng asked curiously. "No matter how deep the abyss is, it must have a bottom!"
No matter how deep the abyss was, there should be a limit. A hundred miles long? A thousand miles long? Ten thousand miles long? It was impossible that there was no bottom …
Who knew that Xun, who had been silent all this time, would speak up. She smiled slightly. "If my guess is right, this should be an endless abyss. There is indeed no bottom. Someone once explored where this abyss leads to and how long it is, but after falling down for 1,400 years, he still didn't reach the bottom. Later, that person flew back from the abyss after 2,000 years …"
Yun Luo nodded. "That's indeed the case. And there is more than one abyss like this in the great wide world."
As soon as Xun's words came out, everyone except Yun Luo was shocked.
This abyss hadn't reached the bottom after falling for 400 years? How, how on earth was this abyss formed …
Everyone silently looked at the abyss in front of them. Looking at the pitch-black entrance, a sense of fear arose in their hearts for no reason. There were too many secrets in this world. Some of them were far from what they could understand at the moment!
However, there were indeed too many lunatics in this world! Someone was willing to spend more than 3,000 years to explore the length of this abyss!
After Luo Zheng threw the jade butterfly down, a full three hours had passed. Then, they heard a loud noise coming from the abyss.
"It's coming." Yun Luo turned her head and walked back. "Leave the edge of the abyss. It would be bad if we were sucked in by it."
The sound that came from the sound was like a clap of thunder, rumbling. Who knew what kind of behemoth was going to climb out of it?
Even if Yun Luo didn't say it, no idiot would stay beside the abyss. Everyone turned around and followed Luo Zheng, leaving the edge of the abyss and walking to a relatively safe place.
"What on earth is that thing?" Ling Yan asked.
No matter what it was, to be able to make such a big noise in this abyss, its size was probably not much smaller than the Ominous Beast called Fierce Scorpion …
"Actually, you've seen it before," Yun Luo replied.
"We've seen it before?" Everyone's expression suddenly changed.
Yun Luo nodded. "That thing is the plant that chased us all the way. Those plants are just its roots that extend from the ground. What's coming up now is its main body."
Xun sneered and said quickly, "It's a Southern Dipper Immortal Root!"
"What's the use of it climbing up?" Luo Zheng asked.
Yun Luo said, "You'll know soon enough."
As the roaring sound became louder and louder, it was like a clap of thunder in everyone's ears. Luo Zheng and the others had to use their zhenqi to temporarily seal their hearing. The thing in the abyss was getting closer and closer to the entrance of the abyss. Everyone's expression became more and more nervous. Such a loud sound actually didn't wake up the huge Ominous Beast! The Ominous Beast called Fierce Scorpion was still in a deep sleep!
"It's coming!" Yun Luo stared into the distance and said.
Soon, everyone saw a huge plant drill out of the abyss, and there was an extremely ugly face on the plant!
The most frightening thing was that this strange face was facing Luo Zheng and the others!
The monstrous face of this huge plant was not much smaller than the head of the incomparably huge scorpion. It was as big as a mountain. The two eyes on the monstrous face were firmly fixed on Luo Zheng and the others!
