Chapter 647
At this moment, everyone's heart almost stopped beating.
Not to mention them, even Divine Sea Realm experts were like ants in front of this demonic creature. They had no resistance at all!
"Turn around. Don't look at it. Cover your ears." Yun Luo turned her head and turned her face to the side.
Everyone turned their heads. Many of them covered their faces. At this time, any resistance was superfluous …
"Roar!"
The Southern Dipper Immortal Root suddenly raised its head and roared. The loud sound caused the entire Mysterious Underworld Cave to tremble!
Many sharp stalagmites grew on the top of the Mysterious Underworld Cave's wall. Amidst this roar, they broke off one by one and then fell down heavily.
One of the stalagmites, more than twenty feet long, just happened to fall in the direction of the crowd. At this moment, Yun Luo's eyes flashed. She raised her hand and shot out a ray of light, instantly shattering that stalagmite into pieces.
This roar was fierce and long. It lasted for a full incense's time before gradually stopping.
Then, Yun Luo turned around and said, "The fierce scorpion has woken up …"
Everyone's expression was incomparably complicated. Although that giant beast had been sleeping, under such a terrifying roar, it was impossible to sleep …
Everyone turned around and looked. Sure enough, that giant beast had opened its eyes!
"Dong!"
When this fierce scorpion had just woken up, there was still a trace of laziness on its face. It seemed to have slept for too long. It even used its huge palm to wipe the top of its head. With this touch, the Mysterious Underworld Death Ray blooming above its head suddenly became several times stronger. The crimson light enveloped the entire Mysterious Underworld Cave, blooming with a red light more intense than the sun!
Ling Yan lowered her head and said, "Even that fog can't stop this Mysterious Underworld Death Ray. If there are other martial artists in the Mysterious Underworld Cave, they won't be able to escape now."
Fortunately, because of the chaotic tide this time, most of the martial artists should have returned to the Sin Tower. Only a small number of martial artists wanted to take advantage of the chaotic tide to obtain greater opportunities. Since these people dared to take the risk, they also had to accept the risk!
The fierce scorpion looked at the giant Southern Dipper Immortal Root in front of it and raised its huge head. Then, it suddenly opened its mouth and roared at the Southern Dipper Immortal Root.
"Roar!"
Yun Luo's expression changed. She suddenly said to Luo Zheng, "Hurry up, make a big hole!"
When the scorpion began to roar, a hurricane that could be seen with the naked eye began to form. Because the abyss was incomparably huge, although the hurricane was huge, it still needed a certain amount of time to blow over.
As soon as Yun Luo finished speaking, Luo Zheng and Meng Chong took the initiative to attack!
Luo Zheng had used all his strength, including the power of the dragon scales. Meng Chong was also charging forward without any regard for his own safety. Only God knew how powerful the hurricane produced by this roar was. It would be fine if he was swept away like this, but the problem was that if he left Luo Zheng's side, he would be shot to death by the Mysterious Underworld Death Ray on the head of the vicious scorpion.
"Peng peng peng …"
Due to the bombardment from the two of them, a 10-zhang-wide crater appeared beneath their feet. No one thought too much about it and they jumped down at almost the same time.
After entering the huge pit, Luo Zheng and Meng Chong continued to dig downwards, digging another 30 meters deep...
After doing this three times in a row, everyone had already sunk into the 30-zhang-deep crater.
Just as everyone steadied themselves at the bottom of the crater, before they could take a breath, they heard the sound of turbulent wind coming from above their heads! The sound of the wind swept past the crater above their heads, and a part of the wind blew in, hitting everyone's faces like a knife cutting through them!
Zhao Fenqin and the others immediately circulated their Protective True Essence, but Tian Hu's movements were slower. As a result, his body was cut by the strong wind, and he looked terrible!
"I wonder if this depth will work?" Luo Zheng looked up nervously!
Everyone saw that the edge of the crater was constantly being shaved off by the strong wind. Layer by layer, it collapsed and was then swept away by the strong wind behind it!
In a few breaths' time, the 30-zhang-deep crater was quickly flattened...
"Continue to go down!" Meng Chong shouted and punched downwards again.
Luo Zheng didn't stop. The others also attacked together and continued to bombard the ground.
"Bang, bang, bang …"
The crater above their heads was constantly being shaved off by the strong wind. Everyone also continued to bombard downwards, hiding even deeper in order to avoid the strong wind above them!
The scorpion's roar was many times louder than the Southern Dipper Immortal Root's roar. With just one roar, the entire Mysterious Underworld Cave was in chaos...
On the tenth floor of the Sin Tower, Purple Jade of the Demon Night Clan had just walked out of the Talent Stele. After taking the Talent Stele's test, her talent actually ranked on the third floor! This result was already very dazzling in the Demon Night Clan!
Right now, the Demon Night Clan's best result in the Sin Tower was her. However, Purple Jade didn't look happy at all. Instead, she was endlessly depressed and lonely.
When she first entered the Sin Tower, she had vowed to challenge Luo Zheng and defeat Luo Zheng. However, when she saw Luo Zheng's golden name, it was as if she was laughing at herself for overestimating herself...
She simply didn't have the qualifications to consider Luo Zheng her opponent!
Purple Jade clenched her teeth. She held her weapon and continued to walk towards the arena. She would never admit defeat. Even if Luo Zheng's talent was greater than hers, she would still work ten times harder. One day, she would be able to surpass Luo Zheng.
However, Luo Zheng had entered the Mysterious Underworld Cave. Moreover, Luo Zheng's speed of improvement was extremely fast. He had already climbed to the fourteenth floor, so she didn't have the chance to challenge Luo Zheng...
Just as Purple Jade walked towards the arena, a violent tremor suddenly came from beneath her feet. Then, a deep roar sounded. It was actually a roar that came from underground!
At this moment, all the beings of the various clans looked at each other with nervous expressions!
Everyone knew that below the Sin Tower, or rather, below the entire Cursed Land, was the Mysterious Underworld Cave. But what was with such a loud roar?
Could it be that some demonic creature had approached the Sin Tower?
"What, what kind of monster is roaring? It's so loud. Heavens!"
"The people who entered the Mysterious Underworld Cave this time are going to be miserable. It seems that there's something strange in the Mysterious Underworld Cave again …"
"You don't know. This time, there was a chaotic tide. Most of the peak War Reverends have already retreated. Only a small portion of people are still inside!"
"I heard that Luo Zheng hasn't returned. Even Ling Yan from the Demon Night Clan hasn't returned! It seems that they're going to die inside this time! "
That violent roar continued for a long time, causing the entire Sin Tower to tremble!
In reality, it wasn't just the Sin Tower that felt the roar. The entire Cursed Land could hear it. Two hours later, the violent roar even spread throughout the entire Sea God Continent!
In the Sky Feather Holy Land, Liu Yu was standing at the top of a huge Holy Tree. She was wearing a purple robe. At this moment, her pointy ears were completely erect. When the violent roar spread out from the ground, the entire Holy Tree of the Yao Night Clan began to tremble.
The Sacred Tree had a soul. It uneasily told Liu Yu its fear. At this moment, all the beings in the Demon Night Clan's Sacred Ground were uneasy. It was as if doomsday had arrived.
"Don't be afraid, don't be afraid …" Liu Yu gently stroked the treetop and softly communicated with the Sacred Tree, comforting the Sacred Tree's uneasiness.
It wasn't just the Sky Feather Sacred Ground. The Human Race's Purple Heart Sacred Ground, the Demon Race's Twin Demon Sacred Ground, the Night Clan's Eternal Night Sacred Ground, and all the beings in the various Sacred Grounds were immersed in uneasiness and fear …
A full two thousand feet!
In a short period of time, Luo Zheng and Meng Chong continuously hammered into the ground. They dug a two thousand feet deep hole. The rocks and soil that fell from above almost buried everyone inside!
But even though they were hidden in such a deep hole, it still didn't ease the uneasiness in their hearts. This roar was simply too exaggerated!

Chapter 648
Under the twisting winds, the soil around the abyss was shaved off by more than a thousand feet.
Even though everyone had dug a two-thousand-feet-deep pit, it was only a small pit of about a hundred feet under the twisting winds …
The hearts of everyone who climbed out of the small pit were still beating violently. They were very lucky to be able to survive this roar.
As soon as they climbed out of the small pit, they saw a fierce battle taking place above the abyss!
After the fierce scorpion finished roaring, it charged toward the Southern Dipper Immortal Root. The Southern Dipper Immortal Root was also very fierce. Even with such a terrifying roar, it did not suffer any damage. Instead, it twisted its roots that were as thick as a mountain, raised its ugly head, and charged toward the fierce scorpion.
"Bang!"
The fierce scorpion looked huge and simple, but its movements were surprisingly agile. Just as the Southern Dipper Immortal Root stretched its head over, the fierce scorpion slapped the Southern Dipper Immortal Root's head.
The sound of this slap was like hundreds of thunderbolts exploding at the same time.
Luo Zheng and the others could only temporarily seal their ears. Otherwise, this slap would definitely deafen them …
"Roar!"
After being slapped, the Southern Dipper Immortal Root turned its head and continued charging toward the fierce scorpion. Then, it slapped again.
If this slap had been used on the Sea God Continent, it would probably have been able to shatter an entire mountain range …
"Bang …"
"Bang …"
"Bang …"
After a few consecutive slaps, the fierce Southern Dipper Immortal Root finally showed signs of defeat. It seemed that it was no match for the fierce scorpion!
Xun glanced at Luo Zheng and transmitted her voice to him. "This Southern Dipper Immortal Root is actually a kind of vegetable."
"Vegetable?" Luo Zheng stared intently at the battle between the giant beasts and asked blankly.
"Yes, a kind of vegetable used to feed super divine beasts. If you don't believe me, ask Qinglong in your mind." Xun suddenly smiled and transmitted her voice.
Luo Zheng was indeed curious. "Qinglong, is that so?"
"Don't listen to her nonsense! I've been fasting for many years! "Qinglong refused to admit it.
"What about before fasting?" Hearing Qinglong's words, Luo Zheng suddenly asked with a smile.
"Before I reach inedia, I won't eat this either!" Qinglong chuckled and denied, "Our True Dragon Realm is rich in natural resources. The Southern Dipper Immortal Root is a great tonic, but its taste … Only fierce beasts like the scorpions can endure it. Why would we True Dragons eat such an ugly thing?"
Actually, what Xun said was right. The size of a divine beast was not bad. An ordinary divine beast was not much bigger than the flood dragon puppet Luo Zheng had seen before. However, divine beasts were often as big as a continent. The amount of food these super divine beasts needed to grow up was astonishing!
Although Super Divine Beasts could capture other Super Divine Beasts as their food, but Super Divine Beasts were a minority in many big worlds. Moreover, the strength of other Super Divine Beasts was not weak. All Super Divine Beasts were at the top of the food chain, but before they could fast, it was very difficult for them to find enough food.
Therefore, most Super Divine Beasts basically relied on vegetarians to grow up …
A huge Southern Dipper Immortal Root like this was one of the food for these huge fierce beasts.
Soon, the Southern Dipper Immortal Root was subdued by the scorpions. The scorpions stretched out their claws, strangled the roots of the Southern Dipper Immortal Root, and opened their huge mouth!
"What do we do now?" Luo Zheng looked at Yun Luo.
Everyone looked at Yun Luo pitifully. They were also tortured and scared by the scene in front of them. Even though Zhao Fenqin, Ling Yan, and the others had cultivated through the Gate of Reincarnation, their moods were still in a mess. The impact of this level of battle was really too strong!
It was just that when Yun Luo had promised to lead everyone out of the fog, they did not expect to come to this place. This was not even half the same as walking out of the fog, right?
"Hold on tight! Everyone, hold on tight to Luo Zheng! "After saying that, Yun Luo reached out and grabbed Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng looked at Yun Luo strangely, but did not understand the meaning behind her words.
The others also did not understand, but at this time, everyone followed Yun Luo's words like a conditioned reflex!
Ling Yan also reached out and grabbed Luo Zheng's arm. The other peak War Reverends also quickly grabbed onto their hands and arms.
Meng Chong's reaction was not slow either. He grabbed onto Luo Zheng's neck.
"Hey, hey …" Being firmly held in such a chaotic manner, Luo Zheng felt uncomfortable all over. However, Luo Zheng's resistance was useless. At this time, saving their lives was more important. Who would be willing to listen to Luo Zheng's words?
The last one, Tianhu, was more embarrassed. Luo Zheng was already tightly surrounded by everyone. There was no gap left for him. In desperation, Tianhu could only hug Meng Chong … Even if he could not catch Luo Zheng, he could still catch Meng Chong.
As for Xun, she directly stuffed the long spear into Luo Zheng's sumeru ring. Then, she turned into a little sword spirit and drilled into Luo Zheng's body.
Just as everyone was ready, they heard the sound of wind whistling in their ears …
"Huff, huff …"
A huge suction force was released from the scorpion's mouth.
Just like the gust of wind that blew out just now, they did not have the ability to resist this suction force!
Almost in an instant, everyone's feet left the ground and were directly pulled into the sky above the abyss. Everyone tightly grabbed onto Luo Zheng and kept tumbling in the air, flying towards the scorpion's big mouth!
"What, what's going on!" Meng Chong was shocked and angry. He roared at Yun Luo. He never thought that Yun Luo wanted everyone to hold on tightly to be sucked into the scorpion's mouth. Wasn't this sending them to their deaths?
"Yun Luo, what the hell is going on?" Even Ling Yan, who was always calm, had an ugly expression at this time.
If they had continued to dig underground and hid in the big pit, they could have avoided being sucked into the scorpion's mouth. Now that they were swallowed by the scorpion, they would not even be enough to fill the gaps between its teeth. Wouldn't everyone be dead?
Zhao Fenqin also cried out with a sad face, "This time we are definitely dead … Yun Luo, didn't you say that you would lead us out of the fog? How did we walk into the mouth of this monster? "
Yun Luo blinked her eyes. Facing everyone's questioning, her expression did not change at all. Instead, she said faintly, "If we want to leave the fog, we must enter its mouth. I did not do anything wrong. In fact, I had already planned to do so."
"Can you give us a hint first …" Zhou Zhuohe tightly held onto Luo Zheng's arm and said with a conflicted expression.
"There's no need. You may not die if you do as I say, but if you don't do as I say, you will definitely die!" Yun Luo blinked her eyes and slightly tilted her head. Her small nose sniffed Luo Zheng's chest. At this time, she actually had the leisure to smell Luo Zheng's body.
Only Luo Zheng said with a bitter smile, "I say … can you guys be a little gentler? My bones are about to fall apart!"
How could everyone be willing to listen to Luo Zheng? As soon as they let go, they would immediately be shot to death by the Xuanming Death Ray. This was a matter of life and death, so naturally, Luo Zheng held on tightly. Everyone continuously fluttered and spun in the air above the abyss, heading towards the scorpion's big mouth to suck. As the scorpion opened its mouth, the Southern Dipper Immortal Root was also sucked into its big mouth piece by piece.
For the Southern Dipper Immortal Root, the scorpion's big mouth could be called a mouth. However, for Luo Zheng and the others, the scorpion's big mouth was like a huge cave that was tens of thousands of feet high. Even if ten mountain ranges were placed side by side, they could still be stuffed into the scorpion's mouth!
To Fierce Scorpion, Luo Zheng's group wasn't even an ant, or even a speck of dust. In Fierce Scorpion's eyes, Luo Zheng's group didn't even exist, they were just too insignificant …
"Bang, bang, bang …"
Some huge rocks that were as wide as ten feet were also sucked into the scorpion's mouth. Some of these huge rocks directly hit Luo Zheng and the others, but before these huge rocks could hit them, they were immediately neutralized by Yun Luo's finger light and the attacks of Meng Chong and the others …
After flying above the abyss for a while, everyone finally entered the scorpion's big mouth. Just as they entered the big mouth, a strange scene occurred. The True Essence in everyone's body suddenly and strangely disappeared! Meng Chong and Tianhu's expressions changed greatly. The Vital Essence in their bodies also disappeared!
Luo Zheng, who cultivated both energy and body, was naturally in the same situation. Whether it was True Essence or Vital Essence, they all disappeared without a trace at the same time.
Although everyone was constantly tumbling in the air, they still relied on their True Essence to stabilize their bodies. Now that their True Essence suddenly disappeared, they were immediately blown around by the turbulence and finally fell heavily into the darkness!

Chapter 649
Whether it was Meng Chong or Ling Yan, the reason why they were powerful was because they relied on their true essence and vital essence.
After losing these things, their bodies were only slightly stronger than ordinary creatures. Bumping into each other in the darkness, everyone was dizzy from the fall.
And the crab ornament on Luo Zheng's chest, because it lost its true essence, the orange light cover also completely disappeared.
Fortunately, the Mysterious Death Ray that blossomed from the head of the vicious scorpion could not illuminate here, otherwise, everyone would have been instantly shot to death by that red light …
Under the unceasing tumbling, everyone could no longer hold onto Luo Zheng's body. One by one, they scattered, unceasingly tumbling downwards.
The space inside this giant beast was so large that it was far beyond everyone's imagination.
The length of the vicious scorpion's entire body was unknown, because it had been lying on the edge of this abyss. Its huge body extended all the way to the bottom of the abyss, but just the part that appeared before everyone's eyes was already indescribably huge.
Luo Zheng was still fine. Although he bumped into each other along the way, there was nothing here that was harder than Luo Zheng's body. Even if the giant stone that was sucked into the vicious scorpion's stomach hit his body, he was still unscathed. On the contrary, he could directly smash that giant stone into pieces.
As for the others, at this time, their days were difficult. For example, Ling Yan, Zhao Fenqin, and the others were unable to circulate their true essence. Naturally, they also did not have true essence to protect their bodies. A single stone smashing over could make their scalps bleed!
Fortunately, the inner walls of this vicious scorpion were soft. Otherwise, under this kind of bumping, they really would have been smashed to death!
Along the way down, they had spent a few incense sticks worth of time. Following Ling Yan's muffled sound, everyone also fell to the bottom one after another. After passing through that dark passage, everyone was surprised to find that there was actually light in this vicious scorpion's stomach, as if there was another world.
Only, apart from Luo Zheng, the others did not have the mind to investigate their surroundings for the time being.
Aside from Yun Luo, the other people's bodies were more or less bruised. The one with the most serious injuries was unexpectedly Ling Yan. Her body was lacerated with several wounds, and a part of her clothes was dyed red with blood.
Even Meng Chong and Tianhu, who were known for their strong bodies, were not in a good condition. Tianhu was limping as he crawled up from the ground, while Meng Chong's face was swollen, making him look extremely comical!
On the other hand, Zhao Fenqin and Zhou Zhuohe did not seem to be seriously injured.
However, although these wounds looked serious, they were nothing to the cultivators. They could only be regarded as superficial wounds. After taking out healing medicines and swallowing them, they began to observe their surroundings.
Luo Zheng and Yun Luo stood not far away, observing the surroundings.
The environment in the Fierce Scorpion's stomach was far beyond Luo Zheng's expectations. It was as large and open as the Mysterious Cave. However, it was still a cave. If they kept flying upwards, they would hit the ceiling of the cave.
Therefore, even though they were in such a large Mysterious Cave, it still gave them a cramped feeling. The dim, grayish yellow environment constantly reminded them that they were still in a cave. However, this cave was extremely large, so large that it occupied a considerable area of the entire Sea God Continent.
It was reasonable to say that although the space in the Fierce Scorpion's stomach was large, it was not as large as the Mysterious Cave. After all, the Fierce Scorpion was also in the Mysterious Cave. Although its size was large, it was still much smaller than the Mysterious Cave.
However, the space here seemed to be boundless. Even the sky was blue. It was a completely different world! Moreover, there were mountains, rivers, and plants in this world. Looking at it, it was no different from the Boundless Universe …
"Why is there such a world in the Fierce Scorpion's stomach?" Luo Zheng asked in surprise.
Yun Luo nodded and said, "Yes, this is the Small Thousand World in the Fierce Scorpion's stomach."
"There's a Small Thousand World in its stomach?" Luo Zheng asked with a face full of astonishment. Luo Zheng had also been to the Small Thousand World at the top of the Tower of Sin. When he was there, he could freely construct the environment inside. The 128 Small Thousand Worlds at the top should have been constructed by the original owner of the Tower of Sin.
At this time, Ling Yan had already changed her clothes, and the wounds on her body had also been treated. After walking over, she said: "It is said that after reaching the Divine Sea Realm, the True Essence in the body will transform into an ocean, shrouded in a hazy True Essence mist, forming a world of its own. That is why it is called the Divine Sea Realm. It is just that the body of a Divine Sea Realm warrior is still unable to nurture terrestrial creatures. Only after reaching a higher level, will a world of its own gradually form.
Yun Luo nodded and said, "It is indeed so. In the Small Thousand Worlds within the bodies of some of the strongest in the Upper Realm, there are even their own countries. There are tens of millions of creatures inside, and there are even wars …"
Hearing their words, Luo Zheng felt even more amazed. Nurturing their own world in the body? The creatures inside could even cultivate?
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng suddenly had a flash of inspiration. He thought of the entire universe and the numerous Boundless Worlds. Could it be that they were also in the body of a certain creature? Thinking of this, Luo Zheng also voiced his doubts.
Before Yun Luo could speak, Xun had already flashed out of Luo Zheng's body. He smiled slightly and replied, "No one can answer this question of yours!"
"Why?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
"We have been searching for the answer to your guess for who knows how many billions of years, but no one can solve it." Xun shook his head and said.
If Luo Zheng could think of this question, there was no reason that the strongest in the Upper Realm could not think of it. Someone had long thought of this. After reaching the Divine Sea Realm, one's body could form a Deity Sea of its own. As one's cultivation increased, the Deity Sea would slowly expand. Heaven and Earth would appear and slowly nurture creatures. Under this inference, the entire universe could also be in the body of a certain creature.
Unfortunately, countless people in the Upper Realm had searched for the exit of the entire universe, but no one had been able to find the exit of the entire universe. Some people also thought that this hypothesis was very stupid and was completely fabricated …
"This is not the problem we should be concerned about now. Yun Luo, what should we do next?" Meng Chong strode over and asked with an unfriendly expression.
In this Boundless World, all Vital Spirit and Vital Spirit were prohibited. The only thing everyone could rely on now was their own physical bodies. The pair of black wings behind Meng Chong's back had disappeared. One could imagine the sense of crisis in his heart!
Who knew that Yun Luo would not care about Meng Chong at all. She only said coldly, "Come with me."
Meng Chong did not have the patience. He reached out his large hand to grab Yun Luo. However, the moment he reached out, Yun Luo's figure suddenly swayed. She turned around on her wooden clogs and climbed onto Meng Chong's thick arm!
After taking two steps on Meng Chong's arm, she reached out her hands to grab Meng Chong's forearm and pressed down hard with the help of her body's center of gravity.
"Crack!"
Everyone heard the sound of bones shattering. Meng Chong's thick arm was powerlessly tilted to the side. At the same time, Meng Chong's blood-curdling screams could be heard.
Tianhu's expression changed. He wanted to rush over, but Yun Luo jumped down from Meng Chong's arm. Her petite figure contrasted sharply with Meng Chong's huge body. Yun Luo coldly glanced at Tianhu and said, "If you want to leave here and return to the Sin Tower alive, don't talk nonsense!"
With a twist of Yun Luo's arm, Meng Chong's arm was broken. Everyone was shocked.
No matter how powerful Yun Luo was, she was only a Tenth Rank Warlord. To be able to break Meng Chong's arm in one move, this …
Yun Luo didn't care about the gazes of the people around her. She just walked forward alone. Meng Chong could only drag his broken arm. He took out a pill from his pocket and swallowed it. Even if the effect of the healing pill was really good, it would take a few days for his broken arm to completely heal.
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed with a glimmer of light.
After entering the giant beast's stomach, everyone could not rely on their True Essence and Vital Essence. Therefore, the only thing that could work here was the lowest level of martial arts.
What Yun Luo used just now was the most commonly used "Soft Arts". Even though Yun Luo's strength was far inferior to Meng Chong's, Yun Luo easily broke Meng Chong's arm with her technique …

Chapter 650
Meng Chong didn't say anything. In the Mysterious Underworld Cave, he still had strength to rely on.

After losing their true essence, a martial artist was equivalent to a tiger without its fangs. If they encountered a slightly more powerful demon, they would find it difficult to resist.

These fierce rats were abnormally agile, but there was nothing special about them.
The problem was that there were more and more fierce rats in front of them. There was a large black mass around them. If they could use their astral essence, any one of them could instantly kill these fierce rats!

Chapter 651

Chapter 652

At that moment, Xun appeared and smiled faintly. "What's so great about having over 10,000 great realms? The human race doesn't dare to wage war against our demon race. Hmph!"

The demon race's individual strength was great, but their reproductive ability was low. This was the greatest flaw of a race! That was why, when the demon race had conquered many great realms, they hadn't been able to send many of their people to garrison them. Even now, the demon race had only conquered over a thousand great realms.
"Hmph, in the upper realms, there are those who measure their strength by the number of great realms they control," Xun said, unconvinced.
Yun Luo shook her head. "Let's not talk about this for now. I'll talk about the conflict between Heavenly Sovereign Oracle and Heavenly Sovereign Original Sin. Originally, I couldn't talk about this in front of you, so I didn't tell Luo Zheng."
The "you" Yun Luo was referring to was Xun.

Chapter 653
The Yao Night Clan was different from the other clans because they had their own soul statues and could easily communicate with the Upper Realm.
Thus, no matter if it was the Yao Night Clan in the Upper Realm or the Yao Night Clan scattered in the Lower Realm, they were all closely connected.
However, the other clans were different. Although some clans could communicate with the Upper Realm through secret techniques, this kind of communication was not close. For example, the Human Clan in the Purple Heart Holy Land knew nothing about the Human Clan in the Upper Realm, let alone that the Human Clan had such an important position in the Upper Realm.
What Yun Luo was most afraid of was that one of the three Kings of the Yao Night Clan, King Xun, who controlled slaughter, was Luo Zheng's Sword Spirit. This was one of the main reasons why Yun Luo was hesitant.
Yun Luo's apprehension was not without reason. The Human Clan and the Yao Night Clan were competitors in the Upper Realm, and the secret in the Xuanming Cave was very important. Moreover, it was an internal conflict between the Human Clan.

Xun had left a clone of himself in the Massacre Sword Mountain in his early years. Yun Luo had cut her soul into millions of pieces and placed them in the Lower Realm to cultivate. Carrying the Heavenly Law Fragment required a complete soul. Both of them couldn't refine the Heavenly Law Fragment.

At the entrance of the Sin Tower stood two Giant Clan guards. When they saw a group of peak War Reverends of the Demon Race appear in front of them, their faces immediately revealed expressions of fear.
How could Meng Tian lower himself to the level of these Giant Clan warriors? He only coldly roared: "Open the door!"
The two Giant Clan guards hurriedly opened the door of the Sin Tower. Following that, many Peak War Reverends of the Demon Race filed in and entered the first floor of the Sin Tower!
On the first floor of the Sin Tower, many creatures were still fighting in the arena. However, when those creatures saw the Peak War Reverends of the Demon Race, they were all shocked.
"Why are these Demon Race back! Didn't Luo Zheng expel them? "
"You're too uninformed. I heard that Luo Zheng died in the exploration of the Xuanming Cave!"
"It can't be. If that's the case, wouldn't the Human Race be in trouble?"
"We can only blame that Luo Zheng for overestimating himself. He's just a war general, yet he dared to explore the Xuanming Cave. Not only is he courting death, he has also harmed the entire Human Race!"
The creatures of the various races discussed animatedly.
There were still many Human Race warriors on the first floor. At this time, all of their expressions were extremely ugly. Back then, the warriors of the Human Race's Purple Heart Holy Land had to become a war general before they could enter the Sin Tower to train. However, after Luo Zheng took control of the Sin Tower, the status of the Human Race in the Sin Tower greatly increased. Many warriors also stepped into the Sin Tower to train.
Now that these warriors saw the return of the Demon Race, they naturally understood what had happened. The entire Human Race in the Tower of Sin would most likely be dealt with. These Human Race warriors wanted to secretly leave the Tower of Sin, but their legs seemed to be filled with lead, they couldn't even take a step!
The Peak War Reverends of the Demon Race did not panic. Their bodies emitted a ruthless aura as they entered in a single file and stepped onto the second floor, the third floor … With so many Peak War Reverends, almost every time they stepped onto a floor, they would attract the attention of other creatures.
Very quickly, Meng Tian finally climbed to the fifteenth floor!
Meng Tian's heavy iron boots stepped on the hall at the top of the tower. Then, he took a deep breath and roared towards the small worlds in the depths: "All seat controllers, get out here with me!"
"Get out here!"
"Get out here for us!"
Behind Meng Tian, many of the Peak War Reverends of the Demon Race roared.
Not long later, the doors to the small worlds opened one by one, and many seat controllers walked out one after another.
The Night Clan's seat controllers were the first to appear, and after those seat controllers came out, they stood beside Meng Tian. The Night Clan had always been a vassal of the Demon Race, and even though the Demon Race had been chased out of the Sin Tower, they were still unable to change this reality. After all, their Night Clan's Eternal Night Sacred Grounds was still under the protection of the Twin Devil Sacred Grounds.
The last to appear were the many Peak War Reverends of the Human Race, as well as the Peak War Reverends of the Demon Night Clan.
Meng Tian swept his gaze across the Peak War Reverends of the Human Race, and suddenly let out a violent laugh, "The Peak War Reverends of the Human Race … Aren't they all the guys who used to be on the fourteenth floor? What qualifications do you have to become the seat controllers? Hahahahaha! "
"Hahahahaha …"
All the Peak War Reverends of the Demon Race laughed along, and even the Peak War Reverends of the Night Clan laughed together.
The faces of the Peak War Reverends of the Human Race were ashen. They were very angry, but there was nothing they could do about it. In the Sin Tower, strength was the reason. Without Luo Zheng, they were nothing!
On the other hand, the Demon Night Clan was relatively calm. After all, they had the powerful Heavenly Feather Sacred Grounds as their backing. Lord Mei coldly said: "Speak, what do you want to do?"
