Chapter 656
The laughter of the peak war reverends from the Yao Night Clan gradually faded away. Meng Tian slowly walked forward, and his aura became more and more domineering. Every time he took a step forward, a small hole would be left on the floor, but it would quickly return to normal.
"What do we want to do? Haha, "Meng Tian coldly stared at Lord Mei and laughed," The Yao Night Clan will give up half of their seats, and all the humans in the Sin Tower, regardless of whether they are peak war reverends or generals, will commit suicide! "
When Meng Tian said this, the expressions of all the humans and Yao Night Clan members on the top floor changed.
"What? All the humans will commit suicide? That's too much! "
"The demons are too domineering! Are they really the only ones in this world? "
"The demons have always been domineering. This time, the humans and Yao Night Clan are going to be in big trouble!"
Meng Tian gave off a domineering aura as he coldly stared at Lord Mei and said, "I wonder if the Yao Night Clan will accept this suggestion?"
If it was in the past, the Yao Night Clan would naturally not accept such a domineering suggestion. Although the Yao Night Clan had a few extra seats, this time, the Yao Night Clan had to give up half of their seats. This was something that the Yao Night Clan could not accept!
The Yao Night Clan did not suffer many losses in this trip to the Mysterious Underworld Cave, but the most important people, Xun and Ling Yan, were left behind in the Mysterious Underworld Cave!
Lord Mei's expression sank as he coldly laughed, "Meng Tian, do you think the Yao Night Clan only has Ling Yan?"
The 128 seats at the top of the Sin Tower were all peak war reverends, except for the humans. There was a difference between peak war reverends, but the difference was not that great. Moreover, the Yao Night Clan was stronger than the demons, so the Yao Night Clan was not necessarily afraid of the demons. The only troublesome thing for the Yao Night Clan was Meng Tian!
Meng Tian suddenly laughed, "What? What else does the Yao Night Clan have to rely on? The Heavenly Feather Holy Land? No matter how strong the Heavenly Feather Holy Land is, it won't dare to rashly start a war with the Twin Demon Holy Land! Moreover, the demons still have war reverends in the Sin Tower! "
Meng Tian did not know whether Meng Chong and Tian Hu were dead or alive. He did not have time to investigate the secret passage, but he had used a sound transmission talisman with Meng Chong, but he did not receive a reply.
However, this did not matter too much. Even if Meng Chong and Tian Hu died, they could still create more Battle-Saints. This was because until now, only the Demon Race knew the secret of the secret passage.
Actually, because of the appearance of Tianqiong and Tianhui, the two Battle-Saints of the Devil Race, many races had already begun to suspect that the Devil Race had done something to the Mysterious Underworld Cave.
This was not a difficult guess. Many races had already come up with all sorts of theories, hypotheses, and experiments. One of them was that breaking through in the Mysterious Underworld Cave would not be affected by the cursed lands. However, due to the existence of the Mysterious Underworld Death Ray in the Mysterious Underworld Cave, even if one broke through to the Battle Saint realm, they would still die.
In the past, a Beastman Peak War Saint broke through in the Mysterious Underworld Cave. He even relied on his strength after breaking through and broke into an ominous area in the outer region of the Mysterious Underworld Cave, obtaining a good opportunity. However, when the Mysterious Underworld Death Ray spread throughout the entire Mysterious Underworld Cave, that Beastman War Saint was forced to return to the Sin Tower. After surviving in the Sin Tower for a month, he finally fell.
It was a pity that the other races did not discover the secret passage at the bottom of the Tower of Sin …
Seeing that Lord Mei was silent, Meng Tian sneered, "You can think about it! However, I want to tell you that I'm not giving you a suggestion, but an order! "
Meng Tian felt that there was no need for him to discuss with the Yao Ye Clan. If it wasn't for the Sky Feather Holy Land and Liu Yu himself, Meng Tian would have massacred both the Human and Yao Clans today!
After saying this, Meng Tian turned his head and looked to the other side. The Peak War Saints of the Human Race all had gloomy expressions, as if they were lambs waiting to be slaughtered!
Before Meng Tian could say anything, one of the Peak War Saints of the Human Race suddenly said, "Our Human Race can take a step back and lead all of our Human Race out of the Tower of Sin!"
This was already a huge step back. Every Holy Land knew how important the Tower of Sin was to a race. Cultivating in the Tower of Sin could save hundreds of years of time. It was countless times faster than cultivating in the outside world!
Leaving the Tower of Sin was equivalent to the Human Race cutting off their own future. In the future, the Purple Heart Holy Land's development speed would fall behind the Holy Lands of the other races. The decline of the entire Human Holy Land was inevitable!
"Take a step back!" Meng Tian sneered, "What qualifications do you humans have to talk to me about taking a step back!"
It could be said that Meng Tian hated the Human Race to death. After all, the Yao Ye Clan was only in an alliance with the Human Race. Moreover, the reason why the Yao Ye Clan interfered was because of Luo Zheng, that human brat's sword spirit! Therefore, the reason why all of the Demon Race were expelled from the Tower of Sin was because of the Human Race. How could Meng Tian let the Human Race off so easily?
"Then what do you want? Our Human Race has already left the Tower of Sin! "A Peak War Saint of the Human Race said angrily.
"Haha!" Meng Tian laughed wildly, his tall and sturdy figure suddenly took a step forward, and in the blink of an eye, he crossed a distance of fifty to sixty feet. His huge hand that was the size of a palm-leaf fan extended out like a bolt of lightning, and in the blink of an eye, he grabbed the head of the Peak war elder of the Human Clan, "What else do you want! Didn't I already say it? "
"Bang!"
Meng Tian sneered, he extended his hand and pinched hard. Before the Peak war elder could even circulate his true essence, his head exploded like a watermelon, and then, Meng Tian threw the corpse of the Peak war elder to the side as if he was still trash, and turned his gaze to the rest of the Human Clan!
Many of the Peak war reverends of the Human Clan had died in the Profound Nether Cave, and the remaining Peak war reverends were indeed not strong enough. Most of them could only stay on the fourteenth floor, and did not have the strength to compete for the seats, so how could they resist an expert like Meng Tian?
A portion of the Peak War Saints of the Human Race looked towards the Yao Ye Clan for help, but the Yao Ye Clan could not even protect themselves, so how could they help the Human Race? Lord Mei and the others pretended not to see anything.
"Meng Tian, you Demon Race are ignoring the rules of the Tower of Sin and attacking here!" A Peak War Saint of the Human Race clenched his teeth and said angrily.
Meng Tian sneered: "Rules? Actually, let me tell you, there have never been any rules in the Tower of Sin. The law of the jungle is the only rule here! Right now, I am the rule here! "
With that, Meng Tian took another step forward and punched the Peak War Saint with his heavy fist.
"Bam!"
The Peak War Saint of the Human Race was like a sandbag as he fiercely smashed into the wall. Blood immediately blossomed on the wall, as if a flower was drawn on the wall.
At this time, the Peak War Saints of the Human Race were so angry that their eyes were about to split open. The True Essence on the surface of their bodies continuously flickered. As Peak War Saints, they did not plan to sit still and wait for death! Just as the Human Race was about to make a move, the bodies of the Peak War Saints of the Demon Race behind Meng Tian also flickered with astral essence.
The faces of the Peak War Saints of the Human Race flashed with determination. They knew very well that they were not Meng Tian's match, but they had no choice now!
"Since you are not willing to commit suicide, then I can only trouble my colleagues to do it!" Meng Tian's face flashed with a hint of madness. Today, he would definitely capture all the humans in the Tower of Sin in one fell swoop.
When Lord Mei saw this scene, he immediately sent a True Essence sound transmission to a member of the Yao Ye Clan beside him, "Have all the humans on the first to fourteenth floors of the Tower of Sin retreat and enter the Cursed Lands."
The Cursed Lands was absolutely safe. Lord Mei's actions were purely out of helplessness!
It had to be known that the warriors sent into the Tower of Sin were all geniuses of the various Holy Lands. The weakest of them was a Mortal Rank genius. The human warriors in the Tower of Sin were the future hope of the Purple Heart Holy Land. If they were all killed, it would be equivalent to destroying the future hope of the Purple Heart Holy Land.
Lord Mei could not save the Peak War Saints in front of her, but she could only chase the other War Saints, Generals, and Warriors out of the Tower of Sin. After all, the Yao Ye Clan and the Human Race were still allies. Even if Luo Zheng was dead, Lord Mei still had to give face to Xun.
In fact, the humans in the Tower of Sin were much smarter than Lord Mei had imagined. Other than a small number of human warriors who were cultivating in the secret cultivation rooms, many humans had already left the Tower of Sin one after another.

Chapter 657
During this year, the Tower of Sin was not very peaceful.
Many of the living beings in the Tower of Sin were also discussing this matter. Some of the more cowardly ones even temporarily followed the human cultivators out of the Tower of Sin and entered the Cursed Lands. If something were to happen and they were implicated, it would not be worth it.
In the Mysterious Underworld Cave, Luo Zheng and the others were moving forward with an orange cover.
After leaving the scorpion's body, Yun Luo opened the ornament on Luo Zheng's chest and pushed Meng Chong and Tian Hu outside!
The moment the two Demon warriors left the protective cover, they also opened their Shield of Great Aether. However, it only lasted for one breath's time before it disappeared. At such a close distance, not to mention a Battle-Saint, even a Divine Sea Realm warrior could not resist the Mysterious Underworld Death Ray shot out from the scorpion's head!
Seeing Luo Zheng's stunned expression, Yun Luo asked indifferently, "When do you plan to make your move?"
"I plan to see where this guy is hiding," Luo Zheng answered.
Yun Luo said indifferently, "Meng Chong will not give you the chance. Once he leaves the fog formation, he will attack you immediately. As long as he can grab the ornament on your chest, we will be the ones to die."
This time, Xun agreed with Yun Luo.
Compared to Yun Luo and Xun, Luo Zheng did not have enough experience in dealing with this kind of situation. Or rather, Luo Zheng was not ruthless enough. In comparison, Yun Luo and Xun had much more experience than Luo Zheng. When exploring dangerous places, a mature warrior would usually survive by himself while everyone around him died. All the treasures would be his own!
When they left the huge scorpion and the Abyss, the fog in the core area had gradually dissipated.
"The fog is dissipating!" Seeing the fog become very light, Luo Zheng suddenly frowned and said.
Yun Luo also blinked and said, "It should be because of the flow of time. The flow of time in the scorpion's body is many times faster than in our Great Thousand World. We stayed in the scorpion's body for a few days, but a long time has passed outside."
"Oh no! So the Mysterious Underworld Cave has recovered? In other words, in the Tower of Sin …? "Luo Zheng's face turned gloomy.
Ling Yan's expression wasn't too good either. Once the Demon Race determined that they were dead, it was very likely that they would return and enter the Tower of Sin. The Yao Night Clan's situation would probably be better, but the Human Race would be in big trouble!
"Let's quickly return to the Sin Tower!" Luo Zheng said as he quickened his pace.
Before they even took two steps forward, when they entered the faint mist, Luo Zheng could see clearly that there were extremely thin golden lines pulsating on the ground.
"These golden lines formed the Fog Array from before." Yun Luo said. She reached out and injected a stream of Qi into the Array Disk in her hand. The Array Disk immediately began to spin.
As the Array Disk continued to spin, the golden lines began to change directions. The originally connected golden lines began to break apart, forming openings. After the Fog Array Disk lost its effect, everyone began to move outside.
In the past, once the Mysterious Underworld Deathly Ray was activated, all the cultivators would return to the Sin Tower. However, Luo Zheng and the others were able to move forward under the full illumination of the Mysterious Underworld Deathly Ray. This was the first time in the history of the Mysterious Underworld Cave that something like this had happened!
Fortunately, the Demons in the Mysterious Underworld Cave couldn't completely resist the illumination of the Mysterious Underworld Deathly Ray. When the Mysterious Underworld Deathly Ray was sufficient, the Demons' activities weren't particularly frequent. On their way from the core area to the inner area, they almost didn't encounter any Demons.
Even when they passed through the Blood Gate, the Tuna Birds and Azure Wolves that were scattered on both sides of the Blood Gate were also dormant in their nests and didn't come out.
"It seems that whenever the Mysterious Underworld Deathly Ray in the Mysterious Underworld Cave is greatly weakened, the Demons' activities are also particularly frequent," Ling Yan said as she blinked her eyes.
Luo Zheng nodded, "That's true!"
Ling Yan's heart was slightly moved, and her heart suddenly burned with passion.
Along the way, Ling Yan already knew that the most important thing in the Mysterious Underworld Cave wasn't the relics of those Sector Lords. The most important thing was that Heavenly Dao fragment. The other things didn't seem worth mentioning in front of the Heavenly Dao fragment.
However, in the eyes of Ling Yan and the other cultivators of the Sea God Continent, the Heavenly Dao fragment wasn't important. Things that were related to the Upper Realm didn't have much to do with them, the lower realm cultivators. Since ancient times, how many people were able to ascend to the Upper Realm? Moreover, in these years, many Divine Sea Realm cultivators of the Sea God Continent were unable to ascend. In the end, they could only watch as their lifespans came to an end and passed away in the Sacred Ground.
In their eyes, the most important thing was the many treasures in the Mysterious Underworld Cave.
Now that Luo Zheng had the pendant that could resist the Mysterious Underworld Deathly Ray and the fog array formation plate, wouldn't he be able to freely enter and exit the Mysterious Underworld Cave in the future? Moreover, when the Mysterious Underworld Deathly Ray wasn't blocked by the fog, the Demons in the entire Mysterious Underworld Cave were basically dormant in their nests. The danger was also greatly reduced!
In addition, under the protection of the pendant, he could even cultivate inside the Mysterious Underworld Cave and break through to become a Battle-Saint!
The pendant on Luo Zheng's chest only needed a small amount of True Essence to operate. As long as the Tower of Sin continuously sent in True Essence Stones, it would be fine. None of this was a problem!
What Ling Yan thought of, Zhao Fenqin and the others naturally thought of as well. This way, whether it was for the Demon Night Clan or the Human Race, it was a huge opportunity.
Previously, Luo Zheng had said that he might choose to leave the Tower of Sin and leave the Sea God Continent. Originally, the cultivators of the Purple Heart Holy Land were worried because once Luo Zheng left, the Human Race would lose control of the Tower of Sin.
Even if Luo Zheng chose to leave quietly, paper couldn't wrap fire. As long as Luo Zheng disappeared from the Tower of Sin for a period of time, the Demon Race would definitely make a move and make a comeback.
Now, as long as the Human Race nurtured a few Battle-Saints and hid them in the Mysterious Underworld Cave, the Human Race would still be able to firmly control the Tower of Sin. All of the problems would be solved!
As for Zhao Fenqin and Zhou Zhuohe, their thoughts were deeper than Ling Yan's. If Luo Zheng left these two treasures behind, even after Luo Zheng left, the Heavenly Feather Holy Land would still be allies with the Purple Heart Holy Land!
Seeing the fiery gazes of Zhao Fenqin and Ling Yan, how could Luo Zheng not understand? Luo Zheng smiled and said, "Don't worry. If I leave the Sea God Continent, I will give these two treasures to Zhao Fenqin for safekeeping. The Human Race can share them with the Demon Night Clan."
When Zhao Fenqin, Zhou Zhuohe, and Ling Yan heard Luo Zheng's words, their faces immediately revealed joy. The value of Luo Zheng's promise was simply too great!
Without the obstruction of the demons, everyone's return speed was very fast. It didn't take long for them to arrive at the bottom of the Tower of Sin. Everyone circulated their True Essence and flew up around Luo Zheng, quickly arriving at the top of the Tower of Sin.
"The exit is closed." Ling Yan glanced at it and said.
This door was the exit at the top of the Tower of Sin. When the Mysterious Underworld Death Ray filled the entire Mysterious Underworld Cave, this exit would normally be closed. Very few people would choose to open it.
Luo Zheng moved towards the door, extended his fist, and pounded on it.
"Bang bang bang …"
At the same time, Meng Tian had just killed two Peak Battle-Saints of the Human Race. Many of the Peak Battle-Saints of the Human Race were preparing to fight to the death, and many of the Peak Battle-Saints of the Demon Race were also preparing to cleanse the Human Race.
At this moment, the sound of someone knocking on the door came from the top floor.
The many Peak Battle-Saints gathered at the top of the tower were suddenly stunned. Why was there still someone outside knocking on the door when the Mysterious Underworld Death Ray had already been activated?
A Peak Battle-Saint of the Human Race shouted in surprise, "Hurry up and open the door! It should be Zhao Fenqin and the others, no, it should be Luo Zheng! "
Another Peak Battle-Saint of the Human Race suddenly rushed over, preparing to climb up the stairs to open the door, but before he could even reach halfway, a fist light flashed in Meng Tian's hand, and directly smashed into that person's shoulder. With this punch, that Peak Battle-Saint flew out, vomiting blood all over the ground, and laid on the ground, unable to get up again.

Chapter 658
The atmosphere in the Sin Tower suddenly froze.
A trace of anticipation rose in the hearts of the humans, but at the same time, they were also very nervous.
When the Mysterious Underworld Death Ray illuminated the entire cave, all the warriors would die inside. There had never been an exception since the Sin Tower's existence! No peak War Reverend could withstand the Mysterious Underworld Death Ray.
However, in the hearts of these peak War Reverends, Luo Zheng was different. No one had been able to leave the word "Heaven Rank" on the Talent Stele before, and no one had been able to control the Sin Tower and control the power of the Sin Tower to kill people!
From the first day that Luo Zheng entered the Sin Tower, he had been constantly creating miracles!
Perhaps this time, he would be able to create another miracle.
As for the peak War Reverends of the Yao Ye Clan, such as Lord Mei, there was also a trace of joy on his face. This was because just yesterday, there was news from the Heavenly Feather Sacred Land that the King Statue still maintained its last trace of red light. That red light did not disappear, which meant that their Yao Ye Clan's King was still alive.
Based on this, it was very likely that Luo Zheng had returned. As for how Luo Zheng was able to travel through the Mysterious Underworld Cave and avoid the Mysterious Underworld Death Ray, Lord Mei did not know.
Meng Tian's face was like the winter sky. His face was filled with coldness as he coldly snorted and said, "No one is allowed to open the door. Perhaps some demons just happened to approach the door at the top of the tower!"
What Meng Tian said was not something that had never happened before. There were demons that approached the Sin Tower before. Many years ago, there was even an extremely powerful demon that crashed into the Sin Tower. Under the impact of that huge demon, the entire Sin Tower shook violently. Moreover, even the walls of the Sin Tower could not stop that powerful demon. Even the peak War Reverends at the top of the Sin Tower trembled in fear!
That time, it even caused panic in the Sin Tower. Many low level warriors fled the Sin Tower and only returned after a long time.
Fortunately, that powerful demon did not destroy the Sin Tower. After crashing into it a few times, it silently left.
"Right, it's impossible for a warrior to return at this time. It must be a demon!" A peak War Reverend of the Night Clan said with a sharp voice.
"Not necessarily." Lord Mei smiled coldly.
Meng Tian pointed at Lord Mei and said coldly, "Don't even think about opening the door!"
Madam Mei shrugged her delicate shoulders and said indifferently, "I won't open the door. If it's Luo Zheng who returns, he can naturally come in!"
Luo Zheng had the authority to control the Tower of Sin. Back then, when he was on the first floor of the Tower of Sin, he directly opened a crack on every floor of the Tower of Sin and directly fell down from the middle. He had done such an unimaginable thing, so how could a mere door stop Luo Zheng?
"Peng peng peng …"
The hurried and rhythmic knocking on the door continued. If it was a magical creature, it wouldn't be knocking on the door like this.
A Peak war elder of the Human Clan said softly: "It must be Luo Zheng! Why would a monster knock on the door like that? "
Another Peak war elder from the Human Clan said, "Let's wait and see! When Luo Zheng returns, I want to see how arrogant these Demon Clansmen can be! "
While everyone was silent, a one-meter-long crack suddenly appeared on the wall next to the top floor of the Sin Tower. Then, the crack opened like a big mouth, and a slightly thin figure swayed and drilled in.
When the Peak war elders of the Human Clan saw this person, their expressions suddenly became excited.
"Luo Zheng! It really is Luo Zheng! Luo Zheng is back! "
"And Zhao Fenqin, Zhou Zhuohe!"
"Ling Yan is also back …"
Not only their clansmen, but the people of the Demon Night Clan also revealed looks of joy. However, the most excited ones were the Peak war elders of the Human Clan. To them, this was already the end of their lives, but they didn't expect Luo Zheng to return at this time! If Luo Zheng returned to the night time, it was very likely that all the Peak war elders of the Human Clan would be destroyed by the Demon Clan!
How could these humans not be excited? Among them, there were many Peak war elders of the Human Clan who had tears in their eyes. After experiencing this kind of life and death situation, any warrior would be extremely excited.
Luo Zheng, Ling Yan, Zhao Fenqin, Zhou Zhuohe, as well as Yun Luo and the other two Peak war elders of the Demon Night Clan, entered through the crack.
Luo Zheng's gaze first fell on the corpses on the ground. There were a total of two corpses on the ground in the hall at the top of the tower. One was lying in the middle of the hall, while the other had fallen to the side of the wall. These two corpses were both Peak war elders of the Human Clan. The blood flowing out of the corpses had gradually faded and was absorbed by the Tower of Sin. Luo Zheng was very familiar with one of the Peak war elders. Previously, when he entered the Xuanming Cave, the Peak war elder had released raging flames to block the Tuna Bird's pursuit.
In addition, there was also a Peak war elder of the Human Clan lying on the ground, on the verge of death. He seemed to be seriously injured.
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng's face completely darkened. His eyes filled with killing intent slowly moved towards Meng Tian. Then, Luo Zheng sighed softly: "Didn't I say it before? As long as I see the Demon Clan appear in the Tower of Sin, I will kill them without mercy. Meng Tian, do you not have a brain, or … Is your memory bad? "
All the Peak war elders of the Demon Clan had ugly expressions on their faces. They did not expect that the Demon Clan would make such a dramatic comeback. When the most terrifying thing became reality, Meng Tian's face finally revealed panic, and he no longer had the domineering aura of a strategist!
When he first left the Tower of Sin, Meng Tian did not panic.
In Meng Tian's opinion, Luo Zheng would definitely enter the Xuanming Cave. Once he entered the Xuanming Cave, he would not be able to escape the pursuit of Meng Chong and Tian Hu. As long as he killed Luo Zheng, it would mean that the Demon Clan had made a comeback! The chances of a comeback were very high!
Therefore, Meng Tian did not think that he was driven away by Luo Zheng when he left the Tower of Sin last time. It was only to temporarily avoid Luo Zheng's edge. In Meng Tian's words, it was called a strategic retreat.
As the time of the Xuanming Cave's opening approached, Meng Tian became more and more expectant. His only worry was that Luo Zheng would not choose to enter the Xuanming Cave this year. After all, Luo Zheng's cultivation was not enough. According to the information obtained from the Night Clan, although Luo Zheng was already a Ninth Step Warrior and could fight with the Peak war elders on the fourteenth floor, his cultivation was still too weak. Maybe Luo Zheng would choose to enter the Xuanming Cave after becoming a war elder.
Meng Tian wasn't in a hurry. At most, he would wait for another year. With an extra year, Meng Tian and Tian Hu could continue to cultivate in the Tower of Sins. After becoming Battle-Saints, they could continue to make breakthroughs, increasing their chances of killing Luo Zheng.
When the Xuanming Cave opened, Meng Tian found out that Luo Zheng had entered the Tower of Sin without hesitation. He was excited for a few days and wondered if Meng Chong and the others had made a move.
In the end, the Xuanming Cave closed. Luo Zheng did not return and they lost contact with Meng Chong …
Although Meng Chong and Tian Hu had died, Meng Tian understood that this transaction was very worthwhile. At least Luo Zheng had died in the Xuanming Cave!
But until now, Meng Tian realized that he was wrong. He was too wrong!
This brat's luck was so strong that Meng Tian could not imagine it. Even such a method could not kill him!
"You, how did you come back …" Meng Tian asked in a deep voice.
Luo Zheng stood on the stairs and looked at Meng Tian with a cold gaze, "Why should I tell you?"
"Where's Meng Chong? Where's Tian Hu? "Meng Tian asked again.
At this time, Ling Yan sneered, "Meng Chong and Tian Hu are your hope? This is a good move, sending war saints to kill us. Unfortunately … "
"Unfortunately what?" Meng Tian asked.
"Unfortunately, they still died." Yun Luo said indifferently without any expression on her face.
Other than the demon race, the other races present did not know about this matter. Why did Meng Chong and Tian Hu, two peak war saints, enter the Xuanming Cave? And why did they chase after Luo Zheng? They were even war saints!
The peak war saints of the human race and the demon race all sucked in a breath of cold air.
"The demon race is still hiding secrets, secrets that we don't know. Last time, Tian Qiong and Tian Hui were also war saints!"
"This is not the main problem. The main problem is that Luo Zheng and the others managed to escape the pursuit of war saints. Furthermore, Meng Chong and the others died. This …"
"I wonder what else can stop Luo Zheng!"
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 659
Meng Tian let out a long sigh. He was not old. According to the calendar of the Sea God Continent, he was only around 60 years old. He was considered extremely young among the warriors.
But with this sigh, Meng Tian seemed to have aged a lot in an instant.
Luo Zheng looked at Meng Tian and said coldly: "You still haven't answered me. Is your memory bad?"
When Luo Zheng said this, many of the Peak War Reverends of the Demon Clan revealed expressions of fear.
Previously, Luo Zheng had said that as long as he saw the Demon Clansmen in the Tower of Sin, he would kill them all. Did he really plan to fulfill this promise?
Meng Tian hesitated for a moment, then said: "We will leave now."
"Leave?" Luo Zheng pointed at the corpse on the ground, and a cold smile appeared on his face, "After killing our Human Clan's Peak war elder, you chose to leave just like that?"
Meng Tian snorted: "What else do you want? Even if you can control the Tower of Sin, our Twin Devil Holy Land is not to be trifled with! "
"Hahaha!" Luo Zheng suddenly burst into hearty laughter, but there was no trace of a smile in his eyes. There was only endless killing intent. "Twin Devil Holy Land, what a great name!"
After Luo Zheng finished speaking, he was about to make a move, but at this moment, Ling Yan stopped him, "Luo Zheng, why don't we just let them go?"
The Peak War Reverends in front of him could be considered the backbone of the Demon Clan. In the future, War Saints and even Divine Sea Realm experts would be born from them. If Luo Zheng annihilated them all, it would indeed be a heavy blow to the Twin Devil Holy Land.
However, this method was equivalent to offending the Demon Clan. Ling Yan's starting point was to consider the overall situation. If the Twin Devil Holy Land was really forced into a corner and fought to the death, it was very likely that the Human Clan's Purple Heart Holy Land would be razed to the ground!
"I know what you're thinking," Luo Zheng said to Ling Yan: "But today, all the Demon Clansmen will die!"
With that, Luo Zheng jumped down from the stairs and sped towards Meng Tian!
"Brat, how dare you!" A Peak war elder of the Demon Clan could not bear it any longer and took the lead to rush towards Luo Zheng. Under the eruption of the Astral Essence in his body, he was like a rapidly moving hill, carrying the power of ten thousand men, fiercely crashing towards Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng waved his finger lightly, and in an instant, an invisible force enveloped the Peak war elder of the Demon Clan, and the sound of cloth tearing could be heard.
"Cough cough …"
The Peak war elder of the Demon Clan was instantly torn into pieces, and brown blood splattered all over the ground.
"I'll use your bodies to feed the Tower of Sin!" Luo Zheng's face was ferocious, and his whole body was overflowing with boundless killing intent. He was like a God of Death that had descended to the world.
Seeing that Luo Zheng no longer had any misgivings, Meng Tian's complexion changed. He said to all the Devil clansmen, "Retreat, leave Sin Tower!"
Seeing that the situation was not good, many of the Peak War Elder s of the Demon Clan rushed towards the back. That was the stairs that led back to the fourteenth floor, how fast was the Peak War Elder? These dozens of Peak War Elder s of the Demon Clan rushed towards the stairs in an instant.
However, Luo Zheng immediately jumped up, and a crack appeared on the ceiling above his head. Luo Zheng immediately jumped up from the crack, and blocked the entrance to the fourteenth floor. Today, he would not let any of these Demon Clan s go!
The Peak War Elder s of the Demon Clan had already climbed up to the fourteenth floor from the top of the tower, but just as they climbed up, they saw Luo Zheng blocking the way. All of the Peak War Elder s of the Demon Clan who were like small mountains revealed expressions of fear, as if Luo Zheng was a human-shaped demon.
"Fight it out with him! In the end, he's just a war general! "
"Kill him!" It's all death! "A few Peak War Elder s of the Demon Clan roared.
Following that, the Peak War Elder s of the Demon Clan rushed forward, their towering bodies were like small mountains, pressing down on Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng only raised his finger slightly, and an invisible force instantly tore the Peak War Elder at the front into pieces. The corpses of the Demon Clan s were scattered all over the steps of the Tower of Sin, while Luo Zheng stepped on the blood-stained steps and walked up.
"Demon, he's a demon …" The Peak War Elder s of the Demon Clan realized that they were far from being Luo Zheng's match, so they retreated with all their might, running towards the top of the tower. But how could Luo Zheng give them the chance to leave? He chased after them, activating the power of the artifact spirit in the Tower of Sin, and quickly killed these Peak War Elder s!
"Ahh …"
"Howl!"
All sorts of miserable screams mixed together, and the remains of the corpses flew in all directions. The steps seemed to have become a terrifying Asura Field.
The many War Elder s on the fourteenth floor were used to seeing all sorts of bloody scenes. When they saw this scene, the faces of the many creatures watching from the back all turned pale. Once upon a time, how envious were these creatures of the Demon Clan? They even regretted not being born in the Demon Clan. They could only resent their own race for not being strong enough.
But at this moment, they were rejoicing that they were not a member of the Demon Clan. Otherwise, they would have faced such a cruel massacre!
Luo Zheng slowly entered the top of the tower step by step, but Meng Tian's figure had disappeared from the top of the tower.
"He entered the small world?" Luo Zheng scanned the area and did not find Meng Tian's figure. There were 128 Minor Chiliocosms at the top of the pagoda. Therefore, Luo Zheng determined that Meng Tian had escaped into one of the Minor Chiliocosms.
Yun Luo waved his hand and pointed to the exit of the Tower of Sin that led to the Profound Nether Cave. "Meng Tian rushed out."
"He rushed out?" Luo Zheng's face flashed with surprise, "Aren't you afraid that the Profound Nether Death Ray will shoot him to death?"
Even so, Luo Zheng did not give up the chase. The Demon Clan was able to break through in the Tower of Sin and enter the Battle-Saint realm. The secret must be at the bottom of the Tower of Sin. As for what the secret was, Luo Zheng did not know, but he knew that Meng Tian would not commit suicide!
So Luo Zheng poured his True Yuan into the pendant on his chest, and an orange protective cover appeared around Luo Zheng's body. Then, he walked up the stairs and entered the Profound Nether Cave.
When Luo Zheng went out, Yun Luo and Ling Yan also followed, including Zhao Fenqin and Zhou Zhuohe. At the same time, Xun also appeared beside Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng was an invincible existence in the Tower of Sin, but outside the Tower of Sin, it would not be easy for Luo Zheng to face Meng Tian alone. If Meng Tian did not commit suicide and really had a way to protect himself, then Luo Zheng would be in trouble!
Outside the Tower of Sin, a strong Profound Nether Death Ray spread out. This kind of fatal ray could shatter one's True Yuan protection in a few breaths of time, and then completely annihilate a living being.
After reaching the top of the Tower of Sin, Luo Zheng flew around the Tower of Sin, and a trace of suspicion appeared on his face. He really did not discover Meng Tian's existence.
Meng Tian could not have gone far. He could not withstand the Profound Nether Death Ray's radiation. Thinking of this, Luo Zheng closed his eyes, and gradually released his perception, slowly investigating the surrounding environment.
Soon, Luo Zheng's face revealed a trace of a sneer. He took out a high-grade Saint level longsword from his spatial ring, and slowly approached in a certain direction!
Zhao Fenqin, Ling Yan, and the others looked at each other. Did Luo Zheng really discover something?
Just as Luo Zheng was slowly approaching that place, a tall and sturdy figure suddenly drilled out from the side of the Tower of Sin. It was Meng Tian who was hiding here!
Meng Tian roared, "Luo Zheng, since you want to die, then die with me!"
At this time, above Meng Tian's head, above his arms, a dazzling light flashed. Meng Tian had chosen to ignite his spiritual altar!
Martial artists who cultivated the True Yuan system could use their dantian to self-destruct, and fight to perish together. Body refiners could ignite their spiritual altars. After igniting their spiritual altars in a short period of time, the eruption of True Yuan could reach a terrifying degree!
However, this kind of burning was also an irreversible action. Once the spiritual altar was ignited, it was impossible to reconstruct it!
As a body refiner, Meng Tian had already opened three spiritual altars. The power of this burning was indeed vast. Besides Luo Zheng, the complexions of Ling Yan and the others all changed.
"Attack!"
Ling Yan, Zhao Fenqin, Zhou Zhuohe, Yun Luo, and Xun all launched their attacks at the same time!
Originally, Ling Yan's strength alone was comparable to Meng Tian's. However, under Meng Tian's desperate attacks, his aura soared and his strength also increased by several times. He even relied on his own strength to dissolve the combined attacks of the few of them.

Chapter 660
Looking at Meng Tian's ferocious expression, a thought flashed through everyone's mind. This guy was really crazy!
Facing the crazy Meng Tian, Ling Yan and the others kept retreating, and Luo Zheng also retreated.
In this way, Meng Tian was always under the illumination of the Mysterious Underworld Death Ray!
Although this was the outer area of the Mysterious Underworld Cave and was very far from the giant scorpion, the power of the Mysterious Underworld Death Ray was far from what a peak war reverend could block.
Moreover, in this state of madness, Meng Tian did not even circulate his Shield of Great Essence. He only wanted to fight Luo Zheng with his life on the line. Even the three spiritual altars in his body had been ignited, so how could he care about his own life? Meng Tian had no intention of living at all!
Luo Zheng retreated at an extremely fast speed, and everyone followed closely behind Luo Zheng. As for Meng Tian, the moment he rushed out of the secret passage where he was hiding, under the illumination of the Mysterious Underworld Death Ray, the skin on his entire body began to be burned layer by layer!
As he continued to sprint, the skin on his body was burned layer by layer, followed by his flesh and blood. His originally tall and sturdy figure became smaller bit by bit, and the originally rough and violent face finally revealed its facial bones, like a skeleton!
"Ah ah …"
As Meng Tian roared in pain, his attacks became fiercer and fiercer, and he even rushed into the orange-yellow light shield.
At this moment, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed, and the long sword in his hand suddenly burst out!
"Formless!"
The so-called formless did not mean that there were no moves, but the moves that were used were completely under Luo Zheng's control, and he could use them freely!
Luo Zheng's sword contained an extremely terrifying power. The raging sword intent roared out and collided with Meng Tian's fist.
"Ge ge ge …"
Meng Tian's body was almost completely burned by the Mysterious Underworld Death Ray. The skin and flesh on his hands had all disappeared, leaving only the black bones of his hands. When his fist collided with Luo Zheng's sword light, his bones were constantly disintegrating and shattering!
"Die!"
Luo Zheng did not stop at all. The power of the dragon scales in his body suddenly erupted, and this sword forced Meng Tian out of the light shield!
Meng Tian fell backward with his head raised. His eyes were filled with unwillingness and helplessness. He had practically lost the entire battle! Like a snowman exposed to the scorching sun, Meng Tian finally turned into a pile of white bones and fell to the bottom of the Dark Nether Cave …
Standing inside the orange-yellow light barrier, Luo Zheng looked down in a daze. It had to be said that the demon race was indeed a fighting race. Meng Tian's plan was indeed very thorough. Unfortunately, Meng Tian had underestimated the complexity of the Mysterious Underworld Cave, and even more so, underestimated his own strength.
"So that's how it is," Ling Yan stared at the side of the Tower of Sin, "It seems that the Demon Race had long discovered that there was a secret passage here that could be used to hide."
Zhao Fenqin said, "So the two Battle-Saints, Tian Hui and Tian Qiong, were hiding here to cultivate. Meng Chong and Tian Hu should be hiding here as well."
Luo Zheng faintly smiled, "In the future, you can arrange for people to hide here as well!"
Whether it was using the crab-like accessory on Luo Zheng's chest or hiding in this secret passage, they were all feasible plans. In other words, the human race and the Demon Night Clan could send more peak Battle-Saints into the Mysterious Underworld Cave.
However, breaking through in the Mysterious Underworld Cave came with a price. They would never be able to leave the Mysterious Underworld Cave, and could only move around in the Tower of Sin for a short period of time. If they were to return to the Mysterious Underworld Cave again, they would not be able to obtain a truly great opportunity in the Mysterious Underworld Cave. Perhaps they would be able to open up a new path. This was destined to be an extremely difficult path!
However, for the sake of the entire race, someone was destined to be sacrificed.
Luo Zheng and the others once again returned to the top of the Tower of Sin. As soon as they entered, they saw the peak Battle-Saints of the Night Clan being controlled by the Demon Night Clan and the human race.
Luo Zheng indifferently glanced at the peak Battle-Saints of the Night Clan and then said indifferently, "It's time to clean up the Night Clan."
Half a year ago, Luo Zheng had expelled the Devil Race, but he did not expel the Night Clan. In the Mysterious Underworld Cave, Meng Chong was able to find him so smoothly because of the Night Clan. Furthermore, when the Devil Race returned, the Night Clan stood firmly on the side of the Devil Race. The Night Clan could not be left alive!
Following that, the Tower of Sin underwent another round of cleansing. All of the Night Clan warriors were wiped out.
The news of all the peak Battle-Saints of the Devil Race being killed by Luo Zheng swept through the entire Sea God Continent like a storm. At the same time, the Sky Feather Sacred Land and the Purple Heart Sacred Land once again entered a state of alert, guarding against any sudden attacks from the Devil Race.
After all, all of the peak Battle-Saints of the Devil Race being killed was a fatal blow to the Twin Devil Sacred Land. After all, all of the peak Battle-Saints of the Devil Race were the geniuses of their race. Almost all of the future experts of the Devil Race were born from these peak Battle-Saints. This matter could very well anger the Twin Devil Sacred Land and cause them to fight back with all their might!
But in the end, the Twin Devil Sacred Land chose to remain silent. They did not have the courage to start a Holy Land War. The Devil Race had already lost the Tower of Sin. If they fought against the Demon Night Clan and the Human Race, the Human Race and the Demon Night Clan would have to pay a considerable price. However, the Devil Race would suffer heavy losses and would never be able to rise again!
After all, the Demon Night Clan and the Human Race had already firmly controlled the Tower of Sin. As long as they were not exterminated, they would be able to quickly recover. However, the Devil Race did not have such an opportunity.
But the Devil Race's compromise was also only a way to prolong their feeble existence. After losing the Tower of Sin, the Devil Race's decline was already destined; it was only a matter of time. As for the Devil Race's vassal Night Clan, they also faced a similar situation. The Night Clan didn't even have the chance to resist. The Night Clan excelled in concealment and assassination. At most, they could organize several meaningless assassinations. If they truly started a Holy Land War, then even resisting the Purple Heart Holy Land would be difficult.
During the remaining time, Luo Zheng continued to cultivate in the Tower of Sin. Occasionally, he would enter the cursed lands to communicate with the stars.
As a transcendent divine might, the power of the Stellar War Body was beyond doubt. But, the greatest difficulty of cultivating it was in stimulating the power of the stars. The more stars Luo Zheng could stimulate, the more terrifying the power of the Stellar War Body would become. But, wanting to stimulate the power of the stars wasn't easy at all. Thus, the first time that Luo Zheng had cultivated the Stellar War Body and stimulated a star, that had already left Red Dragon dumbfounded!
However, Luo Zheng failed several times. It was common for a martial artist to encounter failure in their cultivation, so Luo Zheng didn't care too much about it. From the first day that Luo Zheng stepped onto the road of martial arts, he was well aware that this road was destined to be filled with difficulties. Even if he was a person with a great destiny, it was impossible for him to soar to the highest heavens. He had to take one step at a time. As for whether or not he could reach the peak, that was up to the heavens!
Three months later, at the Heavenly Eagle Prairie, several white clouds hung high in the blue skies. Occasionally, a gentle breeze would blow through the air. Although the sun was bright, it wasn't too strong. Lying flat on the lush grass, one could feel a sense of tranquility!
Over a thousand tents were erected on the grassland. A few muscular human men were sitting on tall horses, driving sheep with half of their shoulders bared.
Ever since the demon race showed signs of weakness, the demon race's forces had begun to rapidly shrink. The Heavenly Eagle Prairie had originally been divided into three parts, with the demon race, the demon race, and the human race each occupying a third of the area. But now, the entire Heavenly Eagle Prairie had been reduced to a pasture for the human race!
The herdsmen in the surroundings began to slowly gather together, forming a large nomadic clan. There were also martial artists in this nomadic clan. If one had to give this nomadic clan a grade, then this nomadic clan would be equivalent to a second-grade sect.
Suddenly, several dozen streaks of light shot through the sky.
The flocks of sheep below could feel the pressure spreading from the sky, and they began to flee in all directions. The herdsmen began to shout, trying to gather the flocks of sheep together.
In the pasture, a little girl looked up at the sky, her black eyes wide open. She wasn't afraid of the dazzling sunlight, and after staring at the sky for a while, her face suddenly lit up with excitement. She immediately turned around and ran back, shouting towards a tent not too far away, "Father, Father, Tuturu, Tuturu, it's the True Divinity, the True Divinity has come!"
A youth suddenly rushed out of the tent. As he heard the little girl's shouts, his face was filled with excitement. In two years, that little boy had grown into a tall and handsome youth!

Chapter 661
When Luo Zheng took Tuturu and his group of humans from the pasture and handed them over to the human forces, this group of humans quickly integrated with the other ordinary herdsmen.
Tuturu had now become a martial artist, and he had impressively stepped into the Bone Refining Stage.
However, this group of human herdsmen had never forgotten Luo Zheng. Although they already knew that Luo Zheng was actually a martial artist, these herdsmen were no longer muddle-headed after interacting with the other herdsmen. They understood the world of martial artists and the situation of the entire Sea God Continent!
Although they understood that Luo Zheng was not what they thought and was a True God-like existence, in their hearts, Luo Zheng was still a True God sent by the heavens to save them!
If it wasn't for Luo Zheng, they would still be reared in captivity by the Demon Race and become cattle and sheep in the Demon Race's pasture.
"Where is he? Tell me quickly!" Tuturu shook the little girl, his eyes full of panic. When the other herdsmen heard the little girl's words, they all came out of their tents.
Tuturu was already a Bone Refining Stage martial artist. How strong was the strength of his hands? Being violently shaken by Luo Zheng like this, the little girl's face suddenly turned red.
"Tuturu, don't pinch her!" A herdsmen scolded, "She can't speak like this!"
Tuturu realized that he was too agitated. He quickly stuck out his tongue and released the little girl's hands.
The little girl glared at Tuturu. At this time, she was too lazy to argue with Tuturu. She immediately pointed to a direction on the horizon and said, "He went that way!"
The direction the little girl pointed to was the place where Luo Zheng had appeared. It was not far from their pasture. Although the entire Sky Eagle Prairie now belonged to the human race, Tuturu and this group of herdsmen still deliberately avoided the former Demon Race pasture. After all, the Demon Race pasture was a nightmare to them.
"Whoosh!"
As soon as the little girl pointed out the direction, Tutulu shot out like a sharp arrow. After stepping into the Bone Refining Stage, he could already control his own bones to the smallest detail. He ran along the undulating grassland with the agility of a gray wolf.
Seeing this, the other human herdsmen also walked out of their tents. That year, they did not even have the time to thank the True God for saving their lives!
These ranches belonged to the Purple Heart Holy Land, and Luo Zheng's name was known to almost everyone on the Sea God Continent. These herdsmen were also able to learn about Luo Zheng from the mouths of some cultivators. However, these herdsmen understood that with their status, it would be extremely difficult for them to meet Luo Zheng. At the very least, they would never be able to set foot in the legendary Tower of Sin.
Even for a martial artist like Tutu Lu, there was no chance at all! After all, the Sin Tower was the place where all the geniuses of the Sea God Continent gathered. Even a Mortal Rank Genius was considered the lowest level of existence in the Sin Tower, not to mention that the vast majority of cultivators couldn't even be called "geniuses"!
Right now, this was probably their only chance to see Luo Zheng.
Liu Yu, who was wearing a purple robe, slowly descended from the sky. His handsome face glanced at the grassland. How sensitive were the senses of a Spirit Sea Realm warrior? Although the giant transmission array was hidden beneath the grassland, Liu Yu instantly found it.
She stretched out her hand and pointed, saying, "The transmission array is here."
Luo Zheng's eyes fell on a large pit to the side. This large pit was where Luo Zheng had fallen. During this time, the large pit was covered with weeds, but the destruction caused by the impact was still vivid in his mind.
"Here, right?" The leader of the Purple Heart Holy Land, Situ Haotian, directly landed on the grassland. He wore a soft robe, and under the gentle breeze, the brocade belt behind his back fluttered. He truly looked like a celestial being.
Situ Haotian suddenly clapped his hands and slammed them towards the ground!
"Pop!"
With Situ Haotian as the center, the surrounding thousand feet of grass suddenly rolled up, as if a complete layer of skin had been peeled off from an animal's body. As the grass continued to roll up, a giant transmission array was revealed below.
Seeing this scene, the deep-sea experts of the Purple Heart Holy Land and the Sky Feather Holy Land slowly descended from the sky.
In addition to the Spirit Sea Realm warriors, there were many Battle-Saints, Ling Yan, Xia Shuang, Yun Luo, and many other human warriors. Today was the day Luo Zheng would leave.
To be personally escorted by the leaders of the two Holy Lands, in the entire Sea God Continent, Luo Zheng was probably the only one who received such treatment.
The Tower of Sin was a foregone conclusion. The Demon Night Clan and the Human Race each had two peak Battle-Saints. Zhao Fenqin of the Human Race and Lord Mei of the Demon Night Clan had both become Battle-Saints. The Demon Night Clan and the Human Race were both planning when to explore the Mysterious Nether Cave again.
Originally, Zhao Fenqin had strongly invited Luo Zheng to stay and at least leave after exploring the Mysterious Nether Cave. However, Luo Zheng knew that he didn't have much time left. In the past few months of cultivation, Luo Zheng had already broken through to the Battle-Saint realm and become a Battle-Saint!
If Luo Zheng was given more time, it wouldn't be too difficult for him to cultivate to the peak Battle-Saint realm through the Tower of Good Fortune. The problem was that Luo Zheng didn't have enough time.
When he left the Central Domain, Luo Zheng knew that he didn't have much time left. Even now, Luo Zheng's heart was still hanging in the air. No one knew when the next Extreme Violet Yin Physique would appear. Luo Zheng prayed that Cui Xie wouldn't find the next Extreme Violet Yin Physique cultivator so soon.
Situ Haotian cupped his hands towards Luo Zheng and said, "Even if we send you off a thousand miles, we will have to part ways in the end. Young Hero Luo Zheng, we can only send you off here today."
Luo Zheng had appeared out of nowhere on the Sea God Continent. With his strength alone, he had reversed the entire human race's position. The one who benefited the most from this was naturally the Purple Heart Holy Land led by Situ Haotian. Situ Haotian was brimming with gratitude. Situ Haotian knew that he couldn't persuade Luo Zheng to stay, so he could only personally leave the Purple Heart Holy Land and send Luo Zheng off. It must be known that on the Sea God Continent, Battle-Saints and above didn't use teleportation arrays. Situ Haotian had truly traveled a great distance to come here.
As for Liu Yu and the other Yao Ye Clan cultivators, they suddenly knelt on one knee and said to Xun, who was beside Luo Zheng, "My King, we hope that you will return to the throne as soon as possible! If there is any danger after we part, even if we are thousands of miles away, my Heavenly Feather Holy Land will come out in full force! "
Liu Yu's words weren't polite at all. After learning that Luo Zheng was about to leave, the Heavenly Feather Holy Land had created a sound transmitting talisman. One end of this sound transmitting talisman was attached to Xun's king statue while the other end was in Luo Zheng's hand.
If Xun needed the help of the Heavenly Feather Holy Land, they could even abandon everything and enter this giant transmission array.
Xun nodded and indifferently said, "I understand."
Yun Luo's nine-colored dress fluttered in the wind. She blinked and finally said to Luo Zheng, "I hope that your choice is the right one. We will meet again one day!"
The Heavenly Dao fragment was a matter of great importance. Even now, Yun Luo wasn't sure if giving it to Luo Zheng was the right choice. However, although Yun Luo's standpoint was different from Tian Miao's, she understood that giving the Heavenly Dao fragment to a participant of the Great Era Struggle was indeed appropriate. It was just that there were still serious disputes between the several Empyreans of the upper realms. She was worried that Luo Zheng would stand on the opposite side of her.
Yun Luo cultivated the Million Spirit Distraction Art. Her main body had already stood at the peak of the upper realms. As long as Luo Zheng continued walking down the path of martial artists, it was only a matter of time before he encountered Yun Luo.
Luo Zheng silently nodded.
Then, the two peak War Reverends continuously took out top grade true essence stones and began to place them in the giant transmission array. The amount of top grade true essence stones required to construct such a giant transmission array was not a small amount. This cost was naturally borne by the Purple Heart Holy Land.
At this time, a figure appeared on the other end of the grassland. This figure was actually running towards Luo Zheng at an extremely fast speed. It was a human youth, and behind this youth were over a hundred herdsmen.
"Mm?" Ling Yan looked over and discovered that it was only a human martial artist at the Bone Refining stage. Her vigilance gradually dissipated.
"Stop them," Situ Haotian lightly said. At this time, how could they be willing to be disturbed by these herdsmen?
Several peak War Reverends were about to step forward to intercept them, but Luo Zheng waved his hand and said, "No need, let them come over."
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 662
The newcomer was the young man from the ranch, Tutu Lu.
When Luo Zheng had just arrived on the Sea God Continent, none of the herdsmen dared to resist. Only this young man named Tutu Lu stood bravely beside Luo Zheng, wanting to resist the Devil Race.
With Tuturu's strength, resistance was just a joke. However, it took a lot of courage to stand up in that kind of situation. At least Tuturu dared to stand up, while the other humans could only lock themselves in a corner and tremble.
Tutu Lu couldn't see the cultivation of these people in front of him. He heard that after becoming a warrior, one could fly in the air, and these people were at least a warrior! But Tutu Lu also knew that his estimation was not accurate. There were warriors in the nomadic tribe he was in now, but the momentum of those warriors was far inferior to the people in front of him.
Maybe the weakest among them was a warrior! Tutu Lu analyzed in his heart.
After he took the lead to rush up, his bright eyes stared firmly at Luo Zheng, and then he suddenly knelt on the ground with a plop. "I, I am also a warrior now. Please, please accept me as your disciple!"
Tutu Lu's frank request made many peak war reverends, including Situ Haotian and Liu Yu, laugh. In their opinion, this human youth was simple and cute.
Upon hearing Tutulu's request, Luo Zheng shook his head slightly and said, "I'm sorry, I don't accept disciples. Moreover … I'm about to leave this place. Your cultivation base is too low to pass through such a long distance Teleportation Formation."
Tutu Lu was more persistent than Luo Zheng imagined. He knelt on the ground and continued, "I will follow you, true god, even if I have to sacrifice my life."
"Hehe, this kid is so determined!" A peak war reverend said lightly.
In fact, when Luo Zheng first came to the Sea God Continent, Tutu Lu had already begged Luo Zheng once, but Luo Zheng refused. His current situation was not suitable for him to establish a sect, so naturally, he couldn't accept disciples. This time, Luo Zheng still couldn't agree.
But now, it seemed that if Luo Zheng didn't agree, this kid didn't intend to get up and kept kneeling on the ground.
At this time, a Peak war elder from the Human Clan suddenly said to Tutu Lu, "If he doesn't want to take you as his disciple, how about I take you as my disciple?"
Tutu Lu was only at the Bone Refining Realm. With this kind of strength, he did not have the qualifications to join the Purple Heart Holy Land. It would be difficult for him to even pass the most simple entrance examination. To many people, having a Venerable as their master was something that would make them smile in their dreams!
This Peak War Reverend of the Human Race clearly wanted to curry favor with Luo Zheng. Although Luo Zheng had left, with Luo Zheng's influence in the Purple Heart Sacred Land, there would definitely be benefits for him to do so. Moreover, what if Luo Zheng returned to the Sea God Continent one day?
Unexpectedly, Tutulu raised his head, rolled his eyes, and shook his head again. "No, I want to worship the True God as my master!"
Everyone's face revealed a surprised expression. They didn't expect this kid to be so persistent. He didn't even want to accept a Peak War Reverend as his disciple.
Luo Zheng still shook his head. "I said, I'm leaving this place. Even if I'm willing to take you in, I won't give you any pointers or benefits."
"But …" Tutu Lu raised his head and looked at Luo Zheng, as if he was searching for an excuse in his mind.
At this time, Situ Haotian laughed. "How about this, how about I take you in as my disciple?"
The moment Situ Haotian opened his mouth, many Peak War Reverends' faces changed! Situ Haotian rarely accepted disciples. Even Xia Shuang, the most outstanding genius of the Purple Heart Sacred Land, still didn't become Situ Haotian's disciple. Even if this kid's talent was ten times stronger, he still wasn't qualified to become Situ Haotian's disciple!
How sharp were everyone's eyes? In their opinion, a Bone Refining stage's cultivation wasn't a problem. No matter how low one's cultivation was, as long as one had potential, their future growth wouldn't be slow. As long as they entered the Tower of Sin, their cultivation would soar like a rocket. However, this kid called Tutulu, whether it was his talent or his root bone, was the worst choice. This was the biggest problem.
In fact, Situ Haotian was originally joking. Seeing how determined Tutulu was, he would definitely be determined to take Luo Zheng as his master! After all, with this Bone Refining stage kid, it was impossible to tell who was strong and who was weak! In Tutulu's eyes, Situ Haotian and the Peak War Reverend were probably similar existences.
However, Situ Haotian had clearly underestimated Tutulu.

Tutulu's eyes were indeed unable to distinguish between Situ Haotian and the Peak War Reverend's strength, but he knew how to read people's expressions! When the Peak War Reverend spoke, no one's expression changed. However, when this middle-aged man in a Daoist robe spoke, everyone's expression changed. He understood that this person in front of him must be an amazing character! However, Tutulu didn't think that Situ Haotian was the leader of the entire Purple Heart Holy Land!
Hearing Tutulu agree, everyone laughed. Liu Yu said with a faint smile, "Situ Haotian, it seems you can't go back on your words!"
Situ Haotian faintly smiled. "Of course I won't go back on my words." He then said to Tutulu, "Becoming my disciple will be very difficult. You must mentally prepare yourself."
If the geniuses of the Purple Heart Holy Land saw that Situ Haotian had accepted a Bone Refining stage youth as his direct disciple because of a joke, they would probably faint on the ground!
In fact, Xia Shuang, Xing Hui, and the others were already about to faint!
When Xia Shuang was the number one genius of the Purple Heart Holy Land, she had asked two Elders of the Purple Heart Holy Land to intercede for her, hoping that she could become Situ Haotian's disciple! Xia Shuang was the most talented martial artist of the Purple Heart Holy Land. Even so, she had still been rejected by Situ Haotian.
One could imagine just how surprised Xia Shuang and the others were at this time!
At this time, over a hundred herdsmen also hurried over. These herdsmen certainly weren't as bold as Tutulu. They stopped far away and then prostrated themselves on the ground, kowtowing to Luo Zheng. It was clear that their gratitude towards Luo Zheng was sincere and sincere.
At this time, the giant transmission array was finally finished with the top grade true essence stones. When all of the top grade true essence stones were placed in place, a blazing white light split the transmission array in two, forming a massive pattern. A blazing white light shot into the sky; this massive transmission array had finally opened.
"It's time to leave," Luo Zheng lightly said. Then, Xun's figure turned into a little Sword Spirit that drilled into Luo Zheng's body. Luo Zheng turned around and said his goodbyes to everyone, then stepped into the transmission array.
Seeing Luo Zheng's figure disappear into the blazing white light, Xia Shuang's tender eyes suddenly turned red.
She might have many things she wanted to say to Luo Zheng, but at this time, it was already an extravagant request for her to send Luo Zheng off. How could she possibly have a chance to speak with Luo Zheng alone? Moreover, even if she wanted to say something, where would she start? Was she supposed to open her mouth and say that she wanted to follow Luo Zheng into the transmission array? This was obviously impossible.
The light of the transmission array extended all the way into the sky, drawing a parabola in the sky before finally disappearing into the horizon.
Situ Haotian looked at the light and said, "Where does this transmission array lead to?"
Liu Yu faintly said, "Perhaps, in the future, we may also be able to enter this transmission array!"
The Sea God Continent was actually a very barren continent. In terms of richness, it was far inferior to the Central Domain. Even its area was slightly smaller. However, the Sea God Continent had given birth to over a dozen Holy Lands while the Central Domain didn't have a single Holy Land. The reason for this was because of the Tower of Sin.
It was precisely because of the existence of the Tower of Sin and the Dark Nether Cave that so many Holy Lands existed on the Sea God Continent.

Chapter 663
The turbulent sea water kept hitting the reefs on the shore. The Chaotic Astral Sea was chaotic most of the year, and it had been like this since ancient times.
There were countless ferocious beasts lurking in this sea area. Not to mention ordinary fishermen, even warriors would not dare to rashly enter this sea area. Only true powerhouses would dare to explore this sea area. After all, there were many unknown treasures hidden in the Chaotic Astral Ocean. Every year, countless powerhouses would fall in this sea area.
The Fish Dragon Sect was a third-grade sect located in the Easternmost Land. The Fish Dragon Sect was backed by the entire Chaotic Astral Sea. Compared with other sects, this third-grade sect obviously knew much more about the Chaotic Astral Sea.
This sect had circled several sea areas near the mainland. They knew very well that although the Chaotic Astral Sea was very dangerous, these sea areas were relatively safe. At least there were few powerful ferocious beasts. Therefore, every year at the beginning of spring, the Fish Dragon Sect would send their disciples to the surrounding sea areas for training!
The mission was to hunt a kind of demonic beast in the water called "Black and Blue Mussel". This kind of Black and Blue Mussel was a fourth-grade demonic beast. Its strength was not strong, but its defensive power was quite terrifying. It was not easy to break the shell of the Black and Blue Mussel.
After killing the Black and Blue Mussel, there was a certain probability of finding the treasure "Black Spirit Pearl" in its body. It was a very precious material for refining elixirs. It was very popular in many markets in the Central Domain, and was often in short supply. It was also one of the main sources of income for the Fish Dragon Sect!
In addition to the Black Spirit Pearl, the meat of the Black and Blue Mussel was also a delicious delicacy. After being caught, dried and sold to the Central Domain, it was also very valuable.
Today was the day for the disciples of the Fish Dragon Sect to train. Tens of thousands of disciples in the Divine Reflection Realm flew along the sea level.
"Senior Brother Lang! Our mission today is to dig out ten Black Spirit Pearls. If we can't complete the mission, we will be punished again. "The one who spoke was a man in black clothes. On the right side of his face was a tattoo of a black scorpion that emitted a gloomy aura.
Even in the surrounding sea area, it was still quite dangerous for warriors of the Divine Reflection Realm. Therefore, when the disciples of the Fish Dragon Sect entered the Chaotic Astral Sea to hunt Black-Green Mussels, they usually traveled in groups of three to five.
The five of them formed a team and began to hunt the Black-Green Mussels. However, compared to the other disciples of the Fish Dragon Sect, the five of them had much stronger killing intent.
"Our luck is pretty good," the cultivator named 'Lang' said, his eyes flashing as he pointed to the sea below. The other two looked at each other, then simultaneously took out a Water Repelling Pill and swallowed it. After taking the Water Repelling Pill, an invisible membrane would appear on the cultivator's body, allowing them to temporarily avoid the seawater. However, it could only be used in shallow waters. In the deep sea, this membrane would be crushed by the seawater.
"Plop!"
"Plop!"
Two voices sounded like flowing water. Two Divine Reflection Realm martial artists rushed into the sea, one on the left and one on the right.
The five of them had a clear division of labor and cooperated with each other with ease. Obviously, this was not the first time they worked together. In fact, the five of them were originally independent martial artists, and they were five pirates in the east of the Central Domain. No less than a hundred warriors had died in their hands!
Right now, the situation in the entire Central Region was changing unpredictably. Many sects seemed to have joined forces to deal with independent cultivators. The lives of these five people were getting more and more difficult. If they were not careful, they might be killed by a powerhouse from a large sect!
Forced to do so, the five of them finally decided to wash their hands and join the Fish Dragon Sect.
The Fish Dragon Sect was relatively weak among the third-grade sects. Because of its remote location, it had not developed much over the years. Because of the situation in the Central Domain, a large number of independent martial artists came to join the Fish Dragon Sect.
Although accepting too many independent martial artists was a hidden danger, these independent martial artists were not loyal to the Fish Dragon Sect. Once something happened, these independent martial artists would be the first to betray the Fish Dragon Sect.
However, independent martial artists already had experience and cultivation, so the Fish Dragon Sect did not need to spend much effort to cultivate them. They could also help the Fish Dragon Sect create a lot of wealth. Therefore, the Fish Dragon Sect chose to turn a blind eye. As long as the independent martial artists were not too notorious, the Fish Dragon Sect would accept them!
Because of this, the number of disciples participating in the sea area training this year suddenly increased by several times, which really benefited the Fish Dragon Sect a lot.
Under the cooperation of the five Divine Reflection Realm martial artists, the Black-Cyan Mussel under the sea had nowhere to hide. Even the defense it was proud of was easily broken through.
After one of the martial artists used a long sword to push the Black-Cyan Mussel away, his face lit up with joy. "Senior Brother Wolf, we are lucky today. We found a Black Spirit Pearl in the first Black-Cyan Mussel!"
"Yes." The martial artist named Wolf nodded lightly, without any joy.
In his opinion, the Fish Dragon Sect was not a good thing. They took advantage of the situation to crazily recruit independent martial artists. On the surface, they were recruiting disciples, but in fact, they were using these independent martial artists as coolies! What bullsh * t sea training? In fact, it was a disguised form of extortion for these independent martial artists.
In fact, in an era when independent martial artists were in danger, the Fish Dragon Sect provided a shelter for them. Now, letting them "train" was actually equivalent to collecting protection fees!
They had to pay ten Black Spirit Pearls to the Fish Dragon Sect every day. This task was indeed a bit heavy, but it was better than being killed by those strong martial artists.
After the five people got the first Black Spirit Pearl, they continued to rush into the depths of the sea area. Although there was not much danger in the coastal waters, the five people were still very careful. They had been pirates before and knew that there were all kinds of strange things in the Chaotic Astral Sea. If a fierce sea beast was bored and came to the sea for a stroll, their lives would be in danger.
Just as they were moving forward carefully, there was a sudden explosion in the sky. It was a thunderbolt!
"Bang!"
Everyone was shocked!
"What, what is this!" One of the Divine Illumination Realm warriors said with a frightened face.
In the distance, they saw an object diving into the sea at an extremely fast speed. The strong impact even set off waves several feet high!
The eyes of the warrior named "Lang" flashed, but he said, "Maybe a rare treasure has appeared!"
It was said that when some rare treasures appeared, there would be lightning and thunder, and there would be strange phenomena in the sky. Today's scene was very likely, not to mention that they had just seen something dive into the water with their own eyes.
"Really? Senior Brother Lang! "A warrior exclaimed with an excited expression.
"It's just a possibility. Let's go and see!" Senior Brother Lang was very experienced. He looked around vigilantly. After confirming that there were no other warriors in the surrounding sea area, he flew in that direction. At the same time, he took out a Water Repelling Pill and swallowed it. Then he dove into the water.
These five people were very good at swimming, and with the help of the Water Repelling Pill, they quickly sank to the bottom of the water.
When everyone saw the scene in front of them, they were surprised. They had thought it was a rare treasure, but it turned out to be a person!
"Why did this person come down from the lightning?" A warrior asked with Genuine Essence.
Senior-apprentice Brother Wolf stared at the man and shook his head. But when he saw the Sumeru Ring hanging on the man's chest, his face suddenly revealed a trace of greed. "This man seems to be injured. He actually chose to meditate in the water to heal himself. Should we …"
Master Lang said and exchanged glances with the others. They were originally pirates who made a living by killing people in the surrounding sea area. It was not the first time they had done such a blatant robbery.
Although they were now disciples of the Fish Dragon Sect, it was not a big deal to go back to their old profession when no one was watching. Moreover, the big sects in the Central Domain claimed to be righteous, so they must have done this kind of thing many times. To put it bluntly, the world of warriors was often so cruel.
"But this person's strength …" A warrior stared at the person meditating at the bottom of the sea and said hesitantly, "Senior Brother Lang, that is a warrior in the False Tribulation Realm!"
Senior Brother Lang also showed a trace of hesitation. They could not fight against a warrior in the False Tribulation Realm. Even if he was seriously injured, it was still a piece of cake for him to take the lives of these warriors in the Divine Reflection Realm.

Chapter 664
Senior Brother Wolf hesitated for a moment and finally chose to retreat. The five warriors moved towards the water and quickly left.

After feeling the warmth of Luo Zheng's body, the three women used true essence sound transmissions to discuss how to get Luo Zheng out of the water.
Luo Zheng didn't move. He was now trying his best to expel the Space Force from his body. With his control over the Space Force, this process wouldn't take long.

The three women kept chattering as they curiously looked at Luo Zheng.

Three Late Divine Core warriors …

When the leading female disciple called Yuan Qingwei saw Sect Elder Hu, she immediately stood up and said, "Sect Elder Hu, there's a warrior here, and it looks like he's injured …"

"Just leave when I tell you to! Leave this to me! "Sect Elder Hu scolded once again.
No matter what, Sect Elder Hu was still a Sect Elder of the Fish Dragon Sect. Hearing his scolding, the three female disciples immediately flew away with resentful expressions.
Right after the three female disciples left, a grin emerged on Sect Elder Hu's face. After that, he took out a spear, and the other two Divine Core warriors also took out their weapons. The three of them couldn't tell how serious Luo Zheng's injuries were, but since he was still meditating in this situation, they knew his injuries couldn't be light.

"You can do whatever you want, just kill them after this!" Sect Elder Hu understood senior disciple Lang's meaning.

At the same time, Sect Elder Hu had finally attacked Luo Zheng. Right when Sect Elder Hu was about to thrust his spear towards Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng's slightly squinted eyes suddenly opened. He stared at the three Divine Core warriors as if he was looking at a dead man, and a faint smile emerged on his face.

Chapter 665
Noticing that Luo Zheng had suddenly opened his eyes, the three Divine Core warriors' expressions changed. The warrior surnamed Hu even shouted out loudly, "Quick, kill him with all your strength!"

Chapter 666
