Yuan Qingwei had given up on resisting, and her face was filled with a determined expression. She had given up on her own life, she just wanted to die!
But right at this moment, Cultivator Lang suddenly stood in front of Yuan Qingwei like a puppet.
Right at this moment, an indifferent voice sounded out. "Try shooting him with your flying sword again, see if you can make him …"
Yuan Qingwei was startled. She looked around, but no one was around. The man in front of her was just standing in front of her, as if someone had cast a Body Freezing Art on him. This was an opportunity for her!
Yuan Qingwei didn't dare hesitate, she controlled the flying sword to circle back to Cultivator Lang's face.
Although the cultivator surnamed Lang couldn't move his body, he could still speak, "You smelly bitch, how dare you!" Looking at Yuan Qingwei's fierce expression, the cultivator surnamed Lang roared again, "Who is it? Who is it?! "
Luo Zheng slowly appeared behind Yuan Qingwei, and he stared at Cultivator Lang with an indifferent expression.
When the cultivator surnamed Lang saw Luo Zheng appear here unscathed, his face filled with shock. Wasn't this guy seriously injured? He was just an Early Void Tribulation warrior, and even if he wanted to fight three Late Divine Core Sect Elders, he couldn't defeat them in a short period of time. Could it be … all of them were dead?
This was impossible …
Cultivator Lang's thoughts ended here. Without any hesitation, Yuan Qingwei's flying sword pierced through his head, causing blood to splash out. After that, the flying sword fell back into the water.
Yuan Qingwei turned her head around and saw the man in front of her. He was the man she had spent a tremendous amount of effort to drag onto the reef.
"Quick, quick, save my junior sister …" Yuan Qingwei's mind had been tense all this time. Moreover, she had just killed two people in a row. She was almost on the verge of collapse. She closed her eyes and fainted.
After a long time, Yuan Qingwei slowly woke up. She instinctively raised her body and saw her two sisters lying beside her. Although their clothes were messy, their breathing was stable. They were unharmed!
Were they all saved by that man?
Yuan Qingwei raised her head and saw that the man was sitting on another reef. He seemed to be healing her.
She flew over and landed lightly on the reef, sizing up the slightly thin man in front of her.
At that moment, Luo Zheng opened his eyes. He glanced at Yuan Qingwei and said, "You're awake."
Yuan Qingwei nodded. "May I ask … what is your name?"
"I'm not your benefactor. If it weren't for me, you wouldn't have been involved in this for no reason. It's your kindness that saved yourselves," Luo Zheng replied.
However, Yuan Qingwei said, "Father always said that people should know how to repay kindness. I only know this."
A helpless smile appeared on Luo Zheng's face. What kind of father raised this daughter of his? She was so pure and innocent. He was asking for death by letting her step into the world of warriors!
Then, he shook his head and said, "My name is Luo Zheng."
"Luo Zheng! You're that Luo Zheng! Luo Zheng from the Cloud Hall? "Yuan Qingwei's eyes widened. She had never expected this.
"You've heard of me?" Luo Zheng asked lightly. Although it was a question, Luo Zheng was not surprised.
There were many people in the Central Domain who knew Luo Zheng. Since Luo Zheng's departure from the Martial Arts Competition, he had become famous all over the world!
Then, he helped the Cloud Hall build the strongest Sect Protection Array. He relied on the Sect Protection Array to challenge Cui Xie. Since then, Luo Zheng's fame had skyrocketed! At that time, Luo Zheng was only in the Divine Reflection Realm. A warrior in the Divine Reflection Realm dared to challenge the strongest person in the Central Domain. This kind of courage and boldness might not be desirable in the eyes of the elders, but among the younger generation of warriors, he had become half a legend!
His story had been repeated over and over again, and it had spawned countless versions. No matter which version it was, it would make people's hearts surge.
"Of course I've heard of you. But aren't you in the Divine Reflection Realm? How come you're already … "Yuan Qingwei sized up Luo Zheng." And there are rumors in the Central Domain that you're already dead! "
Luo Zheng shook his head. He didn't care about himself. Instead, he cared about the situation in the Cloud Hall and news about his sister.
"What's the situation in the Central Domain now?" Luo Zheng asked.
Obviously, Yuan Qingwei wasn't the kind of person who cared too much about the situation. In contrast, she was more willing to listen to other people's stories about Luo Zheng. However, as an inner disciple of the Fish Dragon Sect, she knew more or less about the situation.
"Now all the sects in the Central Domain have united together. It's said that they want to eliminate all the independent warriors," Yuan Qingwei said, curling her lips.
"Why?" Luo Zheng had a strange look on his face.
Yuan Qingwei curled her lips. "Because they want to force all the independent warriors to join the sects! That's why our Fish Dragon Sect is now a mess. There are all kinds of people! "
The eight warriors killed by Luo Zheng today were all independent warriors. However, the old man surnamed Hu joined the Fish Dragon Sect early, so he got the position of Elder.
"What about the Cloud Hall?"
"What about the World Business Alliance?"
"What about the Void Spirit Sect?"
Luo Zheng asked several questions. Yuan Qingwei stared at Luo Zheng with her eyes wide open. Her eyes didn't move, but Luo Zheng understood her expression. She clearly didn't know!
"Do your two sisters know?" Luo Zheng asked. He noticed that the other two female disciples were slowly waking up.
"No …" Yuan Qingwei answered for her sisters without hesitation.
Luo Zheng exhaled and then said, "Well, the Fish Dragon Sect is not far from here, right?"
"Well, I can show you the way!" Yuan Qingwei finally found that she was a little useful.
After the two female disciples woke up, the four of them went to the Fish Dragon Sect. After the two female disciples learned about Luo Zheng's identity, the three of them chattered non-stop along the way, as if they had forgotten about the life and death crisis they had just faced …
The three women threw all kinds of questions at Luo Zheng, which immediately gave Luo Zheng a headache.
On the way, Xun couldn't stand it anymore. She turned into a little red light and rushed out of her body, releasing a strong power toward the three women. Then Xun scolded, "You three, shut up!"
The three women immediately shut up. Xun's sudden appearance really scared them. However, Xun's scolding only lasted for a few breaths of time before the three women surrounded Xun.
"Sister, why is your skin so white?"
"What are your clothes made of? Why do they look like they're made of leaves? But they're very tough! "
"Hey, sister, why are your ears so sharp …"
Xun rolled her eyes helplessly and said to Luo Zheng, "I can't help you!" Then she turned into a little red light again and drilled into Luo Zheng's body.
Fortunately, the Fish Dragon Sect was built on a mountain near the sea, and the sea area under their feet was originally the coastal sea. After flying over this sea area, Luo Zheng saw a large sect!
Although the Fish Dragon Sect was only a third-grade sect, there were many disciples in it. It accounted for about one-third of the Purple Heart Holy Land!
This was also because there were too few humans on the Sea God Continent. Any decent sect in the Central Domain had millions of disciples. Some large sects had millions or even tens of millions of disciples, such as the Cloud Hall and the Void Spirit Sect.
At the gate of the Fish Dragon Sect, hundreds of lights flashed in the entire sect. Those were the lights emitted by the sect's protective array.
After a while, two streams of light flew over. They were two warriors at the middle stage of the False Tribulation Realm. One of them questioned Luo Zheng, "Who are you?"
Yuan Qingwei smiled and said to one of the warriors, "Elder Yu, this is Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng from the Cloud Hall!"
"Luo Zheng from the Cloud Hall?"
The two False Tribulation Realm elders looked at each other. One of them had a strange expression, "Isn't Luo Zheng from the Cloud Hall dead? Why are you pretending to be him? "

Chapter 667
Back then, Luo Zheng had actually left without saying goodbye. Since the opening of the Heavenly Mist Immortal Tomb, many top warriors from various sects had entered and obtained many opportunities. Many of them had died, and the sects in the entire Central Domain had undergone earth-shaking changes.
Luo Zheng was the first to pass through the Wheel of Fortune in the Heavenly Mist Immortal Tomb. However, when the warriors from various sects returned, they discovered that Luo Zheng had disappeared!
No one knew whether he was dead or alive. The Cloud Hall was also tight-lipped about this, which gave the outside world room to speculate. Most warriors believed that Luo Zheng must have died.
There were many warriors who had entered the Immortal Tomb by relying on the Spirit Lamp of the Immortal Tomb. What had happened in the Heavenly Mist Immortal Tomb couldn't be hidden. All kinds of incredible and terrifying things in the Wheel of Fortune had been vividly described. Therefore, it wasn't hard for people to accept that Luo Zheng had died. After all, even a False Tribulation Realm elder would die. At that time, Luo Zheng was only a Divine Reflection Realm warrior!
"Why are there rumors that I'm dead?" Luo Zheng smiled bitterly. It seemed that he really shouldn't have left without saying goodbye. "Forget it. I want to see the Sect Master of the Fish Dragon Sect."
"It doesn't matter whether you are Luo Zheng or not. You have nothing to do with the Fish Dragon Sect. Leave quickly." Another False Tribulation Realm elder wasn't very interested in Luo Zheng's identity. The situation in the entire Central Domain was unpredictable. It was better to be cautious.
At this time, Yuan Qingwei said, "Elder Yu, Luo Zheng just saved our lives …"
"Nonsense, Yuan Qingwei! The three of you, come here! " The elder scolded Yuan Qingwei. Then he turned to Luo Zheng and said coldly, "Friend, this is a special time. If you don't leave, don't blame me for being impolite."
Luo Zheng came to the Fish Dragon Sect with no other request. He just wanted to inquire about the Central Domain through the Fish Dragon Sect. He didn't expect the two elders in the False Tribulation Realm to be so rude. Luo Zheng's face darkened, and then he said coldly, "Just now, people from the Fish Dragon Sect tried to kill me when I was healing. I have to settle this account with the Fish Dragon Sect!"
"Nonsense. How could someone from the Fish Dragon Sect do such a thing?" Elder Yu retorted coldly to Luo Zheng.
"Elder Yu …" Yuan Qingwei wanted to say something, but as soon as she opened her mouth, Elder Yu shouted, "Shut up!"
"Humph, boy, you really came to the Fish Dragon Sect to make trouble! But you have to look carefully. The Fish Dragon Sect is a third-grade sect. You are just a warrior in the early stage of the False Tribulation Realm. I don't know who gave you the confidence! "The other elder in the False Tribulation Realm sneered.
There were three elders in the early stage of the False Tribulation Realm in the Fish Dragon Sect, and the Sect Master was a warrior in the middle stage of the False Tribulation Realm. In the eyes of these two elders, Luo Zheng had reached the False Tribulation Realm at such a young age. But after all, he was only in the early stage of the False Tribulation Realm. How could he fight against four warriors in the False Tribulation Realm of the Fish Dragon Sect alone?
"Friend, please leave quickly." Elder Yu's body began to revolve a pale green true essence. He cultivated a type of poison technique. Although it couldn't compare to the 'Five Poison Scripture', this type of poison technique was still very difficult to deal with.
Luo Zheng glanced at the elder in the False Tribulation Realm. Although there was no killing intent in Luo Zheng's eyes, for some reason, the elder's heart trembled involuntarily. Then Luo Zheng said slowly, "Make trouble? Well, since you said I'm making trouble, then let's make a scene! "
Then Luo Zheng's eyes flashed, and suddenly, a surging Qi Power was released! With the improvement of Luo Zheng's cultivation, his soul became more and more stable, and the pressure released by his powerful soul became more and more terrifying!
The two elders in the False Tribulation Realm suddenly felt as if a mountain was pressing down on them. The powerful pressure made them breathless, and their heads hurt so much that they felt like they were going to split open!
Although Luo Zheng's Spiritual Pressure was released in front of him, there was always a small part of it dissipating in all directions. How could Yuan Qingwei and the other two female disciples beside Luo Zheng withstand it? Their faces suddenly turned pale!
How could they have imagined that the young man, who looked as gentle as the wind just now, would become so terrifying at this time!
Yuan Qingwei's face was as white as a sheet of paper. Although there was a look of fear on her face, her eyes were full of excitement. Luo Zheng really lived up to his reputation. He could suppress the two elders of the Fish Dragon Sect with his aura alone! He was young and had absolute strength, which was very attractive to any girl.
"This … friend, it seems that you really want to fight!" Although Elder Yu's face was ugly, he could not let an outsider show off in the sect. He resisted Luo Zheng's Spiritual Pressure and was about to attack Luo Zheng with his Genuine Essence.
Luo Zheng faintly raised his eyebrows, and Xun suddenly drilled out of Luo Zheng's body. With a long spear in her hand, the tip of the spear stabbed out at lightning speed and placed it on Elder Yu's neck. As long as the tip of the spear shook slightly, Elder Yu's head would fall to the ground.
However, the aura that Xun exuded made her seem like the ruler of all living beings. There was no trace of pity in her clear and beautiful red eyes. Elder Yu knew very well that if he moved even the slightest bit, this woman would not show any mercy!
"Haha, Young Hero Luo Zheng, please forgive me for not coming out to welcome you!" At this time, a loud voice rang out, and a streamer shot out from the Fish Dragon Sect and stopped steadily in mid-air. "May I know why Young Hero Luo Zheng has come to visit me?"
Previously, they had doubted Luo Zheng's identity, but after seeing Luo Zheng's Sword Spirit, how could they not be sure of Luo Zheng's identity? Anyone who had heard of Luo Zheng's legend knew that his Sword Spirit was a beautiful woman from a different race. There were very few swordsmen in the Central Domain who had Sword Spirits, and no one but Luo Zheng had a Sword Spirit who could transform into a beautiful woman!
Previously, Luo Zheng only wanted to visit this person, but Yuan Qingwei did not know anything. This time, Luo Zheng naturally wanted to straighten out the situation in the Central Domain before returning to the Cloud Hall.
However, these two Sect Elders from the Fish Dragon Sect didn't know what was going on, and they wanted to chase him away. Luo Zheng had nearly been plotted against by them, and since they didn't know what was going on, Luo Zheng had no choice but to reason with them.
"Sect Chief Xiao," Luo Zheng didn't have a smile on his face. With a deep voice, he said, "Just now, when I was healing my injuries in the ocean, I was attacked by three Divine Core warriors from your sect. I'm here to ask for an explanation!"
"Is that so?" Sect Chief Xiao's expression darkened as he questioned Elder Yu. "Why is there such scum in my sect? Elder Yu, who are those three Divine Core warriors?"
How could Elder Yu know who they were? When he heard the Sect Chief's question, his eyes flickered as he saw Yuan Qingwei standing not far away from him. With a stern voice, he asked, "Yuan Qingwei, why didn't you report what happened?"
Being questioned like this, Yuan Qingwei was startled for a moment. With a wronged expression, she said, "Elder Yu, you didn't want me to say anything just now, I've told you a few times …"
Sect Chief Xiao coldly harrumphed at Elder Yu. After that, with a kind smile on his face, he said to Yuan Qingwei, "Tell me what happened in detail." Sect Chief Xiao didn't know why Luo Zheng came here.
But, as he was able to become the Sect Chief, he was a smart man. There were only a few dozen Divine Core warriors in the Fish Dragon Sect, and most of them were from the Sect itself. Those independent warriors were arrogant and unruly, and they might have done something like this. If it wasn't the Fish Dragon Sect who did this, Sect Chief Xiao wouldn't have allowed Luo Zheng to frame them with such a crime. But, if it was really the Fish Dragon Sect who did this, then it was a different story.
After that, Yuan Qingwei told Sect Chief Xiao about how they found Luo Zheng at the bottom of the sea, how they brought him up to the reef, how they met Elder Hu, and how they were attacked by those independent warriors.
After listening to the story, Sect Chief Xiao let out a hollow laugh. He glared at Elder Yu, then he turned to Luo Zheng and said politely, "Young hero Luo Zheng, this is the Fish Dragon Sect's fault! I apologize to you on behalf of the Fish Dragon Sect! As for the reason behind this, please come to my pavilion and we'll talk about it in detail! "
In fact, Luo Zheng felt it was strange as well. Although the Fish Dragon Sect was only a Third Grade Sect, it still had some fame in the Central Domain. But, the men he saw just now had strong killing intent, and they didn't look like ordinary disciples.
Since Sect Chief Xiao had invited him, Luo Zheng wouldn't refuse. Besides, Luo Zheng didn't plan to talk about this matter. It was only after the two Void Tribulation Elders made things difficult for him that Luo Zheng brought it up.

Chapter 668
In the Tianshui Pavilion of the Fish Dragon Sect.
Steam curled up from a cup of fragrant tea. As the music played, more than ten maids danced gracefully. They were enchanting and extremely beautiful.
Sect Master Xiao sat on Luo Zheng's left and said with a smile, "It's rumored in the Central Domain that Young Hero Luo Zheng died in the immortal tomb. Seeing that you're safe and sound, this rumor seems to be true."
Luo Zheng didn't care about this rumor at all. Instead, he asked directly, "Sect Master Xiao, why did you explain it to me?"
Sect Master Xiao sighed and said, "It seems that Young Hero Luo Zheng hasn't been in the Central Domain for the past two years. You don't know anything about the situation in the Central Domain."
"That's why I'm here," Luo Zheng said seriously. If it weren't for the news, he wouldn't be bothered to stay in this third-rank sect!
Sect Master Xiao nodded and said, "Since the opening of the Heavenly Mist Immortal Tomb, many fourth-rank and fifth-rank sects in the Central Domain returned. The entire Central Domain was peaceful for a while. However, half a year later, a new force was born in the Central Domain. This force was formed by Cui Xie. It's called the Kingdom of Evil Deities!"
Hearing Cui Xie's news, Luo Zheng's expression suddenly became very serious. He was most concerned about Cui Xie and the Cloud Hall. The former concerned the fate of Luo Yan, while the Cloud Hall concerned the fate of Ning Yudie. However, Luo Zheng only listened and didn't interrupt Sect Master Xiao.
"However, the Kingdom of Gods was self-proclaimed by Cui Xie! The warriors in the Central Domain didn't recognize it at all! "Sect Master Xiao sneered.
When a Sect developed to a Rank 6 Sect, it could be regarded as a Holy Land or Divine Nation. A Holy Land could still be considered a Sect. For example, the Purple Heart Holy Land could be considered a Rank 6 Sect, and the Sky Feather Holy Land could be considered a Rank 7 Sect!
But a God's Kingdom was different. After it was established, the entire God's Kingdom belonged to Cui Xie alone! He Cui Xie would ascend the throne just like the emperor of the mortal world!
Cui Xie had established the Kingdom of Evil Deities in the Central Domain. He wanted to include the entire Central Domain, and all the Fifth Grade Sects, Fourth Grade Sects, and even the Third Grade and Second Grade Sects in the Central Domain would submit to Cui Xie!
How could the other martial practitioners of the Central Domain be willing to lower their heads to Cui Xie?
When Cui Xie had just established the Kingdom of Evil Deities, several large Sects had jointly declared that they refused to recognize the existence of the Kingdom of Evil Deities!
Regardless of whether it was the World Business Alliance or the Void Spirit Sect, none of them objected to Cui Xie establishing a Sect! Regardless of whether Cui Xie named it "Tian Xie Sect" or "Earth Evil Sect", there was no problem. New Sects were born in the Central Domain every year, and new Sects would rise every year. However, it couldn't be called the "Kingdom of Evil Deities"!
However, Cui Xie had already made up his mind. He began to wantonly recruit and recruit independent martial practitioners from the entire Central Domain.
Many years ago, someone had asserted that the strongest power in the Central Domain wasn't the World Business Alliance or the Void Spirit Sect, but independent martial practitioners!
Independent martial practitioners referred to those martial practitioners who didn't join any Sects. These martial practitioners relied on their own experience to grow.
Although their cultivation environment was far inferior to that of the Sects, and most of their talents couldn't be compared to the Sects' disciples, the large Sects would often discover geniuses who stepped into the Martial Dao.
However, independent martial practitioners had the advantage of having a large population. With so many geniuses divided among the Sects, there were bound to be some geniuses left out! Therefore, independent martial practitioners were a power that couldn't be ignored.
Cui Xie had recruited all the independent martial practitioners in the entire Central Domain. The Kingdom of Evil Deities was also rapidly expanding.
"Actually, the Fish Dragon Sect wasn't willing to recruit independent martial practitioners either." Sect Master Xiao smiled bitterly. "Among these independent martial practitioners, some were originally robbers. If they joined the Fish Dragon Sect, it would ruin our reputation! However, under the circumstances, we had no choice but to do so! "
If they recruited independent martial practitioners, then independent martial practitioners would continuously join the Kingdom of Evil Deities. Therefore, the World Business Alliance had no choice but to work with the Void Spirit Sect and the Cloud Hall to inform all the Sects in the Central Domain to also crazily recruit independent martial practitioners!
For many independent martial practitioners who had just stepped onto the Martial Dao, the Sects might be very attractive. However, for those who had grown up, the attraction of joining a Sect was far less intense. After all, after joining a Sect, they would be bound by the Sect's rules. Moreover, those independent martial practitioners were used to being free. How would they be willing to join a Sect?
In fact, this problem also existed in the Kingdom of Evil Deities. However, the Kingdom of Evil Deities had just been established and didn't have many restrictions on the martial practitioners in the Godly Kingdom. Instead, Cui Xie promised many benefits to those independent martial practitioners. In the future, when the Kingdom of Evil Deities was fully developed, these people would all receive great benefits and rewards!
Many Sects in the Central Domain were also unwilling to be left behind. To sum it up in two words, it was coercion and temptation.
After the situation evolved step by step to the present, there was less and less space for independent martial practitioners to survive. They could either choose to join the Kingdom of Evil Deities or choose to join a Sect! No matter which Sect you joined, you had to be a Sect disciple. Otherwise, trouble would come knocking!
"How can this be?" Hearing Sect Master Xiao's words, Luo Zheng also felt that it was somewhat inconceivable.
"In the beginning, no one expected things to develop to this point. Now, almost no one in the Central Domain dares to say that they are independent martial practitioners …" Sect Master Xiao smiled and said, "I think there will be a battle between the Sects and Cui Xie!"
Luo Zheng nodded. When he heard the two words "Cui Xie", his eyes became sharp!
"There is one more thing that I would like to ask Sect Master Xiao," Luo Zheng asked again.
"Just say it!" Sect Master Xiao's expression was very polite. Why was he so angry with Elder Yu just now? First of all, Luo Zheng was not someone he could afford to offend. The Cloud Hall was now a fifth-grade Sect. His Fish Dragon Sect could never catch up to it! Moreover, Luo Zheng's Rune Technique had become a legend in the Central Domain. If he could befriend Luo Zheng, he could ask Luo Zheng to give him a Sect-Protecting Formation. It didn't need to be as powerful as the one in the Cloud Hall. As long as it had half of the power, it would be able to benefit his Fish Dragon Sect immensely!
"Cui Xie's Heavenly Devil Joyous Union Formation … is it completed?" Luo Zheng asked cautiously. In fact, he was very reluctant to ask this question because he was afraid of hearing an affirmative answer! However, Luo Zheng knew that there were some things in this world that he had to face.
Sect Master Xiao knew about the conflict between Luo Zheng and Cui Xie. He knew that Luo Zheng's sister, Luo Yan, had fallen into Cui Xie's hands. Of course, Luo Zheng would be concerned about this problem. Sect Master Xiao immediately said, "No. It is said that the entire Kingdom of Evil Deities is now looking for a woman with the Extreme Purple Yin Body!"
Hearing Sect Master Xiao's words, Luo Zheng immediately breathed a sigh of relief. In the past two years, Luo Zheng had been most worried about this matter.
It seemed that fate was still on Luo Zheng's side!
Seeing Luo Zheng's happy face, Sect Master Xiao hurriedly said, "Young Hero Luo Zheng, don't worry. The Extreme Purple Yin Body is said to be rare. How can it be so easy to find?"
Luo Zheng nodded. Since he had returned to the Central Domain, the hatred between him and Cui Xie would definitely come to an end!
"Young Hero Luo Zheng, you said that you were recuperating. If you don't mind, I can provide you with a place to stay. Our Fish Dragon Sect may not be able to give you other things, but elixirs and the like …" Sect Master Xiao hurriedly said.
Luo Zheng smiled and said, "I was just passing by. Since I have returned to the Central Domain, I naturally have to hurry back. Thank you for your hospitality, Sect Master Xiao. I still have to rush back to the Cloud Hall."
After talking with Sect Master Xiao, Luo Zheng only caught two pieces of information. First, the Cloud Hall was safe and sound. Second, the third person with the Extreme Purple Yin Body had not been found! Luo Zheng was satisfied with these two pieces of information.
"Since Young Hero Luo Zheng has important matters to attend to, I won't keep you any longer." Then Sect Master Xiao stood up and saw Luo Zheng off. He even called the three False Tribulation Realm elders of the Fish Dragon Sect to see him off.
After Luo Zheng turned into a stream of light and disappeared into the horizon, Elder Yu said unwillingly, "Sect Master, why are you so polite to this kid?"
Although Elder Yu knew Luo Zheng's identity, Luo Zheng had nothing to do with the Fish Dragon Sect. Why should he be so polite to him?

Chapter 669
Although Luo Zheng was only in the False Tribulation Realm now, Sect Master Xiao was clear about his relationship with Ning Yudie.
Luo Zheng was bound to become the core figure of the Cloud Hall. Moreover, Sect Master Xiao wanted to find an opportunity to ask Luo Zheng to help set up the Sect Protecting Formation. This time, he couldn't ask. After all, he had no relation to Luo Zheng. However, if he befriended Luo Zheng this time, he could find an opportunity to ask next time …
A purple-black stream of light flashed across the horizon, covering a distance of several kilometers in a single breath.
Even though Luo Zheng's speed was extremely strange, it wouldn't take him a day or two to reach Cloud Sea City with his speed. Therefore, Luo Zheng wasn't heading towards Cloud Sea City, but a large city in the east of the Central Domain, Void Sky City.
Cloud Sea City was built on the basis of the Cloud Hall, while Void Sky City was built on the basis of the Void Spirit Sect. As for the headquarters of the World Business Alliance, it was in Tianqi City. Behind every prosperous and large city stood an even larger sect.
These large cities had teleportation formations built between them. If Luo Zheng passed through Void Sky City, his speed would be much faster.
Two days later, Luo Zheng arrived in the sky above this ancient city.
If Tianqi City was the most prosperous city in the entire Central Domain because of the existence of the World Business Alliance, then Void Sky City was the most ancient city in the Central Domain because the Void Spirit Sect's history was much longer than the World Business Alliance's.
Standing in the sky above Void Sky City, one could see that the entire city was filled with ancient buildings. These buildings more or less had some historical allusions and were mentioned in various ancient poems. It could be considered a historical city.
As soon as he approached Void Sky City, Luo Zheng sensed a Forbidden Flight Restriction. It was said that the Forbidden Flight Restriction in Void Sky City was created by Feng Guanyu, but it was later destroyed. However, the Void Spirit Sect still hired many rune masters and spent a large amount of money to set up this Forbidden Flight Restriction again.
With Luo Zheng's current strength, he could actually completely ignore this Forbidden Flight Restriction, but after thinking about it, he didn't want to cause unnecessary trouble, so he landed on the official road and walked towards Void Sky City.
Several armored soldiers stood at the gate of the Void Sky City, constantly scanning the people entering and exiting the city gate.
Mortals without any True Yuan or True Energy in their bodies could pass through freely. If they were martial artists, they would be stopped and interrogated.
When Luo Zheng raised his head and walked over, he was also stopped by those soldiers.
These soldiers were only Xiantian life forms. They sized up Luo Zheng and found that they couldn't see through Luo Zheng's cultivation. Their expressions changed slightly, and one of them said politely, "Friend, we have a custom in Virtual Sky City. We have to check the affiliation of warriors. Independent warriors are not allowed to enter. May I ask which sect or family you belong to?"
Currently, all the major sects were desperately squeezing the living space of independent warriors. What the Void Sky City did was light. In some places, there were even big conflicts with independent warriors, and hundreds of independent warriors were killed …
"So, independent warriors aren't even allowed to enter the city?" Hearing this rule, Luo Zheng was somewhat speechless. This was really forcing independent warriors to join sects.
"That's right," the soldier said with a smile. "You also know the current situation in the Central Domain. If you're an independent warrior, I can help you find someone. With your strength, it won't be a problem for you to join the Void Spirit Sect."
When that armored soldier saw Luo Zheng dawdling, he treated him as an independent martial artist.
Luo Zheng immediately shook his head. "Thank you for your kindness, but I'm from the Cloud Hall."
"Cloud Hall?" The soldier's eyes flashed. "May I ask how you can prove it?"
Luo Zheng reached out and tapped the ring on his chest lightly. Then, he took out a nameplate from the ring. It was the disciple nameplate that was distributed to Luo Zheng after he joined the Cloud Hall.
The soldier pretended to turn it over and over in his hand several times. The soldier wasn't from the Cloud Hall, so how could he distinguish the authenticity of the Cloud Hall's disciple nameplate? However, even if he was pretending, he still had to check it.
In fact, many stubborn independent warriors were really unwilling to join sects, but they had to enter these big cities to do things, so they had no choice but to entrust others to forge the disciple nameplates of those sects …
After flipping it over for a while, the soldier exchanged the disciple nameplate for Luo Zheng. Then, Luo Zheng made way for Luo Zheng. Immediately, Luo Zheng entered the Void Sky City.
The Void Sky City was an ancient city. Whether it was the streets or the food, it was very special. Luo Zheng didn't rush to the transfer array. Instead, he entered the famous Qin Yue Pavilion in the Void Sky City. He planned to rest for a short while before continuing on his journey.
The Qin Yue Pavilion wasn't very tall. It only had three floors. However, it was adjacent to the Qingshui River. The scenery was very elegant. Since ancient times, many people had come to this pavilion to recite poems. Therefore, the Qin Yue Pavilion's reputation was very great. The cost of living here was also very high.
After casually ordering a few simple and elegant dishes, Luo Zheng's gaze looked into the distance. A few scholars at a nearby table were reciting poems. When they were full of interest, they picked up the chopsticks on the table and knocked on the bowls. The person who was knocking seemed to be proficient in music. The "ding ding dong dong dong" sounded very pleasing to the ears.
Luo Zheng also listened with interest. He even applauded the other party. That person then cast a smiling face at Luo Zheng.
Most of the scholars in the Qin Yue Pavilion weren't martial artists. At this time, Luo Zheng also sighed. Even such an ordinary life was a way to live. However, Luo Zheng also knew that since he had chosen the path of a martial artist, there was absolutely no reason to retreat.
From the first day he set foot on the martial path, he was destined to compete with the heavens, with the earth, and with people. Moving forward on the martial path wasn't a paradise. However, if he retreated, he would definitely be consigned to eternal damnation!
Just as the scholar was singing and playing, several martial artists walked in. These martial artists were all at the Divine Reflection Realm. As soon as they came in, they made a racket, causing the scholars in the Qin Yue Pavilion to glare at them angrily.
However, those martial artists didn't seem to notice. These mortals couldn't enter their eyes.
Luo Zheng also didn't poke his nose into other people's business. He just minded his own business and drank and ate. However, the words of those martial artists attracted Luo Zheng's attention.
"Alas, I'm so tired! This f * cking Void Spirit Sect, when did they treat us as disciples? They simply treat us as slaves! "The martial artist drank a lot of wine and vented the unhappiness in his heart.
"Shh! Brother Cui, don't be so loud. If those inner disciples of the Void Spirit Sect hear you and complain, it will be troublesome! "Another martial artist reminded in a low voice.
Luo Zheng finally understood. These martial artists should have been independent martial artists, but they were forced to be recruited by the Void Spirit Sect. However, they were obviously very dissatisfied with the current situation.
"Alas, I hope this matter will end soon. Otherwise, I'm really tired of walking around in this city all day and being paranoid!"
"I say, that Xi Family's Young Lady should quickly find a man to sleep with. Then everything will be solved and she won't be remembered by Cui Xie!"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng was shocked. His ears suddenly perked up. What did that mean? Xi Family's Young Lady? Remembered by Cui Xie?
As the number one person in the Central Domain, what kind of woman did Cui Xie not have? If he remembered that woman, it must be related to the Extreme Purple Yin Physique!
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng's figure flashed. He originally maintained a sitting posture, but with this flash, his figure suddenly appeared beside the group of martial artists. He still maintained a sitting posture, and the stool under his butt also moved with him. However, he didn't make any sound from beginning to end, as if he was a ghost.
When the group of scholars saw this scene, they thought their eyes were playing tricks on them. They rubbed their eyes and looked again. They found that Luo Zheng had indeed moved his stool more than ten feet away without anyone noticing. They all widened their eyes, thinking that they had met a master.
As for the group of martial artists who were drinking to their hearts' content, they were also shocked. One of the martial artists stared at Luo Zheng and asked, "Brother, what's the matter?"
"Clang," A top-grade true essence stone smashed on the table, and Luo Zheng immediately said, "I'll pay for the food and wine."
Seeing the top-grade true essence stone, the group of martial artists' eyes widened! The food in Qin Yue Pavilion was expensive, far beyond what ordinary people could afford, but that was for ordinary people. For martial artists, one or two low-grade true essence stones could eat here for half a month …

Chapter 670
Those martial artists stared fixedly at this top-grade True Essence Stone, their minds in a state of chaos.
Even if they were Divine Reflection Realm warriors, top-grade True Origin Stones were still a huge fortune for them.
After all, not every warrior had the ability to make money like Luo Zheng. When he was in the Eastern Domain, he could easily earn a lot of square crystals by relying on the Sky Evolution Essence. After he came to the Central Domain, he learned the Divine Rune Technique. With his current level of Divine Rune Technique, as long as he asked, any sect would spend a lot of money to ask Luo Zheng to set up a Sect Protecting Array!
And second-tier third-grade sects like the Fish Dragon Sect didn't even have the qualifications to ask …
"We, we don't need that much …" One of the warriors swallowed and stammered.
"The rest is yours," Luo Zheng said lightly.
The eyes of the warriors suddenly lit up, as if there were two scorching flames burning in them. "Re, really?"
Luo Zheng nodded.
He didn't expect that the several martial artists would simultaneously shake their heads and refuse. These martial artists were very vigilant. If an unimaginably large meat pie suddenly fell from the sky, anyone would feel a sense of danger. Either this was a fatal bait, or Luo Zheng was an idiot. But these martial artists clearly didn't think Luo Zheng was an idiot!
"I don't have any requests. I just want to hear about the young lady of the Xi Family. Tell me and the top-grade True Origin Stones will be yours," Luo Zheng said lightly.
"It's that simple?" A warrior asked.
"Yes," Luo Zheng nodded.
A strange look appeared on the faces of the warriors at the same time. One of them stared at Luo Zheng and said, "You just came to Void Sky City, right?"
Luo Zheng nodded. "Yes, I just arrived."
"No wonder, this matter is not a secret. Now the whole Void Sky City is talking about it …" The warrior said.
"Don't tell me that the young lady of the Xi Family has the one in ten thousand Extreme Violet Yin Body," Luo Zheng said lightly.
The warrior stared at Luo Zheng and asked, "You know?"
"I guessed." Luo Zheng's eyes kept flashing. It seemed that he had guessed correctly. The situation was really troublesome. He could ignore other things, but Luo Yan was Luo Zheng's weakness! Back when he was only at the Divine Reflection Realm, he was able to kill Si Miaoling under immense pressure. Who would have thought that another Extreme Violet Yin Physique would appear in Void Sky City!
"This Xi Clan's Young Miss does indeed possess an Extreme Purple Yin Body," the cultivator replied affirmatively, "She is now being watched by Cui Xie, which is why all the cultivators in Void Sky City are panicking. Will Cui Xie lead his Tian Xie Sect to attack us?"
Although Cui Xie called himself the Country of Evil Deities, the Sects in the world did not recognize it. Therefore, most people still called the Country of Evil Deities as the Tian Xie Sect.
"In that case, quickly find someone to marry. Then there won't be so many problems, right?" Luo Zheng asked rhetorically.
Although the Void Spirit Sect and the Cloud Hall had many competitions, they had the same goal in terms of Cui Xie. The Void Spirit Sect knew very well that if Cui Xie succeeded in arranging the Great Sky Devil Joyous Union Formation, his cultivation would progress further. When he really stepped into the Divine Sea Realm, no one in the Central Domain would be able to stop him. At that time, not only the Void Spirit Sect, but also the World Business Alliance, the Cloud Hall, the Xuanyin Mansion, the Blood Wood Cliff … all the Sects would either kneel down or be eliminated!
Therefore, the Void Spirit Sect also did not want Cui Xie to find a third Extreme Purple Yin Body.
Unexpectedly, the cultivator gave a wry smile and then said, "You don't know. This is what we are complaining about. Do you know the identity of the Xi Clan's Young Miss?"
"What identity can she have?" Luo Zheng was puzzled. Even if the Xi Clan was a third-grade Sect, it was impossible for them to go against the Void Spirit Sect.
"The Sect Master of our Void Spirit Sect, Taoist Priest Qingxu, originally had the surname Xi. At that time, he cultivated with hair! Although he is a Taoist, he also has a wife and children in the Xi Clan, and the Xi Clan's Young Miss is the great-granddaughter of Taoist Priest Qingxu! "The cultivator said.
"So that's how it is." Luo Zheng nodded, but then he said, "But this is an important matter. Not to mention that she's the great-granddaughter of Taoist Priest Qingxu, for the sake of the overall situation, even if she's his biological daughter, he would have to marry her off, right?"
"That's what everyone thought." The cultivators revealed helpless expressions, "But it just so happens that the Xi Clan views the Young Miss as the pearl of their palm, and the Xi Clan's Young Miss is extremely doted on by Taoist Priest Qingxu. Even though the Xi Clan has arranged for a few men for the Xi Clan's Young Miss, and there's no lack of geniuses from the Void Spirit Sect amongst them, she just doesn't like them!"
Luo Zheng frowned. This matter was quite troublesome indeed, but the source of the problem was still Cui Xie. Now that a Extreme Purple Yin Body had appeared in Void Sky City, perhaps it wouldn't be long before a Extreme Purple Yin Body would appear in Ocean of Clouds City as well! Even though the Extreme Purple Yin Body was rare, there were too many humans in the Central Domain, so the probability wasn't small at all.
In the end, he still had to kill Cui Xie and rescue Luo Yan.
However, Luo Zheng had no choice but to intervene in this matter. If Cui Xie really came to Void Sky City to snatch the Xi Clan's Young Miss, then it might be a nightmare for the Xi Clan's Young Miss, and it would be a nightmare for Luo Zheng as well.
The problem was, how should he deal with this matter? Luo Zheng could kill Si Miaoling because she was Cui Xie's subordinate, but the Xi Clan's Young Miss was the great-granddaughter of Taoist Priest Qingxu, so he couldn't just kill her with a single strike!
The cultivator watched Luo Zheng fall into deep thought, and then he said, "In my opinion, it's best to find a man to sleep with her and seize her Primordial Yin. Wouldn't this matter be resolved then? Unfortunately, the Xi Clan deserves to be called a great clan. Coupled with the fact that the clan has a Sect Master, it's too pretentious! "
"Sleep with her …" These words were very crude, but it was indeed very reasonable. As long as the Xi Clan's Young Miss was taken away, it would also cut off Cui Xie's thoughts. The problem was, who should he sleep with?
"Two days ago, we caught a spy from the Tian Xie Sect. In my opinion, that Cui Xie is already preparing to make his move. Maybe it will be in the near future!" Another cultivator said.
These words made Luo Zheng more and more anxious. Originally, he had inadvertently heard this matter, but now it seemed that it would become an urgent matter. Luo Zheng silently got up and walked out of Qin Yue Pavilion.
"Hey, you, your True Essence Stone!" These cultivators still didn't dare to take away the top-grade True Essence Stone.
"I'll give it to you," Luo Zheng said lightly.
Just as Luo Zheng left the Qin Yue Pavilion, he heard the sound of banging and banging coming from the Qin Yue Pavilion. It was the group of martial artists fighting with each other over the top-grade True Essence Stone.
Luo Zheng naturally ignored them. At this time, a little Sword Spirit condensed beside Luo Zheng. Soon, Xun appeared beside Luo Zheng. Her figure gently floated, attracting countless surprised gazes from passers-by.
Xun and Luo Zheng walked at the same speed, but facing Luo Zheng, there was a faint smile on her face. "What are you worried about? The solution to this problem is so simple!"
"Sleep with her!" Xun said with a faint smile.
There were many men and women in the Yao Ye Clan. Although Xun was the Yao Ye Clan's King, she didn't have the slightest taboo in her speech. This was also a difference brought about by different customs.
"Then you still have to find a man," Luo Zheng was also very conflicted.
As soon as her voice fell, her slender finger pointed at Luo Zheng.
"Me?" Luo Zheng hurriedly shook his head. "I can't."
"Then what do you want to do?" Xun asked, blinking her eyes.
"Let's wait and see." Luo Zheng frowned deeply. The key was that the Xi Family's Young Lady had a special identity, but he didn't know what Taoist Priest Qing Xu was planning. Could it be that he was going to let the Xi Family's Young Lady be willful and eventually be snatched away by Cui Xie?
What Luo Zheng didn't know was that Taoist Priest Qing Xu was also very distressed!
The news that the Xi Family had a woman with an Extreme Purple Yin Body had suddenly spread out, and no one could stop it!
If it was in the past, it would be a very happy thing for the Xi Family to have an Extreme Purple Yin Body. However, at this sensitive time, Cui Xie just happened to lack an Extreme Purple Yin Body to form the Great Heavenly Demon Joyous Union Array.
Whether it was the Hall Master of the Cloud Hall or the Alliance Master of the World Business Alliance, they were very concerned about this matter. They had repeatedly urged Taoist Priest Qing Xu to quickly marry off his precious great-granddaughter.
Taoist Priest Qing Xu had also urged countless times, and even asked the Xi Family to take the initiative to use the family law to serve her. He had even threatened and lied to her. However, his great-granddaughter's temper was very strong. She would rather die than comply, saying that she hadn't met a suitable sweetheart.
It must be because he had read too many romance novels that his brain had become muddled! Taoist Priest Qing Xu had lived for hundreds of years. How could he understand the thoughts of a teenage girl?
But in any case, the sooner his great-granddaughter was married off, the sooner Taoist Priest Qing Xu would be relieved!

Chapter 671
The Xi Family was one of the great aristocratic families of Void Sky City. Originally, the Xi Family was only a second-grade family in Void Sky City. At that time, the Xi Family was not ranked in the entire Void Sky City. After all, Void Sky City had been passed down for thousands of years, and there were many third-grade aristocratic families.
Since the Xi Family produced Xi Mingyuan and climbed to the position of Void Spirit Sect Master, the status of the Xi Family naturally rose, and they became the leader of the third-grade aristocratic families of Void Sky City.
The entire Xi Family, which had close to ten thousand people, was settled in the Xi Family Castle in the eastern part of Void Sky City.
In the evening, the lamps at the entrance of the Xi Family Castle were lit up one by one. These lamps relied on true essence to release light, and a single low-grade true essence stone was enough to keep a lamp illuminated for several years. A low-grade true essence stone was not a small fortune for ordinary people, but it was impossible for ordinary families to use it.
However, the Xi Family's lamps were lit one by one, outlining the entire walls of the Xi Family Castle. Ordinary people often came here to admire the night scenery while marveling at the Xi Family's wealth.
After nightfall, groups of people came to the square in front of the Xi Family Castle to stroll around or lean on the small bridge. Some chatted about their daily lives, while others appreciated the lamps. In the entire Void Sky City, this place was the brightest.
It was also because of this that many hawkers came here to set up stalls. Roasted sweet potatoes, skewers of meat, and a rich fragrance permeated the air. There were even people who set up needlework on the ground, as well as all kinds of strange trinkets …
"Is this Xi Family's everlasting lantern really lit with True Origin Stones? So many lamps, wouldn't it cost several hundred true essence stones? " A wanderer, probably on his first day in Void Sky City, couldn't help but sigh as he looked at the endless line of lamps.
"I'm afraid Brother Han is making a fuss over nothing. The Xi Family's lamps have been lit for dozens of years. Who knows how many true essence stones have been burned?" Another wanderer shook his head and said.
"They are really too rich. I heard that even a thousand gold coins can't exchange for a single true essence stone. I'm afraid the Xi Family is burning gold!"
"Humph, the Xi Family is a dignified third-grade aristocratic family. It's not something you can imagine! Gold and silver are worthless to these warrior families. "Another wanderer shook his head.
"Sigh, it's really hard to imagine. I wonder what kind of life these warriors are living. If I had known earlier, I would have given up on literature and joined the martial arts when I was young. What nonsense!" The wanderer grumbled.
"That's not necessarily true. The world of martial artists is extremely bloody. Could it be that the Xi Family's great fortune was exchanged with their lives?" The wanderer chuckled and said, "Rumor has it that the young lady of the Xi family is looking for a husband. Brother Han, you are talented and have an impressive appearance. You can also try."
For an entire month, the Xi family had been hurriedly looking for a groom.
Although the Extreme Purple Yin Body had many implications, the Xi Clan was still the largest aristocratic clan in the Void Sky City. They couldn't really rush the Xi Clan's Young Miss to find a man to marry.
The Xi Clan's groom search had attracted many young elites. After all, she was the Young Miss of a large aristocratic family. Moreover, the Sect Master of the Void Spirit Sect stood behind the Xi Clan. If they could really marry the Young Miss of the Xi Clan, they would probably soar into the sky.
"Me? I'm afraid I can't. "The wanderer surnamed Han shook his head and said.
"Heh heh, you have some self-awareness. These days, no less than a thousand young elites have come to the Xi Clan, and among them, there is no lack of Heaven Grade geniuses. The bone age of these geniuses is only twenty to thirty years old, and their cultivation is frighteningly high. However, the Young Miss of the Xi Clan has really high standards. She didn't take a fancy to any of them!"
The status of the Young Miss of such a large aristocratic family was much higher than that of a princess in the mortal world. The matter of the Xi Clan's Young Miss' groom search was a major event in the Void Sky City. Everyone on the streets was talking about it.
In the depths of the Xi Castle, in a secluded little garden, under two screens made of jade, a twenty-eight year old girl sat cross-legged, meditating and cultivating.
This young girl was dressed in a pure white robe with two light green ribbons hanging down from her shoulders. Her fair and clean face looked especially simple and elegant.
At this moment, there was a knock on the door of the small courtyard.
The girl frowned slightly. Originally, she didn't want to get up and open the door, but the knocking was very persistent, as if it wouldn't stop until she opened the door. In desperation, she could only whisper, "Come in!"
The door of the small courtyard was pushed open, and three people came in. The leader was a young man with starry eyes and a faint smile on his face. His figure flashed, and he directly penetrated the space, passed through the screen, and arrived in front of the girl.
"Sister," the young man called out sweetly.
The girl opened her eyes, but her eyes were full of bitterness. She stared angrily at the young man in front of her and said, "Xiao Jie, can you walk normally?"
This young man was the god grade genius of the Void Spirit Sect, Xiao Jie. His full name was Xi Xiao Jie. This young man was not a member of the Xi Clan, but an adopted son of the Xi Clan. However, he grew up in the Xi Clan, and his relationship with the Xi Clan was very deep, especially with the girl in front of him.
With a smile on his face, Xi Xiao Jie grabbed a bunch of scrolls from behind his back and placed them in front of the girl. He said with a grin, "I've collected so many scrolls again today!"
When the girl saw the scrolls, the anger on her face became even more apparent. She circulated her energy, then a raging flame was unleashed, trying to burn all the scrolls away.
Xi Xiao Jie saw that the situation was getting worse, so he gently pointed his finger in the air. In an instant, the space in front of him began to distort, and although the raging flame was approaching him, it was split in half. Not even a single wisp of flame touched the scrolls.
"Sister, this is a task given to me by big uncle, can you at least let me finish?" Xi Xiao Jie said with a helpless expression.
The girl coldly harrumphed, then she closed her eyes and said with a deep voice, "Then continue with your nonsense!"
Xi Xiao Jie casually picked up a scroll, then slowly unfurled it in front of the girl. After the scroll was unfurled, a picture of a handsome man could be seen. There were even some poems written beside the picture.
"The man in this picture is young master Bo Sun from the Void Sky City, a Mid Divine Core warrior! He's so young, but he's already the Family Chief … "Xi Xiao Jie said with a grin on his face.
"Next." The girl closed her eyes and said with an expressionless face.
"The next man in this picture is Daoist Jiu Huan from the Illusion God Sect, he's 31 years old, and he's a Sect Elder of the Illusion God Sect …"
"Next …"
"This picture …"
While the two of them were asking and answering questions, they introduced the people in the picture scroll one by one. After that, Xi Xiaojie smiled and said, "I wonder if you found anyone you like today?"
"No." The girl remained silent for a moment, then she replied in a mechanical manner.
In fact, this conversation had been repeated more than a hundred times in the past month, three times a day in the morning and evening.
Xi Xiao Jie didn't mind at all. With a smile on his face, he said, "If not, then we'll have to wait until tomorrow! I wonder when sister will get married! "
The girl finally opened her eyes. With a smile on her face, she scolded, "You stinking brat, you're never serious!"
This girl was the girl who possessed the Extreme Violet Yin Body, her name was Xi Youqin.
The Xi family was really anxious right now. Cui Xie's ambition had shrouded the entire Central Domain, and all the sects were in danger. The prosperity of the Void Spirit Sect was closely related to the Xi family, and if the Void Spirit Sect fell, the Xi family would have a hard time as well!
Right now, the most important matter for Cui Xie was the Heavenly Demon Joyous Union Array. For this Heavenly Demon Joyous Union Array, Cui Xie had waited for several decades. But in the end, because of Si Miaoling's death, he had no choice but to delay the setting up of the array.
But now, the Xi family had a girl who possessed the Extreme Violet Yin Body. How could the Xi family not be anxious?
Some time ago, they discovered that the spies of the Tian Xie Sect had sneaked into the Void Sky City, which meant that the Tian Xie Sect had targeted Xi Youqin, causing the entire Void Sky City to be in a state of panic. For a time, rumors spread everywhere that the Tian Xie Sect was going to attack.
The several big families of the Void Sky City repeatedly ran to the Xi family to ask the Xi family to marry off their precious daughter as soon as possible. Their words were indeed a little tactful. If it weren't for Taoist Priest Qing Xu, they probably would have directly snatched her away.
To put it bluntly, there were only two paths in front of Xi Youqin. She could either get married quickly or be killed. In short, she couldn't fall into the hands of Cui Xie.

Chapter 672
After Xi Xiaojie finished laughing, his expression gradually became solemn, and he said seriously, "Today in the Void Spirit Sect, Master told me that I can't let you be willful anymore."
The Master Xi Xiaojie mentioned was naturally the Sect Master of the Void Spirit Sect, Daoist Priest Qing Xu.
If it wasn't for Daoist Priest Qing Xu, how could they allow Xi Youqin to be so willful?
Even if Daoist Priest Qing Xu protected Xi Youqin, the people of the Void Spirit Sect and Void Sky City had a lot of complaints in their hearts. At this time, of course, they had to consider the big picture and sacrifice a daughter of the Xi family. Not to mention marrying those young elites, even if she had to marry a pig or a dog, she would still marry them!
"Did Grandpa say that as well?" Hearing Xi Xiaojie's words, a trace of sadness flashed on Xi Youqin's face.
Xi Xiaojie was a year younger than Xi Youqin, but in the end, he had been training in the Void Spirit Sect, so his thoughts were more mature than Xi Youqin's. He said, "Sister, Master is also under a lot of pressure. In this situation, Cui Xie will probably plan to make a move!"
"How dare he? Our Void Spirit Sect is the number one sect in the Central Domain. If Cui Xie wants to make a move, he won't look for our Void Spirit Sect first," Xi Youqin retorted unhappily.
Xi Xiaojie smiled helplessly and said, "Why won't he look for the Void Spirit Sect? The top three forces in the Central Domain are the Void Spirit Sect, the World Business Alliance, and the Cloud Hall. If the Tian Xie Sect wants to make a move, they will definitely look for one of the three forces. The World Business Alliance has that ancient giant, so Heaven Awaken City can't be taken down in a short period of time. As for the Cloud Hall … Cloud Sea City is nothing, but the Cloud Hall has that extremely abnormal Sect Protecting Formation, so it will be difficult for the Tian Xie Sect to take it down. Although our Void Spirit Sect is called the strongest sect in the Central Domain, it is relatively easy to bully! "
Xi Xiaojie's analysis was indeed very reasonable.
Cui Xie was still very wary of the General Commerce League's influence. As for the Cloud Hall, Cui Xie had suffered a great loss in the Cloud Hall's Sect Guarding Formation. Comparatively speaking, it was the safest choice to start a war between the sects and find a breakthrough in the Void Spirit Sect. Moreover, the Void Spirit Sect had the strongest reputation and was the most powerful sect in the hearts of the martial artists in the central region. If he took down the Void Spirit Sect, there would definitely be a large number of independent martial artists who would defect to the Heavenly Evil Sect!
"If Cui Xie attacks Void Sky City, you are not the biggest reason, but you are definitely one of the reasons," Xi Xiaojie said seriously, "If you marry someone, you will cut off Cui Xie's thoughts. At least when the Tian Xie Sect attacks, no one will throw the responsibility to you!"
Xi Youqin looked gentle and quiet, like an orchid in a deep valley. She looked docile, but there was a stubborn force in her bones. When she heard Xi Xiaojie talking to her seriously, she suddenly said, "So what if I'm responsible? At worst, I'll die in the end. "
Xi Xiaojie also knew that he couldn't talk to her, so he just said, "Sister, I'm afraid you won't be able to die even if you want to."
These words suddenly made Xi Youqin's body tremble, and panic finally appeared on her face. For a moment, she didn't know what to say.
Cui Xie was a man who was neither good nor evil, and if Xi Youqin fell into Cui Xie's hands, it wasn't hard to imagine what would happen to her!
But, Xi Youqin's panic only lasted for a split second, before it was replaced by a ruthless expression. "If that's the case, I'll prepare some poison. If Cui Xie really attacks us, I'll just take the poison and kill myself."
Xi Xiaojie let out a long sigh, then he left the small courtyard with the other two servants.
Early in the morning of the second day, a young man wearing a green robe appeared in the square in front of the Xi family's entrance. It was too late last night, so Luo Zheng had rested for the night. When dawn broke, he finally arrived at the Xi family's castle after traveling through half of Void Sky City.
When he arrived at the square in front of the Xi family's entrance, he saw a lot of people lining up. The line was like a long dragon, and there were over a hundred people in the square.
These people were a mix of good and bad people. There were burly men, scholars with jade crowns, and as for cultivation … there were even some mortals who didn't have any cultivation, and there were even some Divine Core warriors.
"Is this, is this a groom selection?" Luo Zheng who was standing next to the line said with a smile on his face. It looked like the Xi family was really anxious. However, he had heard that this groom selection had been going on for quite some time, and he wondered if the young lady of the Xi family had eyes on her head, and didn't take a fancy to any of the men.
"Newcomer, you! If you want to marry me, go to the back of the line!" When a servant of the Xi family saw Luo Zheng, he pointed his finger at Luo Zheng and scolded him. This servant probably thought Luo Zheng was going to cut the line.
Luo Zheng wasn't angry at all. This servant was just an ordinary man, so of course he couldn't see through Luo Zheng's cultivation. As for the other warriors in the line, when they saw Luo Zheng's Void Tribulation realm cultivation, their expressions immediately changed.
Breaking through to the Void Tribulation realm at such a young age? Which big sect did this guy come from?
In the Sea God Continent, it wasn't rare for warriors to break through to the Combat Sovereign realm at the same age as Luo Zheng. For example, Mu Qingyang, Xia Shuang, and the others could also break through to the Combat Sovereign realm if they wanted to.
With the help of the Tower of Sin, the speed of cultivation was much faster than the outside world.
However, in the Central Region, reaching the Illuminating Spirit Realm at such a young age was a bit inconceivable. Only those large Sects would be able to accomplish this if they poured in all their resources, so none of these martial artists dared to look down on Luo Zheng.
"I'm not here to marry you." Luo Zheng replied with an indifferent tone.
"We're very busy right now. If you're not here to marry me, then don't join in the fun. Go somewhere else and have fun!" The servant bluntly started chasing Luo Zheng away. As a servant of the Xi family, even ordinary warriors wouldn't dare offend him.
Luo Zheng said with an indifferent tone, "I'm here to visit the Xi family's Patriarch, I wonder …"
The servant sneered. "Visit the Xi family's Patriarch? You? " Some warriors thought that their status was special and wanted to go through the family's Patriarch's back door. The servant clearly misunderstood Luo Zheng and said, "Our Patriarch doesn't have time to entertain guests! If you don't leave, I'm going to call for help! "
Right at this moment, an elderly steward walked over from the side and scolded the servant, "You blind fool, how can you open your mouth to speak like that?! Go busy yourself! "
The steward of the Xi Family was a Xiantian lifeform. When he saw the dispute from afar, he came over to take a look. What he saw gave him a fright. That servant was actually berating a False Tribulation realm martial artist!
In the Central Domain, False Tribulation realm warriors were not ordinary existences. The Xi family was a third-grade family, and the current Patriarch was only at the middle stage of False Tribulation realm. The person in front of him was so young, he must have a heaven-defying background. Therefore, the steward of the Xi family hurriedly drove the servant to the side and bowed to Luo Zheng. "Friend, our Xi family's servant has no eyes, please forgive him."
Luo Zheng didn't have much expression on his face. How could he be angry with an ordinary person? He just said lightly, "I don't know if I'm guilty or not, but I want to pay a visit to your Xi family's Patriarch."
"The Patriarch has been busy recently, but if you want to pay a visit, I will naturally help you pass on the message. However, please tell us your name," the steward said respectfully. He had been in the Xi family for dozens of years, so he naturally understood that this person in front of him couldn't be neglected.
Luo Zheng nodded and said to the steward, "Say that Luo Zheng is here to pay a visit to him."
"Luo, Luo Zheng?" The steward suddenly shouted, his eyes staring at Luo Zheng as if he had seen a ghost. Because his voice was too loud, it immediately alarmed the many warriors who had come to propose marriage.
Many warriors also came over with puzzled faces.
"Did you hear that? That kid called himself Luo Zheng, is he the Luo Zheng from the Cloud Hall? "
"Isn't Luo Zheng dead? It's been almost two years, but there's no news about him at all. It's like he disappeared from the world! "
"It's really possible. Besides Luo Zheng, how many people can step into the False Tribulation realm in just two years? Oh, right, there's also the god-level genius of the Void Spirit Sect, Xiao Jie … "
The steward of the Xi family looked at Luo Zheng in a daze, and then asked cautiously, "You, you are really Luo Zheng?"
Luo Zheng smiled faintly. "Of course."

Chapter 673
After a while, several streaks of light flew out from the Xi Family Castle, immediately attracting the attention of many suitors.
One of the streaks of light directly rushed into the square and shouted, "Luo Zheng!"
This person was Xi Xiaojie.
Two years ago, during the Martial Arts Competition, because of a sudden change, Xi Xiaojie missed the opportunity to challenge Luo Zheng, which made him feel very regretful.
In these two years, Xi Xiaojie's strength had also soared, but Luo Zheng had disappeared without a trace, and there were rumours everywhere that he had fallen. Now that he suddenly heard the news of Luo Zheng, how could Xi Xiaojie not be excited? He rushed out almost immediately, and even felt a little nervous because he suspected that someone was pretending to be Luo Zheng.
He didn't expect that it was indeed Luo Zheng!
Seeing Xi Xiaojie, Luo Zheng also showed a surprised expression. He knew that this person was a god-grade genius of the Void Spirit Sect, but he didn't know that he was a member of the Xi Family.
"Xiaojie of the Void Spirit Sect, I trust you have been well since we last met." Luo Zheng nodded, but he didn't show any excitement.
"You have also stepped into the False Tribulation Realm! Let's fight, okay? "Xi Xiaojie wanted to challenge Luo Zheng as soon as they met, so he didn't look at the situation at all.
Of course, Luo Zheng wouldn't agree. He didn't come here to challenge Xi Xiaojie, but for the young lady of the Xi Family.
"Xiaojie, don't mess around!" A middle-aged man followed closely behind Xi Xiaojie. Some sharp-eyed people had already recognized that this person was the Xi Family Patriarch, Xi Xinghuai.
Xi Xinghuai cupped his hands towards Luo Zheng and said, "I heard that Young Hero Luo Zheng has come to visit my Xi Family, and I am deeply honored. This is not a place to receive guests, please come in!"
Luo Zheng also cupped his hands towards Xi Xinghuai, and then followed the cultivators of the Xi Family into the Xi Family Castle.
How fast was Xi Xinghuai's brain working?
There had to be a reason why Luo Zheng had disappeared for two years, and Luo Zheng was neither a relative nor a friend of the Xi Family. It wasn't difficult to guess why he had come to the Xi Family today, it must be for the Extreme Violet Yin Body. Because of Xi Youqin's matter, Xi Xinghuai had been under a lot of pressure recently, and Luo Zheng had come at the right time!
Inside the Xi Family Fort, Luo Zheng had just sat down and exchanged a few simple words with the Xi Family. Cultivators were not scholars, so there was no need for unnecessary formalities. Xi Xinghuai then opened his mouth and said, "Young Hero Luo Zheng, did you come to my Xi Family Fort for my daughter?"
Xi Xinghuai, Xi Xinghuai said, Xi Xinghuai's family, Xi Xinghuai of Xi.......
. "Xi Xinghuai clapped his hands and ordered a servant next to him," Go and find Youqin. "
Xi Xinghuai sighed, "It's my fault for spoiling this child too much. Since Young Hero Luo Zheng came here, you should know the whole story, but now you are in a hurry to find someone to entrust her to, otherwise I really don't know how to deal with this matter."
Xi Xinghuai said this because he wanted to hear Luo Zheng's opinion. Luo Zheng also saw the long line outside, and his words were actually a naked hint.
Xi Xiaojie was silent, but there was a naughty smile on his face. In his heart, he was thinking that if Luo Zheng became his brother-in-law, he would have many opportunities in the future to fight Luo Zheng sooner or later.
How could Luo Zheng not understand Xi Xinghuai's meaning?
But he couldn't really marry her just because this matter was urgent, could he? What's more, Xi Family's Young Lady might not be willing.
Luo Zheng said, "Xi Family's Young Lady has Extreme Purple Yin Body, so it is indeed inappropriate for her to stay in Void Sky City. I want to take her to the Cloud Hall to temporarily avoid the limelight. The Sect Protecting Array of the Cloud Hall should be able to resist for a while."
Xi Xinghuai's face showed a trace of disappointment. Xi Xiaojie was the adopted son of Xi Family, and Luo Zheng was no stranger to Xi Family. It was said that this boy's talent was even better than Xi Xiaojie's. If he could accept Luo Zheng as his Xi Family's son-in-law, the benefits to Xi Family were obvious!
But listening to Luo Zheng's evasive tone, Xi Xinghuai thought, 'Well, maybe after he sees my daughter, he will change his mind. If Luo Zheng agrees, Xi Youqin won't be able to be capricious this time!' Xi Xinghuai thought secretly, and a trace of decisiveness slowly appeared on his face. This matter really couldn't be delayed any longer!
"If Young Hero Luo Zheng can persuade my daughter to go to the Cloud Hall with you, it will naturally be a solution, but as long as my daughter is not married, Cui Xie will not stop thinking about it. We still have to think of a way to solve this once and for all," Xi Xinghuai said.
At this time, Xun suddenly transmitted a voice message to Luo Zheng and said with a smile, "This old guy wants to give his daughter to you! I guess that girl looks like a pig's head, so he can't give her to you! "
Luo Zheng was too lazy to pay attention to Xun's words, and just nodded to Xi Xinghuai. At this time, two maids opened the door, and then a layer of prestige mixed with the fragrance of a young girl came in. A young girl in plain clothes walked in. This was Xi Youqin.
"Youqin, come and greet him," Xi Xinghuai hurriedly said.
The servant only asked Xi Youqin to go over, so how could he know who she was going to meet? She thought that it was some young master from a wealthy family who begged the head of the family to meet her directly. Xi Youqin had always been very resistant to this matter, so how could she have a good expression after coming in?
So when she heard Xi Xinghuai's words, she quickly walked over, and her elegant face like a white lotus seemed to be covered with a layer of frost. Xi Youqin glanced at Luo Zheng indifferently and said coldly, "It doesn't matter whether I greet him or not. You are not qualified to marry me. This young master who came out of nowhere, please go back!"
Seeing this scene, Xi Xiaojie secretly glanced at Xi Xinghuai, and his heart sank. He immediately knew that it was bad!
Sure enough, Xi Xinghuai's face was as gloomy as a piece of black charcoal. He suddenly got up and took a few steps towards Xi Youqin, and said coldly and angrily, "Youqin, you are too presumptuous! Don't think that just because great-grandfather loves you, you can mess around in the Xi family! "
The great-grandfather that Xi Xinghuai mentioned was Taoist Priest Qingxu.
It was because Taoist Priest Qingxu loved Xi Youqin that he, Xi Xinghuai, as her father, didn't dare to control Xi Youqin. However, he didn't expect this girl's temper to be so stubborn. He originally racked his brains to make Luo Zheng speak, but now it seemed that Luo Zheng himself didn't have this intention. However, his own daughter came up and said such words. How could Xi Xinghuai not be angry?
Being despised for no reason, Luo Zheng's face didn't show any anger. What's more, Luo Zheng didn't come to propose marriage.
"How am I messing around? Father, at most, you will force me to death! "Xi Youqin's face showed a resolute expression. This girl's temper was not ordinary.
Xi Xinghuai raised his hand and was about to slap her, but Xi Youqin's face was straight and didn't have the slightest intention of dodging. At this moment, Luo Zheng's figure flashed and stopped Xi Xinghuai. Luo Zheng then said, "Patriarch, please calm down. I have a few words to say to Miss Xi."
In front of outsiders, Xi Xinghuai couldn't lose his composure too much. He could only forcibly suppress the anger in his heart. He was really angry.
Luo Zheng stared at Xi Youqin indifferently and then said, "Miss Xi, you misunderstood. I didn't come to propose marriage."
Xi Youqin's face showed a mocking expression, "I know. You just want to get to know me and show me your strength and eloquence. Let me know which family you are from and how much potential you have in the future … Isn't your final goal the same? People like you can only be described with one word, hypocrite! You are even more hateful than those guys who open their mouths to propose marriage! "
"Pfft …" Hearing his sister's words, Xi Xiaojie couldn't help but burst out laughing.
"Xiaojie, what are you laughing at? What's so funny! "Xi Youqin turned her head and scolded Xi Xiaojie.
Luo Zheng was also speechless. He didn't expect this quiet and elegant Miss Xi to be so fierce and have such a rich imagination.
At this time, Luo Zheng sternly said, "I don't want to get to know you. You have the Extreme Purple Yin Body. It's too dangerous for you to stay here. I want to take you away from Void Sky City."
"Take me away? With just you? "The mocking expression on Xi Youqin's face became even more intense.
"Come with me, or die now. Choose one of the two." Luo Zheng's tone was unusually flat, but no one doubted that he could do what he said.
