Chapter 674
Luo Zheng's words were not a joke. In Luo Zheng's heart, Luo Yan was the most important.
Just like Si Miaoling in the past, Xi Youqin's existence was a threat to Luo Yan. However, Si Miaoling stood in the position of Luo Zheng's enemy, while Xi Youqin in front of him was very innocent.
But regardless of whether Xi Youqin was innocent or not, Luo Zheng could not tolerate Luo Yan being threatened. Therefore, Luo Zheng decisively told this Xi Family's young lady that she could either follow him back to the Cloud Hall or be killed. It was that simple.
So when Luo Zheng said this, Xi Youqin was suddenly stunned.
She was free, intelligent, and lovable. In her generation, other than her adopted son, Xi Xiaojie, she was the youngest. She was the apple of the family's eye. Not to mention the many elders of the Xi Family who loved her very much, even the Grand Grandfather who had always been elusive would take the time to come back to the Xi Family to see her!
After Taoist Priest Qing Xu took charge of the Xu Ling Sect, he rarely appeared in the Xi Family. Even if Xi Xinghuai wanted to see him, it would be very troublesome. However, Taoist Priest Qing Xu could not forget his great-granddaughter. At this time, he was actually willing to send Xi Xiaojie to accompany Xi Youqin because he knew that Xi Youqin and Xi Xiaojie had a good relationship.
It could be imagined to what extent Taoist Priest Qing Xu doted on her?
It was also because of this that the Xi Family would repeatedly endure and give Xi Youqin enough time to be willful. This betrothal search had been going on for so long, but they still had not picked a suitable candidate.
If she was just a concubine's daughter in the Xi Family, she would have been coaxed and scared to marry out.
Xi Youqin never thought that the man in front of her would be so arrogant that he dared to say that he would take her life!
Xi Xiaojie and Xi Xinghuai were also stunned. They could see that Luo Zheng was not joking. As for the other Xi Family members in the hall, such as Xi Youqin's elders, their faces showed unkind expressions.
Although everyone had a headache about Xi Youqin's marriage, it was because they doted on Xi Youqin. You, an outsider, dared to come to the Xi Family's territory and treat Xi Youqin like this?
Xi Xiaojie frowned... Although he was an adopted son, he grew up with Xi Youqin since childhood. They really had a feeling that they were related by blood. They were like biological siblings. How could he let Luo Zheng really hurt Xi? Xi?
Just as Xi Xiaojie was about to step forward and speak to separate Xi Youqin and Luo Zheng, Xi Xinghuai waved his hand and stopped Xi Xiaojie.
Looking at Xi Xinghuai's action, Xi Xiaojie's face showed a confused expression. Xi Youqin was his daughter, wasn't he worried at all?
While puzzled, Xi Xiaojie chose to bide his time.
Xi Youqin was Xi Xinghuai's youngest daughter. How could he not worry about Xi Youqin's safety? However, Xi Xinghuai had an intuition. He felt that Luo Zheng would not really do it, or rather, he felt that his daughter was willful to this extent. Someone should really teach her a lesson!
Xi Youqin's status in the Xi Family was needless to say. No one dared to offend her. Even he, her father, did not dare to do anything. Most importantly, everyone had to give face to Daoist Priest Qing Xu. Although Daoist Priest Qing Xu rarely returned to the Xi Family Castle, he was the backbone of the Xi Family.
Outside of the Xi Family Castle, in this big Void Sky City, who had the qualifications to teach Xi Youqin a lesson?
The young man in front of him was different. The legend of Luo Zheng berating Cui Xie was still vivid in his mind. It had even been compiled into a story that was played and sung in the taverns and teahouses. It had become a story told by storytellers. Not to mention mortals, even some cultivators loved to listen to it.
This Luo Zheng was not even afraid of Cui Xie, so how could he be afraid of Daoist Priest Qing Xu?
He wanted to see if this Luo Zheng had the ability to subdue his daughter!
"Let me die? Do you have the ability? " Xi Youqin's face was deathly pale, but her stubborn temper had not been cultivated in a day or two. Even though she intuitively sensed that Luo Zhenghui would do as he said, she still bit her lips and persisted.
What's more, there were so many elders of the Xi Family present!
The young man in front of her had the cultivation of the False Tribulation Realm, which indeed made Xi Youqin somewhat afraid, but a cultivator at the early stage of the False Tribulation Realm couldn't make a big wave in her Xi Family. At least her father was a cultivator at the middle stage of the False Tribulation Realm, and Xi Xiaojie had just entered the False Tribulation Realm. With his god-grade talent, he should be more powerful than this guy in front of her, right?
Luo Zheng's eyes were calm as he stared at Xi Youqin. "I said, you have two choices. Follow me, or I kill you now. Choose one."
"What if I don't choose?" Xi Youqin stared at Luo Zheng and asked.
"Then I'll help you choose!" Luo Zheng snorted lightly and directly reached out to grab Xi Youqin.
"You dare!" An elder of the Xi Family shouted in a stern voice.
However, Xi Xinghuai glared fiercely at that elder and also stopped him.
Seeing that her father not only didn't help her but also stopped the other members of the Xi Family from attacking, Xi Youqin's face revealed a trace of despair. A teenage girl was the easiest to feel self-pity and hurt. She immediately burst into tears. "If I die, then I die!"
As soon as her voice fell, Luo Zheng's hand had already grabbed her white and flawless neck. It was at this moment that Luo Zheng said, "You're not afraid of death because you never knew what death was!"
Suddenly, killing intent surged out from Luo Zheng's body!
Luo Zheng had accumulated a lot of killing intent in the Tower of Sin and the Mysterious Underworld Cave. When he suddenly brought it out, the rich killing intent suddenly erupted, as if a demon god had descended!
Killing intent was also the aura of slaughter. It wasn't an aura that gradually accumulated from killing. It was also a kind of curse that was left behind by living creatures on the verge of death for Luo Zheng. It was the resentment of those living creatures after they killed Luo Zheng!
When this monstrous killing intent gushed out, the hearts of the tens of thousands of people in the Xi Family Fort suddenly trembled. Even those outside the Xi Family Fort were alarmed.
"Then, then what's wrong with the Xi Family Fort? Could it be that Cui Xie has sneaked in? Why is his killing intent soaring to the sky! "
"I don't think so. Although Cui Xie is both good and evil, he doesn't walk the path of slaughter. He doesn't have such a fierce killing intent!"
"Then who is it? Who dares to mess around in the Xi Family Fort? Are they not afraid of the Void Spirit Sect? "
Many cultivators speculated. They didn't dare to barge into the Xi Family Fort, so they could only make wild guesses.
Xi Youqin felt like she was about to suffocate. She felt like she wasn't facing a person, but a demon that crawled out of hell!
This person would really kill her without hesitation!
She grew up in the Xi Family Fort. She was a flower that grew up under the careful care of a greenhouse. How could she withstand any wind and rain? She was even worse than Yuan Qingwei that Luo Zheng encountered in the Fish Dragon Sect!
She felt that she wasn't afraid of death because she had never encountered the danger of death before. She was also confident that the Xi Family would never leave her alone!
But now, Xi Youqin felt like she was incomparably close to the god of death. The man in front of her only needed to exert a little force and she would definitely die. She was already hovering on the edge of life and death!
Why didn't father save her! Why didn't Xi Xiaojie do anything! Why were the Xi Family Elders indifferent? Xi Youqin finally panicked. In this situation, her survival instinct suddenly rose.
"Father … save me!"
She even forgot to cry. Because of fear, her elegant and clean voice became very sharp.
But Xi Xinghuai was indifferent, as if he didn't hear it.
The evil spirit released by Luo Zheng was still growing. At the same time, he also released a part of his soul pressure. Xi Youqin definitely couldn't withstand Luo Zheng's soul pressure. If the pressure was too great, she might directly faint. So this pressure wasn't very strong, but it was enough to make her feel the threat was constantly increasing!
"Are you sure you still want to choose death?" Luo Zheng stared at the girl in front of him. To be honest, he didn't like to be a bad person. This Xi Family Miss was elegant and quiet. Ordinary men would have a feeling of pity and love when they saw her. Luo Zheng was no exception, but now his behavior was completely opposite.
Xi Family Miss' lips were pale. When Luo Zheng's evil spirit and soul pressure slightly reduced, she immediately said, "I, I choose to go with you!"
All the evil spirit was instantly stored in the Slaughter Sword Mountain by Luo Zheng. At the same time, the soul pressure also disappeared without a trace. At this time, Xi Youqin almost involuntarily took a deep breath, and then gasped for breath.
Seeing Xi Youqin like this, many Xi Family Elders, including Xi Xiaojie and Xi Xinghuai, felt very distressed, but they were indeed helpless.
Xi Youqin was like a frightened bird, staring at Luo Zheng with a face full of fear. When facing death, Xi Youqin chose to retreat, but at the same time, she hated Luo Zheng to the bone.

Chapter 675
Xi Youqin stared at Luo Zheng with a complicated expression, then she asked, "Tell me your name!"
Although Xi Youqin stayed in her room, she knew a lot about the young geniuses in the Central Domain. Although she didn't participate in the Martial Dao Competition two years ago, before Xi Youqin left, she had helped Xi Youqin analyze all his potential opponents.
The man in front of her had broken through to the Void Tribulation realm at such a young age, Xi Youqin knew he wasn't a nobody.
Just now, when Xi Xinghuai had sent a servant to fetch Xi Youqin, of course, the servant wouldn't tell Xi Youqin who she had met today. Xi Youqin had just met Luo Zheng, and she had immediately caused a ruckus. Xi Xinghuai didn't even have the chance to introduce Luo Zheng to her.
"After I tell you my name, I'll find a chance to take revenge?" Luo Zheng stared at Xi Youqin and asked with a smile on his face.
"That's right!" Xi Youqin didn't try to hide anything. Xi Youqin was only an Early Divine Core warrior, but she had the Extreme Purple Yin Body. When the Extreme Purple Yin Body was cultivated to a very high level, it would be extremely frightening! When the Extreme Purple Yin Body broke through to the Divine Core realm, it would be able to stay in the Extreme Purple Realm for a short period of time, and when it broke through to the Void Tribulation realm, it would be able to stay in the Extreme Purple Realm for a long period of time. Not only were there 99 billion gigantic swords in the Extreme Purple Realm, there were also True Spirits! However, the price of summoning a True Spirit was extremely high.
Si Miaoling had tried to summon a True Spirit during the Martial Dao Competition, and according to Qinglong, once Si Miaoling succeeded, Luo Zheng would definitely die.
Hearing Xi Youqin's threat, Luo Zheng replied with a faint smile on his face, "I hope that day will come. My name is Luo Zheng."
"Luo Zheng?" When Xi Youqin heard this name, her brows furrowed slightly. After a moment, her expression suddenly changed and she looked at Xi Xiaojie. "Xiaojie, he's the Luo Zheng you've been talking about all the time!"
Although Xi Youqin had never left Void Sky City, she had heard of Luo Zheng's name more than a thousand times!
Ever since the Martial Arts Conference, every time Xi Xiaojie returned home from the Void Spirit Sect, he would always talk about Luo Zheng. It seemed that not being able to fight with Luo Zheng in this life was a very regrettable thing.
Later on, there were more and more legends about Luo Zheng. It was said that his talent was even higher than Cui Xie's, and he had even used his own rune technique to arrange an incredible Sect Protecting Array, which had forcibly blocked the insufferably arrogant Cui Xie!
Everyone commented that even Feng Guanyu's attainments in rune technique were far inferior to Luo Zheng's.
Feng Guanyu started his career in the Void Spirit Sect, so his name was the most famous in the Void Sky City and the Void Spirit Sect. This legendary figure had a huge statue erected in the south of the Void Sky City. When Xi Youqin was very young, every time she went to the south of the city, someone would tell her how amazing Feng Guanyu was. The Sect Protecting Array that he had set up was still protecting the Void Spirit Sect even now.
However, Luo Zheng had easily surpassed Feng Guanyu!
When Xi Youqin heard that Luo Zheng's talent was greater than Xi Xiaojie's, she felt that it was incredible. After all, in Xi Youqin's eyes, her younger brother was the most talented person in the Central Domain, a god-level genius! As long as he didn't die, he would definitely be able to enter the Divine Sea Realm! He was completely treated like a treasure in the Void Spirit Sect, but there was actually a young elite in the Central Domain who was even more amazing than her younger brother.
In the end, not only was this person's talent in cultivation powerful, even his attainments in rune technique were rarely seen in the world. No, it should be said that he was unparalleled in the world because no one in the Central Domain had ever displayed rune technique to such a high level. What was even more difficult to accept was that Luo Zheng was merely a youth who hadn't even reached 18 years of age at that time …
Therefore, Xi Youqin, who lived in her boudoir, was filled with curiosity towards this person. She thought that if she had the chance, she would like to meet this Luo Zheng and see what kind of person he was. However, after the trip to the Sky Mist Immortal Tomb, Luo Zheng had directly disappeared.
Even Xi Xiaojie, who had participated in the trip to the Sky Mist Immortal Tomb, guessed that Luo Zheng had most likely died in the immortal tomb.
Regarding this, Xi Youqin had always felt regretful. After all, Xi Xiaojie had been nagging at her for a long time, but she hadn't been able to meet this person.
Now, Xi Youqin sized up Luo Zheng. Two years ago, Luo Zheng was 18 years old. Now, Luo Zheng looked to be in his early twenties. Their ages were indeed very similar, but wasn't Luo Zheng dead? Why was he standing in front of her, perfectly fine?
Xi Xiaojie blinked and said helplessly, "Sister, he's that Luo Zheng."
At this moment, Xi Youqin understood why Luo Zheng only gave her two choices. Luo Zheng's sister, Luo Yan, had fallen into the hands of Cui Xie. Everyone in the Central Domain knew about this. When Xi Youqin heard about this, she was very touched. This man was willing to sacrifice everything for his sister.
Now, Xi Youqin naturally understood that if she fell into the hands of Cui Xie, her sister would also be implicated!
But even so, this man was too hateful! He actually dared to treat her like this. Xi Youqin was shocked, but her face didn't change much. This was her personality. Xi Youqin still gritted her teeth and said, "Even if you're Luo Zheng, I will still have my revenge one day!"
"I'll be waiting," Luo Zheng replied with a smile on his face.
Xi Xinghuai, who was next to him, breathed a sigh of relief and said, "Since you've made the decision, it's good. In fact, the Cloud Hall Master of your Cloud Hall has also invited Xi Youqin to temporarily stay, but this child is too stubborn and refuses to go to the Cloud Hall. It's already noon now, and the servants have already prepared lunch. After lunch, we can set off."
Void Sky City and Cloud Sea City were connected by a transmission array, so it was very convenient to go to the Cloud Hall.
Luo Zheng nodded and said politely, "Then I'll have to trouble you."
Only then did everyone move and go to eat. Xi Youqin walked towards another exit and said, "I'm not going to eat with this guy! I'll come out after lunch! "
A trace of a bitter smile appeared on Xi Xinghuai's face. He could only smile at Luo Zheng and said, "There's nothing I can do about it. My daughter is like this."
Luo Zheng naturally didn't care. However, he hadn't expected that because of this lunch, there would be a huge change in the situation!
The transmission array of Void Sky City was built in the west, and this was the most prosperous area of Void Sky City. The two transmission arrays were built in the open space by the Celestial Silkworm River to the west of Void Sky City. One of the two transmission arrays was used to teleport to Cloud Sea City, and the other to Tianqi City!
The cost of constructing a teleportation formation was huge. Even though such teleportation formations couldn't be compared to large-scale teleportation formations, they could still traverse a small portion of the central territory. Moreover, these 3 cities were the largest cities in the central territory, and they had the most cultivators as well. So, countless cultivators utilized these teleportation formations to head to Ocean of Clouds City or Heavenly Awakening City every single day.
Even some ordinary merchants would pay a high price to transport some valuable goods. Similarly, they also needed to use these transmission arrays.
The frequency of use of these transmission arrays was extremely high, so the cost of maintenance was even higher.
Today, there was a long line by the Celestial Silkworm River.
During this month, the two transmission arrays had become even busier. Many people had heard that Cui Xie was going to lead the cultivators of the Tian Xie Sect to attack Void Sky City, so they had no choice but to leave and head to either Tianqi City or Cloud Sea City to avoid being caught in the crossfire.
The cost of using the transmission arrays had risen along with it, and it had doubled. Even then, many people were willing to pay a high price. After all, money was just a mere worldly possession when compared to their lives.
All the cultivators were waiting patiently in line. Right at this moment, two cultivators had started to cut in line.
Some of the cultivators had been waiting for around half a day. When they saw those two cultivators cut in line, the other cultivators couldn't stand it any longer, and one of them criticized, "Hey! Hey! Why are you cutting in line!?"
"Yeah! Even if it's an emergency, it should be first come first served, right?"
"That fellow is really lacking in manners!"
The two cultivators turned around when they heard the others criticizing them, and one of them laughed coldly, "I'm cutting in line today. So what? Do you want to fight me? "
His words caused all the cultivators who were criticizing them to shut their mouths. Most of the cultivators in line were Xiantian realm warriors, and only a few of them were Divine Reflection realm warriors. The two cultivators in front of them were Divine Core warriors, and the gap between them was huge. How could these cultivators afford to offend them?
Seeing that the crowd had fallen silent, the two cultivators revealed mocking smiles on their faces. "All of you have such big mouths, why don't you take a look at yourselves?"
The entire line slowly approached the Teleportation Array in silence. White lights rose up into the sky, indicating that someone had entered the dimensional tunnel. Right when the two Divine Core warriors were approaching the Teleportation Array, one of them said in a lazy manner, "Alright, let's do it!"

Chapter 676
There were some guards from the Void Sky City standing next to the Teleportation Formation, but everything happened too quickly. These guards were only at the Divine Reflection realm, so there was no way they could react in time.
Besides, destroying a Teleportation Formation was something that would harm others without benefiting oneself. Under normal circumstances, no one would intentionally destroy a Teleportation Formation. After all, destroying a Teleportation Formation wouldn't bring any benefits to oneself.
When all the warriors saw the two Divine Core warriors throwing the black beads into the Teleportation Formation, they immediately backed off. Then, they saw a purplish black bead appear in the middle of the Teleportation Formation, and tiny lightning bolts were shooting out from the bead.
After that, the lightning bolts suddenly exploded, emitting a blinding light and a powerful Spatial Energy. At the same time, loud explosive sounds could be heard.
"Clap, clap!"
When the ear-piercing explosive sounds were heard, everything within a 30 meter radius from the Teleportation Formation was devoured by the bright light. All the warriors who were lining up and the guards who were guarding the Teleportation Formation were instantly killed by the bright light. They didn't even have the chance to scream before they were turned into ashes.
"Those two men destroyed the Teleportation Formation!"
"That's a Dimensional Thunder Bead! It contains the Law of Lightning and the Law of Space, and it can completely destroy the spatial tunnel! "
"Why did they destroy the Teleportation Formation?"
Some warriors who were standing further away from the Teleportation Formation had already identified the purplish black bead, and they couldn't help but shout out in shock.
It wasn't an easy task to destroy a Teleportation Formation. Once a Teleportation Formation was built, the entire spatial tunnel would be extremely stable. Even if the foundation of the Teleportation Formation was destroyed, the spatial tunnel wouldn't be damaged. All they had to do was rebuild the foundation.
However, the Spatial Quaking Thunder Bead was different. This kind of weapon was originally developed to deal with teleportation arrays. The power of lightning contained in the bead would destroy the foundation of the teleportation array, and the power of space contained in the bead would stir up the space channel in the teleportation channel, rendering the channel useless.
Right after the two Divine Core warriors destroyed the Dimensional Thunder Bead, another Teleportation Formation that led to Heavenly Awakening City was destroyed at the same time. Right now, both the paths leading to Cloud Sea City and Heavenly Awakening City were completely blocked.
"What, what is going on?"
"I think I know what's going on! These people are most likely from the Lascivious Sect! "
"By destroying the portal, the Cloud Hall and the World Business Alliance won't be able to help. Most likely … Cui Xie is going to make his move …"
Some people quickly guessed the truth of the matter.
In fact, it was not that the Void Sky City did not prepare in advance. The Void Spirit Sect had sent many martial artists to patrol the Void Sky City. The guards of these two teleportation arrays were only ordinary Divine Illumination Realm martial artists, but now, there were several Divine Illumination Realm martial artists!
It could only be said that if the Tian Xie Sect wanted to take action, it was really impossible to guard against it. Even if the Void Spirit Sect sent False Tribulation Realm martial artists to guard it, the Tian Xie Sect would very likely send False Tribulation Realm martial artists to destroy the teleportation array. After all, protecting something was much more difficult than destroying it.
Just now, some people guessed that the Tian Xie Sect was going to make their move, but in fact, the Tian Xie Sect had already made their move.
The entire Void Spirit Sect was built on top of a tall mountain, and it was adjacent to the Void Sky City. Right now, the entire Void Spirit Sect was covered in a light purple barrier, and within the Void Spirit Sect, there were several thousand runes that formed a circular Eight Trigrams symbol.
This Sect Protecting Formation was Feng Guanyu's legacy, and it was called the Thousand Illusionary Formation.
Before the Sect Protecting Formation created by Luo Zheng, the Thousand Illusionary Formation was the strongest Sect Protecting Formation in the Central Domain!
Outside of the Sect Protecting Formation, over ten thousand martial artists were floating in the air. Most of these martial artists were at the Divine Illumination Realm, and a small portion of them were at the Divine Core realm. There were also a few dozen False Tribulation Realm martial artists.
All these martial artists were wearing black robes, and two words were drawn on their chests, "Tian Xie".
These martial artists were the Tian Xie Sect's "Divine Kingdom Army"!
Other people referred to the Tian Xie Sect as the Tian Xie Sect, but the people of the Tian Xie Sect called themselves the Kingdom of Evil Deities.
All the martial artists in the Kingdom of Evil Deities were once independent martial artists.
The world of martial artists was extremely cruel. Compared to those martial artists who grew up in sects, the path of independent martial artists was much more difficult, and they were often bullied by the disciples of sects. Therefore, these independent martial artists more or less hated those big sects!
The reason why Cui Xie was able to successfully build his Divine Kingdom was because he had grasped the thoughts of these independent martial artists. He claimed that he would unite all the independent martial artists and overthrow all the sects in the Central Domain. Once his Divine Kingdom was completed, he would then divide the entire domain!
It was worth mentioning that Cui Xie's plan was perfect. Not only had he grasped the appetite of the independent martial artists, he had also promised them great benefits. Therefore, in just a short period of time, Cui Xie had won over a large number of followers.
When the sects saw that the situation was not right, they finally reacted and began to block the independent martial artists!
But it was too late. Cui Xie's "Kingdom of Evil Deities" expanded at an extremely fast speed and quickly grew into the largest force in the entire Central Domain!
If it was purely based on the sects' ranks, the Kingdom of Evil Deities was already qualified to be a sixth-rank sect. Of course, this "Kingdom of Evil Deities" lacked one or two Divine Sea Realm cultivators.
It was not so easy to break through to the Divine Sea Realm. Now, Cui Xie was also stuck at that bottleneck. Therefore, he was also in a hurry to find the third woman with the Extreme Purple Yin Body so that he could break through to the Divine Sea Realm one day. Only then would his Kingdom of Evil Deities be worthy of its name!
This was also one of the reasons why Cui Xie wanted to attack the Void Spirit Sect!
And if he attacked the Void Spirit Sect, his Kingdom of Evil Deities would become even more famous. At that time, the number of independent martial artists seeking refuge with Cui Xie would increase exponentially. Even the Cloud Hall and the World Business Alliance would not be able to stop them!
"Brothers, this is the number one sect in the world, the Void Spirit Sect! Has anyone been bullied by the Void Spirit Sect in the past? "Cui Xie suddenly shouted as he stood at the front of the Kingdom of Gods' army.
"Yes!"
Tens of thousands of martial artists, the lowest cultivation level was at the Divine Reflection Realm. The aura from this roar could really shake the earth and mountains.
In fact, although the Void Spirit Sect was the number one sect in the world, it was impossible for its power to spread to the entire Central Domain. It was impossible for all of these independent martial artists to have been bullied by the Void Spirit Sect.
However, as independent martial artists, they were more or less suppressed by the martial artists of the sect. For example, when they went to explore a dangerous place, independent martial artists would often travel alone. At most, they would travel in groups of two or three. However, the martial artists of the sect would often travel in groups of dozens. In the end, when they found opportunities and treasures, the independent martial artists would definitely be at a disadvantage!
However, the world of martial artists was like this. Whoever was stronger would get the treasures. It was just the law of the jungle. Thinking about it from another perspective, these independent martial artists would also do the same.
Of course, Cui Xie was stirring up their passion like this, so why would they think about it carefully?
"Very good. Then today is the day the Void Spirit Sect is destroyed!" Cui Xie said coldly.
At this moment, a figure flew out from the Kingdom of Gods' army. It was a fair-skinned scholar. This scholar was a middle stage False Tribulation Realm expert!
The scholar held an ancient folding fan in his hand. When the fan was opened, the word "Xuan" was written on it.
This person was the military advisor of the Kingdom of Gods' army, Xuan Chen. This person was once an independent martial artist and had some fame in the southern part of the Central Domain. However, because he had offended the people of the Mysterious Yin Mansion, his entire family had been killed by the Mysterious Yin Mansion.
Xuan Chen had been living in seclusion all these years. When he heard that Cui Xie had established a Kingdom of Gods, he realized that his opportunity had come.
In Xuan Chen's opinion, whether Cui Xie could really establish a Kingdom of Gods was not important. What was important was that he could borrow Cui Xie's strength to destroy the Mysterious Yin Mansion. Therefore, he and Cui Xie could be said to be using each other.
"I just received news that the young lady of the Xi family is not in the Void Spirit Sect, but in Void Sky City," Xuan Chen said indifferently.
"Oh?" Cui Xie's eyes flashed. "It seems that Taoist Priest Qing Xu really doesn't take me seriously. Haha, so I really should go to Void Sky City!"
"The two transmission arrays in Void Sky City have been destroyed, but I'm afraid that the Xi family would rather die than live in dishonor …" Xuan Chen said.
Cui Xie frowned, and a cautious look appeared on his face. This was indeed a possibility. How could the Xi family be willing to hand over the woman with the Extreme Violet Yin Body to him?

Chapter 677
Seeing Cui Xie's expression, Xuan Chen immediately said, "In addition to Miss Xi, there is another piece of news that you should be interested in."
"What news?" Cui Xie asked.
"Luo Zheng is back," Xuan Chen immediately said.
"Luo Zheng! Hmph, he really is back! "When Cui Xie heard this name, a murderous look flashed across his face!
There were rumors in the Central Domain that Luo Zheng was dead, but only Cui Xie didn't believe it.
Luo Zheng's performance in the Wheel of Providence was very clear. Cui Xie didn't believe that Luo Zheng would die! Unless Luo Zheng was personally killed by Cui Xie, he wouldn't believe that Luo Zheng was really dead.
"What do you plan to do now?" Xuan Chen carefully asked when he saw the murderous look on Cui Xie's face.
"Stop attacking the Void Spirit Sect and station everyone 100 miles away. Wait for Wu Zhanhe's troops to gather before attacking! Wait for me to arrive before attacking! "Cui Xie instructed.
Poison King Wu Zhanhe had also joined Cui Xie's Kingdom of Evil Deities. Wu Zhanhe and Cui Xie were originally not on the same side. It could even be said that there were conflicts between him and Cui Xie!
However, in the face of benefits, Wu Zhanhe still stood on Cui Xie's side. Under Cui Xie's invitation, Wu Zhanhe chose to cooperate with Cui Xie in the grand scheme of the Kingdom of Gods.
At this moment, Wu Zhanhe was rushing over with another group of independent warriors.
Cui Xie did not dare to be the slightest bit careless. The Void Spirit Sect was not called the number one sect in the Central Domain for nothing. Although Feng Guanyu's Sect Protecting Formation could not compare to the Cloud Hall's Sect Protecting Formation, this "Thousand Illusion Maze Array" would still cause considerable trouble for the God Nation's army.
Moreover, the Void Spirit Sect also had Taoist Priest Qing Xu, whose strength even Cui Xie did not dare to underestimate. In addition, the strength of the Void Spirit Sect's Seven Sons was comparable to a Life and Death Realm expert.
To Cui Xie, the Void Spirit Sect was a hard bone to chew, but once he did, then the Kingdom of Evil Deities would benefit greatly!
Cui Xie asked Xuan Chen to pass down the order to postpone the attack on the Void Spirit Sect. Then he sneered, "That brat is so lucky. Let's see how he can escape this time!" After saying that, Cui Xie turned into a stream of light and went straight to the Void Sky City.
Within the Void Spirit Sect, Taoist Priest Qing Xu was standing in the middle of a huge Eight Trigrams, staring at the sky with a complicated expression.
The Seven Sons of Void Spirit Sect had formed a unique formation according to the Seven Stars Formation. With solemn expressions, they were also staring at the countless warriors in the sky.
Most of these warriors were at the Divine Reflection Realm, and they posed no threat to the Void Spirit Sect. In front of the Thousand Illusionary Formation, no matter how many warriors below the Divine Core realm came here, they would all die. However, there were many Divine Core warriors among them, and the number of Void Tribulation warriors was a few times more than the Void Spirit Sect!
It was hard to determine the outcome of a battle.
One month ago, Taoist Priest Qing Xu had a premonition that Cui Xie would attack the Void Spirit Sect. In fact, many people had guessed it, but Taoist Priest Qing Xu didn't expect Cui Xie to come so soon. Facing the strongest sect in the Central Domain, Cui Xie had attacked without hesitation. His ambition and courage made Taoist Priest Qing Xu feel ashamed of his inferiority!
Right when all the Sect Elders and disciples of the Void Spirit Sect were preparing for the battle, Cui Xie had turned into a beam of light and left!
At the same time, all the warriors of the Tian Xie Sect had started retreating in an orderly manner!
"Sect Chief, Cui Xie is flying towards the Void Sky City." One of the Seven Sons of Void Spirit Sect reminded.
Taoist Priest Qing Xu's expression changed. "Cui Xie, he must be heading towards the Xi family! Sigh, that child is so willful, and being spoiled like this is actually harming her … "Taoist Priest Qing Xu really wanted to rush out and stop Cui Xie. Although his overall strength was weaker than Cui Xie's, he wasn't much weaker than Cui Xie in the Void Sky City.
But now, he had to stay in the Void Spirit Sect and take charge of the situation!
Although the Xi family was important to Taoist Priest Qing Xu, the Void Spirit Sect was even more important. The entire Xi family, or even the entire Void Sky City, was built under the protection of the Void Spirit Sect. Taoist Priest Qing Xu could still distinguish which was more important.
"They are retreating. Should we rush out?" One of the Seven Sons of Void Spirit Sect asked.
Now that Cui Xie had just left, it was a chance for the Void Spirit Sect to counterattack and achieve an unexpected effect. However, Taoist Priest Qing Xu still shook his head. Who knew if this was a trap or not?
"No, we will defend to the death." Taoist Priest Qing Xu said in a deep voice, and his eyes were filled with determination. From the moment he became the Sect Chief of the Void Spirit Sect, his life had been firmly tied to the entire sect!
A beam of light flew towards the Void Sky City. When the beam of light approached the Void Sky City, it was immediately blocked by an invisible force. This force was the no-fly restriction in the Void Sky City.
This kind of restriction was very popular a thousand years ago, but due to the opposition of many cultivators, many cities removed the no-fly restriction one after another. Only the Void Sky City maintained the old rule and still kept the no-fly restriction.
"Hmph." Facing this no-fly restriction, Cui Xie snorted coldly and broke the restriction with a casual punch. He came to the sky above the Void Sky City, glanced around, and went straight to the Xi Family Castle.
The lunch in the Xi Family Castle lasted for half an hour. Xi Xinghuai, the patriarch of the Xi Family, still attached great importance to Luo Zheng. Although he said it was a simple meal, in fact, every dish was very particular. Of course, it took a long time.
During the meal, Xi Xiaojie was still thinking about it. He said to Luo Zheng across the table, "Brother Luo Zheng, if there is a chance, I will go to the Cloud Hall to challenge you. You must accept the challenge!"
This Xi Xiaojie was so persistent that Luo Zheng showed a helpless smile on his face. In the end, he still agreed.
Xi Xinghuai next to him also smiled. He also knew his adopted son's temper. Xi Xiaojie was not afraid of losing. Even if he lost, he would lose with peace of mind.
Just as Xi Xiaojie was about to say something, his face suddenly changed. He reached out and took out a token from his spatial ring. The color of the token turned blood red!
"What's wrong?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Even Xi Xinghuai's face became very ugly when he saw the blood-red token.
The Void Spirit Sect's token had a total of three colors. Usually, the token was emerald green. If the Void Spirit Sect entered a state of alert, it would turn yellow. Once a battle started or was about to start, it would turn red. During this period of time, the Void Spirit Sect's token had remained yellow. Now that it suddenly turned red, how could Xi Xiaojie not be anxious?
"Oh no, the Void Spirit Sect is under attack! I have to go! " After Xi Xiaojie entered the False Tribulation Realm, he was also the top battle force of the Void Spirit Sect. At this time, how could he still stay here?
Hearing that the Void Spirit Sect was under attack, Luo Zheng's face suddenly sank. He immediately got up and cupped his hands towards Xi Xinghuai, saying, "I'll take your Miss out of Void Sky City!"
If he didn't eat this lunch, Luo Zheng would have already taken Xi Youqin out of Void Sky City. Now, it was precisely because of this lunch that they were delayed. He didn't expect that there would be a change in the situation. This was indeed something that Luo Zheng never expected.
Xi Xinghuai also knew that this matter was of great importance. He nodded with a dignified expression. "I'll take you to find Xi Youqin!"
Xi Xiaojie rushed to the Void Spirit Sect, while Luo Zheng went straight to Xi Youqin's residence under Xi Xinghuai's lead.
Xi Youqin was alone in her small courtyard. Because she was about to leave the Xi Family Castle, she wasn't even in the mood to eat. Looking at the table full of light and exquisite food, Xi Youqin's eyes drifted, and no one knew what she was thinking.
"Bang!"
Luo Zheng almost directly smashed into Xi Youqin's small courtyard, smashing the door and breaking the screen!
These two screens were both made of Spirit Bone Jade. Not to mention that they were expensive, the key was that Xi Youqin liked them very much. She spent a lot of effort to get them two years ago. She didn't expect that they would be directly smashed by Luo Zheng!
"You, you compensate me for my screens!" Xi Youqin furrowed her brows and said angrily.
This guy was really abominable. He didn't put her in his eyes at all.
"Youqin, you still care about this pair of screens at a time like this!" Xi Xinghuai didn't know how to educate his daughter, not to mention now was not the time to educate her.
Luo Zheng was too lazy to talk to her. He grabbed Xi Youqin and spat out a word, "Let's go!"

Chapter 678
Luo Zheng soared into the sky and smashed the beam. As soon as the beam of the house broke, the whole house began to collapse. Everything in the small garden was smashed to the ground.
"My, my Yu Hua Yuan …" Seeing this scene, Xi Youqin wanted to cry but had no tears.
Luo Zheng sighed in his heart. This rich young lady didn't seem to understand her situation, but he really didn't have the mood, nor did he have the time to sit down with her and analyze the situation and the danger she was in. If this matter wasn't related to Luo Yan, why would he care about this idiot woman's death?
Dragging Xi Youqin along the way, Luo Zheng rushed in the direction of the transmission array. Although the Void Sky City was huge, with Luo Zheng's current flying speed, it wouldn't take long!
However, at this time, a powerful aura suddenly descended from the sky. After Luo Zheng felt the aura, his face suddenly changed.
"Cui Xie!" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly.
When Xi Youqin saw Cui Xie, her face suddenly turned pale. Just a moment ago, Xi Youqin didn't understand what was going on. Now, she finally understood. She didn't expect that the thing that the Xi Family's elders had been worried about all these years would actually happen!
"Luo Zheng!" Cui Xie roared at Luo Zheng from the sky, "Your actions are quite fast! However, if you didn't come to the Xi Family, your life might have been longer. Now, you are really courting death, haha! Today, I will kill you! "
In the past, Luo Zheng really made Cui Xie feel aggrieved. However, Luo Zheng had been hiding outside the Cloud Hall's Sect Protecting Array. Cui Xie couldn't do anything to Luo Zheng.
However, that was then and this is now. Now, there was no Sect Protecting Array to protect Luo Zheng!
Cui Xie was well aware of how terrifying Luo Zheng's luck was. He wouldn't feel at ease unless he personally got rid of such a person!
What was more, in two years, Luo Zheng had reached the False Tribulation Realm. Although he was only at the early stage, Cui Xie was still afraid of his speed of progress. If Luo Zheng could live for another three or five years, wouldn't he be able to break through to the Spirit Sea Realm? At that time, killing him would be as easy as an eagle catching a chick!
Cui Xie's grand plan was to establish the Divine Kingdom. How could he allow such a great threat to remain in this world?
Therefore, today, Cui Xie's killing intent was the strongest, and it was to kill Luo Zheng. Even a major event like attacking the Void Spirit Sect had been postponed. It could be imagined how much importance Cui Xie attached to Luo Zheng.
"Whoosh!"
Luo Zheng wasn't willing to listen to Cui Xie's nonsense. He grabbed Xi Youqin and quickly rushed towards the Heavenly Silkworm River of the Void Sky City. Cui Xie followed closely behind Luo Zheng. They were only a few li apart.
"I hope I can make it in time!" Luo Zheng frowned.
Xi Youqin was tightly dragged behind by Luo Zheng. She could only hear the wind blowing past her ears. Her mind was also in a mess. Just as Luo Zheng arrived at the Heavenly Silkworm River, he saw two columns of black smoke rising into the sky not far away. His heart immediately sank.
Even though he didn't see the Transmission Array, Luo Zheng had already guessed what had happened. When he flew past the two Transmission Arrays, Luo Zheng just lowered his head slightly and saw that the Transmission Arrays were already black and burnt. He didn't slow down at all and continued to fly forward!
"Want to escape through the Transmission Arrays? Wishful thinking! " In order to prevent the support of the Cloud Hall and the World Business Alliance, cutting off the two Transmission Arrays was the most important thing in attacking the Void Spirit Sect. Unexpectedly, Luo Zheng was trapped now!
At this time, Cui Xie didn't use his speed. Luo Zheng was already the prey in his hands. The process of chasing him was also a kind of enjoyment for Cui Xie!
Luo Zheng had no way to solve the current situation. Even if Luo Zheng had great luck, Cui Xie didn't believe that anyone could save Luo Zheng's life, unless Taoist Priest Qing Xu came personally! Taoist Priest Qing Xu didn't dare to walk out of the Void Spirit Sect. As the sect master, how could he dare to leave the sect at this time?
How could Luo Zheng not know what Cui Xie knew?
If he were to fully erupt with his astral essence, then with Luo Zheng's current cultivation and strength, he was confident he could escape from Cui Xie. But right now, he was dragging a burden down, and this burden couldn't be easily shaken off!
With a gloomy expression, he suddenly pulled Xi Youqin's hand over!
"Ah!" Xi Youqin screamed. Luo Zheng directly pulled Xi Youqin into his arms.
A man's breath rushed over. Even in this situation, Xi Youqin still blushed. She immediately said angrily, "What are you doing!"
Luo Zheng ignored Xi Youqin. He immediately reached out and grabbed Xi Youqin's waist, carrying her on his back. The Vital Essence in his body instantly erupted. The innate talent of the Cloud Chasing Lion was mixed with the Vital Essence. In an instant, Luo Zheng's speed doubled!
Cui Xie originally maintained a steady speed, following behind Luo Zheng. Although Luo Zheng's speed was fast enough, it was nothing to Cui Xie. What's more, this guy was dragging a burden?
Unexpectedly, Luo Zheng suddenly turned into a thin line and shot forward. His speed increased by several levels. Even Cui Xie was a little stunned by his speed.
"Damn it!" Realizing that he had once again underestimated Luo Zheng, Cui Xie angrily cursed. The Vital Essence in his body flickered, and he began to increase his speed while following Luo Zheng!
At that moment, Luo Zheng's speed was indeed unparalleled. Even Cui Xie was somewhat inferior. However, the eruption of Vital Essence was very short. It was far from lasting as long as Vital Essence. Therefore, it could only be used for a short period of time.
Now, Luo Zheng had used the eruption of Vital Essence to increase the distance between him and Cui Xie. However, the situation was still not optimistic. Cui Xie was still firmly locked onto him. The Central Domain was not small. Whether it was going to Tianqi City or the Cloud Hall, there was still a considerable distance to travel.
Once Luo Zheng's Vital Essence was exhausted, it was only a matter of time before Cui Xie caught up to him.
Xi Youqin was lying on Luo Zheng's shoulder. The whistling wind made her unable to hear anything. Their speed was too fast. They had left Virtual Sky City in almost an instant.
She had never left Virtual Sky City since she was a child. She had never traveled outside. She did not expect that the first time she left Virtual Sky City would be in such a situation.
After Luo Zheng's first wave of Vital Essence was exhausted, Cui Xie's flying speed also slowly increased. The distance between the two quickly narrowed.
"Cui Xie is catching up!" Xi Youqin, who was lying on Luo Zheng's shoulder, reminded him.
"Shut up, I don't need you to remind me." How could Luo Zheng not know that Cui Xie was rapidly approaching? Purely relying on Vital Essence to fly, Luo Zheng's speed was still not as fast as Cui Xie's.
"Luo Zheng, I advise you to surrender. Today, you can't escape no matter what. Don't waste time!" Cui Xie said coldly.
Luo Zheng's expression did not change in the slightest. Instead, he instilled Vital Essence into his voice and replied to Cui Xie, "Since I'm going to die whether I escape or not, I have to try. In any case, you are the Sect Master. Don't talk nonsense!"
"I am the Monarch of the Heavenly Evil Deities Kingdom, not the Sect Master you speak of." The outside world believed that the Tian Xie Sect was a Sect Master. However, no one dared to mention the Tian Xie Sect in front of Cui Xie. Generally, they said the Heavenly Evil Deities Kingdom!
"Hmph, self-proclaimed Monarch of the Divine Nation. You are so shameless," Luo Zheng sneered.
"You are about to die, yet you still want to provoke me? You will only die more miserably! " Cui Xie suddenly increased his speed. The fist in his hand was already flickering. A great power was brewing in Cui Xie's body. As long as there was an opportunity, Cui Xie would make his move.
Feeling this power, Xi Youqin's face was drained of blood. Her face was filled with grief. Today, she was afraid that she would really die here, die on this man's shoulder …
However, to Xi Youqin's surprise, specks of red light suddenly drilled out from Luo Zheng's side. It was Luo Zheng's sword spirit.
After Xun's body completely condensed, she was very close to Xi Youqin. The two women looked at each other. Xun revealed a mocking expression towards Xi Youqin. Then, she took a mid-grade Sage Ranked Spear from Luo Zheng and turned around to charge towards Cui Xie!
"This is Luo Zheng's Sword Spirit." Xi Youqin had heard that Luo Zheng had a very special Sword Spirit. However, a Sword Spirit was a Sword Spirit. Why did it have an expression? Moreover, that Sword Spirit seemed to be mocking her?
Xi Youqin felt that she was hallucinating.

Chapter 679
Xun's figure flashed, and the long spear in her hand shot towards Cui Xie in the opposite direction, thrusting her spear forward!
"A middle-grade sacred tool!"
Cui Xie's eyes flashed. There were only a few low-grade sacred tools in the Central Domain. Seeing Luo Zheng's sword spirit holding a middle-grade sacred tool, how could Cui Xie not be envious?
But at the same time, Cui Xie's eyes flashed with a trace of doubt. Wasn't Luo Zheng's sword spirit using a huge sword? How come it was now a long spear?
Luo Zheng's luck was really heaven-defying. After not seeing him for two years, not only had his cultivation broken through to the False Tribulation Realm, but he had also obtained a middle-grade sacred tool.
"Not bad, but unfortunately, this middle-grade sacred tool is still mine. All your luck and all your secrets will belong to me!" In Cui Xie's eyes, there was no doubt that Luo Zheng was hiding a huge secret. As long as he obtained this secret, not only would he be able to become an emperor in the future, but he would also be able to seek greater hegemony. Cui Xie's vision was not limited to the Central Domain. There were also the four Divine Kingdoms on the other side of the Chaotic Astral Ocean. The world was so vast, and Cui Xie had been unhindered for thousands of years. In the end, he would stand at the highest point of the world.
And Luo Zheng in front of him was the most crucial step!
Facing Xun's spear, Cui Xie did not hesitate at all. He shot out an earth-yellow fist aura.
"Humph, break for me!"
However, there was no trace of fear on Xun's face. Her long spear trembled, and a formless wave of energy flew out. An astonishing power erupted from the wave of energy, and instantly shattered Cui Xie's fist light.
Seeing this scene, Cui Xie's face revealed a look of shock!
How could Luo Zheng's sword spirit be so powerful?
Although Cui Xie did not use his full strength in this punch, Luo Zheng's sword spirit was able to block his fist aura? How could Cui Xie not be shocked?
Xun looked at Cui Xie with an indifferent expression and sneered, "Are you surprised?"
"You can talk?" Cui Xie's surprised expression became even more obvious. How could a sword spirit talk? Cui Xie had witnessed Luo Zheng's sword spirit before. Although the sword spirit's sword dance was exquisite, it was nothing to Cui Xie's eyes.
How could Cui Xie not be surprised by the Sword Spirit's exquisite spear technique?
However, the surprise on Cui Xie's face quickly dissipated. His expression became incomparably serious. Cui Xie realized that Luo Zheng was not as easy to deal with as he had imagined. He could not treat Luo Zheng as a cultivator who had just entered the Virtual Tribulation Realm! Luo Zheng was just a Divine Reflection warrior, but he was still able to defeat Early Divine Core warriors, and even a Mid Divine Core warrior was no match for him!
"You've really surprised me!" Cui Xie said with a serious expression. An extremely confident and domineering expression emerged on his face, "As the strongest man in the Central Domain, it has been a long time since I've felt this way! But too bad, Luo Zheng, you've come back too early. If you were two or three years later, I might really be no match for you! Haha! "
The domineering aura spread out, as if countless invisible rocks were falling down from the sky, and then falling down from the sky.
Many warriors in the Void Sky City felt their hearts tremble. Fear emerged in their eyes as they raised their heads and stared at the sky.
As for the thousands of mortals in the Void Sky City, they felt as if their heads had been hit by a heavy blow. Their vision turned black, and they couldn't get up from the ground. They only opened their eyes a few hours later, and their faces were filled with absent-minded expressions. They had no idea what had just happened.
For Luo Zheng, Cui Xie's words were just nonsense. Of course, he knew that his best choice was to return to the Central Domain after he had cultivated to the peak of Combat Sovereign realm. If he returned to the Evil Tower, he wouldn't be in such a difficult situation when facing Cui Xie. He might even be able to kill Cui Xie!
But, time waits for no man. If Luo Zheng hadn't returned in time, Cui Xie would have captured the young lady of the Xi family. By the time Cui Xie finished setting up the Heavenly Devil Joyous Union Formation, it would have been too late!
What's more, at that time, Cui Xie would most likely have broken through the Life and Death Realm and become a powerhouse of the deep sea. Luo Zheng might not necessarily be Cui Xie's opponent.
"A mere Life and Death Realm warrior dares to claim to be the strongest person in the Central Domain? What a joke!" While sneering, Xun suddenly revealed a supreme aura. Her aura wasn't deliberate, but was naturally formed by someone who had been in a high position for a long time.
Although Xun's aura wasn't as majestic as Cui Xie's aura, at this moment, Cui Xie unconsciously felt that he was inferior to her!
"You're just a Sword Spirit. How dare you be so rampant in front of me!"
Cui Xie sent out another punch. Xun sneered and also sent out a spear!
Cui Xie was now a fourth layer Life and Death Realm warrior. A year ago, he had passed the fourth tribulation of the Life and Death Realm, and his strength had increased slightly.
According to the division of realms on the Sea God Continent, Cui Xie was now a fourth step Battle-Saint. However, Cui Xie's strength was extremely strong, and he couldn't be regarded as an ordinary Battle-Saint. He was naturally much stronger than Meng Chong, who Luo Zheng encountered in the Mysterious Underworld Cave.
Cui Xie sent out this punch with all his strength. That earthy yellow fist light was sent out, and the rich power of the law of earth contained within it made people feel that this fist light was incomparably heavy, as if it was real.
Seeing this, Xun turned into specks of red light and dissipated. Together with the spear, she shot back towards Luo Zheng.
Xun's purpose was only to slightly obstruct Cui Xie. After returning to Luo Zheng's side, the specks of red light once again condensed and formed her tall body. The spear was naturally held in her arms.
Luo Zheng was already running, but his path was very strange. Originally, he was running towards the outside of the city, but at this moment, he deviated to the other side and began to circle around the periphery of the Void Sky City!
Xun hugged the spear, and her sharp eyes unscrupulously fell on Xi Youqin's face. There was still a faint mocking expression on her face.
She had been unhappy with this woman for a long time. She was a delicate young lady from a wealthy family, but she had no sense of propriety and was opinionated. With Xun's personality, she would have killed this woman the moment she saw her. Why would she become a burden to Luo Zheng?
Luo Zheng's strength might be inferior to Cui Xie's, but if Luo Zheng went all out, it wouldn't be difficult for him to escape from Cui Xie. However, he had to carry this burden and escape.
However, Xun understood Luo Zheng's personality. He wouldn't kill the innocent, and he was soft-hearted towards women. Therefore, she couldn't kill this woman, so as to avoid angering Luo Zheng.
"You, why are you looking at me like that?" Being stared at by Xun like that, Xi Youqin suddenly felt a burst of panic.
Xun sneered coldly and said, "Aren't you not afraid of death? Why are you still holding on to Luo Zheng so tightly? "
Although Xi Youqin was very reluctant to be held by Luo Zheng, she still held on to Luo Zheng's shoulders tightly while flying at high speed.
"I …" Xi Youqin's expression darkened. She didn't know how to respond to Xun's words. However, she suddenly thought of something and her stubborn temperament suddenly came up. Then, Xi Youqin said coldly, "Luo Zheng, let go of me."
"Let go of you?" Xun blinked her eyes. "If I let go of you, Cui Xie will catch you and set up the Great Formation of Heavenly Demon Joyous Union? You are indeed not important, but Luo Yan is a hundred times more important than you! "
From Luo Zheng's point of view, this was indeed the case. Xi Youqin's life or death was not important to Luo Zheng. The reason why he wanted to take Xi Youqin away was for his sister.
"Then, then just kill me …" Xi Youqin finally understood that there was no other way in front of her. There was no other way for her to choose, except for death!
"Xun, forget it," Luo Zheng said lightly.
How could he not know Xun's purpose? It was nothing more than to squeeze Xi Youqin and use Xi Youqin's temper to make him give up on her, or even kill her. However, Luo Zheng would not choose to do so.
Although the world of martial artists was cruel, Luo Zheng could choose to do something or not do anything. Xun's choice was indeed the safest and most convenient, but Luo Zheng did not want to easily lose something!
While carrying Xi Youqin and circling around Virtual Sky City, the distance between Cui Xie and Luo Zheng was getting closer and closer. However, just as Luo Zheng flew over a palace-like building in Virtual Sky City, he suddenly stopped and faced Cui Xie with a strange smile on his face!
Seeing Luo Zheng suddenly stop, Cui Xie's expression changed and a trace of vigilance appeared on his face. It was impossible for this kid to be captured without putting up a fight. His actions must be a trap!
