Chapter 680
Previously, Luo Zheng had run towards the outside of the city. Cui Xie also had other plans. After Luo Zheng left the city, he would use the True Essence Talisman to send a message to Poison King Wu Zhanhe so that Wu Zhanhe could surround Luo Zheng.
However, he didn't expect that not only did Luo Zheng not escape from the city, but he was also circling around in the sky above the Void Sky City. How could Cui Xie not be on guard against this strange behavior?
"What are you worried about?" Luo Zheng asked with a faint smile across the distance of several hundred feet.
Cui Xie's expression sank, and he looked around even more vigilantly. Looking around, Cui Xie didn't find anything wrong. However, looking at Luo Zheng's smile, Cui Xie became even more suspicious!
The two of them faced each other from several hundred feet away.
"In that case, I'm leaving!" Luo Zheng smiled and turned around to leave.
Seeing that Luo Zheng was about to leave, Cui Xie suddenly became angry. Who knew that Luo Zheng would use the empty city tactic?
Cui Xie had no choice. He circulated his True Essence and suddenly accelerated, shooting towards Luo Zheng!
"Attack!"
Seeing that Cui Xie had arrived above the palace, Luo Zheng shouted.
The palace below Cui Xie suddenly bloomed with rays of light. These rays of light connected from dots to lines, and the streets within a radius of hundreds of mu bloomed with red light. It was as if a sun had fallen to the ground. The streets were directly connected to each other and evolved into a huge rune!
At the same time, the power of the Law of Space suddenly fluctuated in the palace. After several blazing white rays of light flashed, Xi Xiaojie, Xi Xinghuai, and the Seven Void Spirit Masters appeared!
"Xiu Xiu Xiu Xiu …"
From the huge rune formed by the streets, small dragons of light emerged. These small dragons rushed into the sky and wrapped around Cui Xie at lightning speed. Some of the small dragons wrapped around Cui Xie's limbs, while others wrapped around his body. In the blink of an eye, these small dragons tightly trapped Cui Xie!
"I've long heard that in addition to the Thousand Illusion Maze Formation, Feng Guanyu also had a peerless masterpiece hidden in the Void Spirit Sect. Today, I've seen this Dragon-trapped Rope. It's really extraordinary!" Luo Zheng said with a faint smile.
Luo Zheng had not seen the power of the "Thousand Illusion Maze Formation" of the Void Spirit Sect, but the power released by this Dragon-trapped Rope still made Luo Zheng sincerely admire it!
After all, the Central Domain was not the Upper World. Unlike Luo Zheng, Feng Guanyu could not obtain the inheritance of the Divine Rune Technique. Therefore, Feng Guanyu had no predecessors to learn from and could only rely on himself to explore and create. But even so, Feng Guanyu could exert the rune technique to this extent. Luo Zheng could feel that the power of this Dragon-trapped Rope was not inferior to some three-star Divine Runes.
Xi Xiaojie and Xi Xinghuai suddenly broke through the void and came to Luo Zheng.
The Seven Void Spirit Masters also slowly flew into the sky. They had maintained vigilance, holding long swords and facing Cui Xie.
In addition to Xi Xiaojie and the Seven Void Spirit Masters, there were also two Void Spirit Sect disciples escorting a servant girl in green clothes.
Xi Youqin suddenly broke free from Luo Zheng's body, retreated a few steps, and fiercely glared at Luo Zheng before flying towards the two Void Spirit Sect disciples. "What are you doing? Why are you capturing Li Hua! Let her go! "
Xi Xiaojie's face revealed a bitter expression, and he said to Xi Youqin, "Sister, this servant girl of yours is someone that Cui Xie and the others placed by your side …"
"Impossible! Li Hua has been with me since young, and we are very close. We are inseparable. How can she be one of Cui Xie's people! "Xi Youqin's face was full of disbelief.
Hearing Xi Youqin's words, Luo Zheng couldn't help but sigh, and his face was full of a bitter smile. Xun rolled her eyes and said in a low voice, "It's best to kill this kind of idiot. Living in this world is a waste of food!"
Li Hua had indeed grown up with Xi Youqin, and they were like sisters, but although she was a servant girl, she was also a Xiantian life form. If Cui Xie wanted her to betray Xi Youqin, it would be too easy. In fact, this kind of thing didn't need Cui Xie's help, but was arranged by Xuan Chen.
"Sister, don't make trouble!" At this time, even Xi Xiaojie couldn't bear it and said coldly to Xi Youqin.
"But Li Hua, she …" Xi Youqin still seemed unwilling.
"Xiu!"
An invisible spear light suddenly shot out. Even the Void Spirit Seven and Xi Xinghuai were very afraid of this spear light, and this spear light was shot towards Xi Youqin!
This spear light almost brushed past Xi Youqin's cheek, and immediately cut off a strand of hair near her ear. At the same time, it left a thin scar on her face, and a little bit of blood seeped out.
"If you speak any more nonsense, I will kill you," Xun said in a deep voice, and her eyes revealed a faint killing intent.
When such killing intent was released, it was as if Xun was really a terrifying True God who controlled slaughter. At this time, Xi Youqin immediately shut her mouth.
Xi Xinghuai saw that his daughter's face had been cut, and although he felt distressed, he couldn't say anything.
"Hahahaha, so you had already planned this! Good, good! The number one sect in the world shouldn't be so weak. Interesting, interesting … Hahaha! "Cui Xie, who was firmly bound by the Dragon-trapping Rope, suddenly began to laugh wildly.
Since Cui Xie led tens of thousands of Godly Nations troops to surround the Void Spirit Sect, Daoist Qing Xu had been guarding the sect with his life, appearing extremely weak.
In fact, Cui Xie's movements and goals had long been known by the Void Spirit Sect, so they chose to beat him at his own game!
If it was to protect Xi Youqin, the Void Spirit Sect would definitely not leave her in the Xi Family Castle. At least, they would let Xi Youqin enter the Void Spirit Sect to temporarily hide. Therefore, Xi Youqin was just a bait, and the goal was to lure Cui Xie!
Originally, Daoist Qing Xu planned to let Xi Xiaojie escape with Xi Youqin and lure Cui Xie to the Dragon-trapping Rope. However, Xi Xiaojie had just entered the Void Tribulation Realm. Although he was proficient in the Law of Space, he might not be able to successfully complete the mission.
Coincidentally, Luo Zheng appeared at this time!
However, when Luo Zheng had just entered the Xi Family Castle, he did not know about the Void Spirit Sect's scheme. When Xi Xinghuai and the others put on a show and finally forced Xi Youqin to leave with Luo Zheng, Li Hua, as Xi Youqin's personal maid, naturally knew about this news!
When everyone was eating lunch, although they were laughing on the surface, they were secretly communicating with each other using true essence sound transmissions. Although Xi Youqin and Li Hua did not eat together, it was very likely that Li Hua was not the only spy in the Xi Family, so everyone was still very careful!
After Li Hua secretly burned the sound transmission talisman, Luo Zheng had already agreed to this matter. Therefore, he directly rushed into Xi Youqin's small courtyard and took Xi Youqin away in a "hurry". At this time, Cui Xie had already received the news and rushed over from outside the Void Sky City.
From the beginning to the end, only Xi Youqin was kept in the dark in the Xi Family.
No matter how stupid Xi Youqin was, at this time, she completely understood that from the beginning to the end, she was just bait …
She thought that she was highly valued in the Xi Family and was doted on by everyone. Whether it was her grandfather, father, or the other elders of the Xi Family, they all held her in their hands. She did not expect that in the end, she would actually play such a role.
"Is this how you treat me …" Xi Youqin's eyes blurred and her expression was miserable. However, everyone's attention was now focused on Cui Xie, so who would pay attention to Xi Youqin?
"Cui Xie, you're about to die and you're still so arrogant. My Void Spirit Sect is known as the number one sect in the world, do you really think it's just an exaggerated title?" The leader of the Void Spirit Seven pointed his sword at Cui Xie and shouted in a stern voice.
There was no trace of fear in Cui Xie's eyes, and they were still full of ridicule. "Indeed, you're a little smarter than I thought, but unfortunately, it's still far from enough!"
When Cui Xie's voice fell, a black mist suddenly erupted from between his eyebrows. The black mist instantly wrapped around him and the Dragon-trapping Rope, and the mist seemed to be constantly corroding the Dragon-trapping Rope!
"Isn't this Dragon-trapping Rope able to seal off vital essence? Why can Cui Xie still operate vital essence? "
"That's not vital essence, it should be some kind of secret method that doesn't need vital essence!"
"We can't let him destroy the Dragon-trapping Rope, kill, kill him quickly!"
The Void Spirit Sect had been planning for a long time, and their purpose was to rely on Feng Guanyu's Dragon-trapping Rope to kill Cui Xie. Although this move was extremely risky, once Cui Xie was killed, the Void Spirit Sect could be considered to have made a supreme contribution! On that day, the Kingdom of Evil Deities would inevitably collapse!
It was because of this reason that Luo Zheng agreed to cooperate with the Void Spirit Sect. After all, Luo Zheng's goal was the same as the Void Spirit Sect's.
This Dragon-trapping Rope was extremely powerful. It was said that if a Life and Death Realm martial artist was trapped inside, even a Life and Death Realm martial artist wouldn't be able to escape. However, Cui Xie was still able to use this secret method!
This scene caused everyone's expressions to change greatly. The Seven Voidspirit Masters' expressions were grave as they raised their swords one after another. Six of them retreated while one of them charged forward. This was the Seven Voidspirit Masters' most powerful formation.

Chapter 681
For the Void Spirit Sect, this was a once-in-a-lifetime opportunity!
How could the Seven Masters of the Void Spirit Sect dare to be negligent at this time?
Although the Dragon-trapping Rope was a trump card of the Void Spirit Sect, Cui Xie was the strongest person in the Central Domain. Who knew what would happen if they delayed? Moreover, the black fog was indeed corroding the Dragon-trapping Rope. If Cui Xie really broke the Dragon-trapping Rope, the Void Spirit Sect's previous scheme would be in vain. How could they miss such a heaven-sent opportunity?
The overall strength of the Seven Masters of the Void Spirit Sect had improved a lot, and the leader had reached the late stage of the False Tribulation Realm.
Moreover, the "Seven Voids Into One" they set up could combine the strength of seven people. This sword strike was bound to take Cui Xie's life!
"Whoosh!"
The sword light in the hand of the leader of the Seven Masters of the Void Spirit Sect soared, and it directly disappeared into the black fog!
Everyone stared at the black fog nervously. Cui Xie was locked by the Dragon-trapping Rope, and at this moment, he couldn't even operate his Genuine Essence. If this sword strike hit him, he would definitely not be able to block it!
Luo Zheng stared at the black fog, and a bad premonition suddenly rose in his heart. From the day Luo Zheng stepped into the Central Domain, Luo Zheng had blocked Cui Xie as his opponent and the biggest obstacle in his martial arts. Luo Zheng didn't believe that this obstacle could be eliminated so easily.
However, compared with Luo Zheng, the others seemed to be very confident. For example, Xi Xinghuai, the head of the Xi Family, could help the Void Spirit Sect eliminate Cui Xie, and his Xi Family would definitely be famous in the world!
"Ding … Ding ding ding!"
However, just after the joint attack of the Seven Masters of the Void Spirit Sect, everyone suddenly heard the sound of metal breaking, and then they saw several beams of light flying out of the black fog!
"Puff puff puff puff!"
The beams of light contained great power, and they instantly hit the chests of four of the Seven Masters of the Void Spirit Sect. The beams of light didn't stop at all and directly penetrated the four of them. Blood burst out in front of their chests and behind their backs, leaving a horrible hole in their chests. Then the four of them fell from the sky!
At this time, everyone saw clearly that the beams of light were fragments of the sword. Not only did the sword strike fail to kill Cui Xie, but it also shot back after it shattered, it instantly killed four of the Seven Masters of the Void Spirit Sect!
"Third Brother!"
"Fifth Brother!"
"Sixth Brother …"
Everyone's expression changed drastically, and the remaining three of the Seven Void Spirit Masters cried out in grief.
The Seven Masters of the Void Spirit Sect ate and slept together, and they had long been connected with each other. They had been in the Central Domain for so many years, and none of them had died. But today, four of them had died in the blink of an eye, and the remaining three were devastated.
The black fog gradually dispersed, and the Dragon-trapping Rope that originally bound Cui Xie had broken layer by layer, and the light dissipated little by little. At this time, Cui Xie's body exuded a violent aura, and layers of scales appeared on his skin.
"It's really good that you can force me to this point. In return, I will crush the entire Void Spirit Sect, hahahaha!" Cui Xie laughed wildly. At this moment, the wind and clouds changed color, and the entire Void Sky City was shrouded under his momentum. Except for him, there seemed to be nothing else.
"Retreat!"
Luo Zheng did not think much, but rushed to Xi Youqin immediately. After grabbing Xi Youqin again, the Zhen Qi in his body began to circulate crazily, and the Vital Spirit burst out at the same time. His figure turned into a black line!
His hunch was indeed correct. Since Cui Xie had the confidence to establish a Divine Kingdom and dared to attack the Void Spirit Sect, then his means were definitely not limited to this!
"I, I'll kill you!"
The Seven Masters of the Void Spirit Sect were like one person. The other four had died, and the remaining three could not live alone!
At this time, the others did not have the impulse to fight. In the face of Cui Xie in such a state, they were simply courting death if they rushed up. Therefore, when Xi Xiaojie and Xi Xinghuai saw Luo Zheng take away Xi Youqin, they did not stop them. In contrast, it was safer for Xi Youqin to follow Luo Zheng than to follow them!
Xi Xiaojie and Xi Xinghuai, however, fled in two different directions.
It was also because three of the Seven Masters of the Void Spirit Sect had bought time for them to escape. Although the remaining three were no match for Cui Xie, they were still Virtual Tribulation Realm powerhouses after all!
Luo Zheng's face was very ugly. He had expected that Cui Xie would not be killed so easily, but he did not expect that Cui Xie's strength would suddenly increase to such an extent! If it was before, Luo Zheng could resist Cui Xie's strength, and with his speed, he could even escape from Cui Xie's hands. But now, Luo Zheng might not be a match for Cui Xie.
Rushing forward, Luo Zheng had rushed out of the Void Sky City in just a short while. Outside the Void Sky City was a vast mountain range. After Luo Zheng chose a direction, he did not stop. Once again, the vital essence in his body erupted, and he transformed into a stream of light and flew through the air.
If he did not use the Transmission Formation, with Luo Zheng's flying speed, it would only take him about three days to arrive at Cloud Sea City!
However, would Cui Xie not chase after him?
Luo Zheng was originally Cui Xie's target, and he had Xi Youqin with him. This woman was also something Cui Xie had to get his hands on. After dealing with the remaining three of the Seven Masters of the Void Spirit Sect, Cui Xie would definitely chase after him.
Sure enough, after an incense stick's worth of time, Luo Zheng saw a black dot appear behind him. That black dot was Cui Xie.
Luo Zheng's speed was extremely fast, but that black dot was not slow either!
As long as Luo Zheng stopped circulating his vital essence, that black dot would be closer to Luo Zheng. However, Luo Zheng's vital essence could not be released all the time. This was the limitation of a body cultivator. Even Luo Zheng could not avoid this. When Luo Zheng had just entered the Sea God Continent, he was also chased by three Demon Race War Sages like this. In the end, they almost caught up with him because he could not replenish his vital essence in time.
"This is troublesome!"
Luo Zheng furrowed his brows deeply.
He did not notice that from the beginning to the end, Xi Youqin was lying on Luo Zheng's shoulder expressionlessly like a piece of wood.
Just as Luo Zheng was thinking about how to deal with it, Xi Youqin suddenly said, "Luo Zheng, kill me."
If he killed Xi Youqin, Luo Zheng would not need to carry such a big person to escape. His speed would inevitably increase, and the probability of escaping would naturally be higher.
If it were someone else, Luo Zheng could just let them go. He believed that Cui Xie would not find trouble with other people in order to chase Luo Zheng. However, Xi Youqin had the Extreme Purple Yin Body. She was also someone that Cui Xie must catch. Being in such a situation was indeed very unfavorable for Luo Zheng.
"Kill you?" Xun, who was flying at the same speed as Luo Zheng, sneered, "Aren't you afraid of death?"
Xi Youqin's face revealed a resolute expression, "I was very afraid before, but now … I'm not."
Today was indeed a sad day for Xi Youqin. She suddenly felt that she was a fool in everyone's eyes. Although everyone in the Xi family flattered her, no one trusted her. Even Xi Xiaojie, who grew up with her, was the same.
Under such circumstances, Xi Youqin's mood suddenly became extremely depressed.
As soon as her voice fell, Xun suddenly pointed her spear. Xun really intended to stab this time. The tip of the spear had already pierced Xi Youqin's forehead. In Xun's opinion, only by killing this woman could Luo Zheng escape with all his strength. She had wanted to kill Xi Youqin for a long time.
"Stop," Luo Zheng suddenly said.
"If she dies, we will have a chance to escape," Xun said lightly.
"I know," Luo Zheng replied, staring ahead.
"Why don't you let me kill her?" Xun asked again. She felt that it was unwise to drag this burden down in this situation.
Luo Zheng took a breath and his Vital Spirit erupted again. After his speed suddenly increased, Luo Zheng said, "I have another way."

Chapter 682
There were countless fierce beasts lurking in the Cloud Dream Swamp. So far, the warriors of the Central Domain had not been able to fully explore the Cloud Dream Swamp.
In the sky, Luo Zheng was locked onto by Cui Xie. It was difficult for him to shake off Cui Xie. However, the Cloud Dream Swamp was extremely complicated. Luo Zheng could shake off Cui Xie!
Luo Zheng headed straight for the Cloud Dream Swamp. At this time, a cold smile appeared on Cui Xie's face. "Enter the Cloud Dream Swamp? That's a good idea. However, Luo Zheng, luck doesn't seem to be on your side this time! "
Then, Cui Xie took out a sound transmitting talisman and pinched it lightly. The sound transmitting talisman began to burn. "Wu Zhanhe, a rat has entered the Cloud Dream Swamp. The Void Spirit Seven have been killed by me. We can take down the Void Spirit Sect at any time. Tell Xuanchen to retreat 150 kilometers. Bring your men over!"
Cui Xie was very clear that Wu Zhanhe had been hiding in the Cloud Dream Swamp all these years. In the entire Central Domain, there was probably no one who knew the Cloud Dream Swamp better than Wu Zhanhe.
Why couldn't Luo Zheng hide anywhere? Why did he have to hide in the Cloud Dream Swamp?
After a while, a flame appeared beside Cui Xie's ear. Wu Zhanhe had burned the sound transmitting talisman. A voice came from the sound transmitting talisman. "Haha, the Cloud Dream Swamp is like my back garden to me. Let me help you catch the rat!"
Wu Zhanhe's strength was only slightly weaker than Cui Xie's. The two of them did not have a subordinate relationship. It was just that Wu Zhanhe had the ambition to establish a Holy Land, while Cui Xie dreamed of establishing his own Divine Kingdom. The two of the most powerful people in the Central Domain chose to join forces.
However, Cui Xie was very clear that the two of them were using each other. However, once they succeeded, there would definitely be a battle between them. Wu Zhanhe was definitely hiding a trump card that Cui Xie did not know about. However, Cui Xie was also confident that he could take down Cui Xie.
No matter what, there were no cracks in this seemingly united alliance for the time being. After all, their goals were shockingly similar, and their ambitions were also shockingly great!
After Luo Zheng entered the Yunmeng Swamp, he immediately lowered his altitude and flew close to the ground.
The terrain of the Clouddream Swamp was extremely complicated. There were shallow depressions everywhere. Some of the depressions looked incomparably clear, but in fact, they contained fatal toxins. Even the most powerful creatures would die immediately after licking them!
Luo Zheng was almost flying close to the ground, trying to restrain his aura as much as possible. In order to reduce the True Essence fluctuations and the leakage of his imposing manner, Luo Zheng even let Xun transform into a little red light that entered his body. Because of this, Luo Zheng's flying speed also slowed down.
After entering the Clouddream Swamp, Luo Zheng did not sense Cui Xie's aura. Perhaps Cui Xie had also concealed his own aura. At this moment, Luo Zheng was unable to determine Cui Xie's position. Then, Cui Xie would also find it difficult to lock onto Luo Zheng. This was also the result that Luo Zheng wanted.
The Clouddream Swamp was very vast, and the terrain was also extremely complicated. As long as he retracted his aura and slowly stealthily increased the distance between him and Cui Xie, then carefully left the Clouddream Swamp, it would not be difficult to get rid of Cui Xie.
"It's useless, you can't get out." At this moment, Xi Youqin suddenly opened her mouth and said.
Just as she finished speaking, Luo Zheng covered her mouth with one hand and said with a ruthless expression, "Shut up, do you want to die?"
Her mouth and nose were covered by Luo Zheng, so she could no longer speak. Her pair of brown eyes coldly stared at Luo Zheng. Now, she really did not seem to know what fear was.
At this moment, Luo Zheng felt a great power being released!
Seeing this, Luo Zheng covered Xi Youqin's mouth and quietly approached a quagmire. Then, his whole body sank into the quagmire.
Cui Xie's perception was very sharp. In order to avoid the perception released by Cui Xie, Luo Zheng did not dare to use Zhenqi to protect his body. He was afraid that Cui Xie would sense the fluctuations of Zhenqi, so the two of them were immediately covered by wet mud.
Xi Youqin's natural disposition was to be clean. If she did not circulate her Zhenqi, her whole body would be covered with a layer of black mud. How could she be willing?
At this moment, she twisted her body and made a fuss in Luo Zheng's arms.
"You're not even afraid of death, yet you're afraid of being dirty?" Luo Zheng lowered his voice and sneered.
Hearing this, Xi Youqin suddenly stopped resisting. Then, Luo Zheng dragged her down to the bottom of the mud!
The two of them were completely wrapped in mud and were immersed in the bottom of the quagmire.
Just as they entered, Luo Zheng felt a perception sweep over them and then slowly leave. Obviously, the owner of this perception was Cui Xie.
The two of them quietly lay at the bottom of the quagmire, motionless. After about the time it took to burn a stick of incense, Luo Zheng dragged Xi Youqin out. However, he still did not dare to make too much noise and carefully moved to the shore of the quagmire.
At this time, the white-clothed Xi Youqin had completely turned into a "black person". In a situation where she could not use Zhenqi to protect her body, her face, nose, and ears were all covered in mud!
She had never been so dirty in her life!
The black mud was like crawling worms, making her feel uncomfortable. At this moment, she wanted to immediately find a place to clean herself.
Xi Youqin was about to speak when she heard Luo Zheng say, "Don't move! Don't use Zhenqi! "
She followed Luo Zheng's gaze and saw a small black snake in the grass beside her!
The small snake's entire body was pitch black. Because its surface was too smooth, it flickered with a faint luster.
"Black Feathered Snake!"
Xi Youqin saw the feather-like crown on the small snake's head and immediately recognized the origin of the small snake.
It was not that Xi Youqin was knowledgeable, but the Black Feathered Snake was too famous. Therefore, Xi Youqin was able to recognize it in an instant.
The Black Feathered Snake was one of the most famous poisons in the Central Domain. The price of the Black Feathered Snake's poison was very high. It was a rare poison in the world and also a precious material.
Even the most powerful martial artist would die within a breath of time after being bitten by the Black Feathered Snake. This small snake's poison was extremely overbearing. Therefore, every year, many martial artists would lose their lives trying to catch the Black Feathered Snake!
The moment Xi Youqin saw the Black Feathered Snake, she wanted to use Zhenqi to resist the Black Feathered Snake. However, Luo Zheng stopped her at this time.
"Hiss!"
The Black Feathered Snake probably sensed that the two of them had discovered it. It suddenly jumped out of its body and bit towards Xi Youqin.
At this moment, Luo Zheng quickly stretched out his hand and blocked in front of Xi Youqin. However, the Black Feathered Snake bit Luo Zheng's arm.
The moment the Black Feathered Snake bit Luo Zheng, Xi Youqin wanted to scream. However, before she could scream, Luo Zheng's other hand covered her mouth. Luo Zheng had expected this woman to scream!
Then, Xi Youqin looked at the Black Feathered Snake with fear in her eyes. "You, it bit you …"
Luo Zheng stretched out his hand and grabbed the Black Feathered Snake in his hand. He casually tore the Black Feathered Snake into several pieces and threw them into the grass. With a calm expression, he said, "I'm fine."
Xi Youqin suddenly looked at Luo Zheng as if she was looking at a monster. Although she had heard about all kinds of incredible things that had happened to Luo Zheng, the Black Feathered Snake's venom was so strong. How could he be fine just like that?
"Follow me." Luo Zheng parted the grass in front of him and motioned for Xi Youqin to follow him.
Xi Youqin stood in place and hesitated for a while. She stared at Luo Zheng's back, which was not particularly broad. Although she knew that Luo Zheng was just afraid that she would use Zhenqi, he did not hesitate to stretch out his hand and let the snake bite him. This made her feel very complicated.
After thinking about it, Xi Youqin stepped forward and followed Luo Zheng.
But, her body was covered in a layer of mud, and she felt very uncomfortable.
She had never lacked any cultivation resources since she was young. Also, she was a Heavenly rank genius, and she had great talent, which allowed her to break through to the Divine Core realm without going through any training. Naturally, she couldn't imagine how difficult it was for cultivators in the outside world, and she couldn't imagine how some cultivators would risk their lives just for a low rank Spiritual Weapon, and work hard for a few years just for a single pill!
She had never experienced how difficult it was to walk the path of cultivation, but she had never experienced it.
In a way, this could be considered her first time venturing out into the outside world …
Both of them walked slowly in the Dreamcloud Swamp. Because they were afraid that they would be discovered if they circulated their Zhenqi, they didn't dare fly.
After walking for a while in the swamp, her eyes suddenly lit up. She saw a crystal clear pool, and she wanted to wash off the mud on her body in the pool.
She walked up to Luo Zheng and patted on his shoulder. After that, she said, "I, I want to take a bath …"
Luo Zheng looked at the black Xi Youqin, then he looked at the crystal clear pool. With a grin on his face, he picked up a green Reed Grass and threw it into the pool.
"Sizzle … sizzle … sizzle …"
When the Reed Grass was thrown into the pool, it immediately turned black, and at the same time, a faint smoke emerged from it. Clearly, the clear water in the pool contained a deadly poison.
"Do you still want to take a bath?" Luo Zheng asked with a smile.

Chapter 683
The entire Yunmeng Marsh was shrouded in a faint mist.
Of course, this kind of mist was different from the dense fog in the Mysterious Underworld Cave. The dense fog in the Mysterious Underworld Cave was arranged using a formation. If one couldn't get the formation plate, they would be trapped and unable to walk out of the fog.
The fog in the Yunmeng Marsh might be a little troublesome for ordinary people and warriors with low cultivation. However, for warriors at Luo Zheng's level, they needed to pay more attention to the complex environment and ferocious beasts.
When Xi Youqin saw the highly toxic pond water, her expression changed dramatically. She didn't have an abnormal body like Luo Zheng's that could resist the poison. If she jumped in like this, her whole body would be corroded by the poisonous water until not even dregs would be left!
"But I want to take a bath …" Xi Youqin really couldn't stand being so dirty.
"Bear with it," Luo Zheng shook his head. He pushed aside the grass at the side and continued to move forward.
The direction he was moving in was the direction Cui Xie's perception had just swept over. According to general logic, Luo Zheng should choose to move in the opposite direction from Cui Xie instead of following Cui Xie because they weren't deep into the Yunmeng Marsh at the moment. Since Cui Xie had already entered, Luo Zheng could escape as long as he left the Yunmeng Marsh in the opposite direction.
However, Luo Zheng didn't do so. According to Luo Zheng's judgment, Cui Xie had released his perception so ostentatiously just now to let him discover him, making him think that Cui Xie had already entered the Yunmeng Marsh. In the end, Cui Xie would come back and kill him.
If that was the case, Luo Zheng would become extremely dangerous.
As Luo Zheng moved forward, he was also thinking about how to deal with it. Suddenly, Luo Zheng's expression changed. He suddenly lowered his body and pulled Xi Youqin into the grass at the same time.
In mid-air, a human figure turned back in the thick fog and shot out of the Yunmeng Marsh at an extremely fast speed.
"Sure enough, I guessed right." Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. If he had just left the Yunmeng Marsh, Cui Xie would have caught up with him sooner or later.
In mid-air, Cui Xie was flying at high speed. This density had a great restraining effect on perception. Even though Cui Xie's perception could not extend too far, it could only cover a small area. The most troublesome thing was that although the Yunmeng Marsh looked very desolate, in fact, there were countless creatures lurking in this desolate marsh. If Luo Zheng was lurking among them, Cui Xie would not be able to distinguish them one by one.
Therefore, Cui Xie could only use this method to create an illusion for Luo Zheng, making him think that he had entered the depths of the marsh. But soon, Luo Zheng turned back and rushed out of the marsh. His speed was like a meteor!
If Luo Zheng had avoided his search, he should have chosen to escape from the Yunmeng Marsh because this was an excellent opportunity. Therefore, if Cui Xie returned like this, it was very likely that he would catch up with Luo Zheng. As long as he locked onto Luo Zheng's figure, that brat would not be able to escape his pursuit even if he flew up to the sky or escaped underground.
However, after Cui Xie left the Yunmeng Marsh, he did not find Luo Zheng's figure. Then, he landed on an open space outside the Yunmeng Marsh. Cui Xie placed a thin, long stick shaped object on the open space. The top of the stick shaped object was inlaid with a purple crystal. Zhenqi was flickering in the crystal.
This object was a simple letter tablet. Cui Xie had placed it here before entering the Yunmeng Marsh. If Luo Zheng really left the Yunmeng Marsh, there would be a record in this letter tablet.
Cui Xie checked for a while, but did not find Luo Zheng's figure in the letter tablet. His face revealed a trace of a grim smile. "That cunning brat is still hiding in the Yunmeng Marsh!"
At this time, Cui Xie was not in a hurry to enter the marsh to find Luo Zheng. Instead, he waited for Wu Zhanhe's arrival. Wu Zhanhe had been lurking in the Yunmeng Marsh for so many years. In the Central Domain, there was probably no one more familiar with the Yunmeng Marsh than Cui Xie!
Before this, Cui Xie had already burned the Sound Transmitting Talisman to inform Wu Zhanhe. Therefore, he did not have to wait for long. Suddenly, dozens of escape lights appeared not far away and shot straight towards Cui Xie. The leading escape light was the fastest. That person was no doubt Wu Zhanhe.
After Wu Zhanhe arrived, he only nodded towards Cui Xie, while the other more than 20 people respectfully cupped their hands towards Cui Xie and shouted, "Greetings, Your Majesty!"
Cui Xie nodded and said to Wu Zhanhe, "Zhanhe, that Luo Zheng brought that girl from the Xi family into the Yunmeng Marsh. Do you have a way to find him?"
A confident smile appeared on Wu Zhanhe's face and he chuckled, "I am very familiar with every area of the Yunmeng Marsh. As long as that brat is still in the Yunmeng Marsh, there will be no place for him to hide! Cui Xie, come with me! " Wu Zhanhe then turned back and said to the False Tribulation Realm warriors, "Also, you guys search the area according to my plan! The Yunmeng Marsh is huge and finding a person in it is as difficult as finding a needle in a haystack. In fact, it is not so. Because there are different types of fierce beasts in each area. Once Luo Zheng passes through these areas, he will definitely provoke those fierce beasts. As long as a battle breaks out, I … will be able to sense his location! "
Hearing Wu Zhanhe's tone, Cui Xie's forehead unconsciously wrinkled. He had once asked Wu Zhanhe to address him as Country Lord. Wu Zhanhe had only agreed to it face to face, but he still called him by his full name.
This meant that Wu Zhanhe did not acknowledge his identity as Country Lord.
The reason why the two of them could work together and plan for the Divine Kingdom was because they had the same goal. However, as Life and Death Realm experts, neither side was willing to submit to the other!
"Forget it, after my Great Demonic Joyous Union Array is completed, it won't be too late to suppress you. At that time, if you still don't listen to me, don't blame me for being ruthless," Cui Xie thought indifferently.
Cui Xie thought this way, but how could Wu Zhanhe not have the same thoughts?
For the Kingdom of Evil Deities to be able to develop to its current state, how could it not have Wu Zhanhe's contribution? Asking him to address Cui Xie as Country Lord, there was no way!
Why was Wu Zhanhe so proactive this time?
Luo Zheng might have to die, but that Xi Youqin would also have to die. Wu Zhanhe also did not wish for Xi Youqin to fall into Cui Xie's hands. The moment Cui Xie's Great Demonic Joyous Union Array was completed, Wu Zhanhe's end would not be much better! The best fate for him would be to become Cui Xie's vassal. If Cui Xie was a little more ruthless, he would probably be the first person that Cui Xie would kill after breaking through.
The two of them seemed to be united on the surface, but they were divided at heart. But together, they were indeed the strongest power in the Central Domain!
Under Wu Zhanhe's orders, everyone entered the Cloud Dream Swamp and used voice transmission talismans to communicate with each other.
Wu Zhanhe was extremely familiar with the Cloud Dream Swamp. The Cloud Dream Swamp was vast, and even Cui Xie had not entered the deepest part of the swamp. Or it could be said that since ancient times, only Wu Zhanhe had lived here before. Thus, he was extremely familiar with every part of the Cloud Dream Swamp.
When Wu Zhanhe entered the Dreamy Cloud Swamp, his body suddenly emitted streaks of green poisonous fog. The moment this poisonous fog was released, it spread rapidly within the thick fog. He slowly floated in mid-air, his eyes closed as though he was trying to communicate with something!
Because they were walking, Luo Zheng and Xi Youqin's speed was not fast. After such a long time, they had only walked less than 10 li.
How difficult was it to walk through the swamp? There was mud and puddles everywhere. Xi Youqin frowned along the way, using her hands to hold up her black skirt. Every time she took a step, she would pout. Looking at her sleeves sinking into the black mud, she could not help but feel disgusted.
Of course, this expression was seen by Luo Zheng, but he was too lazy to educate this arrogant young lady.
The difference in personality between people was too big. Xun's status was thousands of times higher than the young lady of the Xi family. In the upper realm, Xun was also the king of the peak. It could be said that she was born noble and could stand at the highest level of life to look down on all living things.
However, after Xun understood her situation, she quickly determined her position. She was very decisive, almost without any hesitation, and placed all her hopes on Luo Zheng! And this Xi Youqin still did not understand the situation …
When the two of them passed through a patch of low grass, there was a large stretch of swamp in front of them. This swamp was not deep, and the water in the swamp was only waist high. However, the two of them did not dare to use their true essence to fly, so they could only wade through the swamp.
"There's no poison here, right?" Xi Youqin glanced at Luo Zheng and asked.
Luo Zheng stepped into the swamp and nodded, "There's no poison."
Xi Youqin's face revealed a trace of joy. At least now she could take a good bath!
The two of them slowly waded through the swamp. As Xi Youqin walked, she used the clear water to wash the dirt on her body. After bleaching her plain white clothes, the clothes stuck to her body, outlining her slim waist and proud twin peaks.

Chapter 684
Underneath the ripples were numerous crocodiles with grey scales all over their bodies.
The full name of this crocodile was the Fine-Scaled Orange Toothed Crocodile. Its skin could be used to make a Spiritual Weapon armor, but not many warriors were willing to hunt this Orange Toothed Crocodile.
The reason was that this Orange Toothed Crocodile was extremely ferocious, and it had an incredible defensive ability. Even ordinary Divine Core warriors would find it hard to hunt it down. Even if they spent a lot of effort and killed this Orange Toothed Crocodile, they would only get a piece of crocodile skin in return. It was a huge difference between the investment and the reward. Therefore, no warriors would want to hunt this Orange Toothed Crocodile.
Within this swamp, there were over a hundred Orange Toothed Crocodiles quietly approaching Luo Zheng.
Not only did Luo Zheng restrain his True Yuan, he didn't even dare expose his Divine Sense. Therefore, he didn't notice these Orange Toothed Crocodiles.
Both he and Xi Youqin were moving forward through the water, and the faint ripples were getting closer and closer.
"Splash!"
When an Orange Toothed Crocodile got close to Xi Youqin, it suddenly opened its huge mouth and bit toward Xi Youqin.
This Orange Toothed Crocodile was an expert at camouflage. If one didn't look closely, they would think it was just a piece of rotten wood floating in the water. However, once it started moving, its speed wasn't slow at all.
Hearing the sound of water behind her, Xi Youqin abruptly turned her head, only to see a bloody mouth in front of her. She was stunned for a moment. This Orange-toothed Crocodile had two rows of orange-red teeth on its upper and lower jaws. Even if it bit pig iron, it would leave a bite mark!
In fact, ordinary Divine Core warriors weren't afraid of this Orange Toothed Crocodile. Although Xi Youqin was a Divine Core warrior, she had almost zero experience, and she didn't know how to deal with this situation.
Right at this moment, Luo Zheng's figure suddenly moved. He lightly tapped his feet on the surface of the water, then he jumped forward and stomped on the Orange Toothed Crocodile's lower jaw. The upper jaw of the Orange Toothed Crocodile was like a door filled with nails as it slammed down toward Luo Zheng. It was determined to crush this man in its mouth.
However, Luo Zheng didn't hesitate and simply punched toward the upper jaw.
"Crack!"
Luo Zheng's punch directly tore apart the crocodile's mouth. Bright red blood immediately soaked the clear water surface. The Orange-toothed Crocodile was in so much pain that it couldn't help but roll on the water surface, splashing out waves.

Xi Youqin screamed again. Six Orange-toothed Crocodiles appeared around Xi Youqin at the same time. In the eyes of the Orange-toothed Crocodiles, Xi Youqin was clearly a good meal!

These Orange-toothed Crocodiles posed no threat to Luo Zheng. Even if Luo Zheng just stood there and let these Orange-toothed Crocodiles bite him, they wouldn't be able to swallow Luo Zheng. However, it wasn't easy to kill so many Orange-toothed Crocodiles either. If Luo Zheng used his True Yuan, he could easily kill these Orange-toothed Crocodiles. But, the problem was, he was afraid that the ripples produced by his True Yuan would be sensed by Cui Xie.

"This is troublesome!"
Luo Zheng floated past, and the True Yuan in his body began to slowly circulate. Then, Luo Zheng grabbed the frightened Xi Youqin and sped forward, sticking close to the surface of the water.
Those Orange-toothed Crocodiles couldn't fly, so naturally, they couldn't catch up. When they reached the edge of the swamp, Luo Zheng dispersed his True Yuan. Luo Zheng grabbed Xi Youqin and said, "Quickly, leave this place!"
At the same time, Wu Zhanhe, who was quietly floating in the air, revealed a trace of a smile. "Very good, I found you! In the southeast region, Luo Zheng should be near the crocodile pool. Be careful of the Orange-toothed Crocodiles in the water! "
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh!"
In mid-air, several Virtual Tribulation Realm martial artists heard Wu Zhanhe's words and immediately turned into several streaks of light, flying towards the southeast. They didn't have the misgivings of Luo Zheng and Xi Youqin. How fast was their speed when they ran like this?
Wu Zhanhe wasn't slow either. Not only did he want to kill Luo Zheng, but he also wanted to kill the young lady of the Xi Clan! Therefore, he had to be one step ahead of Cui Xie.
In less than half an hour, those Virtual Tribulation Realm martial artists had already arrived at the swamp that Luo Zheng and Xi Youqin had just passed by. They saw an Orange-toothed Crocodile with its mouth wide open and its white belly floating on the water.
"Chase, Luo Zheng should have just passed by here!"
"Killing Luo Zheng is a great merit. The Monarch will definitely reward you handsomely!"
Why were these independent martial artists so dead set on following Cui Xie? It was because Cui Xie's rewards were extremely generous. Moreover, after uniting the independent martial artists, these independent martial artists felt a sense of belonging. At least, if anything happened, the Monarch of the Gods would step in and help them solve it.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh!"
Those several escape lights scattered in several directions, searching for Luo Zheng inch by inch.
At this time, Luo Zheng and Xi Youqin continued to advance into the depths of the Yunmeng Swamp. Suddenly, Luo Zheng felt a Zhen Yuan fluctuation behind him. Then, a shallow escape light passed through the fog.
"Get down!"
Luo Zheng grabbed Xi Youqin and hid in the grass.
That escape light was extremely fast. In the blink of an eye, it passed over Luo Zheng's head.
"A False Tribulation Realm martial artist." Luo Zheng blinked. It seemed that Cui Xie had ordered his subordinates to hunt him down. In other words, Cui Xie was definitely not the only martial artist in the Yunmeng Swamp. In order to hunt him down, this Cui Xie didn't even attack the Void Spirit Sect. He really thought too highly of himself!
The two of them hid in the grass. Naturally, the False Tribulation Realm martial artist didn't discover them. Luo Zheng originally planned to leave with Xi Youqin after the martial artist left. However, at this time, a small spider quietly crawled along the grass towards Xi Youqin.
On the spider's back was a small ghost face. Luo Zheng had encountered this kind of ghost face spider on the road of the Trial Challengers. It wasn't considered a fierce creature.
However, this ghost face spider jumped up and jumped onto Xi Youqin's arm.
Xi Youqin felt something on her arm. When she raised her hand to look, she was immediately shocked. Although she knew that she couldn't make any noise at this time, she still couldn't help but let out a sharp cry.
"Ah!"
Luo Zheng's expression changed. He grabbed Xi Youqin's mouth. As for the ghost face spider, Luo Zheng casually flicked it, shattering it into pieces.
The two of them were still hiding in the shadows, motionless.
Luo Zheng wasn't afraid of the False Tribulation Realm martial artist in front of him. However, once he attacked, he would definitely expose his position and attract Cui Xie's attention.
The False Tribulation Realm martial artist above him had already left. How fast was a False Tribulation Realm martial artist? He flew over Luo Zheng's head. By the time Xi Youqin screamed, he had already flown over a mile.
But at this moment, he suddenly heard a woman's scream.
The False Tribulation Realm martial artist had a strange expression on his face. He floated in the air and thought for a moment. Then, he changed direction and flew towards Luo Zheng.
The sound just now was very sharp, sounding like a woman's scream. In this quiet swamp, it could travel a long distance, but the sound was very sharp, and it was maintained for a very short period of time, causing the Nihility Tribulation Realm martial artist to be suspicious.
He wasn't sure if it was a woman's scream or the cry of a bird.
Since he had doubts, he decided to go back and carefully search this area.
The False Tribulation Realm martial artist slowly floated in the air. He used his perception to sweep the ground inch by inch. After sweeping the swamp, he slowly began to sweep the grass. At this time, he was getting closer and closer to Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng pressed Xi Youqin against his body, revealing only his eyes. He stared at the approaching martial artist who was slowly floating in the air. His calm eyes were filled with pressure. With this method, it was almost certain that he would be able to find Xi Youqin and Luo Zheng.
"I hope you're lucky enough to miss this place." Luo Zheng thought to himself.
As long as this False Tribulation Realm martial artist found him, Luo Zheng would definitely kill him!
But, the False Tribulation Realm martial artist had no idea that he was walking towards the edge of death. In this False Tribulation Realm martial artist's eyes, Luo Zheng was just a new False Tribulation Realm martial artist, and he had an Early Divine Core warrior with him. An opponent like this wasn't difficult to deal with. As long as he could capture Luo Zheng, it would definitely be a great contribution!
Similar to a sect, the martial artists in the Divine Kingdom would compete with each other. If he could stand out from the other False Tribulation Realm martial artists, then in the future, when Cui Xie's Divine Kingdom was completed, as long as he could get a share of the soup, it would definitely bring him endless benefits.

Chapter 685
The Void Tribulation Realm cultivator was only about ten feet away from Luo Zheng. The moment the Void Tribulation Realm cultivator's perception swept over and discovered Luo Zheng, the Astral Essence within Luo Zheng's body suddenly erupted!
Whoosh!
Luo Zheng shot towards the Void Tribulation Realm cultivator like an arrow released from a bow. Before the Void Tribulation Realm cultivator could react, Luo Zheng had already arrived in front of him.
"You!" The Void Tribulation Realm cultivator didn't know how to react at all. He only blurted out one word. In a moment of desperation, he wanted to ask if Luo Zheng was Luo Zheng …
However, he forgot that since he had discovered Luo Zheng here, Luo Zheng would definitely fight to the death. Why would he waste time talking nonsense with him?
Peng!
Luo Zheng's fist landed directly on the Void Tribulation Realm cultivator's chest. After receiving Luo Zheng's thunderous punch, the Void Tribulation Realm cultivator's chest deflated like a deflated balloon. He didn't even have the chance to howl in grief as he fell limply into the grass!
From the moment Luo Zheng gathered his strength, to the moment he attacked, and then to the moment he killed the Void Tribulation Realm cultivator, everything was done in one go without any hesitation.
Xi Youqin was also a cultivator, and her cultivation wasn't low either. However, this scene shocked her to the core!
Ever since she was young, Xi Youqin had watched many battles between cultivators. Within the Xi Family, the various large families of Void Sky City, and the Void Spirit Sect, there were battles almost every year. As the Young Miss of the Xi Family, Xi Youqin always had the highest seat reserved for her. Xi Youqin boasted of her sharp and unique vision. Whenever she watched the cultivators fight in the arena, she would often comment on them. Every time, her estimation would be seventy to eighty percent accurate.
But now, Xi Youqin discovered that the battles in the arena were simply child's play compared to the current battle.
She didn't expect that Luo Zheng, who had just entered the Void Tribulation Realm, would be able to kill a middle stage Void Tribulation Realm cultivator so easily. The Void Tribulation Realm cultivator didn't even have the time to react …

.. Although Cui Xie had been deliberately ordered by Wu Zhanhe to search for another area, he was also rushing in this direction. His speed was also the fastest!
Bits of Sword Spirit's light flickered around Luo Zheng. Since their location had already been exposed, there was no need for Xun to hide. After Xun appeared, she coldly stared at Xi Youqin, and then said to Luo Zheng, "I've already said that this woman is a nuisance. Killing her is the best choice!"

Perhaps Cui Xie and the others would have searched for Luo Zheng for a while, but they couldn't find him and would eventually choose to give up. Now, they had returned to the beginning and were being chased by Cui Xie. The problem was that there wasn't just one Cui Xie, there were other Void Tribulation Realm cultivators!
Everyone was shuttling through the dense milky white fog. They couldn't see each other's figures, but they could feel each other's aura and Zhen Yuan fluctuations.
Luo Zheng didn't speak, and just focused on circulating his Zhen Yuan. The swamp on the ground rapidly receded in Luo Zheng's field of vision, and the surrounding scenery gradually changed.
When Luo Zheng had just entered the Cloud Dream Swamp, the surroundings were still emerald green, but as he continued to enter the depths of the Cloud Dream Swamp, the swamp and plants gradually turned dark brown, and the surrounding aura became more and more lifeless.
While fleeing, Luo Zheng felt that the Zhen Yuan fluctuations behind him were becoming more and more intense. In addition to the weaker Zhen Yuan fluctuations, Luo Zheng also felt two relatively powerful Zhen Yuan fluctuations!
"One should be Cui Xie, but who is the other? His speed is actually not much slower than Cui Xie's! Is he also a Life and Death expert? "Luo Zheng's brows wrinkled. For him, this was definitely not good news.
Luo Zheng was carrying a person, and his Astral Essence couldn't be used for a long time, so the distance between him and the two behind him was getting closer and closer. From the initial forty to fifty li to thirty li, they were now only ten li away from each other!
"Hehe, Luo Zheng, I already said that you can't escape. In the end, you're still wasting your strength!" A steady voice suddenly resounded behind Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng's face sank. He naturally wouldn't respond to Cui Xie, and continued to move forward. However, if this continued, Luo Zheng couldn't see the slightest chance of turning the situation around, and didn't know what to do next.
However, what Luo Zheng didn't expect was that at this moment, a strange voice suddenly sounded in his ears, "Kid, run to the right!"
Hearing this voice, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed, and he immediately sent a Zhen Yuan sound transmission, "Who are you?"
"Who am I? Hehe … "That person didn't answer.
Luo Zheng's face revealed a puzzled expression. This Zhen Yuan sound transmission had come from behind, so it should have come from one of the two martial artists behind him. Separated by the fog, Luo Zheng couldn't tell which one was Cui Xie, but the owner of this voice was definitely one of the Life and Death experts!
Who was it? Why did they give him such a suggestion?
This voice, it was somewhat familiar …
Luo Zheng's memory wasn't bad. He seemed to have heard this voice two years ago, but in his anxiety, Luo Zheng couldn't remember who it was.
"Why do you want to help me?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"Help you? Hehe, I just don't want that woman on your body to fall into Cui Xie's hands, "the voice laughed mischievously.

He was also very hesitant in his heart, because Luo Zheng couldn't completely believe this person's words. If this person was playing tricks and led Luo Zheng into a trap, wouldn't Luo Zheng be walking right into a trap?

At that time, Luo Zheng had been cursed in the Wheel of Fortune, but in the end, Wu Zhanhe gave a chance to dispel the curse to his opponent, a warrior from Divine Kingdom, and dispelled the curse for the Saintess of Divine Kingdom.
After Luo Zheng knew that this person was Wu Zhanhe, he actually believed his words!

It seemed that Wu Zhanhe had indeed joined forces with Cui Xie, but the two of them didn't seem to be on good terms!
This wasn't difficult to understand. How could experts at their level be willing to share benefits with others? As the saying goes, how could one allow others to snore beside one's bed? For the vast majority of warriors, what they hoped for was to be supreme.
It was impossible for someone like Wu Zhanhe to submit to Cui Xie, and it was even more impossible for Cui Xie to tolerate Wu Zhanhe. Although the two of them had joined forces, there was an irreconcilable conflict between them.
If Xi Youqin fell into Cui Xie's hands, and Cui Xie broke through, how could Wu Zhanhe's days be any better?

Wu Zhanhe sent a voice transmission. Then, he coldly said in his heart, "But that underground palace will also take your lives!"
Below the Cloud Dream Swamp, there was a large underground palace. In the entire Central Domain, only he knew of this secret!
However, even though this underground palace was a dangerous place, there didn't seem to be any opportunities in it. Instead, it was extremely dangerous. Wu Zhanhe had explored it twice, but he didn't find any treasures. Instead, he almost died in it. Hence, Wu Zhanhe gave up on exploring this underground palace.
Now, sending Luo Zheng into this underground palace and letting him die inside was the best choice.

Chapter 686
Between cultivators, they all had their own schemes.
Luo Zheng wanted to escape with Xi Youqin, while Cui Xie wanted to kill Luo Zheng and capture Xi Youqin. As for Wu Zhanhe, his goal was to kill Xi Youqin. The higher the grade of the Extreme Purple Yin Body, the easier it would be to communicate with the Extreme Purple Realm, and the more resources one would be able to obtain from the Extreme Purple Realm. Only when a First Grade Extreme Purple Yin Body was opened would one be surrounded by purple flames, and no one would be able to get close to them!
These purple flames were extremely powerful. Not only those Xiantian realm warriors, even those Divine Core and False Tribulation realm warriors couldn't get close to them. Even those Life and Death realm warriors had to use all of their strength to barely resist these purple flames!
These purple flames burned for an entire day and night ….
In the Fire Sun City, this was no small matter. At first, it had attracted countless civilians, then some Xiantian Secret Realm martial artists and a few Divine Reflection Realm martial artists to investigate, but none of them could find out anything.
Finally, the remaining three Divine Core warriors in Sunfire City arrived, but they still didn't dare get close to the purple flames.
After 24 hours, one day and one night, the purple flames finally began to dissipate. Xi Youqin who was sitting cross-legged within the purple flames slowly opened her eyes. In just one day and one night, her temperament had undergone a tremendous change!
During this period of time, Xi Youqin had been cultivating in the Extreme Purple Realm. Not only did she learn about the rules of the Extreme Purple Realm, she also learned about all sorts of secrets, and she finally understood how frightening her innate talent was!
The Extreme Purple Yin Body was a special martial art. This martial art was very different from the normal martial arts. It was a shortcut, but the first step would be limited by the innate talent of the Extreme Purple Yin Body. For example, a normal Extreme Purple Yin Body with a "Light Purple" Sixth Grade would never be able to reach the height of a "Blue" Fifth Grade in their lifetime no matter how high their cultivation was!
However, Xi Youqin possessed the Extreme Purple Yin Body, a First Grade Dark Purple Yin Body!
"I didn't expect there to be such a pretty little girl inside the purple flames!"
"What a pretty little girl! She has broken through to the Divine Core realm at such a young age! Could she be from some big family?"
Everyone was trying to guess Xi Youqin's identity.
Three Divine Core warriors standing not far away from her blinked their eyes. They looked at each other, then a strange smile emerged on their faces.
In the eyes of those Divine Reflection and Xiantian realm warriors, this girl was someone they couldn't afford to offend. However, in the eyes of these three men, she was nothing.
Although this girl in front of them was an Early Divine Core warrior, and although her foundation was solid, her True Yuan was extremely weak! This meant that her actual combat strength was extremely weak!
Judging from the looks of it, this girl must be from some big family, and she must be very wealthy!
According to the information they received, Cui Xie was attacking the Void Spirit Sect, and this girl might be a warrior who escaped from some Third Grade Families in the Void Sky City. Perhaps she had some treasures from those big families?
Even if she didn't have any treasures, just her pretty body alone was a treasure in their eyes!
The Divine Core warrior with a mustache walked over and asked politely, "Young lady, is there anything I can help you with?"
Xi Youqin didn't have much confidence in front of Luo Zheng. How could she possibly be nice to an ordinary warrior? She only threw a cold glance at the mustache man, then she was about to fly up to him.
However, the other two Divine Core warriors were one step ahead of her, and they blocked Xi Youqin's path.
"Young lady, why are you in such a hurry? Since you're here in the Fiery Sun City, you should rest well before you leave! "
"You're right, why don't you accompany us for a drink?"
If it was before the awakening of her Extreme Purple Yin Body, it would have been very difficult for Xi Youqin to get rid of these three men. Although these three warriors were only Early Divine Core warriors, and they were all from Second Grade Sects, so their actual combat strength was nothing to worry about. However, at that time, Xi Youqin was much weaker than them.
However, because of that one day and night, Xi Youqin had undergone a complete transformation.
"Get lost!" Xi Youqin said coldly.
"Don't be so fierce!" One of the Divine Core warriors said.
Another mustache man laughed and said, "It's okay to be fierce, I like fierce girls! Especially when a beauty is fierce, it's a totally different flavor! "
Right at this moment, Xi Youqin blinked her eyes, and the three Divine Core warriors suddenly felt a gust of wind blowing past them. After that, some dark shadows appeared in the sky.
When the three Divine Core warriors raised their heads, they immediately saw an ancient looking huge sword appearing in the sky. There were more than a hundred huge swords! Each huge sword was a few dozen meters long, and they were hanging in the sky, as if they were going to fall down at any moment.
"What, what is this?!"
"Why are there so many swords all of a sudden?!"
Xi Youqin coldly harrumphed, then the hundred huge swords all smashed towards the Divine Core warriors at the same time!
"Bam bam bam bam bam …"
The huge swords smashed onto the ground, leaving deep trenches in the ground. The Fiery-Sun City wasn't a big city, so when these huge swords smashed onto the ground, the entire city began to tremble. Those who didn't know what was going on might even think that an earthquake had occurred!
With over a hundred huge swords smashing onto the ground, how big was the area they covered? Even if these Divine Core warriors wanted to dodge, it was too late. They didn't even have the time to let out a miserable shriek before they were instantly turned into meat paste by these huge swords!
With a cold expression, Xi Youqin flew up into the sky. Although she was a warrior, this was her first time killing someone!
After flying up into the sky, a hesitant expression emerged on Xi Youqin's face. She didn't know whether she should return to the Void Sky City or Cloud Sea City. She stared at the direction where Lu Zheng disappeared, and a sad expression emerged on her face. In the end, she decided to fly back to Cloud Sea City.
Right now, Xi Youqin was very confident in her own strength. The experience she had in the Extreme Purple Realm had allowed her to understand her potential and true strength.

Chapter 690
All the other warriors in the Fiery Sun City stared coldly at the scene in front of them.
After the gigantic swords disappeared, they left behind a huge crater on the ground, and countless sword marks could be seen within the crater.
Looking at the miserable state of the three Divine Core warriors, all the other warriors felt a lingering fear in their hearts.
After the woman left, those Xiantian realm warriors started discussing with each other with fear still lingering in their hearts.
"That woman is really ruthless, she turned three Divine Core warriors into minced meat with just a single strike …"
"Luckily I ran away when I realized the situation wasn't right. If not, I would have been dragged into this as well! Don't be fooled by that woman's pretty face, she's really ruthless! "
"Sigh … this is a chaotic world, I think it's better for us not to get involved."
For one day and one night, Luo Zheng maintained a high speed as he flew back to Cloud Sea City. He was eager to return home, and he didn't want to stop for even a second. Along the way, he had replenished countless True Origin Stones.
Luo Zheng had returned from the Sea God Continent, and he wanted to return to the Cloud Hall as soon as possible. However, he didn't expect to encounter so many obstacles along the way.
A bright red glow began to shine from the horizon, and the morning sun dyed the clouds golden. Looking at Cloud Sea City in front of him, a faint smile finally emerged on Luo Zheng's face. After a long journey, he had finally returned to Cloud Sea City.
"Eh?"
When Luo Zheng approached Cloud Sea City, a doubtful expression emerged on his face.
The sky above Cloud Sea City was empty, and he couldn't see any sign of the Cloud Hall.
The Cloud Hall leaned on a huge floating rock and floated above Cloud Sea City. It was shrouded in clouds all year round, and occasionally, the Cloud Hall would change its position. But, it would always circle above Cloud Sea City.
But now, Luo Zheng realized that the entire Cloud Hall had disappeared!
Could it be that something had happened to the Cloud Hall?
That shouldn't be the case. While flying back to Cloud Sea City, Luo Zheng furrowed his brows.
In the Central Domain, there weren't many forces that could threaten the Cloud Hall. After Luo Zheng arranged the Sect Protecting Great Array, apart from the Tian Xie Sect, no other force could take down the Cloud Hall.
What's more, the Tian Xie Sect was attacking the Void Spirit Sect right now. Cui Xie couldn't take care of both sides at the same time. If the Cloud Hall had been attacked before, the Xi family would have definitely told him.
It was useless to think too much about it. After Luo Zheng entered the city, he headed straight for the Cloud Sect.
The Cloud Sect was one of the 5 great 2nd Rank Sects under the Cloud Hall. However, when compared to the 2nd Rank Sects like the Blue Cloud Sect and the Misty Cloud Sect, the Cloud Sect's status was slightly higher. After all, the Cloud Sect was closely connected to the Cloud Hall. Moreover, the various clans in Ocean of Clouds City would ceaselessly send their disciples to the Cloud Sect, and the elites of the Cloud Sect were slightly more numerous than the Blue Cloud Sect and the Misty Cloud Sect.
Right after Luo Zheng entered the cave, two Divine Core warriors immediately came up to him from below.
"Who are you from the Cloud Sect?" One of the Divine Core warriors asked Luo Zheng. When he discovered that Luo Zheng was a False Tribulation warrior, a fearful expression emerged on his face.
The Sect Chief of a 2nd Rank Sect was only a Late Divine Core warrior. However, since the Cloud Sect was supported by the Cloud Hall, the Divine Core warrior wasn't worried about this man causing trouble. But, now that something had happened in the Cloud Hall, he was worried that this man would take advantage of the situation and loot it!
"Where is the Cloud Sect's Sect Chief?" Luo Zheng asked in an indifferent manner.
"Who are you? Why are you looking for our Sect Chief? "The Divine Core warrior asked again.
"Cloud Hall, Luo Zheng." Luo Zheng told them his name.
The two Divine Core warriors looked at each other. When they heard Luo Zheng's name, a panicked expression emerged in their eyes. "Luo Zheng! You're back? "
Their panicked expressions couldn't escape Luo Zheng's eyes. He was puzzled as well, why did these two men panic when they heard his name? Could it be that someone didn't want to see him back? Although he was thinking about this, his expression didn't change at all.
"That's right, where is the Cloud Sect's Sect Chief? Ask him to come see me! "Luo Zheng asked again.
"Sect Chief, Sect Chief went to the Cloud Hall …" The Divine Core warrior replied.
"Why did the Cloud Hall leave Cloud Sea City? Where is the Cloud Hall now? "Luo Zheng asked again.
The Divine Core warrior stuttered, "I, I don't know!"
Perhaps this Divine Core warrior didn't want to tell Luo Zheng where the Cloud Hall was, but he couldn't think of a way to lie to Luo Zheng. Therefore, he could only say he didn't know.
Seeing the warrior's reaction, Luo Zheng became even more suspicious. With a swoosh, he flew in front of the warrior and said with a cold voice, "You don't know? Or you don't want to tell me? Tell me, where is the Cloud Hall? "While speaking, Luo Zheng stared at the warrior. A faint killing intent emerged on his face.
The other Divine Core warrior said, "Luo Zheng, the Cloud Hall is located 30 miles northwest of Cloud Sea City! You'll find the Cloud Hall if you head northwest! "
"En." Luo Zheng replied with an indifferent tone. He had guessed that something must have happened in the Cloud Hall, but he didn't want to waste his time on these two warriors. Everything would be clear once he returned to the Cloud Hall.
After Luo Zheng turned into a beam of light and left, the Divine Core warrior glared at him and said, "Why did you tell him? Now that this fiend is back, our family's plan is going to be ruined! I'm afraid we'll all be implicated! "
The other Divine Core warrior sneered, "Why didn't you tell him? Are you kidding me? It's fine if Luo Zheng didn't die in the outside world, but now he's back, and he's even broken through to the Void Tribulation realm! He was able to defeat a Divine Core genius when he was just a Divine Reflection warrior! If you didn't tell him just now, I bet he would definitely attack you, and you wouldn't even be able to withstand a single attack from him! "
The warrior fell silent, then he murmured to himself, "This is troublesome, now that Luo Zheng is back, we'll be in big trouble!"
After Luo Zheng received the news, he immediately started flying northwest. 30 miles was just a blink of an eye for Luo Zheng, but Luo Zheng's heart was in his throat.
The two Divine Core warriors were definitely hiding something from him. They clearly knew the relationship between him and Ning Yudie, and if the Cloud Hall was in trouble, they wouldn't have such an expression when they saw him. Since they didn't want to see him return, then Ning Yudie must be in trouble!
Soon, Luo Zheng saw a huge floating palace in front of him. It was the Cloud Hall, and right at this moment, the barrier on the surface of the Cloud Hall was flickering with faint ripples. It was the Light of Water Barrier from the Minor Universal Triple Slaughter Formation that Luo Zheng had set up.
This barrier was able to withstand a full force attack from a Life and Death warrior, and even with Luo Zheng's current cultivation, he could break it apart with just a single strike.
Standing outside the barrier, Luo Zheng looked around. He didn't see any guards at the entrance of the Cloud Hall, and Luo Zheng couldn't find anyone to open the barrier for him!
Bam!
Right at this moment, a bright light flickered from deep within the Cloud Hall, followed by a violent energy fluctuation.
"Someone is attacking me inside the Cloud Hall?" Luo Zheng furrowed his brows.
With that thought in mind, Luo Zheng circled around the Light of Water Barrier and arrived at the bottom of the barrier.
This Minor Universal Triple Slaughter Formation was set up by Luo Zheng himself, and he knew exactly where the eye of the formation was located.
The entire Light of Water Barrier was like a huge eggshell. It covered the entire Cloud Hall from top to bottom and from front to back. When Luo Zheng arrived at the bottom of the Cloud Hall, he gently twisted his body in midair. After circling around a few times, he easily entered the barrier. After that, Luo Zheng didn't think too much about it, he simply rushed into the depths of the Cloud Hall.
Luo Zheng had guessed half of what had happened in the Cloud Hall.
Right now, the entire Cloud Hall was in a state of internal strife.
In fact, compared to other Sects in the Central Domain, the Cloud Hall's defense was the most stable. Even the Tian Xie Sect wouldn't be able to break through the Cloud Hall's Sect Protecting Array. However, this time, the trouble came from inside the Cloud Hall!
After Ning Yudie cultivated the Empyrean Fairy Heart Sutra, she quickly recovered all her strength. After nine times of dissipating her cultivation, Ning Yudie could finally break through to the Life and Death Realm!
Breaking through to the Life and Death Realm wasn't difficult for Ning Yudie. The difficult part was dealing with the Life and Death Tribulation!
Warriors who entered the Life and Death Realm had to go through the Life and Death Tribulation. The difficulty of the Life and Death Tribulation varied from one warrior to another. However, there were still certain rules to follow.
The greater a warrior's potential and strength, the more terrifying the Life and Death Tribulation would be. For example, Yun Luo's Empyrean Fairy Heart Sutra was extremely troublesome to cultivate. However, once she succeeded in cultivating it, her strength would be far greater than other warriors of the same level. After she broke through to the Life and Death Realm, the Life and Death Tribulation she would face would naturally be extremely terrifying!
It was at this critical juncture that the Zhuo Family of Cloud Sea City joined forces with several other third-grade families to launch a sudden attack.

Chapter 691
The rapid rise of the Cloud Hall wasn't just due to Ning Yudie alone. The strength of all the cultivators in the Cloud Hall had steadily increased.
After obtaining the Minor Heavenly Cycle Triple Kill Formation, the Cloud Hall was already ranked as a quasi fifth-grade sect. However, in the eyes of the Central Domain cultivators, the Cloud Hall was already a fifth-grade sect that wasn't inferior to the Void Spirit Sect. Even if it was inferior, it was only inferior to the Void Spirit Sect in terms of foundation.
Once Ning Yudie broke through to the Life and Death Realm, the Cloud Hall's status as a fifth-grade sect would be unshakable. Once Ning Yudie broke through, she would take advantage of the Cloud Hall's soaring momentum and lead the Cloud Hall to become a sixth-grade sect, giving birth to the first Holy Land in the Central Domain!
Who would have thought that when Ning Yudie was breaking through to the Life and Death Realm, the Cloud Hall would have such a serious internal strife!
The seeds of this internal strife had been sown a few decades ago.
The Cloud Hall slowly developed from the Cloud Hall. At first, the prototype of the Cloud Hall was the Cloud Sect. As the Cloud Sect continued to develop, it joined forces with the Zhuo Family, Chen Family, Fei Family, Wu Family, and other third-grade families to form the Cloud Hall.
No matter how the Cloud Hall developed, the position of the Cloud Hall's Hall Master had always been occupied by the descendants of the major families.
At that time, Granny Yu didn't come from any of these major families. Instead, she was a genius disciple from the Cloud Hall's Rosy Cloud Sect.
However, Granny Yu's cultivation was powerful and her talent was extremely high. Although the major families in the Cloud Sea City had some criticisms about Granny Yu's birth, the genius disciples of the major families were useless and couldn't compete with Granny Yu at all!
However, when it came to Ning Yudie's generation, it was different. Everyone knew about Ning Yudie's birth. Although she grew up in the Cloud Sect, she was born as an outsider. Moreover, at this time, there were many people who could shoulder the burden of the Cloud Hall. For example, Mister Zhuo and the others had the strength to compete for the position of the Hall Master of the Cloud Hall!
In the end, Granny Yu passed the position of Palace Master to Ning Yudie. This made the other aristocratic families very confused, especially the Zhuo family led by Mister Zhuo!
From the Zhuo family's point of view, Ning Yudie was indeed more outstanding than Mr. Zhuo in certain aspects. Under Ning Yudie's leadership, the Cloud Hall had indeed undergone considerable changes. For example, Ning Yudie had generously treated Luo Zheng and brought an unparalleled protective array to the Cloud Hall.
However, those aristocratic families were not as far-sighted as Granny Yu. Even if Ning Yudie's existence was indeed beneficial to the Cloud Hall, they should not let an outsider take charge of the Cloud Hall!
This was especially true for Mister Zhuo, who had seized every opportunity to topple Ning Yudie from her position as Hall Master. Unfortunately, Mister Zhuo had never been able to seize this opportunity.
However, Mister Zhuo now saw an opportunity.
Granny Yu, in order to find an opportunity to pass through the next life and death calamity, had gone on an expedition to Heavenly Star with the independent martial artist Venerable Nanhua. Once Granny Yu left, there was no one in the Cloud Hall to support Ning Yudie. Now, Ning Yudie wanted to break through to the Life and Death Realm.
At this time, Ning Yudie was facing a terrifying life and death calamity. She herself was facing a considerable test. Perhaps, with just a little bit of pressure from the side, she would fail the calamity!
How could Mister Zhuo let go of such a good opportunity?
Granny Yu was getting closer and closer to her life and death calamity. Since ancient times, how many people had returned from exploring the sea in search of opportunities?
Once Ning Yudie broke through to the Life and Death Realm and passed through the calamity, Mister Zhuo would no longer have a chance. Therefore, after scheming for a long time, Mister Zhuo finally chose to make his move.
Ning Yudie's Ice Palace was filled with slender ice spikes. The entire Ice Palace looked like a huge hedgehog!
And within the Ice Palace, there were dozens of martial artists resisting with all their strength!
Not all martial artists stood on Mister Zhuo's side. Currently, the entire Cloud Hall was divided into three factions. The first faction was led by Mister Zhuo. They were striving to seize the position of Hall Master. If they didn't succeed, they would die trying!
Once Ning Yudie broke through to the Life and Death Realm, the third rank families that supported Mister Zhuo would be implicated. They might even be wiped out by Ning Yudie!
The second faction was the neutral faction led by Fei Han. These elders of the Cloud Hall weren't willing to overthrow Ning Yudie, but as members of the third rank families of Cloud Sea City, they could only be said to be in a dilemma. They simply didn't help either side. Even so, Fei Han and the others were under great pressure. Take Fei Han as an example. This time, the Fei Family of Cloud Sea City fully supported Mister Zhuo. As a member of the Fei Family, Fei Han refused to help. Naturally, he was under great pressure.
As for the third faction, it was those who stood on Ning Yudie's side.
Of course, the third faction was led by Daoist Dameng. Many disciples of the younger generation were part of this faction as well. After all, these disciples of the younger generation didn't have much of a relationship with the families of Cloud Sea City. On the contrary, after joining the Cloud Hall, they had always regarded Ning Yudie as the core of the Cloud Hall.
For the past few days, Mister Zhuo had led many martial artists to attack Ning Yudie's Ice Palace. However, wave after wave of attacks were resisted by Daoist Dameng and his men.
However, the strength of the martial artists on Daoist Dameng's side wasn't very strong. After all, they didn't have the advantage in numbers. Furthermore, the younger generation of martial artists hadn't fully matured.
