Apart from Daoist Dameng and a few other Void Tribulation Realm elders, most of the other martial artists were only at the Divine Core realm, and some were even at the Divine Reflection realm.
It was a miracle that they were able to resist the attacks for so many days, and the person who created this miracle was none other than Hua Tianming!
Right at this moment, Hua Tianming was sitting on the square in front of the Ice Palace. He was holding a dark green longsword in his hand, and blood was flowing out from both of his eyes. The bloodstain was about 6 meters long on the square.
Pei Tianyao, Red Maple, and a few other men were standing guard beside Hua Tianming. As for Daoist Dameng and the other martial artists, they had completely surrounded them!
During the past two years, Hua Tianming, Pei Tianyao, Red Maple, and a few other men had made great progress in their cultivation. All three of them had broken through to the Divine Core realm, and Hua Tianming had broken through to the Mid Divine Core realm.
Originally, a Mid Divine Core warrior alone wouldn't be able to change the situation.
After all, no matter how talented Hua Tianming was, it would still be difficult for him to fight a False Tribulation warrior. However, Hua Tianming had unintentionally comprehended a higher level of the Heavenly Sword!
He had comprehended the 'Sword Eye' of the Heavenly Sword, which allowed him to absorb the Force of the Heavens into his sword, allowing him to store an unlimited amount of sword intent!
As long as he was given enough time, he could even kill a Life, Death, or Divine Sea warrior with just a single thrust!
Of course, this was only in theory. Although the Force of the Heavens was powerful, Hua Tianming had a limit to how much he could absorb. If he kept using the Force of the Heavens for a long time, it would cause great damage to Hua Tianming's body. Therefore, blood was flowing out from both of his eyes!
But, because Hua Tianming was guarding the entrance of the Ice Palace, no one dared to attack him!
Hua Tianming could only unleash a single thrust with his sword, and anyone who dared to attack him would die. But, as long as anyone could withstand this attack, Hua Tianming would no longer be a threat.
But, who would be willing to withstand this attack?
The last time Hua Tianming attacked with his sword was three days ago, and he had killed the Fei family's Patriarch, Fei Longyang!
Fei Longyang was a late-stage False Tribulation Realm cultivator who cultivated the Laws of Earth, so he was probably the cultivator who was most skilled in defense in the city. Especially his Earthen Dullness technique, it even utilized the Laws of Earth to its limits. It was capable of spreading the attacks he suffered to the ground, allowing the ground to bear the brunt of the damage.
When Fei Longyang unleashed the Earth Art of Foolishness, even Daoist Dameng's attack couldn't withstand it!
It was at that exact moment that Hua Tianming unleashed the Heavens Shaking Sword.
No one had expected this disciple of the Cloud Hall, who was only a Divine Core warrior, to be able to unleash such a powerful attack!
The speed of the sword was incredibly fast, and it carried a tremendous force. Although Fei Longyang was crazily unleashing the Earth Art of Foolishness and spreading the force of the sword across the ground, with Fei Longyang as the center, the ground within a 30 meter radius was instantly turned into dust, and it still couldn't withstand Hua Tianming's sword attack.
In the end, the sword attack still pierced through Fei Longyang's defense, killing him with a single strike!
Even Fei Longyang, who was a Late Void Tribulation warrior, was killed by Hua Tianming with a single strike. So, which Void Tribulation warrior still dared to step forward? Whoever stepped forward would die!
Therefore, Mister Zhuo had no choice but to launch some meaningless attacks at the Ice Palace from afar.
"This can't go on like this!" Originally, Mister Zhuo wanted to take down Ning Yudie in a short period of time, but he didn't expect it to drag on for so long. If Ning Yudie really broke through to the Life and Death realm, all his efforts would be in vain.
"You, go up there!" Mister Zhuo pointed at a Void Tribulation warrior standing next to him. The Void Tribulation warrior was from the Shen family.
As long as they could force Hua Tianming to attack with his sword, they would be able to swarm him. In other words, they were looking for a scapegoat!
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 692
When the Void Tribulation warrior from the Shen family heard this, his expression immediately changed.
Hua Tianming's sword was incredibly sharp, anyone who went up against him would die. Wasn't asking him to go up there seeking his own death?
Which Void Tribulation warrior didn't cherish their own lives?
The reason why these few big families had joined hands was to push Mister Zhuo to the position of Palace Master, but at the same time, these big families had their own motives. If they pushed Mister Zhuo to the position of Palace Master, these big families would also benefit from it.
But, it wasn't worth it for others to pay with their own lives.
"I'm not going, if you want to go, then you go!" The Void Tribulation warrior from the Shen family shook his head.
Mister Zhuo gritted his teeth and said with a cold voice, "If all of you are afraid of death, how are we going to break into the Ice Palace?!"
All the Void Tribulation warriors looked at each other, then one of them said in a low voice. "If you're not afraid of death, then you go …"
"That's right, Mister Zhuo, you can go! You'll be the first to go, and we'll follow behind you! "
"As long as you take the lead, there's no reason for us not to go!"
Mister Zhuo became even angrier when he heard their words. I was the first to rush up and get killed by that brat Hua Tianming. I'm afraid the Zhuo Clan won't get the position of Hall Master. You Shen Clan, Fei Clan, and Wu Clan are trying to take advantage of me?
"Dad, I'll go!"
The man who spoke was Mister Zhuo's eldest son, Zhuo Feiling, who was also Zhuo Bufan's older brother.
"You?" Mister Zhuo was startled, then he shook his head and said, "It's useless!"
Zhuo Feiling was just a Divine Core warrior, and even if he went up, Hua Tianming wouldn't do anything. With Zhuo Feiling's strength, he would be instantly killed by Daoist Dameng and the others.
"If none of you go up, then what do you think will happen when that bitch Ning Yudie breaks through to the Life and Death realm?" Mister Zhuo said with a cold voice.
His words caused everyone to fall silent. Since ancient times, those who failed in a rebellion always had a miserable ending. Not only would they suffer, all the big families would be severely punished as well. Although the Cloud Hall couldn't remove the names of these big families, these big families had taken root in the Cloud Hall, and once they were punished by the Cloud Hall, it would have a great impact on their families.
"Then … should we attack together?" Finally, one of them revealed a ruthless expression. This time, they had indeed thrown caution to the wind. There was no way out. It was only because no one was willing to be the first scapegoat that they had been stuck here for so long.
Mister Zhuo nodded. "Alright, we've been delayed for so long. Let's attack together. We'll definitely be able to take down the Ice Palace and kill that little b * tch, Ning Yudie!"
He had been planning this for a long time, and he had waited so long for this opportunity. Of course, he had to grasp it firmly in his hands!
"The Cloud Hall belongs to the clans of our city, not Ning Yudie!"
"Victory or defeat depends on this!"
"No, we should win! But Daoist Master Big Dream alone can't stop us! "
As long as all of them attacked at the same time, Hua Tianming would only be able to kill one of them. After Daoist Dameng was killed, everything would be settled. The remaining Void Tribulation Sect Elders were nothing to be afraid of. As for those Divine Core juniors, although they weren't afraid of death, the difference in their cultivation levels was too great.
"Swoosh, swoosh, swoosh …"
After finishing their discussion, Mister Zhuo and the other men finally took the first step and charged towards the Ice Palace.
On the platform in front of the Ice Palace, Hua Tianming's eyes were still closed. Blood kept flowing out from his eyes, and he looked horrified.
"They're coming up!" With a gloomy expression, Pei Tianyao reminded Hua Tianming.
"I know." Hua Tianming took out the longsword from his chest and gripped the hilt. Although his Heavenly Sword was powerful, he could only unleash a single strike. After unleashing that strike, he would need to charge up again. Obviously, Mister Zhuo and the other men wouldn't give him the chance to charge up again.
Therefore, Hua Tianming was hesitant about who to kill with this strike. How to unleash the full power of this strike was a huge challenge for Hua Tianming.
Daoist Dameng and the other Sect Elders were standing on the side with serious expressions on their faces. They knew that if Mister Zhuo really led all of his men and charged towards the Ice Palace, their chances of winning would be very small.
However, the people standing here were all people who had pledged their lives to follow Ning Yudie!
Since they had chosen to stand here, they were prepared to give up their lives!
"Draw your weapons!"
"Zeng, zeng, zeng!"
On the platform, everyone had already made their preparations to welcome the slaughter below.
The air was filled with killing intent, and the distance between the two sides was constantly closing. At this moment, Hua Tianming suddenly moved.
It looked like Hua Tianming was sitting on the ground, but in reality, he was two inches off the ground. At this moment, Hua Tianming was sliding along the ground, constantly adjusting his position.
Seeing Hua Tianming move, Mister Zhuo and the other False Tribulation Realm experts' expressions changed. With Hua Tianming's strike, someone would definitely die! As for who would die, it would depend on who Hua Tianming chose to attack!
"Charge, stop him from thrusting his sword!" someone shouted.
All of the False Tribulation Realm experts charged towards Hua Tianming with extreme speed. At this moment, Daoist Dameng and the other Sect Elders also stood in front!
After Hua Tianming adjusted his angle, his eyes, which were constantly bleeding, suddenly opened!
In his pitch-black eyes, there were two faint sword shadows. These sword shadows were brewing in his eyes, causing his gaze to be unusually sharp. If anyone met his gaze, they would feel an unbearable pain, as if their eyes were being pierced by needles!
After opening his eyes, Hua Tianming suddenly attacked!
"Scatter!"
When Mister Zhuo saw Hua Tianming facing him, he was immediately scared out of his wits. He had planned for a long time and was about to obtain the Palace Master's position. If he were to be assassinated by Hua Tianming at this moment, he would truly be wronged. Even if he were to become a ghost, he wouldn't be willing!
Just as Mister Zhuo was desperately dodging, Hua Tianming's sword had already pierced out!
Suddenly, a strange aura spread out from Hua Tianming's eyes. This aura was majestic and spirited. Anyone who stood in front of him would feel insignificant!
This kind of feeling was produced when a Sect Elder was alone and looked up at the heavens!
Hua Tianming's accumulated aura of the heavens erupted at this moment. Everyone suddenly felt as if the heavens and earth had been turned upside down. At this moment, Hua Tianming represented the heavens and everything!
Although everyone knew that this was only an illusion caused by Hua Tianming's attack, their minds were still affected by Hua Tianming's aura.
"Whoosh!"
An extremely sharp sword light pierced through the air!
This sword ray was extremely fast, and there was no time to dodge. Everyone only felt a flash of light in front of their eyes, and then three sounds rang in their ears.
"Pu pu pu!"
These were the sounds of bodies being pierced by the sword ray!
It was as if time had frozen at this moment. No matter if it was Daoist Dameng or Mister Zhuo, they were all frozen on the spot. All the other Sect Elders were frozen on the spot as well.
After a short while, the bodies of the three Void Tribulation Realm warriors swayed. Their eyes were filled with unwillingness and anger.
"Why, why is it me …"
Plop, plop, plop …
A single thrust of the sword had killed three Void Tribulation Realm warriors! Hua Tianming was only a Mid Divine Core warrior. One had to admit that the Heavenly Sword cultivated by Hua Tianming was extremely heaven defying! Even Luo Zheng, who had just broken through to the War Reverend realm, was unable to do this!
"Fei, Fei Cheng!"
"Wu Xiaoran!"
A few angry roars sounded out from Mister Zhuo's group.
Looking at this scene, Mister Zhuo let out a long sigh of relief. Luckily, Hua Tianming's attack wasn't aimed at him, or else he would be a corpse lying on the ground right now …
"Charge! Kill that guy! "Mister Zhuo gritted his teeth and shouted out.
All the remaining Void Tribulation Realm warriors were enraged. They were all warriors from third rank families, and the man who was killed was their brother. How could they not be angry at this moment?
Most importantly, after Hua Tianming unleashed the attack, he needed a long time to gather his strength. After unleashing the attack, he would only be an ordinary Divine Core warrior, and he posed no threat to a Void Tribulation warrior.

Chapter 693
Hua Tianming's attack just now was meant to kill Mister Zhuo!
Mister Zhuo was their leader, and it was because of his ambition that all third ranked families in Cloud Sea City had gathered together.
However, right before he attacked, Hua Tianming changed his mind.
Even if he killed Mister Zhuo, it wouldn't change the entire situation. If he lost this battle, even if he went to Mister Zhuo, there would be someone else who would take his place!
That was why Hua Tianming chose a tricky angle to kill the three Void Tribulation warriors. But even so, the effect wasn't great.
Daoist Dameng and Mister Zhuo's strength were on par with each other, and they quickly engaged in a fierce battle. However, the number of Void Tribulation warriors on their side was still much greater than Hua Tianming's.
As for Pei Tianyao, Red Maple, and the other Divine Core warriors, they were no match for the Void Tribulation warriors. In just the blink of an eye, they were defeated.
Swoosh!
A Void Tribulation warrior finally broke through the line of defense. He was holding a green blade in his hand as he charged towards Hua Tianming.
"You killed my little brother, so you can die as well!"
After saying that, the Void Tribulation warrior swung his blade at Hua Tianming's head with an extremely powerful aura.
Hua Tianming lightly drew a line on the ground, then he struck out with his sword once again.
With a "ding" sound, this sword was easily blocked by the other party.
Hua Tianming's strength came from the Sword Eye of the Heavenly Sword. Without the Sword Eye, he was no match for a Void Tribulation warrior.
Just as his sword was blocked by his opponent, the opponent turned around and punched Hua Tianming right in the chest.
"Boom!"
The Void Tribulation warrior's punch landed on Hua Tianming's chest, causing it to cave in. He was thrown backwards, and only stopped after rolling a few dozen times on the ground. Hua Tianming laid on the ground, gasping for breath. His eyes were filled with stubbornness.
Choosing this path was the result of a discussion between him, Pei Tianyao, and Red Maple.
The main reason why he chose to support Ning Yudie was because of Luo Zheng.
They knew about the relationship between Luo Zheng and Ning Yudie. At first, the three of them were hesitant, but even the timid Red Maple stood up. Hua Tianming and Pei Tianyao had no reason not to do so!
As for whether their choice was right or wrong, Hua Tianming did not know. Perhaps, as a martial artist, one did not necessarily have to weigh everything in terms of benefits. As the saying goes, there are some things to do and some not to do. This was the logic behind it.
"A young Divine Core warrior is actually able to kill a False Tribulation warrior? What a pity, I wonder how far you'll grow in the future!" The False Tribulation warrior slowly walked towards Hua Tianming, the blade in his hand was flickering with a cold light.
At this moment, an agile figure rushed out. That figure was so fast that it was dazzling.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh!"
Because it was moving so fast, it created countless clones that surrounded the False Tribulation warrior. This man was Red Maple!
A strange movement skill like this could be considered outstanding amongst all Divine Core warriors, but for a False Tribulation warrior, it was just a piece of cake. Just as Red Maple was moving around the False Tribulation warrior, the False Tribulation warrior suddenly punched out.
"Bang!"
The punch accurately located Red Maple among all the clones!
With just a single punch, Red Maple was sent flying away.
A faint smile emerged on the False Tribulation warrior's face. "A strange movement skill indeed. Even when I was a Divine Core warrior, I couldn't have such a strange movement skill. Too bad, your cultivation base is too weak!"
Right after he finished speaking, a huge palm suddenly appeared above his head. The palm was so huge that it looked like a real palm, and it forcefully slapped downward.
"Hmph!"
The False Tribulation warrior didn't even raise his head. He simply slashed upward with his blade. A massive blade beam formed from True Yuan instantly shattered the palm!
Pei Tianyao's attack couldn't stop the False Tribulation warrior from moving forward.
Soon, the False Tribulation warrior arrived in front of Hua Tianming. He stepped on Hua Tianming's hand, and placed his blade on Hua Tianming's neck. "Too bad, if you didn't choose to stand on Ning Yudie's side, you would have become a powerful warrior of the Cloud Hall in the future! Since you've chosen to die, don't blame me for granting you death! "
The current Hua Tianming could be considered as a Divine Realm genius. Killing Hua Tianming would be a great loss to the Cloud Hall. After all, they were only here to obtain the position of Palace Master, not to destroy the Cloud Hall!
However, since Hua Tianming had chosen to stand on Ning Yudie's side, they couldn't be blamed for this.
A determined expression emerged on Hua Tianming's face. However, he had a resolute heart. Since he had taken this step, he had already mentally prepared himself for failure.
Looking at the rapidly descending blade, Hua Tianming closed his eyes.
But after a short moment, the blade still didn't descend …
Hua Tianming furrowed his brows. 'Is this guy going to mock me before killing me?'
Just as Hua Tianming opened his eyes, he saw a hand grabbing the edge of his saber. The saber was only a few inches away from him, but it couldn't move any further.
"Who is it? Who can grab the blade of a False Tribulation Realm expert with their bare hands? "
Hua Tianming turned his head to look. His eyes suddenly emitted a bright light, and a faint smile appeared on his face. He had imagined that Luo Zheng would come back, but he didn't expect Luo Zheng to appear at this juncture. Hua Tianming smiled faintly and said, "You're back."
Luo Zheng nodded. The hand that grabbed the blade slightly exerted force, and the high-grade immortal tool bent in Luo Zheng's hand. The tough blade was as fragile as a piece of paper. "Yes, I'm back!"
Everyone was still immersed in the battle, so they didn't notice the movement here. Only Red Maple's face revealed an ecstatic smile. Pei Tianyao let out a long breath and said faintly in his heart, 'Hua Tianming, we made the right choice!'
As for the False Tribulation Realm expert in front of him, he was completely stunned. He stared blankly at the bent blade in his hand, and then at Luo Zheng, as if he couldn't react.
"I'm Luo Zheng, I'm back!" This time, Luo Zheng suddenly roared at everyone. With the infusion of True Essence, Luo Zheng's voice was immediately heard clearly by everyone.
When the martial artists who were still fighting heard this fierce roar, they stopped one after another.
Even Daoist Dameng and Mister Zhuo pushed each other away. Their eyes gathered on Luo Zheng's face.
Of course, the two's expressions were completely different. When Daoist Dameng saw Luo Zheng, a smile suddenly appeared on his face. At the same time, he said, "Luo Zheng! You … are already in the False Tribulation Realm! "
Mister Zhuo's face was unsightly at this moment.
Just now, Hua Tianming made a move and killed three False Tribulation Realm experts with one sword. However, he himself was safe and sound. He knew that the position of Cloud Hall's Hall Master was already his! As long as he entangled Daoist Dameng and waited for the others to clean up these experts, he could break into the Ice Palace and kill Ning Yudie!
Everything was going according to plan. In a few days, the entire Cloud Hall would be his! With the protection of the Minor Heavenly Cycle Triple Kill Formation, Mister Zhuo wasn't afraid even in the face of the Lascivious Sect. After becoming the Cloud Hall's Hall Master, he used the Cloud Hall's resources to make a breakthrough. His future prospects were almost limitless!
But at this time, that damned Luo Zheng actually came back at this critical moment?
Wasn't he dead?
Although most people in the Cloud Hall believed that Luo Zheng wasn't dead, Mister Zhuo stubbornly believed that Luo Zheng was dead without a doubt. There was absolutely no possibility of him coming back.
Now that he saw Luo Zheng standing not too far away and had even broken through to the False Tribulation Realm, how could Mister Zhuo not be shocked?
The expressions of many third-rank aristocratic families' experts were also uncertain. This Luo Zheng suddenly came back to kill them. How should they deal with him?
"Hmph, Luo Zheng! So what if you're back? You're only in the early stage of the False Tribulation Realm. Do you think you can turn the tide by yourself? What a joke! Attack, kill him! "Mister Zhuo ordered.
"That's right, it's just another False Tribulation Realm warrior! They're no match for us! "
"Let's join forces and kill him first!"
Mister Zhuo's words were very useful. There were too few warriors standing on Ning Yudie's side, and most of them were just Divine Core juniors. Now that Luo Zheng was here, they couldn't turn the situation around.

Chapter 694
Four Void Tribulation Realm warriors were attacking Luo Zheng at the same time, and two of them were Mid Void Tribulation Realm warriors. This was enough to show how much importance they attached to Luo Zheng.
When Luo Zheng was still at the Divine Reflection realm, he was able to suppress those Divine Core geniuses. Now that he had broken through to the Void Tribulation realm, no one knew how strong he had become.
However, in their minds, no matter how strong Luo Zheng was, with four Void Tribulation Realm warriors attacking him at the same time, he wouldn't be able to fight back.
Daoist Great Dream's expression changed. He lifted his leg and his figure became erratic. Just as he was about to use the Phantom God Steps to help Luo Zheng, Mister Zhuo stabbed out with his spear. It was this spear that forced Daoist Great Dream back!
Everyone's eyes were focused on Luo Zheng. Hua Tianming, Pei Tianyao, and the others had worried expressions on their faces. Even if Luo Zheng's cultivation had skyrocketed in the past two years, there was no way he could fight back against four Void Tribulation Realm warriors by himself.
Facing the four Void Tribulation Realm warriors, Luo Zheng's eyes flickered. His body was like a fallen leaf floating on water, constantly stretching and drifting in the air!
"Is this an illusion?"
"Or is this some kind of special movement skill?"
Various thoughts flashed through the minds of the four Void Tribulation Realm warriors.
But since they had already attacked, and Luo Zheng wasn't dodging, how could they let go of this opportunity?
For a moment, all sorts of attacks were thrown at Luo Zheng!
However, something strange happened. It was as if none of the attacks could touch Luo Zheng!
It looked like the weapons in their hands were either slashing or stabbing at Luo Zheng's body, but it was as if Luo Zheng's body didn't exist. It was as if their weapons were slashing at the air.
Although Daoist Dameng was being held back by Mister Zhuo, when he glanced around, a shocked expression emerged on his face.
"This is the Law of Space! Daoist Master Dameng couldn't help but exclaim in surprise. Where did Luo Zheng go these past two years?
Although Mister Zhuo was entangled with Daoist Master Dameng, his mind was also on Luo Zheng. Seeing Luo Zheng's inconceivably strange movement technique, how intense was the shock in his eyes?
"This kid …" Mister Zhuo suddenly had a bad premonition. He seemed to have underestimated this kid.
From the first time he saw Luo Zheng, Mr. Zhuo realized that he had underestimated Luo Zheng!
At that time, Mr. Zhuo had thought that Luo Zheng was just a little guy who had climbed up from the Blue Cloud Sect. At the entrance of the Road of Trials, he had wanted to take action against Luo Zheng.
Who would have thought that Ning Yudie would directly slap him in the face and tell him to get lost!
At that time, Mr. Zhuo had already wanted to kill Luo Zheng. Then, Luo Zheng had risen like a comet in the Central Domain. This kid's strength had grown at a speed ten times faster than he had imagined! When Luo Zheng had been in the Divine Reflection Realm, he had already made Mr. Zhuo feel a sense of crisis.
He hadn't expected that after disappearing for two years, he would return as a False Tribulation Realm martial artist. Plus, with this strength … Could Mr. Zhuo defeat Luo Zheng?
Mr. Zhuo didn't have an answer in his heart. However, after a breath, the answer floated clearly in his mind!
Bang, bang, bang, bang!
In an instant, Luo Zheng threw out four punches, sending the four False Tribulation Realm martial artists flying!
These False Tribulation Realm martial artists didn't have the strength to fight back in front of Luo Zheng. After they fell heavily to the ground, they either sat or lay down. Their faces were as white as paper. Clearly, their injuries weren't light!
When Luo Zheng was still a general, he was already able to fight against the peak war elder in the Sin Tower. Furthermore, Luo Zheng had already stepped into the Nihility Tribulation Realm, and was also a war elder expert. It was not too difficult for him to deal with these early and middle stage Nihility Tribulation Realm warriors.
However, what Luo Zheng thought was normal was abnormal in the eyes of others!
Everyone on the platform was stunned. They felt that they were not facing a False Tribulation Realm martial artist, but a Life and Death Realm peerless powerhouse! Only a Life and Death Realm martial artist could easily crush a False Tribulation Realm martial artist.
Hua Tianming said with a faint smile on his face. "Luo Zheng's strength is still normal …"
In the beginning, when Luo Zheng was only a Xiantian warrior, he was able to fight with Divine Reflection Realm martial artists. But, after breaking through to the Divine Reflection Realm, he was able to suppress Divine Core warriors. In fact, the higher one's cultivation level was, the harder it was to maintain this advantage. After all, all the martial artists who could cultivate to the False Tribulation Realm were geniuses amongst geniuses.
This was also the reason why Hua Tianming, Pei Tianyao, and Daoist Dameng were so worried.
But now, they understood that Luo Zheng was still able to maintain this huge advantage. According to their calculations, Luo Zheng did indeed have the strength to fight with Life and Death Realm martial artists!
Life and Death Realm martial artists … they were the strongest existences in this continent. Even Ning Yudie was still trying to break through to the Life and Death Realm. No one expected that after missing for two years, Luo Zheng would possess such frightening strength.
Everyone sighed in their minds.
As for Mister Zhuo, the answer in his mind had finally become clear. With Luo Zheng's current strength, he could easily crush him!
His heart sank, and the hearts of all the martial artists who came with him sank as well.
Luo Zheng looked at Mister Zhuo and said, "Give up resisting, and you'll be given a lighter punishment. If you resist again, you'll be killed without mercy!"
Many False Tribulation Realm martial artists were hesitating. Just now, Luo Zheng had casually shown his strength, and it had planted a seed of fear in their hearts. Many of them were slightly shaken.
"Even if you give up resisting, he won't let you go!" Mister Zhuo's words made all of them tighten their grip on their weapons. They were plotting a rebellion, and any rebellion in a sect was punishable by death! It was the same as a rebellion in a mortal's Imperial Palace. You've already decided to overthrow someone, and you're expecting them to let you go?
Hearing Mister Zhuo's words, Luo Zheng sneered. He took a step forward, crossing layers of space and heading straight for Mister Zhuo. This person was the source of the chaos!
The current situation was different from before. Just now, Hua Tianming didn't kill Mister Zhuo because even if he did, he wouldn't be able to change the situation. But now, Luo Zheng could completely suppress the current situation, so as long as Mister Zhuo was killed, it would be difficult for the others to resist!
Seeing Luo Zheng chasing after him, Mister Zhuo shot backwards!
"Escape? Let's see where you can escape to! "
The Abundant Light enveloped the entire Cloud Hall, so it was impossible for Mister Zhuo to escape from the barrier. Mister Zhuo had just entered the late stage of the False Tribulation Realm, and was only equivalent to a War Reverend of the eighth or ninth stage! How could he be Luo Zheng's opponent!
Being chased by Luo Zheng, Mister Zhuo's face was full of regret. He thought that he had calculated everything, but he had missed this guy.
As a member of the Cloud Hall, how could Mister Zhuo not know how terrifying Luo Zheng was?
As long as this guy didn't fall, in the future, who in the Central Domain could be his opponent?
But Mister Zhuo wasn't an immortal. It was impossible for him to calculate that Luo Zheng would return, and at this critical juncture!
"Luo Zheng, let me go! I will change my name and never set foot in the Central Domain again! "Mister Zhuo fled while begging Luo Zheng behind him.
"You wish. Actually, I've wanted to kill you for a long time, but as an elder of the Cloud Hall, I can't do it. Today, you pushed yourself into a desperate situation. What's the use of begging for mercy now?" Luo Zheng said coldly. His figure constantly shuttled through space. Every time he shuttled through space, he got closer to Mister Zhuo!
"If you kill me, my Zhuo Family and the Cloud Hall will be irreconcilable! All the aristocratic families in the Cloud Sea City will become enemies with the Cloud Hall! "Mister Zhuo threatened again.
"You're just a small third-rank aristocratic family. How could the other aristocratic families in the Cloud Sea City be like your Zhuo Family? Dream on! "Luo Zheng retorted. At this moment, he was only three meters away from Mister Zhuo.
"Die!" Seeing that he couldn't escape, Mister Zhuo suddenly turned around and pulled out a black spear. He turned around and stabbed it at Luo Zheng. "Sun Extinguishing Spear!"
When this spear stabbed out, the spear tip emitted a light that seemed to be able to steal the radiance of the sun and moon. Its might was extremely shocking. As one of the strongest elders of the Cloud Hall, Mister Zhuo's strength was unquestionable.
Facing Mister Zhuo's full strength spear, Luo Zheng didn't dodge. He sneered and directly grabbed the spear tip with his palm. He pushed hard. This high-grade immortal artifact instantly bent under Luo Zheng's push, and at the same time, it started to break into pieces.
Seeing this scene, Mister Zhuo's heart sank into the abyss. The difference in strength between him and Luo Zheng was even greater than he had imagined.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 695
With his weapon broken by Luo Zheng, Mister Zhuo's eyes flashed with a hint of pleading. He immediately said, "Luo Zheng, I am an elder of the Cloud Hall. Even if it is a rebellion, Ning Yudie will be the one to deal with it! I … surrender! You can't kill me! "
Hearing Mister Zhuo's words, Luo Zheng's face showed a trace of hesitation.
Strictly speaking, this was true. After all, Mister Zhuo was one of the pillars of the Cloud Hall. His status and identity were higher than Luo Zheng's. In fact, Luo Zheng was still a disciple of the Cloud Hall and had not been promoted to a position like a deacon.
Mister Zhuo also understood that as long as he was not killed by Luo Zheng, he could let the entire Zhuo family influence Ning Yudie and save his life. As long as he did not die, there was still a chance for him to rise again. After all, the lifespan of a martial artist was too long. Who knew what the situation would be like in a few hundred years?
Many people of the Cloud Hall were watching this scene from various windows and corners of the Cloud Hall.
In this rebellion, most of the disciples of the Cloud Hall chose to be neutral. After all, this rebellion involved the internal strife between the higher-ups and had little to do with the lower-level martial artists of the Cloud Hall.
"Oh my god, Luo Zheng is back, and he is chasing Mister Zhuo with his bare hands!"
"This kid is really a monster. He disappeared for two years and just came back, and he can actually fight with Mister Zhuo!"
"Are you blind? Mister Zhuo has no power to fight back. This is not a fight, this is a crushing, understand? "
For most of the people of the Cloud Hall, the ownership of the Cloud Hall was not important. So at this time, they were watching this with the mentality of watching a show.
Of course, some of the children of aristocratic families were not in a good mood, especially some of the children of the Zhuo family. When they saw Great Master Zhuo being beaten back by Luo Zheng, their faces were as ugly as they could be. However, their cultivation was too low, and the difference in strength was too great, so they could not participate in this.
Zhuo Bufan, who was in the Elite Hall, watched in a daze as his father was chased by Luo Zheng and even begged for mercy. His face was extremely gloomy!
The way he looked at Luo Zheng was also extremely complicated!
How arrogant was Zhuo Bufan when he was on the path of the trial-takers?
At that time, who else could Zhuo Bufan be but me? Among the younger generation of disciples, who could rival him? At that time, Zhuo Bufan did not take Luo Zheng seriously at all!
But two years had passed. In just two years, Luo Zheng had grown to a level that even his father found difficult to contend with, while he himself was still lingering in the Divine Reflection Realm. Even Hua Tianming and the others were far inferior to him...
How could Zhuo Bufan not hate him?
In fact, Zhuo Bufan had a Heaven-grade talent, and his cultivation speed should not have been so slow. However, at that time, one of his hands was crippled by Luo Zheng in the Cloud Hall, and his martial arts heart was greatly damaged. Under the double psychological and physical blow, Zhuo Bufan was depressed for a long time!
At this time, Zhuo Bufan wanted to rush up and stand in front of his father. Unexpectedly, a member of the Zhuo family next to him held him down from behind and shook his head at Zhuo Bufan, indicating that he should not act rashly. Luo Zheng's strength had already exceeded their imagination. Zhuo Bufan would only be asking for humiliation if he went up.
In mid-air, Luo Zheng thought for a moment. The hesitation in his eyes disappeared in a flash, and then a murderous look appeared on his face. "You're right. I can't kill you, but I can kill you on behalf of Ning Yudie!"
After that, Luo Zheng sneered and rushed toward Mister Zhuo again.
Luo Zheng knew very well that raising a tiger would only invite calamity. Mister Zhuo was very cunning. He had been the one who had targeted him several times in the Cloud Hall.
What's more, this time, his goal was to kill Ning Yudie!
There was no problem in seizing the position of the Cloud Hall. With Luo Zheng's current vision, a mere Cloud Hall was not very attractive to him. However, if this person wanted to kill Ning Yudie, he was courting death!
There were not many women in the world who were worthy of Luo Zheng's attention, but Ning Yudie was one of them. If anyone touched her, it would be tantamount to touching Luo Zheng's reverse scale.
When Cui Xie captured Luo Yan, Luo Zheng was no match for him, but he did not show any mercy. Moreover, Mister Zhuo was far inferior to Cui Xie.
Seeing Luo Zheng rushing toward him, Mister Zhuo's heart sank. He said with grief and indignation, "Luo Zheng, you're ruthless!"
"You're right. Since you dared to have designs on Ning Yudie, you should be prepared to die!" Luo Zheng had already taken out the long sword in his hand.
Seeing the sword light shining from the treasured sword, Mister Zhuo's face was full of despair. "Who is Ning Yudie to you? Is she worthy of your protection? Whatever she can give you, I can give you too! "
"Whoosh!"
Luo Zheng slashed out with his sword. His sword intent spread out in the shape of a crescent and covered Mister Zhuo. Then Luo Zheng said with a faint smile, "Sorry, I'm not interested!"
Mister Zhuo took out another spare long spear from his sumeru ring and blocked Luo Zheng's sword intent. Realizing that he could not persuade Luo Zheng, Mister Zhuo's face was full of madness. He roared, "Luo Zheng, you're nothing but Ning Yudie's exclusive property. The Cloud Hall is unfair, Ning Yudie is unfair!"
Knowing that he could not escape death, Mister Zhuo could only roar unwillingly. Now, he was doing his best to smear Luo Zheng and Ning Yudie's reputation.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng's face showed surprise. At the same time, he said with a faint smile, "It seems that you were the one who spread the rumors in the Cloud Hall early on?"
When Luo Zheng first entered the Cloud Hall, because of Ning Yudie's special care, rumors slowly spread among the disciples, deacons, and elders. Some Cloud Hall disciples made groundless accusations that Luo Zheng was Ning Yudie's exclusive property and that Ning Yudie had taken a fancy to him.
"Yes, it's me. Am I wrong? You're a pair of adulterers! "Mister Zhuo brandished his long spear and counterattacked Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng raised his hand and easily blocked Mister Zhuo's spear. Then, he slashed out three times in a row. His long sword was a high-grade sacred tool. How could Mister Zhuo's high-grade immortal tool block it? In an instant, Luo Zheng cut his sword into several pieces.
"You're wrong." Luo Zheng shook his head.
Mister Zhuo threw away the broken long spear and took out another long spear. He said coldly, "You dare to do it, but you don't dare to admit it. Luo Zheng, you're just so-so!"
This was Mister Zhuo's last resort. He couldn't defeat Luo Zheng in a direct confrontation, so he could only use such words to force Luo Zheng to distract him. Maybe he could give himself a chance to defeat Luo Zheng!
But how strong was Luo Zheng's state of mind?
He casually stabbed out with his sword. Mister Zhuo couldn't see through this move, "No Style", let alone block it. The long sword hit Mister Zhuo's chest and penetrated his heart. "I'm not Ning Yudie's exclusive property. I should put it another way. Ning Yudie is my woman."
"Pu!"
Luo Zheng pulled out the long sword, and blood spurted out from Mister Zhuo's chest. Then his body fell down like a kite with a broken string.
A heart-wrenching cry came from below. "Father!" That person was Zhuo Bufan, who was watching the battle below.
Luo Zheng glanced at Zhuo Bufan indifferently, and then flew toward the Ice Palace, leaving the people in the Cloud Hall still in shock.
They were not shocked because Luo Zheng had killed Zhuo Bufan. They were shocked because of Luo Zheng's last words. Ning Yudie was his woman?
Not to mention whether this was true or not, he dared to say this in front of so many people. This was …
Everyone could no longer describe their feelings at this time. Ning Yudie's reputation was not only in the Cloud Hall, but also in the entire Central Domain. First of all, she had a peerless appearance, unparalleled talent and status. She was an absolutely perfect woman. In their hearts, she would always be the high and mighty Hall Master, but she had actually become Luo Zheng's woman?
This sounded unbelievable to them.
Right at this moment, Luo Zheng heard Xun's voice. "Hey, since when did Ning Yudie become your woman?"
Luo Zheng replied with a faint smile, "Who doesn't know how to boast?"
The Ice Palace was located at the highest point in the Cloud Hall. Although many Divine Core Sect Elders didn't leave the platform, they could see the result of the battle. Zhuo Bufan had been killed by Luo Zheng. As for the other Sect Elders who participated in the rebellion, they had completely given up on resisting.

Chapter 696
Luo Zheng didn't care about these things. Pei Tianyao and Red Maple were recuperating, so Luo Zheng didn't want to disturb them. He walked towards Daoist Dameng and asked, "How is Palace Master?"
Daoist Dameng nodded at Luo Zheng. He knew why Luo Zheng was worried, so he said, "Palace Master should be fine."
"If she's fine, why did these guys dare to act rashly?" Luo Zheng asked with a frown.
If Ning Yudie was fine, even if Mister Zhuo was given a hundred guts, he wouldn't dare to rebel. Something must have happened to Ning Yudie, and this was what worried Luo Zheng the most.
Seeing Luo Zheng's expression, Daoist Master Dameng Dream realized that Luo Zheng had misunderstood. Thus, he hurriedly explained, "The Hall Master is undergoing a life and death tribulation. That's why these guys are taking advantage of him!"
"Life and death tribulation!" Luo Zheng was shocked, then asked, "What about Grandma Yu?"
Even if Ning Yudie was trying to break through to Life and Death Realm, with Grandma Yu overseeing the Cloud Hall, how could Mister Zhuo have a chance?
"Grandma Yu went out to look for opportunities …" In fact, Daoist Dameng had also advised Ning Yudie not to try to break through to Life and Death Realm at this time. Not to mention Mister Zhuo's rebellion, this Cloud Hall didn't have a pillar to oversee the situation. If she encountered any trouble, it would be difficult to deal with it.
However, Ning Yudie insisted on breaking through at this time. The Tian Xie Sect was growing stronger day by day. She wanted to enter Life and Death Realm as soon as possible. Perhaps, she would have a chance to fight against Cui Xie!
He didn't expect that Ning Yudie's attempt to break through to Life and Death Realm would lead to such a big dispute. If not for Luo Zheng's sudden appearance, Mister Zhuo might have already led his people into the Ice Palace.
"I want to go in and take a look." Luo Zheng's face revealed a trace of worry. He himself hadn't gone through a life and death tribulation, but he already knew that the death rate was very high. Every life and death tribulation was like walking around the gates of hell. If one's strength wasn't strong enough and rashly tried to pass the tribulation, there was a high chance of dying.
"This … Palace Master strictly ordered that no one is allowed to enter the Ice Palace while she's undergoing tribulation," Daoist Dameng said.
Luo Zheng shook his head and walked towards Ning Yudie. Daoist Master Dameng originally wanted to stop him, but looking at Luo Zheng's back, he didn't dare to say anything. If it weren't for Luo Zheng, the one walking in at this time would probably be Great Teacher Zhuo. It was impossible for Luo Zheng to harm Ning Yudie, so since he wanted to go in, Daoist Master Dameng would let him.
The Ice Palace was filled with dense ice mist. After pushing open the door and entering, Luo Zheng couldn't see anything. Immediately, Luo Zheng operated his Clear Eyes Spirit Pupils and could see the scene not far away.
Ning Yudie was sitting cross-legged on the ground, wearing only a thin layer of clothing. Her entire body was encased in an icicle!
Two years …
Luo Zheng stared blankly at Ning Yudie, a trace of pity appearing on his face. In two years, Ning Yudie's appearance hadn't changed in the slightest. She still retained her incomparably beautiful appearance.
After circling around the ice pillar, Xun quietly came out of Luo Zheng's body and condensed behind him. She glanced at Ning Yudie and said indifferently, "She's already at the final juncture of breaking through to Life and Death Realm. Once she breaks through, a life and death tribulation will descend."
Luo Zheng looked at Ning Yudie and asked, "What exactly is a life and death tribulation? Is it really as terrifying as the legends say? "
"Actually, life and death tribulation is also what you Central Domain call it. In the previous session, we called this tribulation minor tribulation," Xun explained.
"Minor tribulation?"
"Yes, after Life and Death Realm, you'll face a minor tribulation. A martial artist's cultivation is originally going against the heavens, breaking through the limits of life and creating a supreme true body. Since it's going against the heavens, after cultivating to a certain point, you'll face the punishment of the heavens! The stronger the cultivation, the heavier the punishment. That's why there's a difference between minor tribulation and major tribulation! "Xun said slowly.
"Every tribulation in Life and Death Realm is a minor tribulation?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Yes," Xun sized up Ning Yudie, but suddenly shook her head, "This Ning Yudie's minor tribulation will be extremely terrifying. I'm afraid it'll be difficult for her to pass!"
"What do you mean?" Luo Zheng frowned and asked.
If it were someone else, Xun would definitely say that this person was overestimating himself. His talent was not strong enough, yet he still dared to cultivate this kind of technique. He was simply courting death.
Xun knew Luo Zheng's character very well. He was actually most concerned about Ning Yudie, so naturally, Xun would not say it so sarcastically. However, after thinking about it, she came up with the right words and said, "The technique she's cultivating is a technique that focuses on dispersing one's cultivation. This technique is very heaven-defying. Once it's cultivated, her strength will be far stronger than martial artists of the same level."
Luo Zheng nodded, "She's cultivating the 'Empyrean Fairy Heart Sutra'. This technique is also very powerful, but it's difficult for ordinary people to cultivate."
"Difficult to cultivate is only one of the problems," Xun shook her head, "In the Upper Realm, very few people cultivate techniques that require dispersing one's cultivation. This is because this type of technique will bring about a more powerful tribulation, so many martial artists choose to avoid it."
Xun's words puzzled Luo Zheng. Back then, Qinglong had also noticed that the 'Empyrean Fairy Heart Sutra' was not ordinary, but he did not tell Luo Zheng about it. "Qinglong, you didn't mention it back then!"
Qinglong, who had been silent for a long time, thought about it, then said, "Actually, there's a way to eliminate the negative effects of dispersing one's cultivation nine times … but …"
"But she's already started to break through to Life and Death Realm, so there's no chance." Xun suddenly laughed.
"Little girl, don't keep us in suspense. If that's the case, Luo Zheng has a solution too," Qinglong said again.
Luo Zheng asked curiously, "What solution? How come I don't know about it? "
When Luo Zheng asked, Qinglong and Xun fell silent again. Xun only said, "Although this minor tribulation is powerful, Ning Yudie's cultivation technique is not ordinary. It's not necessarily impossible for her to withstand the power of the minor tribulation. If she really can't withstand it, I'll tell you the solution!"
Since Xun said so, and Qinglong looked very confident, Luo Zheng did not pursue the matter. It was not the first time these guys had been so secretive. Luo Zheng had long been used to it …
Standing in the ice fog, Luo Zheng accompanied Ning Yudie for more than twenty hours. Unknowingly, a day and a night had passed. Ning Yudie still maintained the same sitting posture, sitting upright in the icicle.
At this moment, cracks finally began to appear on the huge icicle. These cracks spread rapidly. Soon, the icicle began to disintegrate little by little, turning into countless flying ice shards and falling down rapidly.
When the icicle disappeared, the sky outside the Ice Palace suddenly flashed with dazzling light, followed by deafening thunder. In the blink of an eye, the sky was covered by dark clouds!
"Is the minor tribulation coming?" Hearing the thunder outside, Luo Zheng could not help but ask.
Xun nodded and replied, "Yes, this is the vision of heaven and earth brought by the minor tribulation!"
"A minor tribulation is already so terrifying. What about the major tribulation?" Luo Zheng felt the great power coming from the sky. Even Luo Zheng felt frightened.
Xun said with a smile, "If you are powerful enough, you can trigger nine minor tribulations. Each minor tribulation will be more powerful than the one before. After passing the nine minor tribulations, you will have to face the major tribulation when you enter the Spirit Sea Realm. As for the power of the major tribulation," Xun suddenly pouted and said, "You can't imagine it for now."
The major tribulation was still far away from Luo Zheng, but the minor tribulation was approaching. As long as Luo Zheng continued to improve his cultivation and entered the peak of the False Tribulation Realm, he would also have to face the minor tribulation.
"Boom!"
At this moment, a dragon-shaped lightning struck the roof of the Ice Palace directly!
This Ice Palace was originally built like a fortress, and now the top of the Ice Palace was covered with thick icicles. However, this lightning directly blasted a big hole in the roof of the Ice Palace. Gravel, ice, broken wooden beams and other debris immediately fell into the Ice Palace.
Of course, these things could not cause any harm to Ning Yudie. She closed her eyes tightly, and streams of cold air escaped from her body. At this moment, she was facing the major tribulation of life and death from above.
She was so focused that she could not be distracted. She did not even notice that Luo Zheng was there.
Through the big hole in the roof, Luo Zheng stared at the electric dragons hovering in the sky with a serious expression and asked, "How can these electric dragons penetrate the Bounded Domain?"

Chapter 697
The lightning dragons spiraled and slowly descended from the sky, emitting a tyrannical aura that covered the entire Cloud Hall.
After experiencing the internal strife, all the warriors in the Cloud Hall were in a state of panic. Now that they were facing such an imposing aura, it was as if the whole world was in the midst of a storm!
"The Hall Master's life and death tribulation is coming!"
"I wonder if she can survive it!"
"If the Hall Master can break through to the Life and Death Realm, it will be a good thing for our Cloud Hall!"
The Life and Death Realm was the barrier between the top experts in the Central Domain and the ordinary experts!
Judging by the current number of warriors in the Central Domain, many third grade Sects had False Tribulation Realm experts, but almost none of them had Life and Death Realm experts.
As long as there was a Life and Death Realm expert, no matter if it was a family or a Sect, it could definitely enter the ranks of fourth grade Sects. However, there were very few fourth grade Sects in the entire Central Domain. In the past, there were only Cloud Hall, Blood Wood Cliff, Mysterious Yin Mansion, and a few other Sects.
Moreover, a sect like Blood Wood Cliff indeed had a Life and Death expert in the past. However, after the Life and Death expert overseeing the sect fell during the tribulation, it was difficult for the sect to give birth to a new Life and Death expert.
From this, it could be seen that a Life and Death Realm expert had a great influence on a Sect.
Of course, a Sect's grade also needed to be measured by its comprehensive strength and foundation, but the number of experts was the most direct standard.
Luo Zheng raised his head and watched the white lightning dragons slowly descending, his face full of solemnity. The lightning dragons were so powerful, could Ning Yudie resist?
Luo Zheng was very nervous, but Xun seemed to be used to this kind of scene. She slightly looked at Luo Zheng's stiff face and said with a smile, "Don't be too nervous. Since she chose to undergo the tribulation at this time, she must be fully prepared and have great confidence."
Luo Zheng nodded and his eyes fell on Ning Yudie.
In front of the slowly descending lightning dragons, Ning Yudie's originally petite body seemed particularly small.
When the lightning dragon was less than ten feet away from her head, Ning Yudie's fair hands suddenly flipped over and a small jade tablet appeared in her hand.
She gently flipped the jade tablet, and ice phoenixes shot out from the jade tablet. Then, the cold light began to expand inch by inch, spiraling into the sky!
"What a pure Power of Law!" Looking at the Ice Phoenix in the jade token, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed.
The Ice Phoenix contained pure Law of Ice, and Luo Zheng found the Law of Ice very unfamiliar. This meant that the Law of Ice didn't just contain the first layer of Law, but also the second layer of Law of Ice.
When the Ice Phoenixes rose into the air, they converged with the Lightning Dragons!
"Bang!"
When these two impossible Laws collided with each other, the light that erupted was extremely intense!
People in Ocean of Clouds City who were 15km away suddenly saw a dazzling ray of light appear on the horizon, and those who weren't aware of the situation would think that an enormous star had risen in the sky!
Only a very small portion of cultivators understood that it was a phenomenon of the heavens and the earth caused by the Hall Master of the Cloud Hall overcoming her life and death tribulation.
They were even more clearly aware that once Ning Yudie successfully overcame her life and death tribulation, then the situation in Ocean of Clouds City would probably be settled ….
Actually, the internal strife within the Cloud Hall didn't affect the Cloud Hall the most, and it affected Ocean of Clouds City instead.
Because most of the members of the Cloud Hall maintained a neutral attitude, and the cultivators who chose to rebel were practically the members of the various clans of Ocean of Clouds City.
They were very well aware of Ning Yudie's character. At first glance, Ning Yudie seemed like a delicate little girl. She wasn't tall, and she seemed petite and lovable. But in reality, this woman was extraordinarily forceful. Perhaps she wouldn't really remove all the clans from the list.
After all, there were too many connections between these clans and the Cloud Hall. If Ning Yudie did that, then the impact on the Cloud Hall would be too great.
But she would definitely eliminate the rebels. When it came to this, any Sect Master would be merciless.
It was exactly because of the connections between these Third Rank Clans and the Cloud Hall that some clans couldn't even flee. Not to mention that they were in troubled times now. Instead of fleeing, it was better to obediently pay for the mistakes they'd made. At the very least, these clans would still be under the protection of the Cloud Hall.
"Crack!"
Violent explosions resounded in Luo Zheng's ears. Luo Zheng's expression didn't change at all, and he still stared at Ning Yudie with extreme caution.
Even though these lightning dragons were extremely formidable, it was just as Xun had said. Ning Yudie had made ample preparations. The cold light emanated from the small jade token was ceaselessly weakening the might of the lightning dragons!
Every time a layer of cold light was emitted, it would intertwine with another lightning dragon. Both sides simultaneously released the power of Law, and they melted each other!
Even though there were many lightning dragons, Ning Yudie's jade token also released a large number of ice phoenixes. Every time a lightning dragon fell, an ice phoenix would rise to meet it.
From beginning to end, not a single lightning dragon fell on Ning Yudie's body. They were all offset by the ice phoenixes.
After the time it took for an incense stick to burn, the number of lightning dragons in the sky began to decrease. After a few sparse lightning dragons were offset by the ice phoenixes, Luo Zheng asked, "That's it?"
Xun shook her head, "Not necessarily. There are many types of Heavenly Tribulations, and their might is also different. Almost every martial artist encounters a different Heavenly Tribulation. Some martial artists only face a single wave of Heavenly Tribulation, while others often face several waves of Heavenly Tribulation. However, there is only one rule. The stronger one is, the stronger the Heavenly Tribulation!"
There were some legends among the people that believed that Heavenly Tribulation was a punishment sent down by the Heavens. In fact, this rumor was very absurd.
In fact, Heavenly Tribulation was a method of resistance by the Heavens!
As long as a powerhouse was born in this world, they would attract the attention of the Heavens. This was because the Life and Death Realm was a boundary in the path of cultivation. Breaking through the Life and Death Realm and opening the Spirit Sea, one would truly have the qualifications to threaten the Heavens!
And Heavenly Tribulation was precisely because the Heavens felt this threat that it would descend and eliminate this threat while it was still in its infancy!
There were big and small threats sensed by the Heavens. Some martial artists had amazing talent, and their strength was also extremely powerful. Naturally, the Heavenly Tribulation that descended would be extraordinarily powerful. However, some martial artists' talent was average. Relatively speaking, the Heavenly Tribulation wasn't that great.
In Xun's opinion, although Ning Yudie's 《Nine Heavens Fairy Heart Sutra》 was very powerful, this cultivation method itself also set up a huge obstacle for herself! Compared to other martial artists, the difficulty of her Heavenly Tribulation was much greater!
This was also one of the reasons why there were very few people in the Upper Realm who cultivated powerful cultivation methods like the 《Nine Heavens Fairy Heart Sutra》.
Sure enough, after Ning Yudie extinguished those lightning dragons, a new wave of power began to appear in the sky!
The rolling dark clouds slowly changed color, from black to red. The clouds in the sky were like burning clouds, as if there was a huge flame burning in the sky.
When the red clouds appeared, an invisible heat was suddenly transmitted. All the martial artists in the Cloud Hall felt the heat in the sky, and their faces changed greatly.
"This flame will not burn down the entire Cloud Hall, right?"
"I think the Hall Master shouldn't choose to undergo the Heavenly Tribulation in the Cloud Hall. The power of this Heavenly Tribulation is too great. After she passes it, the entire Cloud Hall will be destroyed!"
"If it's not in the Cloud Hall, where should it be? The martial artists outside are covetously watching. If it's targeted by the Lascivious Sect, it'll be over if it's even a little bit distracted during the Heavenly Tribulation! "
Everyone in the Cloud Hall channeled their True Essence, forming a thin layer of True Essence to resist the heat from the sky.
The power in the fire clouds was powerful, but after all, it was directed at Ning Yudie. The residual heat did not have much impact on the Cloud Hall.
This time, Luo Zheng did not speak. Xun was right. He really underestimated Ning Yudie. After all, he had been away for two years. How could Luo Zheng know what happened in these two years?
At this time, the rolling red clouds also circled down, tilting toward Ning Yudie's head.
Ning Yudie's expression remained the same, and with a slight flip of her finger, this time in addition to activating the jade tablet, her own True Qi also began to overflow little by little, and as soon as her finger gently pointed upwards, the entire ice palace began to tremble.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 698
As the Ice Palace continued to tremble, the ice pillars covering the Ice Palace all disintegrated, revealing thousands of palm-sized crystal stones.
Those crystal stones refracted a cold light at the same time, converging towards Ning Yudie's head.
At this time, a small Ice Phoenix shot out from the jade tablet. The Ice Phoenix began to devour the cold light. Amidst the cold light, the Ice Phoenix began to grow rapidly, eventually turning into a huge monster above Ning Yudie's head.
"Yo!"
The Ice Phoenix let out a cry and rushed towards the rolling red clouds with its head held high.
"Zi … …"
How terrifying was the power of the flames in the red clouds?
As the Ice Phoenix rushed into the red clouds, it began to melt and steam began to rise.
Although the Ice Phoenix was rapidly melting, the thousands of crystal stones in the Ice Palace continued to replenish its cold energy!
As the Ice Phoenix continued to wander in the red clouds, the cultivators in the Cloud Hall suddenly felt the heat slowly recede, and the feeling of being roasted slowly dissipated … …
Xun smiled faintly, "She was more prepared than I thought. The second wave of the Heavenly Tribulation was more violent than the first wave, but I didn't expect her to resolve it so quickly and easily!"
Indeed, this Heavenly Tribulation was much simpler than Luo Zheng imagined, "If this Heavenly Tribulation can be passed so easily, then this life and death tribulation is nothing more than this. It's not as terrifying as I imagined! Although the power of this Heavenly Tribulation is not small, even I can withstand it! "
"Pfft!" At this time, Xun suddenly covered her mouth and laughed, "You? Hehe, when it's your turn to face the minor Heavenly Tribulation, I'm afraid it will be ten times more powerful than this! "
After Xun finished speaking, she saw Luo Zheng staring at her blankly, so she asked, "What? You don't believe me? "
"I believe you," Luo Zheng replied.
"Then why are you looking at me like that?" Xun asked.
"I didn't expect you to smile like this. Your smile is very beautiful," Luo Zheng said with a faint smile.
Ever since Luo Zheng obtained the Sword Spirit, Xun had been expressionless. Although she had an almost perfect appearance, her expression was very mechanical.
Later, when Smoky began to speak in the Tower of Sin, she began to emit a dominating aura. It was as if nothing in the world could enter her eyes. Anyone who stood in front of Smoky would feel short of breath. Those with a weaker aura would not even be able to raise their heads in front of her.
However, in the Heavenly Feather Holy Land, Luo Zheng saw her agitated side. She was cursing at another King of the Demon Night Clan, and this was an eye-opener for Luo Zheng.
Today, Luo Zheng saw Xun's smile, and he suddenly felt a lot closer to her.
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Xun glared at him fiercely. Fortunately, she was now a Sword Spirit, so she didn't blush.
While the two of them were conversing, the weather in the sky changed again!
When Xun raised her head slightly, her expression changed abruptly. "The third wave is actually the Nightmare Tribulation! Why is there a Nightmare Tribulation!? "
Ever since Ning Yudie began her tribulation, Xun's expression had been very calm and relaxed. In Xun's opinion, Ning Yudie's tribulation wasn't very difficult. However, when she saw the third wave, her expression changed. Naturally, Luo Zheng's expression changed as well.
"Nightmare Tribulation?"
Xun said with a complicated expression, "There are many different types of Heavenly Tribulations. According to the statistics of the Upper Realm, there are all kinds of Heavenly Tribulations. The first wave of Heavenly Tribulation that Ning Yudie just experienced was an ordinary Thunder Dragon Tribulation, which is a very common type of Heavenly Tribulation. It's just a bit more difficult than ordinary Thunder Dragon Tribulation. The second type of tribulation is the Fire Cloud Tribulation. Ning Yudie cultivates the Law of Ice. Although the Fire Cloud Tribulation is a bit more difficult than the Lightning Dragon Tribulation, Ning Yudie has an innate advantage in her cultivation method. It won't be difficult for her to go through it! "
"But the Nightmare Tribulation is an extremely rare type of tribulation …" Xun said with a sullen expression. "I don't know what kind of fate Ning Yudie has to bear. How did she attract the Nightmare Tribulation?"
Xun said a lot in one breath, but this only made Luo Zheng even more anxious. "What's so terrifying about this Nightmare Tribulation?"
"I don't know about the Human Race. According to the statistics of the Demon Night Clan, the Nightmare Tribulation has only appeared a little over a thousand times," Xun said.
A little over a thousand times sounded like a lot, but when a cultivator entered the Life and Death Realm, they had to undergo tribulations. After the Life and Death Realm, some cultivators had to undergo tribulations three or four times, while others had to undergo tribulations seven or eight times. The Demon Night Clan controlled a vast and boundless world. How many Life and Death Realm cultivators were there in the Upper Realm? Just the number of cultivators who underwent tribulations every year was unknown. However, according to the statistics, there were only a little over a thousand Nightmare Tribulations. This was already a very small number.
"How many people have survived the Nightmare Tribulation?" Luo Zheng asked.
Xun pursed her lips and said, "One person. This person encountered the Nightmare Tribulation when he was in the Divine Sea Realm. Of course, his cultivation was different, so the Nightmare Tribulation was a hundred times more powerful. Later, this person became a Celestial Venerable!"
Xun's words immediately caused Luo Zheng to suck in a breath of cold air. Originally, the number of martial artists who encountered the Nightmare Tribulation was already quite rare, but in these thousand over Nightmare Tribulations, there was only one person who survived? The probability was too small.
Green petals began to bloom in the sky. Those petals kept circling, slowly rolling toward Ning Yudie.
"Didn't you just say that I have a way to break the Heavenly Tribulation?" Luo Zheng asked immediately.
Xun nodded and said, "If Ning Yudie can successfully pass the Heavenly Tribulation, she won't have to use that thing. If she can't, she will have to use it."
Luo Zheng immediately guessed what Xun was referring to. "You mean the Fragment of the Heavenly Way?"
"Yes." Xun nodded.
"How do I use it?" Luo Zheng wanted to take out the Fragment of the Heavenly Way and go up now to help Ning Yudie share the pressure of the Heavenly Tribulation.
However, Xun stopped him. "Let's wait and see. Don't worry. Although the Heavenly Tribulation is a cultivator's tribulation, it is also a way to temper oneself. If Ning Yudie can't withstand it, you can go up …"
"Is the Fragment of the Heavenly Way used to resist the Heavenly Tribulation?" Luo Zheng asked.
He knew that this thing was very important, but Luo Zheng still didn't understand.
Xun smiled faintly. "If the Fragment of the Heavenly Way is only used to resist the Heavenly Tribulation, it's overkill. This Fragment of the Heavenly Way is actually not the Fragment of the current Heavenly Way."
"The current Heavenly Way? What do you mean? "This statement was really new, and Luo Zheng didn't understand.
Xun said faintly, "The current Heavenly Way is the Firmament, and the Fragment of the Heavenly Way in your hand is the Fragment of the previous Heavenly Way, which is the Winning Heaven!"
"These Heavenly Ways are different?" Luo Zheng was stunned.
At this moment, those petals floated down one by one. Luo Zheng didn't have time to ask about this. Instead, he looked at Ning Yudie, who was in the center of the ice palace, with a face full of concern.
Ning Yudie also felt the power of this Life and Death Tribulation, and her face was full of caution. She gently spread her hands, and the huge Ice Phoenix let out a long cry, turned around, and rushed toward Ning Yudie!
In the blink of an eye, Ning Yudie and the Ice Phoenix merged into one. Luo Zheng also knew that this was Ning Yudie's most complete form. The Ice Phoenix turned into layers of close-fitting armor that covered her body!
After the two merged, Ning Yudie's figure suddenly flashed, and then she soared into the sky, heading straight for the petals.
"These petals don't look scary?" Compared to the previous two Heavenly Tribulations, Luo Zheng didn't feel any power from these petals. Instead, the petals that filled the sky were like rain. What power did these petals have?
"Each petal is a Nightmare Tribulation, and the reason why this Nightmare Tribulation is so difficult to resolve is because it does not target a cultivator's physical body, but rather the soul itself," Xun frowned as she looked at Ning Yudie in the sky.
Just like what Xun had said, those petals looked like ordinary petals, and with just a light touch from Ning Yudie, the petals broke apart. However, after each petal was broken, it was like a nightmare, forming a series of illusions that rushed straight into Ning Yudie's mind!

Chapter 699
A martial artist's soul was the most difficult part to cultivate. Very few martial artists were like Luo Zheng, who could cultivate their body and dantian while simultaneously cultivating their soul.
Ning Yudie's soul was slightly stronger than an ordinary martial artist's, but it was far from Luo Zheng's.
If there were only a few dozen Nightmares attacking Luo Zheng's soul, she could still withstand it. However, there were hundreds, or even thousands of Nightmares in front of her. How could Ning Yudie withstand it?
After a few breaths of time, Ning Yudie's face was as pale as paper, and her body began to stagger. It was difficult for her to resist.
"It's time to make a move!" Seeing Ning Yudie's appearance, Luo Zheng began to worry. Once the soul was injured, it would have a great impact on a martial artist. It could even cause irreversible damage.
"Wait a little longer," Xun still tried to stop him. "Although this Nightmare Tribulation is attacking Ning Yudie, it is also tempering her. This kind of tempering is also beneficial for her."
All martial artists grew through tempering, but Xun understood how to grasp the propriety of it.
How could Luo Zheng not understand this? However, as the saying goes, 'Concern leads to confusion'. He was concerned about Ning Yudie, so how could he bear to see her get hurt?
Just as the petals continued to hit Ning Yudie, she suddenly took out a bronze-colored medicinal pill and swallowed it. Then, Ning Yudie's face flashed with a touch of red!
Before breaking through to the Life and Death Realm, Ning Yudie had repeatedly reminded herself that she could only succeed in breaking through to the Life and Death Realm.
Now that the Tian Xie Sect was rising in the east, it had become the biggest threat in the Central Domain. The entire Central Domain had fallen into chaos because of this. And because her master was facing a life and death calamity, she had no choice but to go overseas to look for opportunities!
Ning Yudie and Granny Yu knew this very well, so did Granny Yu. Therefore, when Granny Yu left, she explained everything about the Cloud Hall before leaving.
When Granny Yu left, she let out a long sigh. She knew that this sigh was for herself, and also for Luo Zheng.
If only that little guy was still here …
Ever since the trip to the Heavenly Mist Immortal Tomb and Luo Zheng's strange disappearance, Ning Yudie had gone to the Chaotic Astral Ocean to search for him several times, but every time she failed.
However, she firmly believed that Luo Zheng would definitely not fall. This brat would definitely return one day!
Unfortunately, two years had passed in the blink of an eye, and Luo Zheng was nowhere to be found. She couldn't find any news about Luo Zheng in the vast central region. It was as if he had completely vanished into thin air. Even though she believed it, she was still worried.
Now that Granny Yu was missing in the Central Domain, Ning Yudie's choice to break through to the Life and Death Realm was actually a gamble!
In order to break through to the Life and Death Realm, Ning Yudie had made ample preparations. She had used almost all the resources in the Cloud Hall. For example, the jade token in her hand was borrowed from the World Business Alliance at a great cost.
If it were any other time, no matter how much Ning Yudie paid, it would be impossible for her to receive this jade token. This kind of jade token could increase the chances of passing the Life and Death Tribulation by 20%. It was a fatal attraction to any late stage False Tribulation Realm martial artist.
When Granny Yu wanted to borrow it, she was flatly rejected by the World Business Alliance.
However, with the rise of the Tian Xie Sect like a comet, the World Business Alliance became anxious. Clearly, in the entire Central Domain, only the fourth grade and above Sects could resist the Tian Xie Sect. The first to bear the brunt were the Void Spirit Sect, the World Business Alliance, and the Cloud Hall.
Normally, the competition between these three Sects was quite fierce. However, when they faced the same powerful enemy, they could only choose to band together.
One more Life and Death Realm martial artist meant one more chance to fight against Cui Xie. Even a child would understand this principle, so how could the ancient tycoon of the World Business Alliance not understand it?
After obtaining this jade token, Ning Yudie finally had the confidence to break through to the Life and Death Realm.
When facing the first and second waves, Ning Yudie was indeed very successful. It was so successful that she found it hard to believe. Generally speaking, after breaking through to the Life and Death Realm, the first test of the Life and Death Tribulation wouldn't be too difficult. Some Life and Death Realm martial artists would only face one wave before passing the test.
However, Ning Yudie had to face two waves of the Life and Death Tribulation, which already exceeded the difficulty of ordinary Life and Death Tribulations. She didn't expect that she would face the third wave of the Life and Death Tribulation!
However, the third wave of the Life and Death Tribulation was actually a soul attack!
From an outsider's point of view, the petals that hit her body didn't seem to cause any harm. However, to Ning Yudie, it was a great torture!
Every time a petal hit her body, an illusory nightmare would appear in her mind. The nightmare would corrode her soul, bringing her a heart-wrenching pain.
"This, why is the third wave of the Life and Death Tribulation so difficult?" Ning Yudie's face was full of confusion!
However, although she was a woman, her heart of martial arts didn't lose to any male martial artist. Since she had chosen to break through to the Life and Death Realm, there was no turning back. Right now, she could only endure it bit by bit!
However, the pain brought about by the corrosion of the soul wasn't something that an average person could endure.
The physical pain would slowly become numb, and a person might even faint from the pain. In fact, this was also a kind of self-protection ability.
However, the pain of the soul couldn't be avoided. This pain was very subtle, but also incomparably real!
In a moment of desperation, Ning Yudie swallowed a "True Soul Pill". This pill was refined from the brain of a Shadow Beast. It could double the strength of the soul for a short period of time! The effect of this pill was heaven-defying, but it was also very precious. This "True Soul Pill" was the ancestral treasure of the Cloud Hall. It had been passed down for hundreds of years, and Ning Yudie swallowed it in one gulp.
Ever since Ning Yudie stepped into the Cloud Hall Hall Master's position, she had indeed squandered a lot of the Cloud Hall's resources. For example, when Luo Zheng was on the Rising Dragon Arena, Ning Yudie had used the Ci Lie Beast's Law Eye to block Cui Xie's punch.
However, the resources that Ning Yudie had consumed also brought many things to the Cloud Hall. Now, the Cloud Hall was a fifth-grade Sect. Moreover, because of Luo Zheng, the Cloud Hall had an indestructible Sect Protecting Array.
If Ning Yudie could break through to the Life and Death Realm this time, the Cloud Hall might even become a sixth-grade Sect in the future, becoming the first Holy Land in the Central Domain.
Therefore, Ning Yudie swallowed the "True Soul Pill" without hesitation.
Just as she swallowed the "True Soul Pill", a powerful medicinal power immediately poured into her body. She suddenly felt her soul become a lot stronger, and her perception of her surroundings also became a lot clearer.
However, Ning Yudie didn't want to waste the slightest bit of soul power to perceive her surroundings. If she did so, she would discover that Luo Zheng was already standing in her Ice Palace.
Facing a life and death calamity, Ning Yudie didn't dare to be careless. She completely ignored everything in the outside world and focused all her attention to resist these damned petals.
After Ning Yudie swallowed the "True Soul Pill", the corrosive effect of the flower petals on Ning Yudie was indeed weakened. Her eyes, which were already on the verge of collapse, regained their luster as she endured the attack of the flower petals.
At this time, Ning Yudie had already endured the attacks of a hundred Nightmare Demons.
However, the petals in front of her seemed endless, and she wasn't sure if she could really endure it.
After an incense stick's worth of time, Ning Yudie's originally rosy cheeks turned pale bit by bit.
"I still can't endure it?" A trace of sorrow appeared on Ning Yudie's face. She could feel the medicinal effect of the True Soul Pill slowly fading away, but the petals in front of her were endless and didn't show any signs of weakening.
Of course, Ning Yudie didn't know how hard it was to endure this Nightmare Demon Calamity. At this time, she only thought that her strength wasn't enough.
Following the attacks of the Nightmare Demons, Ning Yudie gradually couldn't endure it anymore. She felt as if her head was stuffed with balls of fire, as if her entire head was about to be burnt.
"Did I still fail?" A sad expression appeared on Ning Yudie's face. She was very clear about the consequences of failure. The vast majority of the Life and Death Realm martial artists in the Central Domain didn't die from the exhaustion of their lifespan, but from the great calamity of life and death.

Chapter 700
Ning Yudie narrowed her eyes slightly. At this time, her mind was already in a mess. Everything she saw was a blur.
This was what happened after the soul was damaged. Like a drunk person, it was easy to hallucinate.
She actually saw Luo Zheng …
